# crossly

Crossly - the Ruby gem for the Crossly Seller API

The seller-facing Crossly API — listings, crossposting, orders, analytics. Authenticate with a Personal Access Token (`Authorization: Bearer crossly_pat_…`) or a seller OAuth token (`crossly_oat_…`), scoped `<domain>:<read|write>`. Buyer endpoints are a SEPARATE API with a separate principal — see the Crossly Buyer API.

This SDK is automatically generated by the [OpenAPI Generator](https://openapi-generator.tech) project:

- API version: 1.0.0
- Package version: 0.3.0
- Build package: org.openapitools.codegen.languages.RubyClientCodegen

## Installation

### Build a gem

To build the Ruby code into a gem:

```shell
gem build crossly.gemspec
```

Then either install the gem locally:

```shell
gem install ./crossly-0.3.0.gem
```

(for development, run `gem install --dev ./crossly-0.3.0.gem` to install the development dependencies)

or publish the gem to a gem hosting service, e.g. [RubyGems](https://rubygems.org/).

Finally add this to the Gemfile:

    gem 'crossly', '~> 0.3.0'

### Install from Git

If the Ruby gem is hosted at a git repository: https://github.com/GIT_USER_ID/GIT_REPO_ID, then add the following in the Gemfile:

    gem 'crossly', :git => 'https://github.com/GIT_USER_ID/GIT_REPO_ID.git'

### Include the Ruby code directly

Include the Ruby code directly using `-I` as follows:

```shell
ruby -Ilib script.rb
```

## Getting Started

Please follow the [installation](#installation) procedure and then run the following code:

```ruby
# Load the gem
require 'crossly'

# Setup authorization
Crossly.configure do |config|
  # Configure Bearer authorization: PersonalAccessToken
  config.access_token = 'YOUR_BEARER_TOKEN'
end

api_instance = Crossly::AIApi.new
create_ai_categorize_request = Crossly::CreateAiCategorizeRequest.new({image_url: 'image_url_example'}) # CreateAiCategorizeRequest | 

begin
  #Taxonomy guess from a single image URL.
  result = api_instance.create_ai_categorize(create_ai_categorize_request)
  p result
rescue Crossly::ApiError => e
  puts "Exception when calling AIApi->create_ai_categorize: #{e}"
end

```

*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**create_email_event_confirm**](docs/ActivityApi.md#create_email_event_confirm) | **POST** /v1/email-events/{id}/confirm | Confirm an email-detected event: re-run it as certain (a possible sale records the sale and delists the item elsewhere). When several listings could be meant, pass platformListingDbId to choose one.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**create_email_event_dismiss**](docs/ActivityApi.md#create_email_event_dismiss) | **POST** /v1/email-events/{id}/dismiss | Dismiss an email-detected event (\"not a sale\"): closes the card without acting.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**create_email_event_reprocess**](docs/ActivityApi.md#create_email_event_reprocess) | **POST** /v1/email-events/{id}/reprocess | Re-process a marketplace email with the current templates (one Crossly did not recognise, could not finish, or ignored). Refused (409) for an email that already changed something, or once its 30-day copy is gone.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**create_event_policy_decision_confirm**](docs/ActivityApi.md#create_event_policy_decision_confirm) | **POST** /v1/event-policies/decisions/{id}/confirm | Confirm a review decision from a webhook, order poller or app relay (canConfirm true): the event runs again with the seller confirmed, so a review rule runs the strongest allowed non-review action for that cell. Returns the new decision. 409 when the decision was already confirmed or is not confirmable (email review decisions are confirmed on their review card).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**delete_detected_event_rule**](docs/ActivityApi.md#delete_detected_event_rule) | **DELETE** /v1/detected-events/rules/{id} | Delete a detection rule. Deleting your edit of a default (its id, or default:<key>) brings the shipped default back; deleting a translated event-policy rule deletes that policy rule too.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**delete_event_policy_rule**](docs/ActivityApi.md#delete_event_policy_rule) | **DELETE** /v1/event-policies/rules/{id} | Delete a rule: its cell falls back to the next scope (platform → global → the built-in default).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_action_log**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_action_log) | **GET** /v1/action-log/{id} | One Activity Log event by id, with `view` including its steps and, for bulk jobs, its children.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_action_log_export**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_action_log_export) | **GET** /v1/action-log/exports/{exportId} | The state of one Activity Log export.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_action_log_export_download**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_action_log_export_download) | **GET** /v1/action-log/exports/{exportId}/download | Download a finished Activity Log export (for 24 hours after it completes).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_action_log_facet**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_action_log_facet) | **GET** /v1/action-log/facets | Filter options of the caller's Activity Log (last 90 days): areas with their actions and counts, the team members who acted, platforms and outcomes.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_detected_event_event**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_detected_event_event) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/events/{id} | One detected event with every report of it (first + corroborations, with correlation and contradiction), the guards that held actions back, the plan and each executed action's outcome and reversibility.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_detected_event_overview**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_detected_event_overview) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/overview | What Crossly does today in each situation for the store, computed from the store's real rules the way the engine decides: per situation its topic, title, the outcome in words, the rule ref that decides it, whether it differs from the recommended default, and narrower rules (one marketplace, account or app) that decide it differently there. Also the headline situations per topic, the starting point (hands_on / balanced / hands_off / custom), how many rules are your own and how many events wait for you. The store is X-Storefront-Id (omitted: the default store).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_detected_event_rule**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_detected_event_rule) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/rules/{ref} | One detection rule by its ref: a stored rule's id, or default.<key> for a situation (the store's effective rule for it: your edit when there is one, else the shipped default). Returns the rule, the situation it decides (topic, title, why it is the default), the choice cards offered for that situation (each a complete rule body, the recommended one first) and which card the rule is (custom when none). A retired key (order.notify, account) opens the key it was split into. 404 when the ref names nothing.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_detected_event_starting_point**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_detected_event_starting_point) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/starting-point | The store's starting point: hands_on, balanced (recommended), hands_off, or custom once a default was edited by hand. Preview a change with POST starting-point/preview, apply it with PUT starting-point.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_email_event**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_email_event) | **GET** /v1/email-events/{id} | One marketplace email Crossly read: what it did with it (outcome), the fields the template extracted, and the mailbox it came from.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_email_event_preview**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_email_event_preview) | **GET** /v1/email-events/{id}/preview | A marketplace email's sanitized preview: text only, buyer address lines hidden, links without their query. Crossly keeps each email 30 days; after that this is a 409.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**get_event_policy**](docs/ActivityApi.md#get_event_policy) | **GET** /v1/event-policies | What Crossly does with each detected marketplace event (sale, listing ended, offer, cancellation, label, shipped, delivered, refund, return, payout, message), per who owns the item (crossly, imported, app_managed, other_tool, untracked). Each matrix cell lists the allowed actions and the effective one, with the scope it comes from (app > platform > global > default). Pass platform (or appId) to see the matrix as that platform's (or app's) items see it. Also returns the rules, platforms and connected apps.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_action_log**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_action_log) | **GET** /v1/action-log | List Activity Log events, newest first. Each event carries `view` (title, sentence, fields, failure advice, who did it) and typed `detail`. Page with `cursor`; `pagination.total` counts matches.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_action_log_calls**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_action_log_calls) | **GET** /v1/action-log/{id}/calls | The outbound platform HTTP calls under an event (oldest first) — platform, operation, method, host, status, latency, error class/message, proxy + recipe id. Answers \"what was sent / what went wrong\". The URL path, request/response bodies and persisted-query hash are never returned.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_action_log_histogram**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_action_log_histogram) | **GET** /v1/action-log/histogram | Events per UTC day for the list filters (default: the last 30 days; at most 366).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_detected_event_events**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_detected_event_events) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/events | Every event Crossly detected (sales, order changes, listing changes, offers, messages, account notices), newest first: all its axes, the rule that decided it, its corroborating reports, the plan per target and how each action went, with canConfirm (an open ask) and canUndo. Cursor-paged; filter by kinds, platforms, statuses, accounts and channels (comma-separated), one item, listing, order or deciding rule, and since (ISO time). statuses=asked is what waits for you; dismissed is an ask closed without acting. Rows from before the rules update carry legacy: true and have no actions. Per store: X-Storefront-Id (omitted: the default store, which also keeps events recorded before stores existed).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_detected_event_rules**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_detected_event_rules) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/rules | The seller's detection rules, top-down in the order they are evaluated (first match wins unless a rule says continue): the seller's own rules, translations of their event-policy rules (source legacy), then the shipped default set (source default, ids default:<key>; a stored edit of a default replaces it). Each rule has a filter (event kinds, platforms, accounts, apps, tags, items), an optional condition over any event axis, the actions it runs (then / else) and its guard loosenings.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_detected_event_situations**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_detected_event_situations) | **GET** /v1/detected-events/situations | Every situation Crossly decides (a sale of an item in Crossly, a sale not in Crossly with no match, a return opened, a connection lost…), grouped by topic (sales, orders, listings, buyers, account): its title, whether it is a headline row of the Overview, why the default is what it is, its rule ref (default.<key>) and the choice cards offered for it. To pick a card, PUT its body to the rule default:<key>. The same catalog for every seller; GET overview says what each does today.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_email_events**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_email_events) | **GET** /v1/email-events | List email-detected events waiting for the seller (status=needs_review): a possible sale, ended listing, cancellation or other event an email reported that was not certain enough to act on automatically.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**list_event_policy_decisions**](docs/ActivityApi.md#list_event_policy_decisions) | **GET** /v1/event-policies/decisions | The decision log, newest first: for each detected event, what the rule or default asked for, what was done (after capability fallback and the email confidence ceiling), the rule that decided it, and the outcome (done / skipped / failed / review). Cursor-paged; filter by event, ownership, platform or outcome (outcome=review: still waiting for the seller). Kept 90 days.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**update_detected_event_rule**](docs/ActivityApi.md#update_detected_event_rule) | **PUT** /v1/detected-events/rules/{id} | Replace a detection rule. An id default:<key> stores your edit of that shipped default (delete it, or reset-defaults, to go back to the shipped one).
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**update_detected_event_starting_point**](docs/ActivityApi.md#update_detected_event_starting_point) | **PUT** /v1/detected-events/starting-point | Apply a starting point to the store: rewrites the store's untouched default rules as ordinary rules (balanced removes them, so the shipped defaults apply). Defaults you edited and your own rules stay as they are; starting points do not stack. Logged to Activity. Returns the starting point now in effect.
*Crossly::ActivityApi* | [**update_event_policy_rule**](docs/ActivityApi.md#update_event_policy_rule) | **PUT** /v1/event-policies/rules | Set what Crossly does with one kind of detected event for one kind of item: scope global (scopeValue null), platform (scopeValue = platform id) or app (scopeValue = OAuth app id, app_managed items only). Replaces the rule for the same cell and scope. The action must be one the matrix allows for that cell; import actions exist only for untracked items.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_ad_offsite_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_ad_offsite_campaign) | **POST** /v1/ads/offsite/campaigns | Launch an offsite campaign for an item.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_ad_offsite_resume**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_ad_offsite_resume) | **POST** /v1/ads/offsite/resume | Clear an auto-pause and resume offsite spend.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_listing_ad_bulk_add**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_listing_ad_bulk_add) | **POST** /v1/listing-ads/bulk-add | Add listings to a campaign by master listing id: each listing's live row on `platform` joins campaignId (a Crossly campaign uuid, or the marketplace's campaign id). A listing not live there is its own failed result.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_listing_ad_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_listing_ad_campaign) | **POST** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/campaigns | Create a campaign on the marketplace: program (promoted_listing_rate = eBay General, a % of the sale; cpc_campaign = eBay Priority, a daily budget), name, ratePct or dailyBudgetCents, startsAt/endsAt, and the program fields. Idempotency-Key supported.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_listing_ad_campaign_listing**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_listing_ad_campaign_listing) | **POST** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/campaigns/{campaignId}/listings | Promote listings in a marketplace campaign. platformListingIds are Crossly platform-listing ids on that marketplace; ratePct sets the ad rate (eBay General). One result per listing.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_listing_ad_campaign_listing_remove**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_listing_ad_campaign_listing_remove) | **POST** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/campaigns/{campaignId}/listings/remove | Stop promoting listings in a marketplace campaign. One result per listing.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_sponsored_ad_billing_topup_link**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_sponsored_ad_billing_topup_link) | **POST** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/topup-link | A Stripe Checkout link that adds amountCents ($5–$10,000) to the ad balance when the seller pays it. Nothing is charged until they do. Use an Idempotency-Key to get the same link back on a retry.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_sponsored_ad_boost_remove**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_sponsored_ad_boost_remove) | **POST** /v1/sponsored-ads/boosts/remove | Stop boosting listings in bulk.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_sponsored_ad_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_sponsored_ad_campaign) | **POST** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns | Create a Sponsored Listings campaign: name, dailyBudgetCents (≥100), optional totalBudgetCents, defaultBidCents (5–10000; the max bid under bidStrategy \"auto\"), placements (search, browse, product_page, home_rail, storefront, worlds_surface), goal (sales | visits), optional targetCategories / targetRegions (ISO countries) / targetDevices (mobile, desktop, app), optional startsAt/endsAt, listings [{platformListingId, bidCents?, boostBps? (100–2000, pay-on-sale Boost; null clears)}], keywords [{keyword, matchType broad|phrase|exact, negative?, bidCents?}], worldTargets [{kind showroom|convention, id, bidCents?, status?}] (your showroom or a convention you host, promoted in the Crossly World directory; at least one listing or world target), status active|draft. A catalog boost: targetType \"wholesale_catalog\" + targetCatalogId (your catalog), no listings, keywords or world targets; it ranks Sponsored in the wholesale directory. bidStrategy defaults to your account default. Clicks are charged from your ad funds (ad balance and/or CBX, ad credit first); with none it waits as out_of_budget.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_sponsored_ad_campaign_duplicate**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_sponsored_ad_campaign_duplicate) | **POST** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns/{id}/duplicate | Copy a campaign (settings, listings, keywords) as a new draft.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**create_sponsored_ad_campaign_status**](docs/AdsApi.md#create_sponsored_ad_campaign_status) | **POST** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns/{id}/status | Set a campaign active (launch/resume), paused, or ended (final). Resuming with no ad funds to pay for clicks leaves it out_of_budget until you top up.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_ad_offsite**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_ad_offsite) | **GET** /v1/ads/offsite | Your offsite-ads opt-in and its terms.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_ad_offsite_eligibility**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_ad_offsite_eligibility) | **GET** /v1/ads/offsite/eligibility | Whether an item can run offsite, and why not.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_ad_offsite_report**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_ad_offsite_report) | **GET** /v1/ads/offsite/report | What your offsite budget bought — including the misses.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_listing_ad_listing**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_listing_ad_listing) | **GET** /v1/listing-ads/listings/{listingId} | Every promotion of one listing: its Crossly campaigns and Boost, and each marketplace ad with status, campaign, rate and last sync.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_billing**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_billing) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing | What pays for your ads: headroom across the billing rails (ad balance and/or CBX), unbilled click spend, settlement charges, daily spend, Boost fees and CBX ad-credit grants; funds is the full funds summary (same as /v1/sponsored-ads/funds). refresh=true re-reads the balance.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_billing_statement**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_billing_statement) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/statements/{month} | One month's ads statement: opening and closing ad balance, top-ups, click spend by campaign, how clicks were paid, credits, refunds and Boost fees.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_billing_statement_pdf**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_billing_statement_pdf) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/statements/{month}/pdf | One month's ads statement as a PDF.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_billing_topup_receipt**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_billing_topup_receipt) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/topups/{id}/receipt | One ad balance top-up's receipt as a PDF (each top-up in the billing history carries its id and receipt number).
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_campaign) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns/{id} | One campaign: settings, its listings (bid, suggested bid, live, per-listing stats) and keywords. A catalog boost (targetType wholesale_catalog) has `catalogs`: per boosted catalog impressions, clicks, spend, attributed trade orders and revenue, CTR and ROAS.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_fund**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_fund) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/funds | What can pay for clicks now: the billing rails in effect (ad balance and/or CBX), the ad balance and ad credit, CBX headroom, unbilled clicks, available cents, auto top-up, the saved payment method and the refundable balance. refresh=true re-reads CBX.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_report**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_report) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/report | Sponsored report for a range: totals (spend, impressions, clicks, CTR, average CPC, attributed orders and revenue, ROAS), a zero-filled daily series, per-campaign rows (targetType listings | wholesale_catalog) and `catalogs`, one row per boosted catalog. Listing orders are Crossly purchases of the advertised listing within 7 days of a click by the same buyer; catalog orders are trade orders with that seller within 7 days of a catalog click by the same buyer. Catalog impressions count once per viewer per day.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**get_sponsored_ad_setting**](docs/AdsApi.md#get_sponsored_ad_setting) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/settings | Ads quick settings: adsPaused, accountDailyCapCents and spentTodayCents, defaultBidStrategy, autoTopup.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_listing_ad_campaigns**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_listing_ad_campaigns) | **GET** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/campaigns | Your ad campaigns on one marketplace (cached up to 15 minutes; refresh=true asks the marketplace now).
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_listing_ad_platforms**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_listing_ad_platforms) | **GET** /v1/listing-ads/platforms | The ads registry: each marketplace that sells promotion, its programs (kind, scope, campaign and listing fields), which verbs are wired / need a capture / are unsupported, and whether you are connected there.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_sponsored_ad_billing_history**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_sponsored_ad_billing_history) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/history | Billing history newest first: top-ups (status, receipt number), click charges (how each was paid: ad balance, credit, CBX, written off), credits, refunds and Boost fees. kind filters; before (ISO time) pages; limit ≤ 100.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_sponsored_ad_billing_statements**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_sponsored_ad_billing_statements) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/statements | Months (YYYY-MM) with any ad billing activity, newest first.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_sponsored_ad_boosts**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_sponsored_ad_boosts) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/boosts | Boosted (pay-on-sale) listings: the rate, sales and fees taken so far. A boost lifts organic ranking by up to 10% and costs its rate only when the listing sells on Crossly.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_sponsored_ad_campaigns**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_sponsored_ad_campaigns) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns | Your Sponsored Listings campaigns with status, pacing (spent today, daily cap reached), budgets, bids, placements and stats for the range (from/to, YYYY-MM-DD; default last 30 days).
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**list_sponsored_ad_listings**](docs/AdsApi.md#list_sponsored_ad_listings) | **GET** /v1/sponsored-ads/listings | Per-listing ad performance across campaigns for a range, plus suggestions: live listings with views but few sales that are not sponsored yet, with a suggested bid.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_ad_offsite**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_ad_offsite) | **PUT** /v1/ads/offsite | Turn offsite ads on or off. Yours alone to set.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_listing_ad_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_listing_ad_campaign) | **PATCH** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/campaigns/{campaignId} | Change a marketplace campaign: name, dailyBudgetCents, endsAt (null clears), status paused | running | ended.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_listing_ad_listing_rate**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_listing_ad_listing_rate) | **PUT** /v1/listing-ads/{platform}/listings/{platformListingId}/rate | Change one promoted listing's ad rate (percent of the sale) in the campaign it is in.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_sponsored_ad_billing_auto_topup**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_sponsored_ad_billing_auto_topup) | **PUT** /v1/sponsored-ads/billing/auto-topup | Auto top-up: enabled, thresholdCents (top up when the ad balance drops below it), amountCents (how much). Needs a saved payment method (the first top-up saves it).
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_sponsored_ad_boost**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_sponsored_ad_boost) | **PUT** /v1/sponsored-ads/boosts | Boost listings in bulk (platformListingIds, upliftBps 100–2000 = 1–20%), or change their rate. Your own live Crossly listings only.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_sponsored_ad_campaign**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_sponsored_ad_campaign) | **PATCH** /v1/sponsored-ads/campaigns/{id} | Edit a campaign. Any of the create fields; listings and keywords, when given, replace the current set. Raising the total budget resumes a campaign stopped by it.
*Crossly::AdsApi* | [**update_sponsored_ad_setting**](docs/AdsApi.md#update_sponsored_ad_setting) | **PATCH** /v1/sponsored-ads/settings | Change ads quick settings: adsPaused (pause / resume every ad), accountDailyCapCents (null = no cap), defaultBidStrategy (auto | manual) for new campaigns, autoTopup {enabled, thresholdCents?, amountCents?}, railPreference (cash_first | cbx_first, both rails live only).
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**create_analytic_experiment**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#create_analytic_experiment) | **POST** /v1/analytics/experiments | Start a listing experiment. split: 2-4 live platform listings observed over the same window; sequential: one platform listing, before vs after changeAt (optionally applying a new price).
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**create_analytic_experiment_declare**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#create_analytic_experiment_declare) | **POST** /v1/analytics/experiments/{id}/declare | Record the outcome: a winning arm, no difference, or inconclusive. Concludes the experiment.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**create_analytic_experiment_stop**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#create_analytic_experiment_stop) | **POST** /v1/analytics/experiments/{id}/stop | Stop an experiment: no more data is counted after now.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**delete_analytic_experiment**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#delete_analytic_experiment) | **DELETE** /v1/analytics/experiments/{id} | Delete an experiment. The listings are untouched.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_bookkeeping**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_bookkeeping) | **GET** /v1/analytics/bookkeeping | Monthly P&L + per-platform breakdown for a calendar year.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_by_platform**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_by_platform) | **GET** /v1/analytics/by-platform | Sales, net sales, fees and net profit by platform for the last N days.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_compare**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_compare) | **GET** /v1/analytics/compare | Compare 2-4 of your listings: columns of price, quality, channels, views, favorites, offers and outcome, engagement curves, the fields that differ, and observational tests of favorite rate, view-to-offer rate and views per day.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_dashboard**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_dashboard) | **GET** /v1/analytics/dashboard | Composite dashboard: KPIs + breakdowns + recent activity.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_experiment**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_experiment) | **GET** /v1/analytics/experiments | Your listing A/B experiments, newest first, with status, days running and the current verdict.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_experiment_by_id**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_experiment_by_id) | **GET** /v1/analytics/experiments/{id} | One experiment: arms with their windows, exposure and rates, the primary-metric test per arm against A, guardrail tests, the daily series and what to do next.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_inventory**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_inventory) | **GET** /v1/analytics/inventory | Unsold stock: aging buckets with cost, listed value and market value, the 20 listings most in need of attention, shelf value over time, cohort sell-through at the chosen maturity, listed vs sold velocity and data health.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_item**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_item) | **GET** /v1/analytics/items | Per-item P&L for sold inventory.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_item_by_listing_id**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_item_by_listing_id) | **GET** /v1/analytics/items/{listingId} | One listing in depth: channels with views, favorites and offers, the merged timeline, engagement series, price history, comps, money waterfall, days on market, offers, messages, experiments and suggestions.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_marketplace**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_marketplace) | **GET** /v1/analytics/marketplaces | Every marketplace side by side: net sales and profit, units, ASP, take rate, margin, shipping subsidy, days to sell, sell-through, cancel and return rates, offers and engagement; with vs=2-3 platforms, a head-to-head over listings live on all of them.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_marketplace_by_platform**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_marketplace_by_platform) | **GET** /v1/analytics/marketplaces/{platform} | One marketplace in depth: its row, the money series, fee breakdown, days-to-sell and price-realization histograms, categories, top items, at-risk listings, a day-by-hour sales heatmap and offer stats.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_overview**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_overview) | **GET** /v1/analytics/overview | KPIs with deltas (net sales, net profit, units, ASP, margin, sell-through, median days to sell, active listings, take rate), the trend series, by-marketplace split, top categories, brands and items, sell-through cohort, data health and insights.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_performance**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_performance) | **GET** /v1/analytics/performance | Per-listing performance: channels, views, favorites, offers and conversations in the period, units sold, net sales and profit, margin, days to sell and a 0-100 health score, paged and sortable.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_program**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_program) | **GET** /v1/analytics/programs/{program} | One program's report over a date range (default the last 30 days, at most 366): KPIs, a daily trend and a breakdown table. Money in integer cents.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_segment**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_segment) | **GET** /v1/analytics/segments | Niches along one dimension: listed and active counts, units sold, cohort sell-through, median days to sell, net sales and profit, margin, ROI, ASP and discount from ask; rows under minN are flagged lowSample.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_segment_by_key**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_segment_by_key) | **GET** /v1/analytics/segments/{dim}/{key} | One niche in depth: its row, series, by-marketplace split, days-to-sell histogram, price bands, top items and the 10 oldest unsold listings in it.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_summary**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_summary) | **GET** /v1/analytics/summary | Headline KPIs for the last N days (the same numbers as the analytics overview).
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_timesery**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_timesery) | **GET** /v1/analytics/timeseries | Sales and net sales over the last N days, zero-filled, one row per calendar day.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**get_analytic_today**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#get_analytic_today) | **GET** /v1/analytics/today | Today's checklist + 14-day activity streak.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**list_insight_by_platform**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#list_insight_by_platform) | **GET** /v1/insights/by-platform | Platform velocity + margin insight (90-day window): units, net sales, median days to sell, ASP and net after the real marketplace fees.
*Crossly::AnalyticsApi* | [**update_analytic_experiment**](docs/AnalyticsApi.md#update_analytic_experiment) | **PATCH** /v1/analytics/experiments/{id} | Rename an experiment or change its hypothesis, notes or planned end.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_command_ack**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_command_ack) | **POST** /v1/listing-commands/{id}/ack | Acknowledge a command (you will act on it). Extends its deadline: 24 h for listing commands; tracking 2 h, cancel 4 h, refund 24 h, message 1 h.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_command_complete**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_command_complete) | **POST** /v1/listing-commands/{id}/complete | Report a command done on the marketplace. relist: newExternalId when a new id was issued; refund_order: platformRefundId + amount (required); submit_tracking: trackingNumber/carrier as the marketplace holds them; message_buyer: platformMessageId.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_command_fail**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_command_fail) | **POST** /v1/listing-commands/{id}/fail | Report a command failed, with a reason the seller will see.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_event**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_event) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/events | Report a marketplace event (item.updated / ended / sold / relisted) for a listing your app manages.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_event2**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_event2) | **POST** /v1/listings/events | Report up to 100 marketplace events keyed by platform + externalId. One bad event never fails the rest.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_link**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_link) | **POST** /v1/listings/link | Link a live marketplace listing your app already manages into Crossly (no re-listing). Idempotent.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_link_bulk**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_link_bulk) | **POST** /v1/listings/link/bulk | Link up to 100 live marketplace listings. Each item succeeds or fails on its own; re-sending is safe.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_platform_reclone**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_platform_reclone) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/platforms/{platform}/reclone | End this marketplace item and relist it under Crossly. Requires consent: true.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_platform_reclone_cancel**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_platform_reclone_cancel) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/platforms/{platform}/reclone/cancel | Cancel a relist still waiting for you to end the old item.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_platform_reclone_seller_ended**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_platform_reclone_seller_ended) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/platforms/{platform}/reclone/seller-ended | You ended the old item on the marketplace yourself (Done).
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_platform_take_over**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_platform_take_over) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/platforms/{platform}/take-over | Take over an app-managed item: Crossly acts on the marketplace itself (needs your own connection).
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_listing_unlink**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_listing_unlink) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/unlink | Stop the app managing this listing. It stays live everywhere; the app's Crossly Shop fee ends for future sales.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_order_event**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_order_event) | **POST** /v1/orders/events | Report up to 100 order events (shipped, cancelled, refunded, delivered) keyed by platform + platformOrderId. Each completes the matching open order command. One bad event never fails the rest.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**create_order_take_over**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#create_order_take_over) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/take-over | Take over the app-managed item behind an order; its actions then go through your own connection.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**get_listing_management**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#get_listing_management) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id}/management | Which app manages this listing (null when none). Its fee is charged only on Crossly Shop sales, never on eBay or other marketplaces.
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**get_listing_platform_reclone**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#get_listing_platform_reclone) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id}/platforms/{platform}/reclone | The latest relist-under-Crossly run for this listing on a platform (null when none).
*Crossly::AppManagedListingsApi* | [**list_listing_commands**](docs/AppManagedListingsApi.md#list_listing_commands) | **GET** /v1/listing-commands | Your app's listing (delist/update/relist) and order (submit_tracking, remove_tracking, cancel_order, refund_order, message_buyer) commands for this seller (default: open ones), oldest first.
*Crossly::AppsApi* | [**get_app**](docs/AppsApi.md#get_app) | **GET** /v1/apps/{clientId} | Get one listed app by client id or profile slug. 404 if it is not in the directory.
*Crossly::AppsApi* | [**get_app_earning**](docs/AppsApi.md#get_app_earning) | **GET** /v1/apps/{id}/earnings | Your store's OAuth app's revenue-share earnings: summary (with the store they pay out to) + ledger. The app's store owner, or a teammate with apps:read on it.
*Crossly::AppsApi* | [**get_app_listing_allowance**](docs/AppsApi.md#get_app_listing_allowance) | **GET** /v1/apps/{id}/listing-allowance | Your OAuth app's listing allowance (read-only): whether staff let sellers list above their plan through it, its limitMode (separate pool or shared with the plan), limitPeriod (total or monthly), the per-seller limit and the platform scope. Owner only.
*Crossly::AppsApi* | [**list_apps**](docs/AppsApi.md#list_apps) | **GET** /v1/apps | Browse the public Crossly app directories: seller apps, buyer apps or both (search, category, sort, featured, paging).
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_automation_rule**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_automation_rule) | **POST** /v1/automation/rules | Create an automation rule.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_automation_rule_import**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_automation_rule_import) | **POST** /v1/automation/rules/import | Import one or more rules from recipe JSON (single or bundle).
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_automation_rule_run_now**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_automation_rule_run_now) | **POST** /v1/automation/rules/{id}/run-now | Fire an automation rule immediately.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_automation_rule_toggle**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_automation_rule_toggle) | **POST** /v1/automation/rules/{id}/toggle | Flip an automation rule between active and inactive.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_automation_rule_validate_recipe**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_automation_rule_validate_recipe) | **POST** /v1/automation/rules/validate-recipe | Dry-run validate one or more recipes against the live catalog.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_reprice_event_approve**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_reprice_event_approve) | **POST** /v1/reprice-events/{id}/approve | Apply a pending reprice proposal: the recorded price goes to the recorded platform listing. Proposals older than three days expire instead.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_reprice_event_skip**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_reprice_event_skip) | **POST** /v1/reprice-events/{id}/skip | Skip a pending reprice proposal; the price stays as it is.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**create_repricing_assignment**](docs/AutomationApi.md#create_repricing_assignment) | **POST** /v1/repricing/assignments | Reprice a listing set, a saved listings view or a category with one strategy. The floor is never breached (breakEvenFloor raises it to cover cost); approvalMode \"ask\" records each change for approval instead of applying it.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**delete_automation_rule**](docs/AutomationApi.md#delete_automation_rule) | **DELETE** /v1/automation/rules/{id} | Delete an automation rule.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**delete_repricing_assignment**](docs/AutomationApi.md#delete_repricing_assignment) | **DELETE** /v1/repricing/assignments/{id} | Stop a repricing assignment. Its past decisions stay in /v1/reprice-events.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**get_automation_catalog**](docs/AutomationApi.md#get_automation_catalog) | **GET** /v1/automation/catalog | Supported triggerType / actionType / conditionType values for automation rules.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**get_automation_rule**](docs/AutomationApi.md#get_automation_rule) | **GET** /v1/automation/rules/{id} | Get a single automation rule.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**get_automation_rule_export**](docs/AutomationApi.md#get_automation_rule_export) | **GET** /v1/automation/rules/export | Export the user's full rule library as a portable recipe bundle.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**get_automation_rule_export_by_id**](docs/AutomationApi.md#get_automation_rule_export_by_id) | **GET** /v1/automation/rules/{id}/export | Export a single automation rule as a portable recipe.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**get_repricing_strategy**](docs/AutomationApi.md#get_repricing_strategy) | **GET** /v1/repricing/strategies | The repricing strategies with the settings each takes, the settings every strategy takes, how often an assignment can check, and the approval modes.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**list_automation_rules**](docs/AutomationApi.md#list_automation_rules) | **GET** /v1/automation/rules | List automation rules.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**list_automation_runs**](docs/AutomationApi.md#list_automation_runs) | **GET** /v1/automation/runs | Per-fire history for automation rules and workflow chain runs.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**list_reprice_events**](docs/AutomationApi.md#list_reprice_events) | **GET** /v1/reprice-events | Reprice decisions, newest first: applied, held at a floor, or pending approval (pending=true for only those waiting).
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**list_repricing_assignments**](docs/AutomationApi.md#list_repricing_assignments) | **GET** /v1/repricing/assignments | Repricing assignments: which listings are repriced with which strategy, floor and approval mode, with the last price moved, the next check and how many changes wait for approval.
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**update_automation_rule**](docs/AutomationApi.md#update_automation_rule) | **PUT** /v1/automation/rules/{id} | Update an automation rule (full replace).
*Crossly::AutomationApi* | [**update_repricing_assignment**](docs/AutomationApi.md#update_repricing_assignment) | **PATCH** /v1/repricing/assignments/{id} | Change a repricing assignment (any field; a new strategy starts from the params sent with it), or pause / resume it with enabled.
*Crossly::BillingApi* | [**create_billing_upgrade**](docs/BillingApi.md#create_billing_upgrade) | **POST** /v1/billing/upgrade | Start an upgrade of a store's plan.
*Crossly::BillingApi* | [**get_billing_usage**](docs/BillingApi.md#get_billing_usage) | **GET** /v1/billing/usage | A store's plan and its usage against the plan's limits (used of limit, per metered limit).
*Crossly::BillingApi* | [**list_billing_app_allowances**](docs/BillingApi.md#list_billing_app_allowances) | **GET** /v1/billing/app-allowances | Your listing allowances through OAuth apps (used of limit, per app, with its platform scope).
*Crossly::BillingApi* | [**list_billing_invoices**](docs/BillingApi.md#list_billing_invoices) | **GET** /v1/billing/invoices | List a store's plan invoices, newest first, with search and filters.
*Crossly::BillingApi* | [**list_billing_plans**](docs/BillingApi.md#list_billing_plans) | **GET** /v1/billing/plans | List the plans with their prices, limits and features.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**create_book_expense**](docs/BooksApi.md#create_book_expense) | **POST** /v1/books/expenses | Log a business expense: date, amountCents, a Schedule C category, vendor, description, receipt link, optionally recurring monthly or yearly.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**delete_book_expense**](docs/BooksApi.md#delete_book_expense) | **DELETE** /v1/books/expenses/{id} | Delete a logged expense. It leaves the P&L and the exports.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**get_book**](docs/BooksApi.md#get_book) | **GET** /v1/books | Profit and loss for a year and period on the Schedule C lines: income, COGS, gross profit, expenses (marketplace fees, postage, mileage, your categories), net profit, monthly table, per-marketplace table with 1099-K expectations, Part III COGS, estimated tax and sales tax collected by marketplaces. Money in cents.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**get_book1099k**](docs/BooksApi.md#get_book1099k) | **GET** /v1/books/1099k | Per marketplace for a year: the 1099-K gross you recorded beside the gross Crossly computed, the difference, and whether a form is expected (federal: over $20,000 and over 200 transactions).
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**get_book_expense**](docs/BooksApi.md#get_book_expense) | **GET** /v1/books/expenses | Your logged business expenses in a year and period (recurring ones expanded), with their Schedule C category. Amounts in cents.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**get_book_tax_profile**](docs/BooksApi.md#get_book_tax_profile) | **GET** /v1/books/tax-profile | Your tax profile: time zone for books boundaries, filing status, state, set-aside and rate assumptions, accounting method, and business name, EIN and address.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**get_book_transaction**](docs/BooksApi.md#get_book_transaction) | **GET** /v1/books/transactions | The sale rows behind any P&L line, newest first: gross, refunded, fee and fee source, shipping, COGS, tax collected and net, in cents.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**update_book1099k**](docs/BooksApi.md#update_book1099k) | **PUT** /v1/books/1099k | Record what a marketplace reported on its 1099-K for a year (gross in cents, transactions, notes). Answers the year's reconciliation, as GET /v1/books/1099k does.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**update_book_expense**](docs/BooksApi.md#update_book_expense) | **PATCH** /v1/books/expenses/{id} | Change a logged expense.
*Crossly::BooksApi* | [**update_book_tax_profile**](docs/BooksApi.md#update_book_tax_profile) | **PUT** /v1/books/tax-profile | Update your tax profile: time zone, filing status, state, set-aside and rate assumptions (basis points), accounting method, business name, EIN and address. Fields left out are unchanged.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**create_brand_drop**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#create_brand_drop) | **POST** /v1/brand-drops | Create a brand drop as a draft (name, dropAt in the future, optional description, cover image and Crossly platform-listing ids). Approved partners only (403 drop_partner_required); counts against your monthly allowance (403 drop_quota_exhausted).
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**get_brand_drop**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#get_brand_drop) | **GET** /v1/brand-drops/{id} | One of your brand drops, with its status and any note from Crossly’s review.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**get_brand_drop_partner**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#get_brand_drop_partner) | **GET** /v1/brand-drops/partner | Your drop-partner terms (fee, promotion tier, monthly allowance, whether you may schedule without review) and how many drops you have created this month. partner is null when you are not an approved partner — apply at crossly.net/drops/apply.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**list_brand_drop_listings**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#list_brand_drop_listings) | **GET** /v1/brand-drops/{id}/listings | The listings a brand drop releases when it goes live.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**list_brand_drops**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#list_brand_drops) | **GET** /v1/brand-drops | Your brand drops, newest start time first. Filter with ?status=draft|pending_review|scheduled|live|sold_out|ended|cancelled. Empty when you are not a partner.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**update_brand_drop**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#update_brand_drop) | **PATCH** /v1/brand-drops/{id} | Edit a brand drop and/or change its status: draft (withdraw / unschedule), pending_review (submit for review), scheduled (only with canSelfPublish — 403 drop_review_required otherwise), cancelled. Editing name, time or description of a reviewed drop sends it back to review. Live and finished drops cannot be changed.
*Crossly::BrandDropsApi* | [**update_brand_drop_listing**](docs/BrandDropsApi.md#update_brand_drop_listing) | **PUT** /v1/brand-drops/{id}/listings | Replace the listings a brand drop releases, by master listing id (up to 100). Each must already be listed on Crossly — otherwise 400 listing_not_on_crossly naming them. Not allowed once the drop is live or finished.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_drop**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_drop) | **POST** /v1/branding/drops | Create a drop as a draft. `config` is the full drop config (what, where: shop|world|live|storefront|featured, format, access, limits, pricing, schedule, promotion, fulfilment). Returns rule problems to fix before publishing.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_drop_control**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_drop_control) | **POST** /v1/branding/drops/{id}/controls/{control} | Live controls: pause, resume, extend (body endsAt), release-wave, end, draw (raffle).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_drop_publish**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_drop_publish) | **POST** /v1/branding/drops/{id}/publish | Publish: scheduled, or pending_review for a brand partner without self-publish. Brand partners need a verified business on Stripe (403 business_verification_required).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_drop_restock_run**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_drop_restock_run) | **POST** /v1/branding/drops/{id}/restock-run | Restock run for a sold-out or ended drop: a link-only group buy on one of its restocked listings ({ platformListingId?, tiers, goalQty?, minQtyToProceed?, perBuyerMax?, days?, paymentMode?, priorityHours?, title?, description?, shareCopy? }). Waitlisted, raffle-losing and notify entrants of the drop are told and join first for priorityHours. One run per drop (409 after).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_event_cancel**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_event_cancel) | **POST** /v1/branding/events/{id}/cancel | Cancel a published event with the reason attendees see.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_event_check_in**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_event_check_in) | **POST** /v1/branding/events/{id}/check-in | Check a ticket in at the door by its QR token.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**create_branding_event_message**](docs/BrandingApi.md#create_branding_event_message) | **POST** /v1/branding/events/{id}/messages | Message the event's attendees: all, going, or ticketed.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**delete_branding_drop**](docs/BrandingApi.md#delete_branding_drop) | **DELETE** /v1/branding/drops/{id} | Delete a draft, or cancel a scheduled drop. A live drop is ended with the end control.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_brand**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_brand) | **GET** /v1/branding/brand | Your brand profile (approved brand partners).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_brand_term**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_brand_term) | **GET** /v1/branding/brand/terms | Your partner terms (fee, drops per month, units per drop, self-publish, pre-bids, promotion tier), this month's drop usage and the terms history.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_business_verification**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_business_verification) | **GET** /v1/branding/business-verification | Whether your Stripe account is a verified business (company, EIN/tax id verified, payouts on), with reasons and the page to fix it.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_drop**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_drop) | **GET** /v1/branding/drops/{id} | One of your drops.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_event**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_event) | **GET** /v1/branding/events | Your events: upcoming grouped by date (today, this_week, later), or past.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**get_branding_setting**](docs/BrandingApi.md#get_branding_setting) | **GET** /v1/branding/settings | The store’s Branding defaults new drops start from: timezone, notifyFollowers, botQueue, botCaptcha, cartHoldMinutes, leftovers.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**list_branding_brand_seller_search**](docs/BrandingApi.md#list_branding_brand_seller_search) | **GET** /v1/branding/brand/sellers/search | Find a store to authorise by its name or slug (?q=, at least 2 characters).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**list_branding_brand_sellers**](docs/BrandingApi.md#list_branding_brand_sellers) | **GET** /v1/branding/brand/sellers | Your authorised sellers (approved brand partners), each with its store name and status.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**list_branding_drop_buyers**](docs/BrandingApi.md#list_branding_drop_buyers) | **GET** /v1/branding/drops/{id}/buyers | A drop's buyers: public username, units claimed, order id and when, oldest first.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**list_branding_drops**](docs/BrandingApi.md#list_branding_drops) | **GET** /v1/branding/drops | Your drops, newest first, with config, waves, counts (entries, holding, sold, buyers) and phase. ?scope=upcoming|live|past|drafts|all.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**update_branding_brand**](docs/BrandingApi.md#update_branding_brand) | **PATCH** /v1/branding/brand | Edit your brand profile: logoUrl, bannerUrl, story, links.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**update_branding_brand_seller**](docs/BrandingApi.md#update_branding_brand_seller) | **PUT** /v1/branding/brand/sellers | Authorise a seller to sell your brand, or revoke them: { sellerUserId, status: active|revoked, note? }.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**update_branding_drop**](docs/BrandingApi.md#update_branding_drop) | **PATCH** /v1/branding/drops/{id} | Edit a drop that is not live yet. A published drop refuses rule problems (400 drop_config_problems).
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**update_branding_event_setting**](docs/BrandingApi.md#update_branding_event_setting) | **PUT** /v1/branding/events/{id}/settings | Save an event's full settings (where, access, reminders, check-in, recording, linked drops) and ticket tiers.
*Crossly::BrandingApi* | [**update_branding_setting**](docs/BrandingApi.md#update_branding_setting) | **PATCH** /v1/branding/settings | Change the store’s Branding defaults (any subset of the fields).
