*Crossly::ChannelInventoryApi* | [**get_channel_inventory**](docs/ChannelInventoryApi.md#get_channel_inventory) | **GET** /v1/channel-inventory/{platform}/{slot} | Deprecated alias of GET /api/v1/channel-accounts/:accountId/settings (removed next release).
*Crossly::ChannelInventoryApi* | [**list_channel_accounts**](docs/ChannelInventoryApi.md#list_channel_accounts) | **GET** /v1/channel-accounts | List every platform account of the store with its inventory and restock settings.
*Crossly::ChannelInventoryApi* | [**list_channel_inventory**](docs/ChannelInventoryApi.md#list_channel_inventory) | **GET** /v1/channel-inventory | Deprecated alias of GET /api/v1/channel-accounts (removed next release).
*Crossly::ChannelInventoryApi* | [**update_channel_account_setting**](docs/ChannelInventoryApi.md#update_channel_account_setting) | **PUT** /v1/channel-accounts/{accountId}/settings | Set a platform account's quantity cap, buffer, sync toggle, out-of-stock action, exclusions and restock auto-republish in the store (merge write).
*Crossly::ChannelInventoryApi* | [**update_channel_inventory**](docs/ChannelInventoryApi.md#update_channel_inventory) | **PUT** /v1/channel-inventory/{platform}/{slot} | Deprecated alias of PUT /api/v1/channel-accounts/:accountId/settings (removed next release).
*Crossly::CompWatchlistsApi* | [**create_comp_watchlist**](docs/CompWatchlistsApi.md#create_comp_watchlist) | **POST** /v1/comp-watchlists | Create a sold-comp watchlist.
*Crossly::CompWatchlistsApi* | [**create_comp_watchlist_scrape**](docs/CompWatchlistsApi.md#create_comp_watchlist_scrape) | **POST** /v1/comp-watchlists/{id}/scrape | Manually trigger a watchlist scrape.
*Crossly::CompWatchlistsApi* | [**delete_comp_watchlist**](docs/CompWatchlistsApi.md#delete_comp_watchlist) | **DELETE** /v1/comp-watchlists/{id} | Delete a sold-comp watchlist.
*Crossly::CompWatchlistsApi* | [**list_comp_watchlist_recent**](docs/CompWatchlistsApi.md#list_comp_watchlist_recent) | **GET** /v1/comp-watchlists/{id}/recent | Recent external sold comps matching this watchlist.
*Crossly::CompWatchlistsApi* | [**list_comp_watchlists**](docs/CompWatchlistsApi.md#list_comp_watchlists) | **GET** /v1/comp-watchlists | List the seller's sold-comp watchlists.
*Crossly::ConnectionsApi* | [**delete_device**](docs/ConnectionsApi.md#delete_device) | **DELETE** /v1/devices/{id} | Unpair a machine. Its access ends at once and any waiting print job is cancelled.
*Crossly::ConnectionsApi* | [**get_connection_health**](docs/ConnectionsApi.md#get_connection_health) | **GET** /v1/connection-health | Health of each connected marketplace account, with a plain-English diagnosis.
*Crossly::ConnectionsApi* | [**list_devices**](docs/ConnectionsApi.md#list_devices) | **GET** /v1/devices | Machines paired to this account, and what each can do.
*Crossly::ConnectionsApi* | [**update_device**](docs/ConnectionsApi.md#update_device) | **PATCH** /v1/devices/{id} | Rename a paired machine.
*Crossly::ConversationsApi* | [**create_conversation_message**](docs/ConversationsApi.md#create_conversation_message) | **POST** /v1/conversations/{id}/messages | Reply to the buyer, on the marketplace the conversation is on (Crossly marketplace: straight into the thread). The message is in the thread at once with deliveryStatus 'sending'; it settles to 'sent' or 'failed'. reply_not_supported when the thread cannot take a reply (a no-reply sender, a platform with no reply wire).
*Crossly::ConversationsApi* | [**list_conversation_messages**](docs/ConversationsApi.md#list_conversation_messages) | **GET** /v1/conversations/{id}/messages | A conversation's messages, oldest first. sender is buyer | seller; a reply you sent shows deliveryStatus sending → sent | failed. since (ISO 8601): only messages sent at or after it.
*Crossly::ConversationsApi* | [**list_conversations**](docs/ConversationsApi.md#list_conversations) | **GET** /v1/conversations | List buyer conversations, newest activity first. orderId: the conversation(s) about that order (same platform, its listing, its buyer). listingId: about that listing. updatedSince (ISO 8601): only threads with activity at or after it, for polling. search: contains-match on the buyer username or the subject (case-insensitive). Pair with the message.received webhook.
*Crossly::CreatorApi* | [**create_creator_experience_key**](docs/CreatorApi.md#create_creator_experience_key) | **POST** /v1/creator/experience-keys | Issue an experience API key for a back-end or CI: name, creator scopes (worlds:*, items:write — only ones your token holds), an IP allowlist (CIDRs; 0.0.0.0/0 + ::/0 for anywhere), optional experienceIds and expiresInDays. The token is in the response ONCE.
*Crossly::CreatorApi* | [**create_creator_experience_key_rotate**](docs/CreatorApi.md#create_creator_experience_key_rotate) | **POST** /v1/creator/experience-keys/{id}/rotate | Rotate a key: a replacement with the same settings (its token shown ONCE); the old key keeps working for overlapHours (default 24, 0 = revoke now, max 168).
*Crossly::CreatorApi* | [**delete_creator_experience_key**](docs/CreatorApi.md#delete_creator_experience_key) | **DELETE** /v1/creator/experience-keys/{id} | Revoke an experience key now. Never needs a plan: a credential must always be revocable.
*Crossly::CreatorApi* | [**get_creator_entitlement**](docs/CreatorApi.md#get_creator_entitlement) | **GET** /v1/creator/entitlements | What you can do in Creator Studio: your plan, whether you can author, grandfathering, and every limit with used and remaining. Read by web, apps, SDK and MCP alike.
*Crossly::CreatorApi* | [**list_creator_experience_keys**](docs/CreatorApi.md#list_creator_experience_keys) | **GET** /v1/creator/experience-keys | Your experience API keys, newest first (never the secret). ?includeRevoked=true for the history; ?experienceId= for the keys that reach one game (a game's own keys are narrowed to it alone). Cursor-paged: pass pagination.nextCursor back as ?cursor=.
*Crossly::CreatorRewardsApi* | [**get_creator_reward_formula**](docs/CreatorRewardsApi.md#get_creator_reward_formula) | **GET** /v1/creator-rewards/formula | The Creator Rewards formula: the pool's share of Crossly's Crossly Carnival take in force, the counting rules, and any scheduled change (announced at least 90 days ahead).
*Crossly::CreatorRewardsApi* | [**get_creator_reward_me**](docs/CreatorRewardsApi.md#get_creator_reward_me) | **GET** /v1/creator-rewards/me/{month} | One month of your Creator Rewards (YYYY-MM).
*Crossly::CreatorRewardsApi* | [**get_public_creator_reward_reconciliation**](docs/CreatorRewardsApi.md#get_public_creator_reward_reconciliation) | **GET** /v1/public/creator-rewards/reconciliation | Public monthly reconciliation of Creator Rewards (no auth): per month the pool funded from Crossly's take, carried in, distributed, paid (from the ledger), carried over, the formula version and weights, eligible experiences and every check; plus the formula in force and scheduled changes (90-day notice).
*Crossly::CreatorRewardsApi* | [**list_creator_reward_me**](docs/CreatorRewardsApi.md#list_creator_reward_me) | **GET** /v1/creator-rewards/me | Your Creator Rewards: each month, each experience you own or are paid for (counted minutes and players, exclusions, its amount, your part).
*Crossly::CreatorRewardsApi* | [**list_creator_reward_months**](docs/CreatorRewardsApi.md#list_creator_reward_months) | **GET** /v1/creator-rewards/months | Every calculated month: Crossly's take, the funded share, the pool, what was distributed and carried, counted minutes and exclusions by reason.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_game**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_game) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/games | Compose a card game from your card sets: a game id, its own set code, a name and the set ids; optionally the deck size (8-40), copies per card (1-4), the win condition (most points, or first to N) and the cover (a card with art, or a colour). Paid seller plans only.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_game_check**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_game_check) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/games/{id}/check | The full check publish runs: issues, tcgStaticConformance and bot-vs-bot games of the composed rules.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_game_publish**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_game_publish) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/games/{id}/publish | Publish: the composition must pass its check; its cards, the composed rules, art and cover are packaged and submitted as a new immutable version for review, through the same intake as every card game.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_set**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_set) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/sets | Start a card set: a game id (3-24 of a-z, 0-9, -), a set code (MG1) and a name. Answers a starter draft that already passes conformance. Paid seller plans only.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_set_art**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_set_art) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id}/art/{slug} | One card's art: a PNG (base64, at most 1 MB and 2048 px), scanned and stored until the version is approved.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_set_check**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_set_check) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id}/check | The full check publish runs: draft issues, tcgStaticConformance and bot-vs-bot games of the set.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_card_set_publish**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_card_set_publish) | **POST** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id}/publish | Publish: the set must pass its check; it is packaged (game.json, cards.json, the designer rules, art) and submitted as a new immutable version for review, through the same intake as a hand-made card game.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_creation_submit**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_creation_submit) | **POST** /v1/creator/creations/{format}/{id}/submit | Send one creation to review through its format's own submit (wearable, model, decor, interactive, card: its whole set). Games are published from world-kit.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_import**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_import) | **POST** /v1/creator/imports | Validate a filled-in template (manifest text, CSV text, every path in the upload): issues with file, line and column, and a preview. Creates nothing.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_import_commit**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_import_commit) | **POST** /v1/creator/imports/{id}/commit | Create the drafts of a validated template upload (drafts only; nothing is submitted to review).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_model**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_model) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/models | Upload a .glb (base64, 4 MB): validated, measured and stored (quarantined until approved). An over-budget model is refused with every failing number.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_model_check**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_model_check) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/models/check | The budget report for a .glb (base64), storing nothing: every limit (triangles, draw calls, texture edge per phone/desktop; size, nodes, meshes, images, animations; bones for wearables) with pass/fail, problems and LOD suggestions.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_model_submit**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_model_submit) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/models/{id}/submit | Send an uploaded model to art review.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_preview**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_preview) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/previews | Upload a preview PNG (base64, at most 1 MB and 2048 px): parsed, scanned and stored on the Crossly CDN; answers its https URL.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_wearable**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_wearable) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/wearables | Start a wearable from a template: slug, name, colourways (panel -> #rrggbb), a COSM price and optional edition size. Text is filtered; answers the automated checks. Paid seller plans only.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_item_wearable_submit**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_item_wearable_submit) | **POST** /v1/creator/items/wearables/{id}/submit | Submit a wearable whose checks pass: each colourway becomes a COSM design (inactive) filed for review. Needs an https preview image (POST /v1/creator/items/previews).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_review_hide**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_review_hide) | **POST** /v1/creator/reviews/{reviewId}/hide | Hide a buyer review from your public page (it is kept, and its rating still counts).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_review_reply**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_review_reply) | **POST** /v1/creator/reviews/{reviewId}/reply | Reply to a buyer review of your creation (or change your reply).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_review_report**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_review_report) | **POST** /v1/creator/reviews/{reviewId}/report | Report a buyer review to Crossly (abuse, spam, not a real buyer).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**create_creator_review_unhide**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#create_creator_review_unhide) | **POST** /v1/creator/reviews/{reviewId}/unhide | Show a hidden buyer review on your page again.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**delete_creator_card_game**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#delete_creator_card_game) | **DELETE** /v1/creator/cards/games/{id} | Delete a composed card game that was never published.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**delete_creator_card_set**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#delete_creator_card_set) | **DELETE** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id} | Delete a set draft that was never published.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**delete_creator_item_model**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#delete_creator_item_model) | **DELETE** /v1/creator/items/models/{id} | Delete a draft or rejected model.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**delete_creator_item_wearable**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#delete_creator_item_wearable) | **DELETE** /v1/creator/items/wearables/{id} | Delete a draft wearable.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_card_game**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_card_game) | **GET** /v1/creator/cards/games/{id} | One composed card game with its check: issues by field and conformance problems.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_card_set**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_card_set) | **GET** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id} | One set draft with its check: draft issues by field and tcgStaticConformance problems.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_card_set_progress**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_card_set_progress) | **GET** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id}/progress | A set's three steps: live on COSM, the mint threshold toward NFTs, and the print-run cost (trading_cards estimate) against production funding banked.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_collection**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_collection) | **GET** /v1/creator/collections/{collectionId} | One collection (a card set, a launch or a claimed contract): its six-stage road to the chain, the launch (phase timeline, proceeds, recent mints), sales by marketplace, royalty estimate, holders, its cards or creations with their earnings, holder perks set at mint, and which actions apply.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_collection_perk**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_collection_perk) | **GET** /v1/creator/collections/{collectionId}/perks | A collection's holder perks (percent off at your shop, early access, a World item, a loyalty tier), whether they can be edited, and why not (a card set not started yet, a contract claimed from elsewhere).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_creation**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_creation) | **GET** /v1/creator/creations/{format}/{id} | One creation: its status timeline and review history, the files it uses, where it sells, its sales, its buyer reviews and its mint status.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_creation_status**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_creation_status) | **GET** /v1/creator/creations/{format}/{id}/status | Where one creation stands: its status, the reviewer's last note and its status timeline.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_earning_export**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_earning_export) | **GET** /v1/creator/earnings/export | Download your creator earnings: format=csv (every ledger row for the filter) or format=pdf (a statement with per-rail totals for the same filter: rail, source, group, gameId, state; period defaults to this month).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_earning_reconcile**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_earning_reconcile) | **GET** /v1/creator/earnings/reconcile | Reconcile your creator earnings: every source recomputed directly from its own ledger, side by side with the earnings view. ok is true when every source matches.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_import**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_import) | **GET** /v1/creator/imports/{id} | A stored template validation.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_item_model**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_item_model) | **GET** /v1/creator/items/models/{id} | One of your models with its budget report.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_item_template**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_item_template) | **GET** /v1/creator/items/templates | Wearable templates: each garment the Crossly avatar wears, its recolourable panels, default colours and measured cost (triangles, draw calls, bones, bytes, phone LOD), plus the per-wearable budget.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_item_wearable**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_item_wearable) | **GET** /v1/creator/items/wearables/{id} | One of your wearables with its checks, review state and note.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_minting**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_minting) | **GET** /v1/creator/minting | Everything you take on chain as collection rows with a six-stage status (designed, eligible, drafted, deploying, live, ended): gettingReady and launched. Game items (source game_item: tradeable game products, NFT declarations for your games) carry href and gameId.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_overview**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_overview) | **GET** /v1/creator/overview | Creator Studio overview: earnings this month and last by rail, live creations, review queue, publish lock and next steps.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_template**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_template) | **GET** /v1/creator/templates/{format} | The template files for a format (wearable, model, decor, interactive, card_set): crossly-creation.json, creations.csv where it applies, and a README.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**get_creator_template_zip**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#get_creator_template_zip) | **GET** /v1/creator/templates/{format}/zip | A format's template as a .zip download (the same files as GET /templates/:format, plus its empty folders).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_card_games**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_card_games) | **GET** /v1/creator/cards/games | Your card games composed from several card sets: the sets, deck size, copies per card, win condition and cover, each with its check.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_card_sets**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_card_sets) | **GET** /v1/creator/cards/sets | Your card set drafts, each with its road from COSM to print: live on COSM, the mint threshold (collectors, packs opened, resales) and the print-run cost against what NFT sales have banked.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_creation_reviews**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_creation_reviews) | **GET** /v1/creator/creations/{format}/{id}/reviews | Buyer reviews of one creation, newest first (hidden ones included).
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_creations**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_creations) | **GET** /v1/creator/creations | Every creation of your shop in one list: wearables, 3D objects, decor, interactive objects, each card of your card sets, Classic games and card games, with status, collection, rating, earnings and mint status.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_earning_trend**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_earning_trend) | **GET** /v1/creator/earnings/trend | Your creator earnings over time: net (available + paid) per day, week or month (UTC) and rail, for the same filter as the ledger. meta.totals holds the per-rail and per-source totals of that filter.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_earnings**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_earnings) | **GET** /v1/creator/earnings | Your creator earnings ledger across every rail (cash, COSM, CBX), newest first. Each row names the source ledger row it is. meta.totals holds per-rail and per-source totals for the same filter; meta.nextCursor pages.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_item_models**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_item_models) | **GET** /v1/creator/items/models | Your uploaded 3D models with their budget reports and review state.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_item_wearables**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_item_wearables) | **GET** /v1/creator/items/wearables | Your wearables: drafts, in review, live or with changes requested, each with its automated checks and per-colourway designs.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_items**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_items) | **GET** /v1/creator/items | Every creation of your shop — wearables, decor, uploaded 3D models, interactive objects, Classic games, card games (incl. unpublished designer sets), physical runs — with status, price and earnings.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**list_creator_reviews**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#list_creator_reviews) | **GET** /v1/creator/reviews | Every creator submission (items, interactive objects, Classic games, card games) and its review decision with the reviewer note.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**update_creator_card_game**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#update_creator_card_game) | **PUT** /v1/creator/cards/games/{id} | Save the whole composition: name, description, setTitle, the set ids, deck size, copies per card, win condition and cover. Every word goes through the text filter. Answers the check.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**update_creator_card_set**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#update_creator_card_set) | **PUT** /v1/creator/cards/sets/{id} | Save the whole draft: cards (name, type, rarity, rules text, flavour, power/draw/guard), COSM prices and supply, frame colours, print plan. Every word goes through the text filter. Art is merged: a card without `art` (or with art as read) keeps its stored art, `art: null` clears it; only the art upload sets art. Answers the check.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**update_creator_collection_perk**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#update_creator_collection_perk) | **PUT** /v1/creator/collections/{collectionId}/perks | Replace a collection's holder perks: `{ perks: [...] }`, at most one of each type: percent_off { percent 1-50 }, early_access { hours 1-720 }, world_item { creationRef, name }, loyalty_tier { tierId, tierName } (one of your loyalty tiers), each with an optional note. 409 when the collection has no launch yet.
*Crossly::CreatorStudioApi* | [**update_creator_item_wearable**](docs/CreatorStudioApi.md#update_creator_item_wearable) | **PATCH** /v1/creator/items/wearables/{id} | Edit a draft wearable (name, description, colourways, price, edition). Submitted wearables are frozen.
*Crossly::CrosslyWalletApi* | [**create_tcg_nft_reconcile**](docs/CrosslyWalletApi.md#create_tcg_nft_reconcile) | **POST** /v1/tcg/nfts/reconcile | Re-read your wallets on chain so your card counts follow what you hold (cards you moved away leave; cards you received join).
*Crossly::CrosslyWalletApi* | [**create_wallet_crossly**](docs/CrosslyWalletApi.md#create_wallet_crossly) | **POST** /v1/wallet/crossly | Create your Crossly wallet now (it is otherwise created for you in the background). Idempotent.
*Crossly::CrosslyWalletApi* | [**get_wallet_crossly**](docs/CrosslyWalletApi.md#get_wallet_crossly) | **GET** /v1/wallet/crossly | Your Crossly wallet (self-custody Solana): status, address, whether it is configured, and your card NFT counts by status.
*Crossly::CrosslyWalletApi* | [**list_tcg_nfts**](docs/CrosslyWalletApi.md#list_tcg_nfts) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/nfts | Your card NFTs for a Crossly TCG game: each copy, its mint status, asset id and whether it is soulbound (starter).
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_customer_bulk_delete**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_customer_bulk_delete) | **POST** /v1/customers/bulk-delete | Bulk blocklist customer handles.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_customer_bulk_export**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_customer_bulk_export) | **POST** /v1/customers/bulk-export | Bulk export aggregated customers as CSV.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_customer_refresh_profile**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_customer_refresh_profile) | **POST** /v1/customers/{handle}/refresh-profile | Queue a fresh read of a customer's buyer profiles on every platform they ordered on.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_airdrop_cancel**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_airdrop_cancel) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/airdrops/{id}/cancel | Cancel a queued or failed airdrop (one already sent is the member’s).
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_airdrop_check**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_airdrop_check) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/airdrops/{id}/check | Read a submitted airdrop’s receipt again now.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_airdrop_prepare**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_airdrop_prepare) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/airdrops/{id}/prepare | The mint transaction for your launch owner wallet to send: chain, from, to (your launch contract) and calldata, to the member’s verified wallet. Refused airdrop_not_sendable with a reason when it cannot be built.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_airdrop_submit**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_airdrop_submit) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/airdrops/{id}/submit | Record the hash your wallet broadcast; Crossly reads the receipt (now, then on a schedule) and marks it sent or failed.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_program_invite**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_program_invite) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/program/invite | Invite Crossly buyers to your loyalty program by user id (up to 500); returns how many were newly invited.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_program_member_override**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_program_member_override) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/program/members/{id}/override | Pin a member to a tier (tierId null clears the pin), with a reason.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_program_member_point**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_program_member_point) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/program/members/{id}/points | Give a member points by hand (Idempotency-Key required: the same key awards once). Fires loyalty.points_awarded.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**create_loyalty_tier**](docs/CustomersApi.md#create_loyalty_tier) | **POST** /v1/loyalty/tiers | Add a tier: name, look, threshold and typed perks (% off, free shipping, early access, members-only listings, wholesale access, token gates, airdrops, Bucks, World items, event tickets, …).
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**delete_loyalty_reward**](docs/CustomersApi.md#delete_loyalty_reward) | **DELETE** /v1/loyalty/rewards/{rewardId} | Take a reward off the shelf. Perks already redeemed stay with their buyers.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**delete_loyalty_tier**](docs/CustomersApi.md#delete_loyalty_tier) | **DELETE** /v1/loyalty/tiers/{id} | Delete a tier; its members are re-evaluated.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_customer**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_customer) | **GET** /v1/customers/{handle} | Get one customer: recent 50 orders, buyer profiles per platform, and stats over all of their orders.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_customer_note**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_customer_note) | **GET** /v1/customers/{handle}/notes | Your private notes on a customer (empty when none). Works before their first order.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_loyalty_airdrop**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_loyalty_airdrop) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/airdrops/{id} | One loyalty NFT airdrop: member, collection, recipient wallet, transaction hash and status.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_loyalty_club**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_loyalty_club) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/club | Your loyalty club: tiers and how many members are in each.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_loyalty_program**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_loyalty_program) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/program | Your loyalty program: qualification (spend, orders, points), window, joining, downgrade and grace, and its tiers with typed perks.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**get_loyalty_program_member**](docs/CustomersApi.md#get_loyalty_program_member) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/program/members/{id} | One member: standing, tier, progress, perks held, history and any override.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_customers**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_customers) | **GET** /v1/customers | List aggregated customers (group-by lower(buyer_username)), each with its best avatar, display name and star rating.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_airdrops**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_airdrops) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/airdrops | NFT airdrops your loyalty tiers earned members: queued to send, submitted (waiting on the receipt), sent, failed (with why) or cancelled.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_members**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_members) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/members | Your loyalty club’s members, by spend with you.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_program_invite_candidates**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_program_invite_candidates) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/program/invite-candidates | Crossly buyers who bought from you and are not loyalty members yet (q searches by name): who you can invite.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_program_member_points**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_program_member_points) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/program/members/{id}/points | A member’s points history: purchases, hunts, event check-ins, photo reviews, group buys, manual grants and redeemed rewards.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_program_members**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_program_members) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/program/members | Your loyalty members with tier, standing, grace and overrides; search, tier, status and sort filters.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**list_loyalty_rewards**](docs/CustomersApi.md#list_loyalty_rewards) | **GET** /v1/loyalty/rewards | Your loyalty rewards shelf: typed perks members buy with points, each with its cost, stock left, per-member limit and how many were redeemed.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_customer_note**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_customer_note) | **PUT** /v1/customers/{handle}/notes | Save your private notes on a customer (an empty string clears them).
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_loyalty_club**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_loyalty_club) | **PUT** /v1/loyalty/club | Create or update your loyalty club: name, on/off, and up to 6 spend tiers each with a discount (≤ 30%) and perks.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_loyalty_program**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_loyalty_program) | **PUT** /v1/loyalty/program | Create or update your loyalty program settings.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_loyalty_reward**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_loyalty_reward) | **PUT** /v1/loyalty/rewards | Replace the rewards shelf (up to 50 rewards). A price perk (% off, $ off, free shipping) is redeemed as a single-use code.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_loyalty_reward_by_reward_id**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_loyalty_reward_by_reward_id) | **PUT** /v1/loyalty/rewards/{rewardId} | Add a reward to the shelf, or replace the one with this id.
*Crossly::CustomersApi* | [**update_loyalty_tier**](docs/CustomersApi.md#update_loyalty_tier) | **PUT** /v1/loyalty/tiers/{id} | Replace a tier. Members holding it get the edited perks.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**create_dao_proposal**](docs/DAOApi.md#create_dao_proposal) | **POST** /v1/dao/proposals | Raise a proposal. Starts as a draft.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**create_dao_proposal_finalise**](docs/DAOApi.md#create_dao_proposal_finalise) | **POST** /v1/dao/proposals/{id}/finalise | Close a proposal whose window has passed and record the outcome.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**create_dao_proposal_open**](docs/DAOApi.md#create_dao_proposal_open) | **POST** /v1/dao/proposals/{id}/open | Put your draft to a vote. Snapshots voting power.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**create_dao_proposal_veto**](docs/DAOApi.md#create_dao_proposal_veto) | **POST** /v1/dao/proposals/{id}/veto | Operator block. There is no matching power to PASS.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**create_dao_proposal_vote**](docs/DAOApi.md#create_dao_proposal_vote) | **POST** /v1/dao/proposals/{id}/vote | Cast or change a vote while the window is open.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**delete_dao_proposal**](docs/DAOApi.md#delete_dao_proposal) | **DELETE** /v1/dao/proposals/{id} | Withdraw your own DRAFT.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**get_dao_proposal_tally**](docs/DAOApi.md#get_dao_proposal_tally) | **GET** /v1/dao/proposals/{id}/tally | The live count.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**get_dao_subject_participation**](docs/DAOApi.md#get_dao_subject_participation) | **GET** /v1/dao/subjects/{subjectId}/participation | One holder's participation: votes, voting power over time, share.
*Crossly::DAOApi* | [**list_dao_proposals**](docs/DAOApi.md#list_dao_proposals) | **GET** /v1/dao/proposals | The proposal queue.

*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps | Register an OAuth app for one of your stores (storefrontId; default: the store in X-Storefront-Id, else your default store). The store is paid what the app earns. clientSecret is returned ONCE.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_certification_submit**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_certification_submit) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/certification/submit | Send the application to Crossly review. Needs a passing conformance run on this client version and report-menu evidence.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_certification_withdraw**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_certification_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/certification/withdraw | Take the application out of review to edit it.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_client_version**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_client_version) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/client-versions | Register a new release of your certified client and start its conformance run. A pass on the same major version carries the certification automatically; a new major version goes to Crossly review; a failure keeps your previous certified version.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_client_version_run**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_client_version_run) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/client-versions/{versionId}/run | Start a new conformance run for a release whose run failed, expired or was aborted.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_conformance_run**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_conformance_run) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/conformance-runs | Start a conformance run: a test experience only your app, signed in as you, can join. Join it with POST /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/join and follow the instruction.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_conformance_run_abort**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_conformance_run_abort) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/conformance-runs/{runId}/abort | Abort an open run.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_verification_check_domain**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_verification_check_domain) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/verification/check-domain | Look up the DNS TXT record crossly-app-verification=<token> on your domain (or _crossly.<domain>) now.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_verification_submit**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_verification_submit) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/verification/submit | Send the verification to Crossly review (each scope is decided separately).
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**create_developer_app_verification_withdraw**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#create_developer_app_verification_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/verification/withdraw | Take the verification out of review to edit it.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId} | One of your OAuth apps, with the store that owns it.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_certification**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_certification) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/certification | The Crossly Carnival client certification application (null until you create it): status, client, evidence, the passing run and what still blocks submission.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_client_version**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_client_version) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/client-versions/{versionId} | One client release and its re-certification.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_conformance_run**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_conformance_run) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/conformance-runs/{runId} | A run's step, instruction and every check with its verdict.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_health**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_health) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/health | Your app's API health: write-request counts by status class (24 h, 7 d) across every seller using it, top error codes with advice, a retry-storm flag, the last 50 failed requests (message and correlation id only on requests made with your own account), and from Atlas: how many people authorized the app and with which scopes (counts, never who) and the token endpoint's OAuth errors by code (e.g. invalid_grant) with the latest one.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_profile**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_profile) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/profile | Your app's public directory profile: every editable field, the listing status, what still needs review, and `preview` — the profile exactly as /seller-apps or /buyer-apps will show it.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_review**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_review) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/review | Your app's Crossly Carnival client certification, verification and review history in one read.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**get_developer_app_verification**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#get_developer_app_verification) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/verification | The app verification for worlds:publish / worlds:monetize (null until created): domain proof, policies, justifications and per-scope decisions.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**list_developer_app_client_versions**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#list_developer_app_client_versions) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/client-versions | Releases of your certified client, newest first: the re-certification status (awaiting_run, running, carried, ops_review, approved, rejected, failed, superseded), the rule that decided it and its checks.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**list_developer_app_conformance_runs**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#list_developer_app_conformance_runs) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/conformance-runs | Your app's conformance runs, newest first.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**list_developer_apps**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#list_developer_apps) | **GET** /v1/developer/apps | Your stores' OAuth apps (and any of yours still waiting for a store): filter by storefrontId, status or q; page with page + pageSize. meta.stores lists the stores you may register apps for.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app) | **PATCH** /v1/developer/apps/{appId} | Edit an app's name, redirect URIs, scopes and revenue share. Existing installs keep the terms they agreed to.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app_certification**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app_certification) | **PUT** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/certification | Create or edit the application: client name, platform, version, build and source URLs, description and evidence (report menu, chat, Policy UI). Changing the client clears the passing run.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app_listing**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app_listing) | **PUT** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/listing | Turn \"Listed publicly\" on or off. On: an app Crossly approved whose name, scopes and audience are unchanged is listed at once; anything else goes to review (pending_review). Off: unlisted immediately.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app_profile**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app_profile) | **PATCH** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/profile | Edit your app's directory profile: name, slug, tagline, description, longDescription (markdown), category, audience (auto|seller|buyer|both), logoUrl, bannerUrl (4:1, 1600×400), screenshots (≤6), demoVideoUrl, homepageUrl, installUrl, support/privacy/terms links, docsLinks [{label,url}] and pricingNote. Absent keys are kept, null clears. Images not on Crossly's CDN are copied onto it. Renaming a listed app or changing its audience sends it back to review.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app_store**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app_store) | **PUT** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/store | Choose (or change) the store that owns an app: a store of the same business. Its unpaid earnings move with it; client id, secret, installs and webhooks don't change.
*Crossly::DeveloperAppsApi* | [**update_developer_app_verification**](docs/DeveloperAppsApi.md#update_developer_app_verification) | **PUT** /v1/developer/apps/{appId}/verification | Create or edit the verification: domain, requested scopes with a justification each, privacy policy, terms, support email, demo.

*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_automatic_discount_preview**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_automatic_discount_preview) | **POST** /v1/automatic-discounts/preview | Preview a hypothetical cart of your listings against your automatic discounts (or an unsaved draft): what each line gets.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_discount_code**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_discount_code) | **POST** /v1/discount-codes | Create a discount code: percent, fixed, free shipping, buy X get Y, spend tiers or a set price, with requirements, item scope, limits (total, per buyer, per day, budget, regions), schedule and stacking.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_discount_code_generate**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_discount_code_generate) | **POST** /v1/discount-codes/{id}/generate | Generate up to 5,000 unique single-use codes with this code’s rules (their uses count toward its limits).
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_discount_code_pause_all**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_discount_code_pause_all) | **POST** /v1/discount-codes/pause-all | Pause every active discount code of the store at once (paused: true), or resume every paused one (paused: false). Archived codes are left alone; returns how many changed.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_discount_code_status**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_discount_code_status) | **POST** /v1/discount-codes/{id}/status | Pause, resume (active) or archive a discount code.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**create_discount_holder**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#create_discount_holder) | **POST** /v1/discounts/holders | Create a Collection holders deal in the store (X-Storefront-Id; default store when omitted).
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**delete_automatic_discount**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#delete_automatic_discount) | **DELETE** /v1/automatic-discounts/{id} | Delete an automatic discount.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**delete_discount_holder**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#delete_discount_holder) | **DELETE** /v1/discounts/holders/{id} | Delete a Collection holders deal. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**get_discount_code**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#get_discount_code) | **GET** /v1/discount-codes/{id} | One discount code with its rules and usage.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**get_discount_code_share**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#get_discount_code_share) | **GET** /v1/discount-codes/{id}/share | The share link that pre-applies the code at checkout (the store page with ?code=) and a QR code of it as a PNG data URL.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**get_discount_holder**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#get_discount_holder) | **GET** /v1/discounts/holders/{id} | One Collection holders deal with its usage (uses, cents given).
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**list_automatic_discounts**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#list_automatic_discounts) | **GET** /v1/automatic-discounts | Your automatic discounts (buy more, save more): quantity and mixed-item tiers, scope, channels and schedule.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**list_discount_code_redemptions**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#list_discount_code_redemptions) | **GET** /v1/discount-codes/{id}/redemptions | A code’s usage history: orders, masked buyers, region, channel and the amount given, newest first.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**list_discount_codes**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#list_discount_codes) | **GET** /v1/discount-codes | Your discount codes: kind, status (active, scheduled, paused, expired, exhausted, archived), every rule, and usage.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**list_discount_holders**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#list_discount_holders) | **GET** /v1/discounts/holders | The store’s Collection holders deals: a percent off for buyers holding any token of a collection (by chain + contract), with scope, channels, schedule, stacking and limits.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**update_automatic_discount**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#update_automatic_discount) | **PUT** /v1/automatic-discounts/{id} | Replace an automatic discount.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**update_discount_code**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#update_discount_code) | **PATCH** /v1/discount-codes/{id} | Update a discount code. The code itself can be renamed only until it is first used.
*Crossly::DiscountsApi* | [**update_discount_holder**](docs/DiscountsApi.md#update_discount_holder) | **PUT** /v1/discounts/holders/{id} | Replace a Collection holders deal.
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**create_drop**](docs/DropsApi.md#create_drop) | **POST** /v1/drops | Set up a drop: { title, listingIds (1-10 of your live Crossly listings), units (at most their stock), opensLineAt, releaseAt (ISO), eligibility: \"signed_in\" | \"verified\" }. The line opens at opensLineAt with a published seed commitment; the draw runs at releaseAt.
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**delete_drop**](docs/DropsApi.md#delete_drop) | **DELETE** /v1/drops/{id} | Call a drop off before its line opens.
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**get_drop**](docs/DropsApi.md#get_drop) | **GET** /v1/drops/{id} | A drop: its listings, units, times, eligibility, the sha256 seed commitment and the entry count. After release also the revealed seed, the sorted entry ids and the draw order (recompute it with drawWinners(seed, dropId, entryIds, units)).
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**list_drop_mine**](docs/DropsApi.md#list_drop_mine) | **GET** /v1/drops/mine | Your drops, latest release first, with entries, winners, claims, the waitlist and expired holds.
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**list_street_drops**](docs/DropsApi.md#list_street_drops) | **GET** /v1/streets/{slug}/drops | Upcoming, open and just-released drops on a Crossly World street, one per shop, soonest first, with the plot (or live stall spot) each line forms at.
*Crossly::DropsApi* | [**update_drop**](docs/DropsApi.md#update_drop) | **PATCH** /v1/drops/{id} | Change a drop before its line opens (any of the set-up fields).
*Crossly::EmbedsApi* | [**create_embed_key**](docs/EmbedsApi.md#create_embed_key) | **POST** /v1/embeds/keys | Mint a publishable key for a site.
*Crossly::EmbedsApi* | [**delete_embed_key**](docs/EmbedsApi.md#delete_embed_key) | **DELETE** /v1/embeds/keys/{id} | Revoke a publishable key.
*Crossly::EmbedsApi* | [**list_embed_keys**](docs/EmbedsApi.md#list_embed_keys) | **GET** /v1/embeds/keys | Your publishable keys.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**create_field_rule**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#create_field_rule) | **POST** /v1/field-rules | Create a per-channel field rule.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**create_field_rule_preview**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#create_field_rule_preview) | **POST** /v1/field-rules/preview | Preview field-rule output for a listing on one channel.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**create_field_rule_reorder**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#create_field_rule_reorder) | **POST** /v1/field-rules/reorder | Set the run order of several field rules.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**delete_field_rule**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#delete_field_rule) | **DELETE** /v1/field-rules/{id} | Delete a field rule.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**get_field_rule**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#get_field_rule) | **GET** /v1/field-rules/{id} | Get one field rule.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**list_field_rules**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#list_field_rules) | **GET** /v1/field-rules | List the seller's per-channel field rules.
*Crossly::FieldRulesApi* | [**update_field_rule**](docs/FieldRulesApi.md#update_field_rule) | **PATCH** /v1/field-rules/{id} | Update a field rule.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_apply**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_apply) | **POST** /v1/funding/apply | Apply for Crossly Funding. Decided at once on the checklist rules: approved opens your line (limit = 25% of 90 days of settled Crossly payouts), declined lists why, needs_review goes to a person.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_draw**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_draw) | **POST** /v1/funding/draws | Draw on your line for a supplier order. Send the supplier, the invoice (an uploaded invoiceFileId from POST /v1/funding/invoice-files, a reference, or a URL) and the APR from the quote you accepted (acceptedAprBps); refused if your rate changed. The advance is held as in-platform Crossly balance, never cash. Idempotent on idempotencyKey.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_draw_approve**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_draw_approve) | **POST** /v1/funding/draws/{id}/approve | Approve a held draw. The two-person rule needs two distinct approvers; the requester never approves their own.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_draw_quote**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_draw_quote) | **POST** /v1/funding/draws/quote | The disclosure for a draw of amountCents: APR, the simple-interest fee for the full term, the total and the due date, plus your available headroom. Nothing is drawn.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_draw_reject**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_draw_reject) | **POST** /v1/funding/draws/{id}/reject | Reject a held draw. Nothing was drawn, so nothing is owed.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_draw_use**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_draw_use) | **POST** /v1/funding/draws/use | Request a draw for an approved use. Refused by the spend controls, held for approval (stage requested — no debt until disbursed), or disbursed at once as restricted funding CBX. A DEBT with interest: show the disclosure first.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_invoice_file**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_invoice_file) | **POST** /v1/funding/invoice-files | Sign an upload for a supplier invoice: a PDF or image (application/pdf, image/jpeg, image/png, image/webp, image/heic) of at most 15 MB. PUT the bytes to uploadUrl with the same Content-Type and exactly contentLength bytes, then draw with invoiceFileId = file.id. Stored privately; one draw per file.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pay_crossly_fee**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pay_crossly_fee) | **POST** /v1/funding/pay/crossly-fees | Pay an open invoice of your Crossly plan with restricted funding CBX (no card is charged; the invoice is marked paid). Idempotent per invoice.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pay_shipping_label**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pay_shipping_label) | **POST** /v1/funding/pay/shipping-labels | Top up your shipping wallet with restricted funding CBX; labels you buy are then debited from it. Idempotent on idempotencyKey. Refused when the spend controls forbid shipping labels or it is over your approval threshold with no approved draw.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pay_vault_fee**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pay_vault_fee) | **POST** /v1/funding/pay/vault-fees | Ship a stored Vault unit home and pay its fee (shipping, storage owed, early withdrawal) with restricted funding CBX instead of your card.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pool_stake**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pool_stake) | **POST** /v1/funding/pool/stake | Stake earned CBX into the funding pool. Refused, with the reason, until the pool is live and staking is open.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pool_unstake**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pool_unstake) | **POST** /v1/funding/pool/unstake | Queue an unstake. Paid first-in first-out once your lockup has passed.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pool_unstake_cancel**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pool_unstake_cancel) | **POST** /v1/funding/pool/unstake/{id}/cancel | Cancel a queued unstake that has not been paid yet.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_pool_waitlist**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_pool_waitlist) | **POST** /v1/funding/pool/waitlist | Join the staker waitlist: told the moment staking into the funding pool opens.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**create_funding_repay**](docs/FundingApi.md#create_funding_repay) | **POST** /v1/funding/repay | Repay. source \"balance\" (default) takes it from your available Stripe payout balance and applies it at once. source \"card\" returns a PaymentIntent client secret to confirm with Stripe; source \"checkout\" returns checkoutUrl, a hosted Stripe Checkout page the seller pays on (no Stripe SDK needed). source \"cbx\" pays early from your EARNED CBX at the obligation rate (granted and funding CBX never repay a line) and applies at once. Card and checkout apply when the payment succeeds. Never more than you owe; idempotent on idempotencyKey.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**delete_funding_member**](docs/FundingApi.md#delete_funding_member) | **DELETE** /v1/funding/members/{memberUserId} | Remove a team member’s funding limit: they can no longer draw.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_control**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_control) | **GET** /v1/funding/controls | Your funding spend controls: approved uses, supplier allow/block lists, the approval threshold, the two-person rule and each team member’s funding limit.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_draw**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_draw) | **GET** /v1/funding/draws/{id} | One draw: amount, use, supplier, stage and its timeline (requested → approved → disbursed → spent → repaid).
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_draw_invoice**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_draw_invoice) | **GET** /v1/funding/draws/{id}/invoice | A 15-minute signed link to a draw's uploaded supplier invoice, with the file's name, type and size. 404 when the draw has no uploaded file.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_invoice_advance_eligibility**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_invoice_advance_eligibility) | **GET** /v1/funding/invoice-advance-eligibility | What your Crossly Funding line could advance against your open wholesale invoices: per invoice its balance due, the advance at the rate (80%), what the line can cover now within its headroom (earliest due first), and why not (overdue, no line, line unavailable, over headroom). Eligibility only: an advance cannot be drawn against an invoice yet. X-Storefront-Id narrows to one store.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_line**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_line) | **GET** /v1/funding/line | Your line: limit, available, outstanding (principal + interest), APR, interest accrued since the last posting, the payoff figure, next due date, anything overdue, the per-draw schedule and your auto-repay share.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_offer**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_offer) | **GET** /v1/funding/offer | Your pre-qualified Crossly Funding limit and the eligibility checklist: each rule, whether you pass it, and what to do if not. Works with or without a line.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_pool**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_pool) | **GET** /v1/funding/pool | The Crossly Funding pool: live or not and why (no token yet, no pool, no price, no capacity), capacity, utilisation, the reserve, and the uses and suppliers it allows.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_pool_stake**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_pool_stake) | **GET** /v1/funding/pool/stake | Your position in the funding pool, the trailing APY reading (never a promise), lockup, unstake queue, history and the risk disclosure.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_spendable**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_spendable) | **GET** /v1/funding/spendable | What your restricted funding CBX can pay right now, in cents, for each Crossly use (shipping labels, Crossly fees, ads, Vault fees). 0 = none, or your spend controls refuse that use.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**get_funding_statement**](docs/FundingApi.md#get_funding_statement) | **GET** /v1/funding/statements | A monthly statement (?month=YYYY-MM, default the latest): opening balance, every draw, interest posting and payment, and the closing balance; plus the months available.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**list_funding_approvals**](docs/FundingApi.md#list_funding_approvals) | **GET** /v1/funding/approvals | Draws your spend controls hold for approval, with the approvals so far and how many are needed.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**list_funding_draws**](docs/FundingApi.md#list_funding_draws) | **GET** /v1/funding/draws | Every draw on your line: what it paid for (a supplier order, or a Crossly checkout), its APR, estimated fee and due date.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**list_funding_receipts**](docs/FundingApi.md#list_funding_receipts) | **GET** /v1/funding/receipts | Your funding payments to Crossly, newest first: the use, what it paid for, cents, status (pending, paid, failed) and the draw it was stamped against.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**list_funding_repayments**](docs/FundingApi.md#list_funding_repayments) | **GET** /v1/funding/repayments | Manual repayments (card / bank, or from your payout balance), newest first.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**update_funding_autorepay**](docs/FundingApi.md#update_funding_autorepay) | **PUT** /v1/funding/autorepay | Set the share of each Crossly payout applied to what you owe: a whole percent from 10 to 100 (default 20). Never more than you owe is taken.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**update_funding_control**](docs/FundingApi.md#update_funding_control) | **PUT** /v1/funding/controls | Change your funding spend controls. Enforced at every spend: wholesale checkout, labels, ads, Crossly and Vault fees.
*Crossly::FundingApi* | [**update_funding_member**](docs/FundingApi.md#update_funding_member) | **PUT** /v1/funding/members/{memberUserId} | Set a team member’s funding limit (per draw or per month).
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_grading_run_reserve**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_grading_run_reserve) | **POST** /v1/grading/runs/{id}/reserve | Hold a slot in a Grading Run. Free now; charged on your saved card only if the run locks. Full-access token and Idempotency-Key required.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_grading_submission_cancel**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_grading_submission_cancel) | **POST** /v1/grading/submissions/{id}/cancel | Pull a reserved card out of its run. Only while it is reserved; nothing was charged.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_grading_submission_shipped**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_grading_submission_shipped) | **POST** /v1/grading/submissions/{id}/shipped | Say you mailed a charged card to the hub, with optional tracking.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_grading_verify_cert**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_grading_verify_cert) | **POST** /v1/grading/verify-cert | Look a cert number up with its grader. found=false when the grader has no record.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading_order**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_vault_market_grading_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders | Start a grading submission: cards (vault units or inventory items) to one grader at one service. Priced from the price book; vault units are held. Nothing is charged until you pay.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading_order_cancel**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_vault_market_grading_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a draft submission; its vault units go back on their shelf. A paid submission cannot be cancelled here.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading_order_pay**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_vault_market_grading_order_pay) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders/{id}/pay | Pay for a draft submission. savedCard: true charges your saved card (paid, or requires_action with a client secret for 3-D Secure). Full-access token and Idempotency-Key required.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading_order_pay_confirm**](docs/GradingApi.md#create_vault_market_grading_order_pay_confirm) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders/{id}/pay/confirm | After a 3-D Secure step or a card form: confirm the PaymentIntent with Stripe and mark the submission paid.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**get_grading_price_book**](docs/GradingApi.md#get_grading_price_book) | **GET** /v1/grading/price-book | The grading price book: every grader, its services (per-card price, minimum cards, max declared value, turnaround) and add-ons, plus Crossly handling. The same data the dashboard prices from.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**get_grading_setting**](docs/GradingApi.md#get_grading_setting) | **GET** /v1/grading/settings | Your grading defaults: defaultGrader (null = ask each time), defaultMissMode (roll_over | release, if a run does not fill), defaultReturnMode (always vault: every graded card goes into your vault) and defaultPrescreen.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**get_grading_worth**](docs/GradingApi.md#get_grading_worth) | **GET** /v1/grading/worth/{inventoryItemId} | Worth grading? Raw value vs what each grade is worth at each grader, against the cost. Never predicts the grade.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**get_vault_market_grading_order**](docs/GradingApi.md#get_vault_market_grading_order) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders/{id} | One grading submission: its timeline, every card with its grade, cert number and photos, the costs and the tracking.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**list_grading_rate_card**](docs/GradingApi.md#list_grading_rate_card) | **GET** /v1/grading/rate-card | Every grader and tier Crossly can send to, with the per-card price in cents (null = paused at the grader).
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**list_grading_runs**](docs/GradingApi.md#list_grading_runs) | **GET** /v1/grading/runs | Grading Runs taking reservations now, soonest cutoff first. meta.open is false while runs are switched off.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**list_grading_submissions**](docs/GradingApi.md#list_grading_submissions) | **GET** /v1/grading/submissions | Your cards out for grading (runs and Direct), newest first. active=1 keeps only the ones still in flight.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**list_vault_market_grading_candidates**](docs/GradingApi.md#list_vault_market_grading_candidates) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grading/candidates | What you can send to grading: raw units already in your vault and inventory items, each with a declared value to start from and the reason when one cannot go.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**list_vault_market_grading_orders**](docs/GradingApi.md#list_vault_market_grading_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grading/orders | Your grading submissions, newest first. Filter by status, active, grader, tier and dates; q searches the number, card names and cert numbers.
*Crossly::GradingApi* | [**update_grading_setting**](docs/GradingApi.md#update_grading_setting) | **PUT** /v1/grading/settings | Change your grading defaults (any of defaultGrader, defaultMissMode, defaultPrescreen; defaultReturnMode is always vault, and an old \"return\" is saved as vault). Logged to Activity. Returns the saved defaults.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**create_group_buy**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#create_group_buy) | **POST** /v1/group-buys | Run a group buy on a listing with stock: price tiers by committed quantity, a goal and minimum to proceed, a per-buyer max, a deadline, authorize-now or pay-now-refund-the-difference, a goal-miss policy (cancel, proceed, extend once), visibility, placement and share copy. The original body (tiers by buyer count, minBuyers, days) is still accepted.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**create_group_buy_cancel**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#create_group_buy_cancel) | **POST** /v1/group-buys/{id}/cancel | Cancel a group buy: every hold is released and every charge refunded.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**create_group_buy_close**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#create_group_buy_close) | **POST** /v1/group-buys/{id}/close | Close a group buy now: every buyer pays the price reached (the minimum and goal-miss policy still apply).
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**create_group_buy_extend**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#create_group_buy_extend) | **POST** /v1/group-buys/{id}/extend | Move the deadline later by N days (authorize-now pools run at most 6 days, pay-now pools 30).
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**create_group_buy_message**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#create_group_buy_message) | **POST** /v1/group-buys/{id}/message | Send a notification to every buyer committed to the group buy.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**get_group_buy**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#get_group_buy) | **GET** /v1/group-buys/{id} | One group buy: progress towards each tier, committed buyers (masked), time left, revenue at the current tier and the activity feed.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**get_group_buy_participants_csv**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#get_group_buy_participants_csv) | **GET** /v1/group-buys/{id}/participants.csv | Every participant of a group buy as CSV: quantity, status, amounts and ship-to.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**list_group_buys**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#list_group_buys) | **GET** /v1/group-buys | Your group buys, open ones first: tiers, buyers joined, the price reached and when it ends.
*Crossly::GroupBuysApi* | [**update_group_buy**](docs/GroupBuysApi.md#update_group_buy) | **PATCH** /v1/group-buys/{id} | Edit a group buy. Copy, visibility, placement and the per-buyer max change any time; tiers, goal, minimum, payment mode and goal-miss policy only before anyone commits.
*Crossly::ImportsApi* | [**create_import**](docs/ImportsApi.md#create_import) | **POST** /v1/imports | Start a bulk-import job for an existing platform connection.
*Crossly::ImportsApi* | [**get_import**](docs/ImportsApi.md#get_import) | **GET** /v1/imports/{id} | Get one import job.
*Crossly::ImportsApi* | [**get_import_site**](docs/ImportsApi.md#get_import_site) | **GET** /v1/imports/sites | Which sites (eBay marketplaces) an account's listings are on, with counts — to choose what an import brings in.
*Crossly::ImportsApi* | [**list_imports**](docs/ImportsApi.md#list_imports) | **GET** /v1/imports | List bulk-import jobs.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_ai_suggest**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_ai_suggest) | **POST** /v1/inbox/ai-suggest | AI reply suggestion for a conversation.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_canned_response**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_canned_response) | **POST** /v1/inbox/canned-responses | Create a canned response (added to the end of the list).
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_conversation_bulk**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_conversation_bulk) | **POST** /v1/inbox/conversations/bulk | Bulk mark read / mark unread / soft-delete conversations.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_conversation_bulk_ai_respond**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_conversation_bulk_ai_respond) | **POST** /v1/inbox/conversations/bulk-ai-respond | AI reply suggestion for multiple conversations — draft or send.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_conversation_offer_action**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_conversation_offer_action) | **POST** /v1/inbox/conversations/{id}/offer-action | Accept / counter / decline an active offer on a conversation.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_message_triage**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_message_triage) | **POST** /v1/inbox/messages/{id}/triage | Manually re-triage a buyer message.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_offer**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_offer) | **POST** /v1/inbox/{id}/offer | Accept, counter, or decline an offer on a conversation.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_reply**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_reply) | **POST** /v1/inbox/{id}/reply | Send a reply to a conversation thread.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_inbox_send_offer**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_inbox_send_offer) | **POST** /v1/inbox/{id}/send-offer | Make the buyer an offer inside a conversation (seller → buyer). Only where the conversation's canSendOffer is true (Grailed today). `amount` is in the listing's currency, above 0 and below the listing price (whole dollars on Grailed). The offer is in the thread at once as a message with offerAmount + offerStatus, deliveryStatus 'sending' until the platform confirms.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_video_request_answer**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_video_request_answer) | **POST** /v1/video-requests/{id}/answer | Answer a video request with a clip you uploaded (POST /v1/video-requests/upload-url, then PUT the file to uploadUrl). The buyer is notified and the clip shows on the listing.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_video_request_decline**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_video_request_decline) | **POST** /v1/video-requests/{id}/decline | Decline a video request, with an optional note to the buyer.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**create_video_request_upload_url**](docs/InboxApi.md#create_video_request_upload_url) | **POST** /v1/video-requests/upload-url | A presigned upload for a video answer: PUT the file to uploadUrl with the same Content-Type and exact Content-Length, then answer with publicUrl.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**delete_inbox_canned_response**](docs/InboxApi.md#delete_inbox_canned_response) | **DELETE** /v1/inbox/canned-responses/{id} | Delete a canned response.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**get_inbox**](docs/InboxApi.md#get_inbox) | **GET** /v1/inbox/{id} | Get one conversation with its messages and the item it is about (itemTitle, itemThumbnail, itemPrice, itemCurrency, itemUrl on the platform, listingId in Crossly, crosslyListingSlug, canSendOffer). An offer made in the thread is a message with offerAmount + offerStatus.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**get_inbox_canned_response**](docs/InboxApi.md#get_inbox_canned_response) | **GET** /v1/inbox/canned-responses | List canned responses in the seller's order.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**get_inbox_conversation_message**](docs/InboxApi.md#get_inbox_conversation_message) | **GET** /v1/inbox/conversations/{id}/messages | Paginated messages for a conversation.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**get_inbox_conversation_unread_count**](docs/InboxApi.md#get_inbox_conversation_unread_count) | **GET** /v1/inbox/conversations/unread-count | Unread conversation count (threads only; /v1/inbox/counts adds offers).
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**get_inbox_count**](docs/InboxApi.md#get_inbox_count) | **GET** /v1/inbox/counts | Inbox badges: unread and needs-reply, split into conversations and offers.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**list_inbox**](docs/InboxApi.md#list_inbox) | **GET** /v1/inbox | List conversations. `filter` narrows to the rows GET /v1/inbox/counts counts.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**list_video_requests**](docs/InboxApi.md#list_video_requests) | **GET** /v1/video-requests | Buyers asking for a video of your items. status: open (default), answered, declined or all.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**update_inbox_canned_response**](docs/InboxApi.md#update_inbox_canned_response) | **PUT** /v1/inbox/canned-responses/{id} | Update a canned response.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**update_inbox_canned_response_order**](docs/InboxApi.md#update_inbox_canned_response_order) | **PUT** /v1/inbox/canned-responses/order | Reorder canned responses.
*Crossly::InboxApi* | [**update_inbox_conversation**](docs/InboxApi.md#update_inbox_conversation) | **PATCH** /v1/inbox/conversations/{id} | Mark read / change status / close conversation.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_crm_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_crm_integration) | **POST** /v1/crm/integrations | Connect a custom webhook CRM or Mailchimp. HubSpot, Salesforce, Pipedrive and Zoho connect by signing in from the dashboard.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_crm_integration_test**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_crm_integration_test) | **POST** /v1/crm/integrations/{id}/test | Send a sample buyer to the CRM and report whether it arrived, in one sanitized line.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_design_tool_import**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_design_tool_import) | **POST** /v1/design-tools/import | Import Figma frames or Canva pages as images on the Crossly CDN, for the site editor, share cards, ad creatives, Branding drops or lookbooks.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_email_provider**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_email_provider) | **POST** /v1/email-providers | Connect an email sender (16 providers). The key is encrypted; the event webhook URL is returned once.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_email_provider_test**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_email_provider_test) | **POST** /v1/email-providers/{id}/test | Send a real test email through the sender; the result is one sanitized line.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_email_provider_webhook_url**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_email_provider_webhook_url) | **POST** /v1/email-providers/{id}/webhook-url | Make a new event webhook URL for the sender (the old one stops working). It is returned once.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_notification_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_notification_integration) | **POST** /v1/notification-integrations | Deprecated alias: add a Slack/Discord/JSON webhook for automations. Use POST /api/v1/webhooks with kind.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_notification_integration_test**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_notification_integration_test) | **POST** /v1/notification-integrations/{id}/test | Deprecated alias: send a test message to a destination. Use POST /api/v1/webhooks/{id}/test.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_notify_action**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_notify_action) | **POST** /v1/notify/actions | Decide a notification action from its signed token (an offer, a reprice, a label buy, a workflow approval). Single use; the token must be your own. Errors: expired (410), already decided (409).
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_print_mail_credential**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_print_mail_credential) | **POST** /v1/print-mail/credentials | Save a Lob or PostGrid key (tested against the provider first), with the return address and your per-piece prices.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_print_mail_credential_test**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_print_mail_credential_test) | **POST** /v1/print-mail/credentials/test | Check a Lob or PostGrid key with the provider before saving it. Saves nothing, mails nothing.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**create_print_mail_credential_test_by_id**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#create_print_mail_credential_test_by_id) | **POST** /v1/print-mail/credentials/{id}/test | Re-check a saved Lob or PostGrid key; the result is one sanitized line.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**delete_crm_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#delete_crm_integration) | **DELETE** /v1/crm/integrations/{id} | Disconnect a CRM. Buyers stop flowing to it; its delivery log goes with it.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**delete_design_tool**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#delete_design_tool) | **DELETE** /v1/design-tools/{tool} | Disconnect Figma or Canva for the store.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**delete_email_provider**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#delete_email_provider) | **DELETE** /v1/email-providers/{id} | Disconnect an email sender. The next newest becomes the default.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**delete_notification_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#delete_notification_integration) | **DELETE** /v1/notification-integrations/{id} | Deprecated alias: delete a notification destination. Use DELETE /api/v1/webhooks/{id}.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**delete_print_mail_credential**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#delete_print_mail_credential) | **DELETE** /v1/print-mail/credentials/{id} | Remove a print and mail account.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**get_design_tool**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#get_design_tool) | **GET** /v1/design-tools/{tool} | Whether Figma or Canva is connected for the store, and as whom. Never the token.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**get_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#get_integration) | **GET** /v1/integrations | Every connected integration of the store (AI, CRM, email senders, design apps) as uniform rows.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**get_print_mail_estimate**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#get_print_mail_estimate) | **GET** /v1/print-mail/estimate | Estimate a printed lookbook send: pieces × the seller's own per-piece price, per format the account's provider prints.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**list_crm_integration_logs**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#list_crm_integration_logs) | **GET** /v1/crm/integrations/{id}/logs | A CRM connection's delivery log, newest first, paged; filter by status.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**list_crm_integrations**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#list_crm_integrations) | **GET** /v1/crm/integrations | The store's CRM connections: provider, name, sync settings, health. Secrets are redacted.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**list_notification_integrations**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#list_notification_integrations) | **GET** /v1/notification-integrations | Deprecated (one release; use GET /api/v1/webhooks): every webhook of the seller as an old-style notification destination (provider slack/discord/webhook, plus kind), and any destination not yet copied to webhooks.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**list_print_mail_credentials**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#list_print_mail_credentials) | **GET** /v1/print-mail/credentials | The store's Lob and PostGrid accounts: mode, key as last 4, return address, per-piece prices.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**update_crm_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#update_crm_integration) | **PATCH** /v1/crm/integrations/{id} | Rename, pause, re-key or change what a CRM receives (sources, opted-in only, field mapping). Blank secrets keep the stored ones.
