*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**update_design_tool**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#update_design_tool) | **PUT** /v1/design-tools/{tool} | Connect Figma with a personal access token, or save your own Canva Connect app (client id + secret; finish the Canva sign-in in the dashboard).
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**update_email_provider**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#update_email_provider) | **PATCH** /v1/email-providers/{id} | Edit an email sender: label, from address, reply-to, default, settings. Blank secrets keep the stored ones.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**update_notification_integration**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#update_notification_integration) | **PATCH** /v1/notification-integrations/{id} | Deprecated alias: edit a notification destination. Use PATCH /api/v1/webhooks/{id}.
*Crossly::IntegrationsApi* | [**update_print_mail_credential**](docs/IntegrationsApi.md#update_print_mail_credential) | **PATCH** /v1/print-mail/credentials/{id} | Change a print and mail account: label, return address, per-piece prices (cents per format).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_catalog_match_confirm**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_catalog_match_confirm) | **POST** /v1/catalog-matches/{id}/confirm | Confirm a catalog-match suggestion: links the item to the catalog product (sets its market_sku_id), so sold comps and market pricing apply to it.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_catalog_match_dismiss**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_catalog_match_dismiss) | **POST** /v1/catalog-matches/{id}/dismiss | Dismiss a catalog-match suggestion. That product is never suggested for this item again.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory) | **POST** /v1/inventory | Create a new inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_bulk_archive**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_bulk_archive) | **POST** /v1/inventory/bulk-archive | Bulk archive inventory items (soft).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_bulk_delete**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_bulk_delete) | **POST** /v1/inventory/bulk-delete | Bulk delete inventory items (delinks listings).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_bulk_label**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_bulk_label) | **POST** /v1/inventory/bulk-labels | Bulk add/remove labels on inventory items.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_bulk_quantity**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_bulk_quantity) | **POST** /v1/inventory/bulk-quantity | Set / add / subtract stock across many items, or set a stock per item; syncs live listings.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_bulk_restore**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_bulk_restore) | **POST** /v1/inventory/bulk-restore | Restore archived inventory items (status returns to active, or sold_out when no stock).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_catalog_match_refresh**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_catalog_match_refresh) | **POST** /v1/inventory/{id}/catalog-matches/refresh | Run catalog matching for one item now. An identifier (UPC, style code, catalogue number) that resolves to exactly one product links it; otherwise likely products are returned as suggestions to confirm.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_csv_export**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_csv_export) | **POST** /v1/inventory/csv/export | Export inventory as CSV. Round-trips back through csv/import.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_csv_import**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_csv_import) | **POST** /v1/inventory/csv/import | Import a CSV. Rows whose sku matches an existing item update it; others are added. Pass dryRun to preview.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_hold_release**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_hold_release) | **POST** /v1/inventory/holds/{holdId}/release | Release an inventory hold.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_label_rename**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_label_rename) | **POST** /v1/inventory/labels/rename | Rename a label across every inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_merge**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_merge) | **POST** /v1/inventory/merge | Merge inventory items that are the same physical item into one (stock is not summed).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_unit_identifier**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_unit_identifier) | **POST** /v1/inventory/{id}/units/identifiers | Record a serial, IMEI, or licence key against an inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_inventory_unit_lookup**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_inventory_unit_lookup) | **POST** /v1/inventory/units/lookup | Find a unit by identifier.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_deed_lease**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_deed_lease) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/deeds/{id}/lease | Offer your plot for lease.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_deed_list**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_deed_list) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/deeds/{id}/list | List your plot for resale.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_deed_maintenance**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_deed_maintenance) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/deeds/{id}/maintenance | Pay a plot's maintenance forward.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_lease_accept**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_lease_accept) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/leases/{id}/accept | Take a plot on lease.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_lease_end**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_lease_end) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/leases/{id}/end | End a lease (owner or tenant).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_lease_rent**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_lease_rent) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/leases/{id}/rent | Pay rent forward.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_listing_buy**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_listing_buy) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/listings/{id}/buy | Buy a plot on the resale market, in CBX.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_property_listing_cancel**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_property_listing_cancel) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/listings/{id}/cancel | Take your plot off the market.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_shop_staff_pay**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_shop_staff_pay) | **POST** /v1/spatial/shop/staff/{grantId}/pay | Pay a staff member's wage forward.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_street_claim**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_street_claim) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/claim | Put your shop on a street.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_street_owned_upkeep**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_street_owned_upkeep) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/owned/{id}/upkeep | Pay a street's upkeep forward.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_street_plot_buy**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_street_plot_buy) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/plots/{number}/buy | Buy a vacant plot from Crossly, in CBX.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_street_request**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_street_request) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/requests | Ask to found a street.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**create_spatial_street_request_found**](docs/InventoryApi.md#create_spatial_street_request_found) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/requests/{id}/found | Found an approved street, in CBX.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**delete_inventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#delete_inventory) | **DELETE** /v1/inventory/{id} | Soft-archive an inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**delete_spatial_street_plot**](docs/InventoryApi.md#delete_spatial_street_plot) | **DELETE** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/plot | Take your shop off a street.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_game_event_summary**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_game_event_summary) | **GET** /v1/games/events/{eventId}/summary | The results of one of your metaverse events (a Branding event of type scavenger_hunt or pack_break): for a hunt -- status, place, coins and clues, players, finishers, winners (place, masked name, when) and the rewards paid or failed per kind; for a pack break -- status, whether it is live on the stage, spots, spots sold and hits revealed. 404 for an event that is not a World event of yours.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_game_hunt_build**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_game_hunt_build) | **GET** /v1/games/hunts/{id}/build | One of your scavenger hunts as the in-world builder sees it: place, rules (days, time limit, hints, winners, ordered), rewards, the build document (coins and clue signs with positions), what still blocks publishing, and buildLink -- the Worlds app link that opens the builder. Read-only: coins are placed in the World.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_game_leaderboard**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_game_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/games/leaderboards | Game leaderboards: for each of Price Guess (wins, then points), scavenger hunts (finished) and speed trades (won), the top ten this week (Monday 00:00 UTC on) under week and of all time under all -- rank, name, named, value and, for Price Guess, score. A name appears only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in Crossly World; everyone else is \"A player\" in their place.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_home**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_home) | **GET** /v1/homes/{slug} | A home by its address, as you may see it: open homes to anybody, friends-only homes to mutual follows, locked homes to their owner. 404 when you may not enter.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_home_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_home_mine) | **GET** /v1/homes/mine | Your Crossly World home: its address, privacy (open / friends / locked), theme, the room, the layout (each piece with what it shows -- things you no longer own are left out at read time) and the guestbook. Made on first read.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory) | **GET** /v1/inventory/{id} | Get one inventory item with platform listings.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_aging**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_aging) | **GET** /v1/inventory/aging | Unsold stock by days since first listed (unlisted, 0-7, 7-30, 30-90, 90+), each bucket with items, units, known cost (items without a cost counted separately) and listed value.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_catalog_match**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_catalog_match) | **GET** /v1/inventory/{id}/catalog-matches | One inventory item's catalog binding (if confirmed) and its pending match suggestions.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_facet**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_facet) | **GET** /v1/inventory/facets | Distinct brands + categories across this user's inventory.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_label**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_label) | **GET** /v1/inventory/labels | List every distinct label across this user's inventory.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_label_stat**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_label_stat) | **GET** /v1/inventory/labels/stats | List distinct labels with usage counts + colors.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_sell_through**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_sell_through) | **GET** /v1/inventory/sell-through | Cohort sell-through: of the items first listed in the window (default 30-60 days ago), the share sold within 30 days of listing, with n, immature items left out, and the change vs the previous window in percentage points (pp).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_sku_exist**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_sku_exist) | **GET** /v1/inventory/sku-exists | Check whether a SKU is already in use on this user's inventory.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_inventory_value_history**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_inventory_value_history) | **GET** /v1/inventory/value-history | Cost and listed value of unsold stock over time, oldest first (one point per nightly snapshot).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_main_stage**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_main_stage) | **GET** /v1/main-stage | The Crossly World Main Stage right now: the live show (title, kind, host lineup), the stream on its big screen while a lineup host is on air, how many people are there (it holds 64 per copy and opens copies as it fills) and what is on next.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_pet_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_pet_mine) | **GET** /v1/pets/mine | Your Crossly World pets: every kind (cat, dog, slab bot) with whether you own it, when it was earned and the ways to earn it (a quest or a reputation level -- pets are never sold), plus the pet walking beside you and the one greeting visitors at your shop door.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_reputation**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_reputation) | **GET** /v1/reputation/{profile} | Somebody's public reputation levels by their storefront handle: the level on each track and their nameplate badge. 404 for an unknown handle.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_reputation_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_reputation_mine) | **GET** /v1/reputation/mine | Your Crossly World reputation: four tracks (trader, collector, seller, host) with points, level, what each level has unlocked and the next unlock, and how the points were earned (recency-weighted; a lost dispute or a failed authentication costs points). Earned from real activity only; nothing buys a level.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_address**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_address) | **GET** /v1/spatial/address/{street}/{number} | The shop at an address.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_auction_public**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_auction_public) | **GET** /v1/spatial/auction/public/{slug} | A shared saleroom, opened by its public share handle.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_auction_room**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_auction_room) | **GET** /v1/spatial/auction/rooms/{sceneId} | What the rostrum says in this room, right now.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_emote**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_emote) | **GET** /v1/spatial/emotes | Your Crossly World emotes: every emote pack (unlocked or not, with the quest that unlocks it), your 8-slot emote wheel as stored and as it shows (labels, locks, ready dots), and the moments earned and not yet played.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_employee_config**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_employee_config) | **GET** /v1/spatial/employee/config | How the shop employee in one of your rooms is configured.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_party**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_party) | **GET** /v1/spatial/party | Your Crossly World party: party is null when you are in none, else its id, leader and up to 8 members (each with the place and copy they are in right now, or null), plus the invites it has out. invites lists the invites waiting for you.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_property**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_property) | **GET** /v1/spatial/property | Your plots and tenancies.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_public**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_public) | **GET** /v1/spatial/public/{slug} | A shared room, as a visitor sees it.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_scene**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_scene) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id} | A solved room: every item, where it sits, and why.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_scene_warehouse**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_scene_warehouse) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse | A warehouse room: your racks, where they stand, and what is on each.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_shop_staff**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_shop_staff) | **GET** /v1/spatial/shop/staff | Your shop's staff and their wages.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_street**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_street) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug} | A street: every plot and the shop open on each.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_street_founding**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_street_founding) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/founding | What founding a street costs.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_spatial_street_plot_quote**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_spatial_street_plot_quote) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/plots/{number}/quote | What a plot costs.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_world_pulse**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_world_pulse) | **GET** /v1/world/pulse | Crossly World's pulse, aggregated with no people in it: the public world shops that made a sale today (slugs only -- never counts, amounts or buyers), the times the world's shops passed each sales milestone today (each fires a fireworks show), and what Crossly Cares took in this month against the Commons fountain's mark.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_world_room_funnel**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_world_room_funnel) | **GET** /v1/world/rooms/{sceneId}/funnel | Your room's funnel over the last `days` (1-35, default 30): visitors (people, once a day), looks, items opened, checkouts started from the room, paid sales and revenueCents. Sales count only lines the room itself put in a cart.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_world_shelf**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_world_shelf) | **GET** /v1/world/shelf/{listingSlug} | Where a Crossly listing sits on a shelf in a public room right now (roomSlug, roomName, itemId), or data:null when it is in no room.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**get_world_skus_cheapest**](docs/InventoryApi.md#get_world_skus_cheapest) | **GET** /v1/world/skus/{skuId}/cheapest | The cheapest copy of a catalogue piece for sale on Crossly now, matched by the catalogue id (listingSlug, priceCents, and the room and item when it stands on a shelf), or data:null.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_catalog_matches**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_catalog_matches) | **GET** /v1/catalog-matches | Pending catalog-match suggestions: inventory items that look like a catalog product, awaiting your confirm.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_festivals**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_festivals) | **GET** /v1/festivals | Live and upcoming Crossly World festivals, soonest first: a weekend when every street wears one decor and the festival quest line (limited pins) counts. meta.live is the festival on now, if any.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_game_break_upcoming**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_game_break_upcoming) | **GET** /v1/games/breaks/upcoming | Pack breaks staged on shop stages in Crossly World, live first then soonest: the break, its shop, start time, spots (taken or not, buyer masked), hits revealed so far, and buyUrl -- the break's page on crossly.net, the only place a spot is bought (null once every spot has sold).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_game_hunt_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_game_hunt_mine) | **GET** /v1/games/hunts/mine | Your scavenger hunts (spec WD6: made from Branding events, built in Crossly World), newest first: title, place slug, the event it belongs to (eventId), your discount code if it rewards one, whether it is a draft (not yet built and published), live or ended, its coins, and how many people finished.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_game_hunts**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_game_hunts) | **GET** /v1/games/hunts | Scavenger hunts running now in Crossly World shops and pop-up stalls, ending soonest first: title, shop (name and slug -- for a hunt at a stall, the street slug, with stall.bookingId set), how many tokens, what finishing unlocks (\"10% off\" -- never the code), when it ends and how many people have finished. Optional place filters to one shop or street slug.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_game_result_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_game_result_mine) | **GET** /v1/games/results/mine | Your market-game results, newest first: game (price_guess, price_guess_solo, hunt, speed_trade, break_host), the round/hunt/trade/break id, score, whether you won, Bucks prize in cents (0 while prizes are pins only) and detail. meta.unlocks lists the hunt coupons you earned (with their codes); meta.hits the break hits pulled for spots you bought, with the order each ships with.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_home_mine_builders**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_home_mine_builders) | **GET** /v1/homes/mine/builders | Your co-builders: friends you let rearrange your home in the world (move, turn, take out your things, add free furniture; never your bought pieces). Each: userId, name, friends (false once the friendship ended: listed but powerless).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_home_of_the_week**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_home_of_the_week) | **GET** /v1/homes/of-the-week | Home of the week: open homes by how many different people signed their guestbook in the last 7 days, most first.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory) | **GET** /v1/inventory | List inventory items.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory_activity**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory_activity) | **GET** /v1/inventory/{id}/activity | Activity log for an inventory item (created/sold/edited/etc.).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory_hold_issues**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory_hold_issues) | **GET** /v1/inventory/holds/issues | List the stock problems the holds ledger finds.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory_holds**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory_holds) | **GET** /v1/inventory/{id}/holds | List the holds on an inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory_ids**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory_ids) | **GET** /v1/inventory/ids | Filter inventory → return matching id list.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_inventory_units**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_inventory_units) | **GET** /v1/inventory/{id}/units | List the individually identified units of an inventory item.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_job_appraisals**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_job_appraisals) | **GET** /v1/jobs/appraisals | Open price checks appraisers can answer (Collector level 3+ holding 3+ pieces in the category), newest first, optionally one market category. Each has its bounty; meta.payOn says whether bounties are paid yet.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_job_shifts**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_job_shifts) | **GET** /v1/jobs/shifts | Open shifts on the Crossly World Jobs board, soonest first: shop staff shifts in boutiques and grading-desk helper shifts, each with its window, place and pay. meta.payOn says whether Crossly is paying jobs yet.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_main_stage_events**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_main_stage_events) | **GET** /v1/main-stage/events | Live and upcoming Main Stage events, soonest first: brand launches, creator meetups, talks, live sales and festival shows, each with its window and host lineup.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_auction_room_lots**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_auction_room_lots) | **GET** /v1/spatial/auction/rooms/{sceneId}/lots | The catalogue: every lot in the session this room is hosting.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_auction_rooms**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_auction_rooms) | **GET** /v1/spatial/auction/rooms | Rooms of yours that are hosting an auction.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_deed_listings**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_deed_listings) | **GET** /v1/spatial/deeds/listings | Plots for resale.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_employee_sessions**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_employee_sessions) | **GET** /v1/spatial/employee/sessions | The itemised bill: one row per customer-conversation.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_employee_usage**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_employee_usage) | **GET** /v1/spatial/employee/usage | What your shop employees cost over a period, per room.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_moments**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_moments) | **GET** /v1/spatial/moments | Your moment emotes, newest first: unbox (a Crossly purchase delivered), slab_flip (a card graded through Crossly Grading), handshake (a trade-table shake settled). Each plays once in a room; playedAt is null until then. ready=true lists only the unplayed ones.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_party_wishlist**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_party_wishlist) | **GET** /v1/spatial/party/wishlist | Your party's shared wishlist, newest first: each listing (title, price in cents, image, shop slug while it is live), who added it, and every member's thumb up or down with the totals. Empty when you are in no party.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_public**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_public) | **GET** /v1/spatial/public | Public rooms anyone can walk into.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_public_offers**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_public_offers) | **GET** /v1/spatial/public/{slug}/offers | What is for sale in a shared room.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_scene_movements**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_scene_movements) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/movements | Stock movements in a room over a time window.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_scenes**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_scenes) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes | The rooms this account has.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_shop_addresses**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_shop_addresses) | **GET** /v1/spatial/shop/addresses | Your shop's addresses.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_staffing**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_staffing) | **GET** /v1/spatial/staffing | Shops you work in.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_street_mine**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_street_mine) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/mine | Streets your store holds.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_spatial_streets**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_spatial_streets) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets | Every street in the world.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_world_blocks**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_world_blocks) | **GET** /v1/world/blocks | The people you blocked in Crossly World, as you saw them (label, never the account behind a pseudonym), oldest first. Blocks are made in a room, on the person.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_world_item_search**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_world_item_search) | **GET** /v1/world/items/search | Find an item on a shelf anywhere in Crossly World: what public rooms have for sale now, by title or catalogue name (q, 2+ characters). Each hit names the room (roomSlug) and the item (itemId); /c/<roomSlug>?item=<itemId> opens it there.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**list_world_room_wants**](docs/InventoryApi.md#list_world_room_wants) | **GET** /v1/world/rooms/{sceneId}/wants | Items your room shows but does not sell that visitors said they would buy (\"I'd buy this\"), most wanted first: itemId and how many people.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**update_inventory**](docs/InventoryApi.md#update_inventory) | **PATCH** /v1/inventory/{id} | Update an inventory item (partial).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**update_inventory_assignee**](docs/InventoryApi.md#update_inventory_assignee) | **PATCH** /v1/inventory/assignee | Assign many inventory items to a teammate, round robin across several, or unassign them (null).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**update_inventory_assignee_by_id**](docs/InventoryApi.md#update_inventory_assignee_by_id) | **PATCH** /v1/inventory/{id}/assignee | Assign one inventory item to a teammate, or unassign it (null).
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**update_spatial_employee_config**](docs/InventoryApi.md#update_spatial_employee_config) | **PUT** /v1/spatial/employee/config | Create or replace the shop employee in one of your rooms.
*Crossly::InventoryApi* | [**update_spatial_shop_staff_wage**](docs/InventoryApi.md#update_spatial_shop_staff_wage) | **PUT** /v1/spatial/shop/staff/{grantId}/wage | Set a staff member's weekly wage.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_ai_detection_dispute**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_ai_detection_dispute) | **POST** /v1/ai-detections/{id}/dispute | Dispute an AI-image detection on your photo (sends it to Crossly review).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_event**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_event) | **POST** /v1/events | Create an event. A draft unless publish:true. Linked listings, the live show, the drop and the discount code must all be yours.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_event_publish**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_event_publish) | **POST** /v1/events/{id}/publish | Publish an event: its page goes live, and with notifyFollowers your followers are told once.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing) | **POST** /v1/listings | Create a listing and fan out crosspost jobs across platforms.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk | Create up to 50 listings in one call, with a result per item.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_check_status**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_check_status) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-check-status | Check listing status on platforms
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_crosspost**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_crosspost) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-crosspost | Bulk crosspost (no delist phase)
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_delete**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_delete) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-delete | Bulk archive + delist
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_delist**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_delist) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-delist | Bulk delist from platforms
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_delist_preview**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_delist_preview) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-delist-preview | Preview which marketplaces a delist would touch
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_hard_delete**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_hard_delete) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-hard-delete | Permanently delete listings (live ones need allowLive)
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_relist**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_relist) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-relist | Bulk relist across platforms
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_bulk_update**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_bulk_update) | **POST** /v1/listings/bulk-update | Bulk update listing fields
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_by_id**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_by_id) | **POST** /v1/listings/by-ids | Fetch hydrated listings by ID
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_check_duplicate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_check_duplicate) | **POST** /v1/listings/check-duplicates | Check whether the seller already owns something matching this title/photo, and what to do about it.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_combine**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_combine) | **POST** /v1/listings/combine | Combine duplicate listings into one: sums their stock, delists and archives the rest.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_discrepancy_resolve**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_discrepancy_resolve) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/discrepancies/{discrepancyId}/resolve | Resolve a detected marketplace-drift discrepancy: accept the platform value, push ours back, relist to apply it, or dismiss.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_import_by_url**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_import_by_url) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/import-by-url | Attach a real platform listing to this listing by pasting its live URL.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_magic_fill**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_magic_fill) | **POST** /v1/listings/{id}/magic-fill | Auto-fill empty fields on one platform tab from the master listing + AI/deterministic taxonomy resolution.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_merge**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_merge) | **POST** /v1/listings/merge | Merge the same item listed on different marketplaces into one listing; all stay live.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_tag_bulk**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_tag_bulk) | **POST** /v1/listings/tags/bulk | Add and/or remove tags on many listings (yours only; max 30 tags a listing, 40 chars a tag). `dryRun` reports what would change.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_listing_tag_rename**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_listing_tag_rename) | **POST** /v1/listings/tags/rename | Rename a tag on every listing; merges into `to` when it already exists.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream) | **POST** /v1/live-streams | Schedule a live show: title, start time, optional cover, category and the listings to feature (becomes the lineup, in order).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream_connection**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream_connection) | **POST** /v1/live-streams/{id}/connection | The show’s video connection, minted on first call and identical after: RTMP(S) server + stream key, SRT URL, WHIP URL for browser publishing (null where the ingest has none) and the HLS playback URL. configured=false (with a reason) when the deployment has no streaming provider.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream_connection_rotate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream_connection_rotate) | **POST** /v1/live-streams/{id}/connection/rotate | Replace the show’s stream key (e.g. after it was shown on screen). Refused while the show is live.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream_end**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream_end) | **POST** /v1/live-streams/{id}/end | End the show. Idempotent.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream_pin_launch**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream_pin_launch) | **POST** /v1/live-streams/{id}/pin-launch | Pin one of your live NFT launches on your live show; viewers mint it from the stream. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_live_stream_start**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_live_stream_start) | **POST** /v1/live-streams/{id}/start | Go live: the show’s status becomes live and followers are notified once. Idempotent while live; refused once ended.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport) | **POST** /v1/passports | Register a product passport for a Crossly listing or an inventory item you sell, optionally bound to an NFC tag UID (hex). Active at once. Returns the URL to write to the NFC tag or print as a QR; ownership then follows the item’s sales. For several items with photos and labels, use the drafts flow (POST /passports/drafts).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_draft**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_draft) | **POST** /v1/passports/drafts | Start a passport draft for up to 100 inventory items (or Crossly listings): one draft passport per item with its code reserved. Items whose every unit already has a passport come back in skipped.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_draft_activate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_draft_activate) | **POST** /v1/passports/drafts/{id}/activate | Activate every passport of a draft: their pages open for anyone who scans the tag or QR. Closes the draft’s capture link.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_draft_capture_link**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_draft_capture_link) | **POST** /v1/passports/drafts/{id}/capture-link | A link (and its QR as SVG) any phone opens with no sign-in to add photos, serials and NFC tags to this draft. Open 30 minutes after its last use, 4 hours at most; minting a new one closes the old.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_print**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_print) | **POST** /v1/passports/print | Print passport labels: sent to your printer agent (the Crossly desktop app includes one) when it is online and prints the template, else a signed PDF link with the reason. Templates: sticker-2in, sticker-1in, label-4x6, hang-tag, sheet-letter, sheet-a4, transfer-card.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_revoke**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_revoke) | **POST** /v1/passports/{id}/revoke | Revoke a passport (a lost or misprinted tag). Refused once the item has sold — the passport is then part of its history.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_passport_tag**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_passport_tag) | **POST** /v1/passports/{id}/tag | Bind (or re-bind) a passport to the NFC tag you wrote its nfcUrl to: the tag UID (hex), and whether you locked the tag read-only. Refused once the item has sold, for a locked tag, or a tag already on another item.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_rental_bulk_rentable**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_rental_bulk_rentable) | **POST** /v1/rentals/bulk-rentable | Make up to 200 Crossly listings rentable with the same options (a day rate, or a share of each price).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_shop_limit_request**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_shop_limit_request) | **POST** /v1/shop-limits/requests | Ask for a higher Crossly Shop selling limit.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_story**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_story) | **POST** /v1/stories | Share a story: 1–10 slides played in order. Each slide is a photo or video plus optional overlays (positions are fractions of the 9:16 frame). Listing stickers must be your own Crossly listings and also tag the slide; @mentions must be real shops. audience: everyone | followers; expiresInHours: 6, 12, 24 or 48.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_variation_group**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_variation_group) | **POST** /v1/variation-groups | Group listings you already have: a variation (one product, an axis such as Size) or a purchase-rule group (\"buy any 3\").
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_variation_group_generate**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_variation_group_generate) | **POST** /v1/variation-groups/generate | Turn one listing into a variation group: each new value becomes its own listing (a copy, with its own price, quantity and SKU) and all of them one group.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_variation_group_generate_matrix**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_variation_group_generate_matrix) | **POST** /v1/variation-groups/generate-matrix | Turn one listing into a variation group over up to three axes (Size × Color): each other cell is a listing you already have (listingId) or a new copy of this one with its own price, quantity and SKU.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_variation_group_publish**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_variation_group_publish) | **POST** /v1/variation-groups/{id}/publish | Publish a variation group per platform: one listing with a variation picker where the platform supports it (eBay, Shopify, Etsy), one per option elsewhere. Each platform reports its own outcome.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_video_studio_finalize**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_video_studio_finalize) | **POST** /v1/video-studio/finalize | Check an uploaded listing video (exists, MP4, size) and, with listingId, set it as that listing's video.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**create_video_studio_upload_url**](docs/ListingsApi.md#create_video_studio_upload_url) | **POST** /v1/video-studio/upload-url | Presign an R2 PUT (video/mp4, 15 min) for a listing video; finalize it after the upload.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_event**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_event) | **DELETE** /v1/events/{id} | Delete a draft, or cancel a published event (its page then says it was called off).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_listing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_listing) | **DELETE** /v1/listings/{id} | Delist a listing (optionally narrowed to specific platforms via ?platforms=).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_listing_tag**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_listing_tag) | **DELETE** /v1/listings/tags/{tag} | Remove a tag (any case) from every listing.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_live_stream_pin_launch**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_live_stream_pin_launch) | **DELETE** /v1/live-streams/{id}/pin-launch | Unpin the launch from your live show. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_rental_offer**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_rental_offer) | **DELETE** /v1/rentals/offers/{platformListingId} | Stop offering a listing for rent (existing bookings stand).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_story**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_story) | **DELETE** /v1/stories/{id} | Take one story slide down before it expires.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**delete_variation_group**](docs/ListingsApi.md#delete_variation_group) | **DELETE** /v1/variation-groups/{id} | Dissolve a group. Its options stay as ordinary listings.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_event**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_event) | **GET** /v1/events/{id} | One of your events.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_event_analytic**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_event_analytic) | **GET** /v1/events/{id}/analytics | Views, RSVPs, and what the linked listings sold on Crossly during the event (orders, units, revenue in cents).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_listing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_listing) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id} | Get one listing with its platform rows.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_listing_facet**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_listing_facet) | **GET** /v1/listings/facets | Distinct brands + categories across listings + inventory.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_listing_sku_exist**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_listing_sku_exist) | **GET** /v1/listings/sku-exists | Check whether a SKU is already used by one of this user's items.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_live_stream_recap**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_live_stream_recap) | **GET** /v1/live-streams/{id}/recap | The show’s numbers: duration, peak viewers, items sold and gross, tips, chat messages, auctions run, lineup size and the replay URL.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_passport**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_passport) | **GET** /v1/passports/{id} | One passport: its item and every listing of it, the event history (created, printed, tag bound, transferred, claimed…), scans per day for 30 days, custody, grading.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_passport_draft**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_passport_draft) | **GET** /v1/passports/drafts/{id} | A passport draft and its passports (photos, serial, tag and label state of each).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_passport_print**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_passport_print) | **GET** /v1/passports/print/{jobId} | Where a passport print job sent to your agent is: queued, sent, done, failed, expired or duplicate.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_pricing_setting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_pricing_setting) | **GET** /v1/pricing/settings | Selling options: own-price match (Crossly sells your items at your own lower price for the same item on another marketplace; dollar-priced copies only) and layaway (buyers may put items on layaway). Both on by default.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_pricing_sold_comp**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_pricing_sold_comp) | **GET** /v1/pricing/sold-comps | What one catalog product (market_sku_id, in one condition) actually sold for across Crossly in the last 180 days: median, p25/p75, p10/p90 (null under 10 sales), sale count, last sold, and days from listing to sale (median, p25, p75). Pass marketSkuId (+ optional condition) or one of your listingIds. comps is null until there are 3+ sales from 2+ sellers; holdCode says why there is nothing (no_market_sku, mixed_inventory, below_privacy_floor) — the same reasons a sold_median reprice rule holds.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_rental_policy**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_rental_policy) | **GET** /v1/rentals/policy | The return and payment policies a rental inherits from Settings › Policies, resolved for the store (or for one listing with ?listingId): preset ids and names, the level each came from, the return line, protections, arrival photos, the deposit claim window in days, a required deposit in basis points, accepted methods, and editPath.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_rental_setting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_rental_setting) | **GET** /v1/rentals/settings | The store’s rental defaults (every item inherits them) and its payout timing.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_shop_limit**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_shop_limit) | **GET** /v1/shop-limits | Your Crossly Shop selling limit: live listings and listed value, used of limit, per store, and the next step.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_stockx_catalog**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_stockx_catalog) | **GET** /v1/stockx/catalog | Search the StockX catalog (style code, SKU or words) to pick the product a StockX ask is on.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**get_stockx_catalog_variant_market_data**](docs/ListingsApi.md#get_stockx_catalog_variant_market_data) | **GET** /v1/stockx/catalog/{productId}/variants/{variantId}/market-data | Lowest ask, highest bid and suggested prices for one StockX size.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_event_attendees**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_event_attendees) | **GET** /v1/events/{id}/attendees | Who RSVPd: display name, avatar, going or interested, and when. No contact details.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_events**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_events) | **GET** /v1/events | Your events (launches, sales, live shows, drops, meetups, pop-ups, workshops, parties): schedule, status, phase, and going/interested counts.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listing_ai_detections**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listing_ai_detections) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id}/ai-detections | Crossly's AI-image detections for a listing's photos.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listing_discrepancies**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listing_discrepancies) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id}/discrepancies | List detected marketplace-drift discrepancies for a listing.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listing_ids**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listing_ids) | **GET** /v1/listings/ids | Filter listings → return matching id list (no pagination).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listing_tag_suggest**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listing_tag_suggest) | **GET** /v1/listings/tags/suggest | Deterministic tag suggestions for one listing (brand, category, size, condition, colors, style tags, era words in the title); `known` = you already use the tag.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listing_tags**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listing_tags) | **GET** /v1/listings/tags | Your listing tags with how many listings carry each, most used first (case-insensitive; `q` filters).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_listings**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_listings) | **GET** /v1/listings | List active platform listings.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_live_streams**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_live_streams) | **GET** /v1/live-streams | Your live shows, newest scheduled first: status, cover, category, connection method and peak viewers.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_passport_events**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_passport_events) | **GET** /v1/passports/{id}/events | A passport’s event history, newest first (never a buyer or a price).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_passport_print_targets**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_passport_print_targets) | **GET** /v1/passports/print-targets | Your printer agents (online, printers, default) and the passport label templates each can print.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_passports**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_passports) | **GET** /v1/passports | Product passports you registered (optionally for one Crossly listing): the code and URL each tag carries.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_rental_offers**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_rental_offers) | **GET** /v1/rentals/offers | What you offer for rent. Each offer carries `policy`: the return and payment policies it inherits from Settings › Policies, resolved (preset ids and names, the level they came from, protections, claim window, deposit, accepted methods, and editPath), with `returnPolicyOverride` when the offer writes its own return text.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_stockx_catalog_variants**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_stockx_catalog_variants) | **GET** /v1/stockx/catalog/{productId}/variants | List the sizes (variants) of a StockX catalog product.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**list_stories**](docs/ListingsApi.md#list_stories) | **GET** /v1/stories | Your active story slides (newest run first, each run in play order), with their overlays: text, listing stickers, @mentions, links, emoji and drawing.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_event**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_event) | **PATCH** /v1/events/{id} | Edit an event. Omitted fields are unchanged; null clears.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_hotspot**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_hotspot) | **PUT** /v1/hotspots | Tag your listings at points on a story or lookbook photo (shop the look). Replaces that photo’s tags; at most 12.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_listing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_listing) | **PATCH** /v1/listings/{id} | Edit a listing and fan out update jobs to existing platform listings.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_listing_assignee**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_listing_assignee) | **PATCH** /v1/listings/assignee | Assign many listings to a teammate, round robin across several, or unassign them (null).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_listing_assignee_by_id**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_listing_assignee_by_id) | **PATCH** /v1/listings/{id}/assignee | Assign one listing to a teammate, or unassign it (null).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_listing_custom**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_listing_custom) | **PUT** /v1/listings/{id}/customs | Set a listing’s customs: HS code (6–10 digits), country of origin, and countries this item must not ship to on Crossly. Omitted fields are left alone; null (or [] for restrictedCountries) clears.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_listing_platform_listing**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_listing_platform_listing) | **PATCH** /v1/listings/{id}/platform-listings/{plId} | Set one marketplace copy's price (e.g. the ebay.com copy of a listing also live on ebay.co.uk), in that copy's own currency.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_live_stream**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_live_stream) | **PATCH** /v1/live-streams/{id} | Edit a show that has not ended: title, description, cover, category, start time (not once live) or connection method (browser | phone | encoder).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_live_stream_lineup**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_live_stream_lineup) | **PUT** /v1/live-streams/{id}/lineup | Set a live show’s lineup: your Crossly listings, in the order you will sell them. Buyers see it before they join; a sale of a lineup item while the show is live is recorded at its replay moment.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_passport**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_passport) | **PATCH** /v1/passports/{id} | Change what a passport says (title, condition, maker, year, materials, notes) or add photo URLs (photos append: a passport keeps the ones it was registered with).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_passport_draft**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_passport_draft) | **PATCH** /v1/passports/drafts/{id} | Move a draft to a step, pick its label template, add items or remove draft passports.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_pricing_setting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_pricing_setting) | **PUT** /v1/pricing/settings | Turn own-price match and/or layaway on or off for every one of your stores. One store: PUT /v1/storefronts/{id}/settings.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_rental_offer**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_rental_offer) | **PUT** /v1/rentals/offers/{platformListingId} | Offer a Crossly listing for rent: day rate, optional week rate, deposit, min/max days, turnaround buffer, rent-only (off sale while offered), seller approval, unavailable date ranges (blackoutRanges, inclusive YYYY-MM-DD) and a return policy. Keeps the listing’s own rental terms in step. Needs a ship-from address.
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_rental_setting**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_rental_setting) | **PUT** /v1/rentals/settings | Set the store’s rental defaults and payout timing (on_start needs 3 rentals that came back).
*Crossly::ListingsApi* | [**update_variation_group**](docs/ListingsApi.md#update_variation_group) | **PATCH** /v1/variation-groups/{id} | Retitle a group, rename its axis, or change a group's purchase rule (written into every member's description).
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_lookbook**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_lookbook) | **POST** /v1/lookbooks | Create a lookbook. Pass templateId (classic, editorial, hero, magazine, drop, sale, postcard) to start it from a template styled with your storefront brand kit.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_lookbook_campaign**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_lookbook_campaign) | **POST** /v1/lookbook-campaigns | Email a lookbook to customers now or at scheduledAt. Audience kind subscribers | all | repeat | segment {filters} | handles {handles}; only people with marketing consent who have not unsubscribed receive it.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_lookbook_campaign_cancel**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_lookbook_campaign_cancel) | **POST** /v1/lookbook-campaigns/{id}/cancel | Cancel a scheduled campaign or stop one that is sending (emails already sent stay sent).
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_lookbook_campaign_preview**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_lookbook_campaign_preview) | **POST** /v1/lookbook-campaigns/preview | Preview a lookbook email campaign without sending: how many of the audience can receive it (opted in, not suppressed) and the rendered HTML/text.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_lookbook_share_link**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_lookbook_share_link) | **POST** /v1/lookbooks/{id}/share-links | Make a share link for someone without a Crossly login: role view, comment or edit; expires after ttlHours (1 to 2160, default 168). The url is shown once.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_print_mail_job**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_print_mail_job) | **POST** /v1/print-mail/jobs | Draft a printed lookbook mailing (beta): renders the print-ready PDF with bleed and estimates the cost from your per-piece price. Mails nothing until confirmed.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**create_print_mail_job_confirm**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#create_print_mail_job_confirm) | **POST** /v1/print-mail/jobs/{id}/confirm | Confirm a drafted print job: acknowledgeCharges must be true and pieces must equal the job's piece count. Your Lob or PostGrid account is billed.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**get_lookbook_campaign**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#get_lookbook_campaign) | **GET** /v1/lookbook-campaigns/{id} | One lookbook campaign and its counts.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**get_lookbook_design**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#get_lookbook_design) | **GET** /v1/lookbooks/{id}/design | A lookbook design document (pages of text, photo, listing, shape and sticker elements) with live listing title/price/photo, your brand kit and rendered page images.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**list_email_providers**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#list_email_providers) | **GET** /v1/email-providers | Your BYOK email providers (SMTP, Mailgun, SendGrid, SES, Resend, Postmark, Brevo, Mailjet, SparkPost, MailerSend, Mandrill, Plunk and more): label, from address, default, last test. Secrets are never returned.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**list_lookbook_campaign_recipients**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#list_lookbook_campaign_recipients) | **GET** /v1/lookbook-campaigns/{id}/recipients | Per-recipient results for a campaign: status (queued, sent, failed, skipped + reason), provider message id, opens, clicks, bounce, unsubscribe.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**list_lookbook_campaigns**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#list_lookbook_campaigns) | **GET** /v1/lookbook-campaigns | Your lookbook campaigns, newest first, with sent / failed / skipped / opened / clicked / bounced / unsubscribed counts.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**list_lookbooks**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#list_lookbooks) | **GET** /v1/lookbooks | Your lookbooks: id, slug, title, published, current design version and item count.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**list_print_mail_jobs**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#list_print_mail_jobs) | **GET** /v1/print-mail/jobs | Print-and-mail jobs (beta): provider, format, pieces, estimate, status and per-piece results.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**update_email_provider_us**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#update_email_provider_us) | **PUT** /v1/email-providers/{id}/uses | Set what an email provider sends: uses ⊆ [lookbooks, invoices, receipts]. lookbooks is always on; invoices = wholesale invoices and quotes, receipts = other buyer notices, sent from this address instead of from Crossly.
*Crossly::LookbooksApi* | [**update_lookbook_design**](docs/LookbooksApi.md#update_lookbook_design) | **PUT** /v1/lookbooks/{id}/design | Save a new design version. Images must already be on the Crossly CDN; listings must be your own Crossly listings. Pass baseVersion to get a 409 instead of overwriting a newer save.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_auto_decision_undo**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_auto_decision_undo) | **POST** /v1/magic/auto/decisions/{decisionId}/undo | Undo an automated Magic List decision, deleting its unpublished draft.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_auto_item**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_auto_item) | **POST** /v1/magic/auto/items | Scan one item and match it automatically (drafts only, never publishes).
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_auto_run**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_auto_run) | **POST** /v1/magic/auto/runs/{runId} | Score a Magic List run automatically; a confident match in auto mode becomes a draft.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_auto_run_swipe**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_auto_run_swipe) | **POST** /v1/magic/auto/runs/{runId}/swipes | Record a seller swipe session (kept and rejected match keys) for a Magic List run.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_scan**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_scan) | **POST** /v1/magic/scan | Run a Magic List image scan. Optional matchMode (off | auto | review_all) sets this scan's automated matching; omitted = your remembered default.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**create_magic_scan_synthesize**](docs/MagicApi.md#create_magic_scan_synthesize) | **POST** /v1/magic/scan/{runId}/synthesize | Synthesize a draft from confirmed matches.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**get_magic_auto_setting**](docs/MagicApi.md#get_magic_auto_setting) | **GET** /v1/magic/auto/settings | The seller’s Magic List automated-matching mode.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**get_magic_draft**](docs/MagicApi.md#get_magic_draft) | **GET** /v1/magic/drafts/{draftId} | Get a synthesized Magic List draft.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**list_magic_auto_run_decisions**](docs/MagicApi.md#list_magic_auto_run_decisions) | **GET** /v1/magic/auto/runs/{runId}/decisions | Every match decision round (automated and seller) for a Magic List run.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**list_magic_recent**](docs/MagicApi.md#list_magic_recent) | **GET** /v1/magic/recent | Recent Magic List scans for this seller.
*Crossly::MagicApi* | [**update_magic_auto_setting**](docs/MagicApi.md#update_magic_auto_setting) | **PUT** /v1/magic/auto/settings | Set the Magic List automated-matching mode (off | auto | review_all).
*Crossly::MobileApi* | [**create_mobile_push_test**](docs/MobileApi.md#create_mobile_push_test) | **POST** /v1/mobile/push-test | Fire a no-op test push to this user's devices.
*Crossly::MobileApi* | [**create_mobile_push_token**](docs/MobileApi.md#create_mobile_push_token) | **POST** /v1/mobile/push-token | Register an Expo push token for this user.
*Crossly::MobileApi* | [**delete_mobile_push_token**](docs/MobileApi.md#delete_mobile_push_token) | **DELETE** /v1/mobile/push-tokens | Clear ALL registered push tokens for this user.
*Crossly::MobileApi* | [**list_mobile_push_tokens**](docs/MobileApi.md#list_mobile_push_tokens) | **GET** /v1/mobile/push-tokens | List registered Expo push tokens (masked).
*Crossly::NFTMarketApi* | [**create_nft_market_external_resale**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#create_nft_market_external_resale) | **POST** /v1/nft-market/external-resale | Ops: record a Crossly NFT resold on ANY marketplace — maps mint → design and funds the pot. Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::NFTMarketApi* | [**create_nft_market_listing**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#create_nft_market_listing) | **POST** /v1/nft-market/listings | List an NFT you own for resale on the Crossly marketplace.
*Crossly::NFTMarketApi* | [**create_nft_market_listing_cancel**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#create_nft_market_listing_cancel) | **POST** /v1/nft-market/listings/{id}/cancel | Cancel one of your open NFT listings.
*Crossly::NFTMarketApi* | [**create_nft_market_listing_confirm_sale**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#create_nft_market_listing_confirm_sale) | **POST** /v1/nft-market/listings/{id}/confirm-sale | Ops: record a finalized on-Crossly NFT sale (verifies the royalty landed, funds the pot). Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::NFTMarketApi* | [**get_nft_market_listing**](docs/NFTMarketApi.md#get_nft_market_listing) | **GET** /v1/nft-market/listings | Browse open on-platform NFT listings (Crossly cosmetics for resale in CBX/SOL).
*Crossly::NeighbourhoodsApi* | [**get_neighbourhood**](docs/NeighbourhoodsApi.md#get_neighbourhood) | **GET** /v1/neighbourhoods/{slug} | One neighbourhood street by slug.
*Crossly::NeighbourhoodsApi* | [**list_neighbourhoods**](docs/NeighbourhoodsApi.md#list_neighbourhoods) | **GET** /v1/neighbourhoods | Every neighbourhood street (Sneaker Row, Card District, Vintage Alley, Vinyl Lane): open or \"opening soon\" (6 shops + open stalls, or a live street event), shops, stalls today, the next street event and its /c/<slug> URL.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**create_network_pool**](docs/NetworkApi.md#create_network_pool) | **POST** /v1/network/pool | Join the Crossly Network reciprocal engagement pool.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**delete_network_pool**](docs/NetworkApi.md#delete_network_pool) | **DELETE** /v1/network/pool | Leave the Crossly Network pool.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**get_network_pool**](docs/NetworkApi.md#get_network_pool) | **GET** /v1/network/pool | The seller's Crossly Network pool membership row.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**get_network_pool_size**](docs/NetworkApi.md#get_network_pool_size) | **GET** /v1/network/pool/size | Total members in the Crossly Network pool.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**list_network_pool_log**](docs/NetworkApi.md#list_network_pool_log) | **GET** /v1/network/pool/log | Recent engagement history — both sent and received.
*Crossly::NetworkApi* | [**update_network_pool**](docs/NetworkApi.md#update_network_pool) | **PATCH** /v1/network/pool | Update per-action toggles + platforms on pool membership.
*Crossly::OffersApi* | [**create_offer_respond**](docs/OffersApi.md#create_offer_respond) | **POST** /v1/offers/{id}/respond | Accept, decline, or counter a buyer offer on a Crossly marketplace listing. The response carries floorCents and floorSource (listing | item | break_even | null): your floor for that listing, null for a bundle.
*Crossly::OffersApi* | [**get_listing_offer_floor**](docs/OffersApi.md#get_listing_offer_floor) | **GET** /v1/listings/{id}/offer-floor | The lowest price an offer may be accepted or countered at for this listing on a platform: grossCents, origin (listing | item | break_even | null), text, and the same as floorCents + floorSource.
*Crossly::OffersApi* | [**get_offer**](docs/OffersApi.md#get_offer) | **GET** /v1/offers | List buyer offers on your Crossly marketplace listings, including bundles.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_live_shipment_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_live_shipment_label) | **POST** /v1/live-shipments/{id}/label | Buy ONE label for a show box, rated for all its orders packed together; every order takes its tracking and the buyer’s extra postage is refunded.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_live_shipment_ship**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_live_shipment_ship) | **POST** /v1/live-shipments/{id}/ship | Ship a show box with your own label: every order is marked shipped with this tracking and the buyer’s extra postage is refunded.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_bulk_delete**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_bulk_delete) | **POST** /v1/orders/bulk-delete | Bulk soft- or hard-delete orders by id.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_bulk_export**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_bulk_export) | **POST** /v1/orders/bulk-export | Bulk export selected orders as CSV.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_bulk_mark_disputed**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_bulk_mark_disputed) | **POST** /v1/orders/bulk-mark-disputed | Bulk mark orders as disputed with a shared reason.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_bulk_mark_shipped**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_bulk_mark_shipped) | **POST** /v1/orders/bulk-mark-shipped | Bulk flip status=shipped, clear the reserved-stock marker, and notify each platform.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_bulk_packing_slip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_bulk_packing_slip) | **POST** /v1/orders/bulk-packing-slips | Bulk packing slips PDF.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_cancel**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel an order before it ships. Crossly-marketplace orders refund the buyer (also needs orders:refund) and restock; crossposted orders go to the marketplace, or fail not_supported_on_platform (recordOnly: true records a cancel made on the marketplace). Fires order.cancelled.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_correct_tracking**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_correct_tracking) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/correct-tracking | Correct a wrong tracking number the marketplace already holds.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_dispute**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_dispute) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/dispute | Flag an order as disputed.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_import**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_import) | **POST** /v1/orders/import | Manually (re-)pull one or more connected platforms' order history for a day-window.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_label) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/label | Purchase a Crossly Shipping label. An order that already has a label answers 409 label_exists unless allowSecondLabel is true.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_label_print**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_label_print) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/label/print | Send the label to your print agent: mode agent (jobId), pdf (url, reason no_agent | offline | not_capable) or skipped (already_printed; pass reprint true to print again).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_label_void**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_label_void) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/label/void | Void a Crossly Shipping label and refund it to the shipping wallet.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_link_listing**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_link_listing) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/link-listing | Link an unmatched sale to one of your listings (marks it sold, delists it elsewhere).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_message**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_message) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/message | Message the order's buyer on the marketplace.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_order_chat**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_order_chat) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/order-chat | Send a message to the order's buyer chat on the marketplace. Mirrored into the Crossly inbox.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_pack_scan**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_pack_scan) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/pack-scan | Check a scanned item against the order being packed: ok true with matchedBy (sku | custom_label | item_id) and expectedQuantity, or ok false with reason (wrong_item | no_item) and the expected item.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_pickup**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_pickup) | **POST** /v1/orders/pickups | Book a carrier pickup for labelled orders (one carrier per pickup; window at least an hour, up to 14 days ahead).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_pickup_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_pickup_label) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/pickup-label | Buy the carrier label for a Crossly order the buyer collects from a pickup point (debits your shipping wallet). 402 when the wallet is short. An order that already has a label answers 409 label_exists unless allowSecondLabel is true.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_platform_label_package**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_platform_label_package) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/package | Save the package (weight oz, box inches) the marketplace prices the label from.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_platform_label_package_quote**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_platform_label_package_quote) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/package/quote | Quote what a change to the marketplace shipment's package costs (cents).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_platform_label_pull**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_platform_label_pull) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/pull | Pull the marketplace's prepaid label into Crossly (every platform with a label descriptor).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_platform_label_purchase**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_platform_label_purchase) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/purchase | Buy a replacement / extra marketplace label, or have the marketplace generate it, and record it.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_platform_label_quote**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_platform_label_quote) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/quote | Quote what buying a marketplace label costs, before you are charged.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_pull_platform_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_pull_platform_label) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/pull-platform-label | Pull a pre-paid label from cookie platforms (Poshmark/Mercari).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_rate**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_rate) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/rates | Crossly Shipping rate quote for an order.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_refund**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_refund) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/refund | DEPRECATED: use POST /v1/orders/{id}/refunds. Full or partial refund (amount in dollars). Same idempotency and cap as /refunds: the same request twice refunds once (Idempotency-Key, else the body), never past what the buyer paid.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_refund_by_id**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_refund_by_id) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/refunds | Refund an order, in full or in part. `amount` is dollars; omit it for everything left (or the line items' value). Crossly-marketplace orders refund the buyer's card; crossposted orders refund through the marketplace, or fail not_supported_on_platform. Never refunds past the order (refund_exceeds_remaining). The same request twice refunds once: send an Idempotency-Key (without one, the body is the key). A partial refund keeps the order's status. What the platform takes (full/partial, its own reason keys, a comment) is GET /v1/orders/{id}/refund-options; a request outside it fails partial_refund_unsupported, invalid_refund_reason or refund_exceeds_item_amount. Fires order.refunded.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_refund_resolve**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_refund_resolve) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/refunds/{refundId}/resolve | Settle an `unverified` refund (one Crossly could not confirm with the marketplace after its request was interrupted) once you have checked the marketplace: succeeded (with the amount actually refunded, dollars) or failed (nothing moved; the same Idempotency-Key can then try again). Crossly-marketplace refunds are confirmed by support instead.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_scan_form**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_scan_form) | **POST** /v1/orders/scan-forms | Create SCAN forms (one per carrier account and ship-from address) for labels the carrier has not scanned yet.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_tracking**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_tracking) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/tracking | Submit tracking number + carrier and notify the platform.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_whatnot_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_whatnot_label) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/label | Generate the Whatnot shipping label and record it on the order.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_whatnot_package**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_whatnot_package) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/package | Save a Whatnot shipment package (weight oz, box inches).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_order_whatnot_package_quote**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_order_whatnot_package_quote) | **POST** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/package/quote | Quote what a Whatnot package change costs (cents).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_approve**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_approve) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/approve | Approve a requested rental (offers with requireApproval): the renter’s held card is charged.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_cancel**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_cancel) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/cancel | Cancel a booked rental that has not shipped: the renter is refunded in full.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_claim**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_claim) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/claim | Claim part or all of the deposit (cents, a note, photo URLs): within 3 days of the return, or 14 days after the end if it never came back.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_decline**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_decline) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/decline | Decline a requested rental: the hold on the renter’s card is released.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_delivered**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_delivered) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/delivered | Mark a rental delivered to the renter.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_extension_decide**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_extension_decide) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/extensions/{extensionId}/decide | Approve or decline a renter’s request for more days.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_inspect**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_inspect) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/inspect | Record the return inspection: the checklist, photos, and optionally release the deposit.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_label**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_label) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/labels | Buy both labels for a booked rental: outbound to the renter, and the return label they use.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_not_returned**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_not_returned) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/not-returned | Report a rental not returned (14 days past due): the deposit goes to you.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_release_deposit**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_release_deposit) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/release-deposit | Release the deposit now, without a claim.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_returned**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_returned) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/returned | Mark a rental back with you. You then have 3 days to claim damage before the deposit goes back.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**create_rental_shipped**](docs/OrdersApi.md#create_rental_shipped) | **POST** /v1/rentals/{id}/shipped | Mark a booked rental shipped, with at least 2 photos of its condition as it leaves (URLs you uploaded) — the evidence any later claim is judged against.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_device_scale**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_device_scale) | **GET** /v1/devices/scale | Read your USB postal scale: ok true with weightOz and stable, or ok false with reason (no_agent | not_capable | offline | failed | timeout).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id} | Get one order, with its sale price, fees, SKU and line items.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_cancel_eligibility**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_cancel_eligibility) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/cancel-eligibility | Whether this order can be cancelled, and which reason codes the marketplace accepts.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_count**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_count) | **GET** /v1/orders/counts | Tab badge counts by status.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_delivery_photo**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_delivery_photo) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/delivery-photo | The courier's delivery photo for this order, pulled automatically, with where it came from.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_evidence**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_evidence) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/evidence | Everything recorded about how this order was packed and how it arrived.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_label_print**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_label_print) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/label/print | The order's shipping label as a PDF sized for printing (cropped, upright, fitted).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_order_chat**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_order_chat) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/order-chat | The order's buyer chat thread on the marketplace (separate from listing comments): { orderId, platform, messages: [{ id, sender (buyer|seller|platform), body, sentAt, attachments? }], canSend }, oldest message first.

*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_pick_list**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_pick_list) | **GET** /v1/orders/pick-list | The items to pull for a batch of orders, grouped by storage bin: groups[{bin, lines[{orderId, orderNumber, platform, title, sku, quantity, buyer}]}], orderCount, itemCount. orderIds (comma list, at most 500) or every order still to ship; storefrontId for one store; format=pdf for the printable list.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_platform_label_option**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_platform_label_option) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/options | What buying a marketplace label offers for this order (weight tiers, mailbox state).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_platform_label_packing_slip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_platform_label_packing_slip) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/packing-slip | A fresh link to the marketplace's own packing slip PDF.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_platform_label_state**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_platform_label_state) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/state | The marketplace's shipment for an order (package, label, tracking). Records a label found on it.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_platform_label_url**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_platform_label_url) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/platform-label/url | A fresh, short-lived link to the marketplace's label sheet. Never stored.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_proof_of_delivery**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_proof_of_delivery) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/proof-of-delivery | Proof-of-delivery evidence from the carrier's scan record (PDF, or JSON).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_refund_option**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_refund_option) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/refund-options | What a refund on this order may be, from the platform's declared capability: whether Crossly can refund it at all, full and/or partial, what a partial is measured against (total = what the buyer paid, item = the item price only), whether it may precede shipment, the platform's own reason keys (when listed, a refund's reason must be one of them), whether a comment is sent, whether the refund IS cancelling the sale (cancelsSale: Mercari, full only), whether the window closes when the order completes (untilCompleted; windowClosed = it has) and a one-line note on what the platform does not allow.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_sales_tax**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_sales_tax) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/sales-tax | Who collected sales tax on one Crossly marketplace order, where, and the seller notice.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_scan_form**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_scan_form) | **GET** /v1/orders/scan-forms/{id} | One SCAN form; a queued form is re-read from the carrier and its PDF appears once built.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_shipment**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_shipment) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/shipments | List the parcels an order shipped in.
