*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_whatnot_packing_slip**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_whatnot_packing_slip) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/packing-slip | A fresh link to Whatnot's packing slip PDF.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_order_whatnot_shipment**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_order_whatnot_shipment) | **GET** /v1/orders/{id}/whatnot/shipment | Whatnot shipment for an order (package, label, tracking). Records a label found on it.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_rental**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_rental) | **GET** /v1/rentals/{id} | One rental: its timeline, both sides’ condition photos, payments, extensions and what can be done next.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_rental_calendar**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_rental_calendar) | **GET** /v1/rentals/calendar | Every rentable item’s bookings (per unit), turnaround buffers and blackouts between two dates (≤ 93 days).
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**get_rental_inventory**](docs/OrdersApi.md#get_rental_inventory) | **GET** /v1/rentals/inventory/{inventoryItemId} | An inventory item’s rentals: units out with a renter now (reserved on the item), upcoming bookings and returns waiting for inspection.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**list_layaways**](docs/OrdersApi.md#list_layaways) | **GET** /v1/layaways | Your items on layaway (active and paid off). Do not ship an active one: its order moves to needs_fulfillment when paid in full.

*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**list_order_pickups**](docs/OrdersApi.md#list_order_pickups) | **GET** /v1/orders/pickups | Booked carrier pickups, newest first.

*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**list_rentals**](docs/OrdersApi.md#list_rentals) | **GET** /v1/rentals | Rentals of your items, with the ship-to and the outbound label.
*Crossly::OrdersApi* | [**update_order**](docs/OrdersApi.md#update_order) | **PATCH** /v1/orders/{id} | Update order (status, notes, tracking).

*Crossly::PolicyPresetsApi* | [**list_policy_preset_accounts**](docs/PolicyPresetsApi.md#list_policy_preset_accounts) | **GET** /v1/policy-presets/accounts | The connected platform accounts a policy can apply to (\"Where this applies\").

*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**create_product_feed_refresh**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#create_product_feed_refresh) | **POST** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/refresh | Drop a scheduled feed’s stored copy so the next fetch builds it fresh.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**create_product_feed_revoke_previous**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#create_product_feed_revoke_previous) | **POST** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/revoke-previous | End a rotation’s grace window now: the previous URL stops answering.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**create_product_feed_rotate**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#create_product_feed_rotate) | **POST** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/rotate | Give the feed a new secret URL. The old URL keeps answering for graceHours (default: the feed’s setting; 0 = stops at once).
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**delete_product_feed**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#delete_product_feed) | **DELETE** /v1/product-feeds/{id} | Delete a feed. Its URL stops answering immediately.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**delete_product_feed_item**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#delete_product_feed_item) | **DELETE** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/items/{listingId} | Remove one listing’s override from this feed.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id} | One feed: its settings, URLs, store and domain, and when it was last fetched and by whom.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed_fetch**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed_fetch) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/fetches | Who fetched the feed and when (90 days): the channel, status, served from copy or live, item count, size.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed_issue**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed_issue) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/issues | The feed’s flagged items one page at a time, by issue code and severity. Pass nextCursor back as after.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed_item**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed_item) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/items | The feed’s per-item overrides (title, GTIN, category, custom labels… for this feed only).
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed_preview**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed_preview) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/preview | A page of the feed exactly as the channel reads it: every column per item, and the raw document text.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**get_product_feed_report**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#get_product_feed_report) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/report | Validate a feed: counts per issue, items with errors (held back) and warnings (sent, limited reach), sample items, store domain and currency.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**list_product_feeds**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#list_product_feeds) | **GET** /v1/product-feeds | List the store’s hosted product feeds (Google, Meta, TikTok, Pinterest, Bing, Snapchat, RSS/Atom, JSON, CSV) with their URLs and last-fetch stats.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**update_product_feed**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#update_product_feed) | **PATCH** /v1/product-feeds/{id} | Edit a feed: name, format, on/paused, which listings (filters), field mapping, currency and region, images, UTM, refresh schedule.
*Crossly::ProductFeedsApi* | [**update_product_feed_item**](docs/ProductFeedsApi.md#update_product_feed_item) | **PUT** /v1/product-feeds/{id}/items/{listingId} | Set one listing’s override in this feed (the listing itself is unchanged). All fields empty removes it.
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**create_product_set**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#create_product_set) | **POST** /v1/product-sets | Create a product set (filters, explicit picks, or both).
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**create_product_set_bulk**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#create_product_set_bulk) | **POST** /v1/product-sets/{id}/bulk | Run a bulk action (delist / relist / check-status) against the set's current members.
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**create_product_set_member**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#create_product_set_member) | **POST** /v1/product-sets/{id}/members | Add / exclude / remove explicit picks for a set.
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**delete_product_set**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#delete_product_set) | **DELETE** /v1/product-sets/{id} | Delete a product set (its id is also dropped from any channel exclusion lists).
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**get_product_set**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#get_product_set) | **GET** /v1/product-sets/{id} | Get one product set (its filters and pick counts).
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**list_product_set_members**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#list_product_set_members) | **GET** /v1/product-sets/{id}/members | Evaluate a set now and return a page of its member listings.
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**list_product_sets**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#list_product_sets) | **GET** /v1/product-sets | List your saved product sets with their live member counts.
*Crossly::ProductSetsApi* | [**update_product_set**](docs/ProductSetsApi.md#update_product_set) | **PATCH** /v1/product-sets/{id} | Update a set: rename, description, or replace its filters.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_candidate_accept**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_candidate_accept) | **POST** /v1/production/candidates/{id}/accept | Accept a graduated design's production terms (edition size, cost, holder access).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_candidate_po**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_candidate_po) | **POST** /v1/production/candidates/{id}/po | Ops: open a purchase order for a candidate. Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_candidate_vote**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_candidate_vote) | **POST** /v1/production/candidates/{id}/vote | Back (support: true) or un-back a design on the production ballot. One vote per person per design; voters must have bought on Crossly.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_design_brief**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_design_brief) | **POST** /v1/production/designs/{cosmeticId}/brief | Set a design's production brief (materials, finish, box, reference images).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_design_consent**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_design_consent) | **POST** /v1/production/designs/{cosmeticId}/consent | Opt a design in or out of physical production.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_design_spec**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_design_spec) | **POST** /v1/production/designs/{cosmeticId}/spec | Set a design's production spec (category, materials, complexity, packaging).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_estimate**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_estimate) | **POST** /v1/production/estimate | Estimate a run's landed cost from a spec + edition size (overshoots for safety).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_production_run_purchase**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_production_run_purchase) | **POST** /v1/production/runs/{id}/purchase | Buy a limited-edition unit of a physical run (holder pricing applies).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**create_receipt_relist**](docs/ProductionApi.md#create_receipt_relist) | **POST** /v1/receipts/{id}/relist | Sell it again: a draft listing of something you bought on Crossly, carrying its purchase receipt so the next buyer inherits its provenance.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_ballot**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_ballot) | **GET** /v1/production/ballot | The ballot: designs waiting for a physical run, most backed first, and whether you may vote.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_candidate**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_candidate) | **GET** /v1/production/candidates | Your designs that have graduated toward a physical run.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_design_pref**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_design_pref) | **GET** /v1/production/designs/{cosmeticId}/prefs | Read a design's production preferences (spec, brief, opt-out).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_drop**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_drop) | **GET** /v1/production/drops | Limited-edition units you own, with fulfillment status.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_earning**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_earning) | **GET** /v1/production/earnings | Your CBX earnings from physical production (royalties + balance + lines).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_line**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_line) | **GET** /v1/production/lines | Your production lines dashboard (candidate + run + inventory).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_po**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_po) | **GET** /v1/production/pos | Ops: list purchase orders (optionally for one candidate). Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_po_reconcile**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_po_reconcile) | **GET** /v1/production/pos/{poId}/reconcile | Ops: reconcile a PO's actuals against the estimate. Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_production_run**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_production_run) | **GET** /v1/production/runs | Browse open limited-edition physical runs (graduated designs).
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**get_shop_listing_provenance**](docs/ProductionApi.md#get_shop_listing_provenance) | **GET** /v1/shop/listings/{slug}/provenance | Crossly provenance behind a shop listing: owners on Crossly and since when (never who or for how much). Null when not bought here.
*Crossly::ProductionApi* | [**update_production_po**](docs/ProductionApi.md#update_production_po) | **PATCH** /v1/production/pos/{poId} | Ops: update a PO (supplier, freight, actuals, QC, status). Admin PAT only.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**create_profile_milestone_celebrate**](docs/ProfileApi.md#create_profile_milestone_celebrate) | **POST** /v1/profile/milestones/{key}/celebrate | Celebrate an earned milestone with a storefront code: percentOff (default 10) off, ending hours (default 48) from now, with a storefront banner (bannerText, default like \"We just hit 1,000 sales\"). One code per milestone: a second call returns the same code (200, existing: true). A milestone you have not earned is a 404.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**create_profile_publish**](docs/ProfileApi.md#create_profile_publish) | **POST** /v1/profile/publish | Publish the draft: crossly.net/seller/<handle>, its share cards and link previews switch to it, and a version is kept (label optional, up to 80 characters).
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**create_profile_share_card**](docs/ProfileApi.md#create_profile_share_card) | **POST** /v1/profile/share-cards | Make a share card for your profile, one stat (ref.key + ref.timeframe) or one earned milestone (ref.key). Returns pageUrl (the link to post: Discord, X and iMessage unfurl it with the card), imageUrl (the 1200x630 PNG) and discordText (a ready line ending in the link). A stat you do not show, or a milestone you have not earned, is a 404. settings overrides the card design (template, background, accent…).
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**get_profile**](docs/ProfileApi.md#get_profile) | **GET** /v1/profile | Your seller profile: the @handle, the PUBLISHED document (what crossly.net/seller/<handle> shows), the DRAFT the editor is working on, when it was last published and whether the draft has unpublished changes. A profile never edited reads as the default profile built from your account.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**get_seller_profile**](docs/ProfileApi.md#get_seller_profile) | **GET** /v1/sellers/{handle}/profile | Any seller's public profile: the person, the published document, resolved stats (a rounded or tier stat never carries its exact value), shown marketplaces, stores, milestones and the share card URLs. 404 when the handle does not exist or the profile is private.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**list_profile_milestones**](docs/ProfileApi.md#list_profile_milestones) | **GET** /v1/profile/milestones | Milestones you earned (first sale, $100k verified sales, 1-year streak, 10 countries…) with when, and whether each is verified. Earned only from verified data; each has its own share page and card.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**list_profile_sources**](docs/ProfileApi.md#list_profile_sources) | **GET** /v1/profile/sources | The accounts, stores and marketplace connections your profile may show: your own account, plus every team you belong to whose owner granted you profile:showcase. Toggle them in document.sources.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**list_profile_stats**](docs/ProfileApi.md#list_profile_stats) | **GET** /v1/profile/stats | Profile stats in the OWNER view: exact values and coverage, whatever display mode the public page uses. keys is a comma list (gmv, items_sold, orders, avg_sale_price, best_sale, sell_through_rate, median_days_to_sale, rating, vault_value, …); timeframe is a rolling token (7d 30d 90d 6mo 12mo ytd all) or a custom range cYYYYMMDD-YYYYMMDD in your time zone. Money is USD cents, rates bps, durations days. verified is true only for numbers backed by marketplace-reported or Crossly-checkout sales.
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**update_me**](docs/ProfileApi.md#update_me) | **PATCH** /v1/me | Update the authenticated user's profile (display name, etc).
*Crossly::ProfileApi* | [**update_profile_document**](docs/ProfileApi.md#update_profile_document) | **PUT** /v1/profile/document | Replace your profile DRAFT with a whole ProfileDocument (theme, hero, ordered sections, socials, sources, share card, privacy). Nothing public changes until you publish. Read the current draft from GET /v1/profile, change it, and send it back. A 400 lists every invalid field.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_brand**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_brand) | **GET** /v1/brands | Search the Crossly brand index. Returns up to 50 matches.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_category**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_category) | **GET** /v1/categories | List Crossly's canonical category tree.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_department**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_department) | **GET** /v1/departments | Search the eBay-sourced \"Department\" item-specific values.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_gender**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_gender) | **GET** /v1/genders | Search the eBay-sourced \"Gender\" item-specific values.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_pattern**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_pattern) | **GET** /v1/patterns | Search the eBay-sourced \"Pattern\" item-specific values.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_reference_postal**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_reference_postal) | **GET** /v1/reference/postal/{country}/{code} | Look up a postal code: canonical shape, state and US metro areas.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_size_system**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_size_system) | **GET** /v1/size-systems | Search the Poshmark + Vestiaire size-system union (US/UK/EU/AU/FR/KR).
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_style**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_style) | **GET** /v1/styles | Search the eBay-sourced \"Style\" item-specific values.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**get_type**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#get_type) | **GET** /v1/types | Search the eBay-sourced \"Type\" item-specific values.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**list_reference_countries**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#list_reference_countries) | **GET** /v1/reference/countries | Every country with its delivery-address rules.
*Crossly::ReferenceApi* | [**list_reference_metros**](docs/ReferenceApi.md#list_reference_metros) | **GET** /v1/reference/metros | Search US metro areas (CSA / CBSA) for location rules.
*Crossly::RestockPromptsApi* | [**create_restock_prompt_dismiss**](docs/RestockPromptsApi.md#create_restock_prompt_dismiss) | **POST** /v1/restock-prompts/{id}/dismiss | Dismiss a pending restock prompt.
*Crossly::RestockPromptsApi* | [**create_restock_prompt_republish**](docs/RestockPromptsApi.md#create_restock_prompt_republish) | **POST** /v1/restock-prompts/{id}/republish | Republish a restock prompt to platforms.
*Crossly::RestockPromptsApi* | [**get_restock_prompt_setting**](docs/RestockPromptsApi.md#get_restock_prompt_setting) | **GET** /v1/restock-prompts/settings | Deprecated: whether every platform account of the store republishes restocked items automatically.
*Crossly::RestockPromptsApi* | [**list_restock_prompts**](docs/RestockPromptsApi.md#list_restock_prompts) | **GET** /v1/restock-prompts | List pending restock prompts.
*Crossly::RestockPromptsApi* | [**update_restock_prompt_setting**](docs/RestockPromptsApi.md#update_restock_prompt_setting) | **PUT** /v1/restock-prompts/settings | Deprecated: turn automatic republish on or off for every platform account of the store at once. Use PUT /api/v1/channel-accounts/:accountId/settings { autoRepublish } per account (removed next release).
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**create_policy_return_received**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#create_policy_return_received) | **POST** /v1/policy-returns/{id}/received | Mark a return received: refunds the buyer, less the restocking or try fee you keep.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**create_return**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#create_return) | **POST** /v1/returns | Open a return record on an order (rejects if another open return exists).
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**create_return_accept**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#create_return_accept) | **POST** /v1/returns/{id}/accept | Accept a Crossly-marketplace return request: a prepaid return label to your return address (the buyer has 14 days to ship), a return record linked to it, and optionally a refund now (refund: { amount? } in dollars; needs orders:refund). Without one, the rest is refunded when the return is delivered. Safe to repeat. Crossposted orders: not_supported_on_platform.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**create_return_decline**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#create_return_decline) | **POST** /v1/returns/{id}/decline | Decline a Crossly-marketplace return request with a reason (required, 8-2000 chars), posted to the buyer as your response. It does not close the request: the buyer can reply or ask Crossly to decide. Not after the label is issued.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**get_return**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#get_return) | **GET** /v1/returns/{id} | Get one return record.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**list_policy_returns**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#list_policy_returns) | **GET** /v1/policy-returns | Returns Crossly buyers opened under your return policy or try at home; filter by orderId.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**list_returns**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#list_returns) | **GET** /v1/returns | List returns. Default: your return records (the physical-return workflow), status=open|closed|<exact>. source=request: the OPEN return requests buyers made on the Crossly marketplace, newest first; accept or decline each with POST /v1/returns/{id}/accept | decline.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**update_return**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#update_return) | **PATCH** /v1/returns/{id} | Transition return status (received/inspected/restocked) and bump inventory on restock.
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**update_return_assignee**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#update_return_assignee) | **PATCH** /v1/returns/assignee | Assign many returns to a teammate, round robin across several, or unassign them (null).
*Crossly::ReturnsApi* | [**update_return_assignee_by_id**](docs/ReturnsApi.md#update_return_assignee_by_id) | **PATCH** /v1/returns/{id}/assignee | Assign one return to a teammate, or unassign it (null).
*Crossly::SalesApi* | [**create_sale_bulk_delete**](docs/SalesApi.md#create_sale_bulk_delete) | **POST** /v1/sales/bulk-delete | Bulk soft-delete sales rows.
*Crossly::SalesApi* | [**list_sales**](docs/SalesApi.md#list_sales) | **GET** /v1/sales | List sales (each unique sale event).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_reset**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_reset) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/reset | Restore the sandbox seller to its seed. Your grant and webhook subscriptions are kept.
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_inventory**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_inventory) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/inventory | Change stock (set, increase or decrease); the new quantity is pushed to every channel.
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_listing_sold_out**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_listing_sold_out) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/listing.sold_out | A listing sells out on a marketplace with no order (stock moves, other channels delist).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_order**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_order) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/order | Simulate a sale through the real sale pipeline (order.created, sale.detected, stock and cross-channel updates).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_order_address**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_order_address) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/order.address | The buyer address of an order simulated with address \"delayed\" arrives now (order.address_available).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_order_cancelled**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_order_cancelled) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/order.cancelled | The buyer or the marketplace cancels an order (order.cancelled).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_order_delivered**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_order_delivered) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/order.delivered | The carrier delivers a shipped order (order.delivered).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**create_sandbox_simulate_refund**](docs/SandboxApi.md#create_sandbox_simulate_refund) | **POST** /v1/sandbox/simulate/refund | Refund an order in full or in part (refund.issued).
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**get_sandbox**](docs/SandboxApi.md#get_sandbox) | **GET** /v1/sandbox | The sandbox seller this token acts on, its simulated channels and the failure modes.
*Crossly::SandboxApi* | [**list_sandbox_events**](docs/SandboxApi.md#list_sandbox_events) | **GET** /v1/sandbox/events | The simulated marketplaces' call log: verb, channel, outcome, latency.
*Crossly::SavedViewsApi* | [**create_saved_view**](docs/SavedViewsApi.md#create_saved_view) | **POST** /v1/saved-views | Create a saved view preset.
*Crossly::SavedViewsApi* | [**delete_saved_view**](docs/SavedViewsApi.md#delete_saved_view) | **DELETE** /v1/saved-views/{id} | Delete a saved view preset.
*Crossly::SavedViewsApi* | [**list_saved_views**](docs/SavedViewsApi.md#list_saved_views) | **GET** /v1/saved-views | List the seller's saved view presets.
*Crossly::SavedViewsApi* | [**update_saved_view**](docs/SavedViewsApi.md#update_saved_view) | **PATCH** /v1/saved-views/{id} | Update a saved view preset.
*Crossly::ScanApi* | [**list_scan_captures**](docs/ScanApi.md#list_scan_captures) | **GET** /v1/scan/captures | Your capture sessions and what they produced.
*Crossly::ScanApi* | [**list_scan_models**](docs/ScanApi.md#list_scan_models) | **GET** /v1/scan/models | Which items already have a 3D model.
*Crossly::SearchApi* | [**get_search**](docs/SearchApi.md#get_search) | **GET** /v1/search | Search everything in your business at once: inventory, listings, orders, sales, customers, conversations, offers, shipments and returns. Exact identifiers (SKU, barcode, order number, tracking number, buyer email, marketplace listing id or a pasted listing URL) rank first, then prefixes, then fuzzy title / brand / buyer matches. Operators: sku:, order:, buyer:, tracking:, in:<type>, status:, platform:. Groups your token has no read scope for are left out.
*Crossly::SellerProfilesApi* | [**get_profile_insight**](docs/SellerProfilesApi.md#get_profile_insight) | **GET** /v1/profile/insights/{timeframe} | Your seller profile's views, share actions and link previews (Discord, X, iMessage…) per day.
*Crossly::SellerProfilesApi* | [**get_seller_share_target**](docs/SellerProfilesApi.md#get_seller_share_target) | **GET** /v1/sellers/{handle}/share-targets | What a public seller profile can be shared as: its shown stats and earned milestones, each with its share page and card image.
*Crossly::ShareImagesApi* | [**get_share_image_drop**](docs/ShareImagesApi.md#get_share_image_drop) | **GET** /v1/share-images/drops/{id} | The share card (og:image) of one of yours: { kind, imageUrl, pageUrl, bucket, title, description }. The image changes only when its state bucket does (progress in tenths, tier reached, time left, sold out).
*Crossly::ShareImagesApi* | [**get_share_image_event**](docs/ShareImagesApi.md#get_share_image_event) | **GET** /v1/share-images/events/{slug} | The share card (og:image) of one of yours: { kind, imageUrl, pageUrl, bucket, title, description }. The image changes only when its state bucket does (progress in tenths, tier reached, time left, sold out).
*Crossly::ShareImagesApi* | [**get_share_image_group_buy**](docs/ShareImagesApi.md#get_share_image_group_buy) | **GET** /v1/share-images/group-buys/{id} | The share card (og:image) of one of yours: { kind, imageUrl, pageUrl, bucket, title, description }. The image changes only when its state bucket does (progress in tenths, tier reached, time left, sold out).
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**create_sku_preset**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#create_sku_preset) | **POST** /v1/sku-presets | Create a named SKU preset in the store (optionally the default).
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**create_sku_preset_preview**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#create_sku_preset_preview) | **POST** /v1/sku-presets/preview | Generate a SKU from a saved preset (presetId) or an unsaved template, for the given listing fields (or a sample listing when fields is omitted). Nothing is written.
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**delete_sku_preset**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#delete_sku_preset) | **DELETE** /v1/sku-presets/{id} | Delete a SKU preset of the store.
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**get_sku_preset**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#get_sku_preset) | **GET** /v1/sku-presets/{id} | Get one SKU preset of the store.
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**list_sku_presets**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#list_sku_presets) | **GET** /v1/sku-presets | List the store's SKU presets, which one is the default, and the template new listings auto-fill their SKU from (meta.autoFill).
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**update_sku_preset**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#update_sku_preset) | **PATCH** /v1/sku-presets/{id} | Rename a SKU preset, change its template, or make it the store's default.
*Crossly::SkuPresetsApi* | [**update_sku_preset_default**](docs/SkuPresetsApi.md#update_sku_preset_default) | **PUT** /v1/sku-presets/default | Choose the store's default SKU preset, which auto-fills the SKU of new listings created without one. presetId null clears it (manual SKUs).
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**create_source_sync_sync**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#create_source_sync_sync) | **POST** /v1/source-sync/{platform}/sync | Reconcile a store now: re-read its whole catalogue and apply every change (runs in the background; 202).
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**get_source_sync_location**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#get_source_sync_location) | **GET** /v1/source-sync/{platform}/locations | POS only (lightspeed_r, lightspeed_x, square): the shops/outlets/locations on the account and which one Crossly syncs stock and sales with.
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**list_source_sync**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#list_source_sync) | **GET** /v1/source-sync | List source-capable stores with their continuous-sync settings and status.
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**list_source_sync_events**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#list_source_sync_events) | **GET** /v1/source-sync/events | Recent source-sync activity: each webhook delivery and reconcile change, with its outcome.
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**update_source_sync**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#update_source_sync) | **PATCH** /v1/source-sync/{platform} | Update a store's source-sync settings: on/off, source-owned fields, deletion handling, auto-export channels.
*Crossly::SourceSyncApi* | [**update_source_sync_location**](docs/SourceSyncApi.md#update_source_sync_location) | **PUT** /v1/source-sync/{platform}/location | POS only: choose the shop (R-Series) / outlet (X-Series) / Square location whose stock and sales Crossly syncs. Must be one GET .../locations lists.
*Crossly::SourcingApi* | [**create_sourcing_receipt**](docs/SourcingApi.md#create_sourcing_receipt) | **POST** /v1/sourcing/receipts | Append a parsed receipt to the sourcing ledger.
*Crossly::SourcingApi* | [**get_sourcing_receipt**](docs/SourcingApi.md#get_sourcing_receipt) | **GET** /v1/sourcing/receipts | List parsed sourcing receipts in this user's ledger.
*Crossly::SourcingApi* | [**list_sourcing_demand**](docs/SourcingApi.md#list_sourcing_demand) | **GET** /v1/sourcing/demand | Items buyers looked for on other sites that Crossly did not have.
*Crossly::SourcingApi* | [**list_sourcing_demand_mine**](docs/SourcingApi.md#list_sourcing_demand_mine) | **GET** /v1/sourcing/demand/mine | Unmet buyer demand for items you hold or have sold before.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**create_stall_book**](docs/StallsApi.md#create_stall_book) | **POST** /v1/stalls/book | Book and pay for a pop-up stall: { street, spotIndex, rail, unit: \"day\", startDate, days } or { ..., unit: \"event\", eventIds }, optional booth { canopy, pattern, lights, counter, piece } (premium values need holding; checked before paying). Paid up front; full-access token and Idempotency-Key required.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**create_stall_booking_cancel**](docs/StallsApi.md#create_stall_booking_cancel) | **POST** /v1/stalls/bookings/{id}/cancel | Cancel a stall before it opens: a full refund on the rail it was paid on. Final once it has opened.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**get_stall_booking_counter**](docs/StallsApi.md#get_stall_booking_counter) | **GET** /v1/stalls/bookings/{id}/counter | A stall counter: the listings you picked (in order), its booth kind and capacity.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**get_stall_quote**](docs/StallsApi.md#get_stall_quote) | **GET** /v1/stalls/quote | Quote a stall: street, spot, and either unit=day with start (YYYY-MM-DD) and days, or unit=event with events (comma-separated event ids). Returns the line items in cents, the rails the spot accepts and whether it is bookable.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**get_stall_street_aerial**](docs/StallsApi.md#get_stall_street_aerial) | **GET** /v1/stalls/streets/{slug}/aerial | A street from above for booking: every spot with its owner, booth size, counter capacity and price, the street events, and who is booked on each date.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**list_stall_listing_search**](docs/StallsApi.md#list_stall_listing_search) | **GET** /v1/stalls/listing-search | Search ANY of your live Crossly listings for a stall counter or convention booth: q, category, min, max (cents), street (marks what its terms allow), limit, offset.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**list_stall_mine**](docs/StallsApi.md#list_stall_mine) | **GET** /v1/stalls/mine | Your stall bookings, newest first, with visits, offers and sales while the stall was up.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**list_street_events**](docs/StallsApi.md#list_street_events) | **GET** /v1/streets/{slug}/events | Current and upcoming street events (Night Market, Swap Meet) on a street, soonest first.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**list_street_stalls**](docs/StallsApi.md#list_street_stalls) | **GET** /v1/streets/{slug}/stalls | Today's pop-up stalls on a street (each with its live listings, awning, sign and seller), plus the spots still free today. meta carries the street, the phase (day / night_market / swap_meet) and the free spots.
*Crossly::StallsApi* | [**update_stall_booking**](docs/StallsApi.md#update_stall_booking) | **PATCH** /v1/stalls/bookings/{id} | Change a booked stall before or while it is up: listingIds (up to 6 of your live listings), awning { colour, pattern }, booth { canopy, pattern, lights, counter, piece } (premium values need holding), sign, attendant.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_highlight**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_highlight) | **POST** /v1/storefront/highlights | Create a highlight: title (up to 24 characters), optional coverUrl, and optional storyIds (your live or archived stories) to save into it. Up to 50 per store.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_highlight_item**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_highlight_item) | **POST** /v1/storefront/highlights/{id}/items | Save stories into a highlight (appended in the order given). Each is copied, so it stays after the story expires. A story already in the highlight is skipped.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_profile_suggest_shelve**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_profile_suggest_shelve) | **POST** /v1/storefront/profile/suggest-shelves | Profile shelves proposed from your top tags and categories (not saved).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_section**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_section) | **POST** /v1/storefront/sections | Add a section at the bottom: kind hero | listings | collections | about | policies | social | announcement | faq (default listings), with its config.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_preview**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_preview) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/preview | Resolve an unsaved design page like the live site would: every section with its listings and extras (preview: true).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_publish**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_publish) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/publish | Publish the draft: it becomes the live site and a publish version is kept (the last 30 versions are kept).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_resolve**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_resolve) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/resolve | The live listings a ListingSource (manual, tags, category, smart rule or all) selects from your store, and how many match.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_starter**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_starter) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/starter | Replace the draft with a starter site (vintage, sneakers, streetwear, collectibles, boutique, home, minimal, drop, general). The current draft is kept as a version first.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_suggest**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_suggest) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/suggest | Auto-build: website sections proposed from your top tags and categories (not saved).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_site_version_restore**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_site_version_restore) | **POST** /v1/storefront/site/versions/{id}/restore | Copy a saved version into the draft (the current draft is kept as a version first). Publish to make it live.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_trade_site_publish**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_trade_site_publish) | **POST** /v1/storefront/trade-site/publish | Publish the trade site draft (a version is kept).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_trade_site_resolve**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_trade_site_resolve) | **POST** /v1/storefront/trade-site/resolve | The listings a ListingSource selects on your trade site: only items in your active wholesale catalogs (narrowed further by source.catalogIds), and how many match.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_trade_site_starter**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_trade_site_starter) | **POST** /v1/storefront/trade-site/starter | Replace the trade site draft with a trade starter (trade_classic, trade_line_sheet, trade_brand_house, trade_apply_first). The current draft is kept as a version first.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**create_storefront_trade_site_suggest**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#create_storefront_trade_site_suggest) | **POST** /v1/storefront/trade-site/suggest | Trade Auto-build: trade site sections proposed from your wholesale catalogs (a spotlight, a rail per catalog, terms, apply banner). Not saved.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**delete_storefront_highlight**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#delete_storefront_highlight) | **DELETE** /v1/storefront/highlights/{id} | Delete a highlight and its saved stories. The original stories are unaffected.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**delete_storefront_highlight_item**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#delete_storefront_highlight_item) | **DELETE** /v1/storefront/highlights/{id}/items/{itemId} | Remove one saved story from a highlight.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**delete_storefront_section**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#delete_storefront_section) | **DELETE** /v1/storefront/sections/{id} | Remove a section. Listings on a removed shelf stay live.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront) | **GET** /v1/storefront | Your storefront design: banner, logo, tagline, bio, theme, SEO title and description, social links, and whether it is published.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront_merch**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront_merch) | **GET** /v1/storefront/merch | Your store's live listings by tag, category, condition and brand, with the price range: what product sources can select.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront_profile**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront_profile) | **GET** /v1/storefront/profile | Your shop profile (the Crossly Shop page): banner, logo, tagline, bio, accent, announcement, shelves, blocks and All items options.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront_shipping**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront_shipping) | **GET** /v1/storefront/shipping | Your Crossly shipping policy (domestic and international services, which countries you ship to) and the default ship-from address of the store named by X-Storefront-Id (else your default store).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront_site**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront_site) | **GET** /v1/storefront/site | Your store website: the draft design, what is live, when each changed, whether the site is switched on, and its custom domain.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**get_storefront_trade_site**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#get_storefront_trade_site) | **GET** /v1/storefront/trade-site | Your store's trade (wholesale) site at /site/:slug/trade: the draft, what is live, whether it is on and its gating (public | prices_private | approved_only).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**list_storefront_highlights**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#list_storefront_highlights) | **GET** /v1/storefront/highlights | Your store’s story highlights in display order, each with its saved stories (media, overlays, product tie-in, when posted). Empty highlights included.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**list_storefront_sections**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#list_storefront_sections) | **GET** /v1/storefront/sections | Your storefront sections in display order: kind, title, visible, config, and listing ids for listings shelves.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**list_storefront_site_versions**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#list_storefront_site_versions) | **GET** /v1/storefront/site/versions | Saved versions of the website, newest first (publish, restore and template points; up to 30).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**list_storefront_story_archive**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#list_storefront_story_archive) | **GET** /v1/storefront/stories/archive | Your stories, live and expired, newest first: what can be saved to a highlight. Paginated: pass before = meta.nextCursor of the previous page.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront) | **PUT** /v1/storefront | Update your storefront design. Omitted fields are unchanged, null clears. published=false keeps the design as a draft and buyers see the standard storefront.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_highlight**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_highlight) | **PATCH** /v1/storefront/highlights/{id} | Rename a highlight or change its cover (coverUrl null = use the first story).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_highlight_item_order**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_highlight_item_order) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/highlights/{id}/items/order | Set the order of a highlight’s saved stories: every item id, first to last.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_highlight_order**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_highlight_order) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/highlights/order | Set the highlight order in one call: every highlight id, first to last.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_profile**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_profile) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/profile | Replace your shop profile (a complete ShopProfile). Saved straight away.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_section**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_section) | **PATCH** /v1/storefront/sections/{id} | Rename, show/hide, refill (listings shelves) or reconfigure a section.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_section_reorder**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_section_reorder) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/sections/reorder | Set the section order in one atomic call: every section id, top to bottom.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_shipping**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_shipping) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/shipping | Set your shipping policy (international.enabled, shipsTo worldwide or ISO codes, excludes) and/or the default ship-from address of the store named by X-Storefront-Id, else your default store (any country; 422 for a sanctioned origin).
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_site_draft**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_site_draft) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/site/draft | Save the website draft (a complete SiteDesign). Validated strictly (a bad field is a 400 naming its path) and sanitized; buyers see nothing until you publish.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_site_enabled**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_site_enabled) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/site/enabled | Switch the website on or off. On requires a publish first; off makes /site/:slug 404 and the custom domain show the shop profile.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_trade_site_draft**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_trade_site_draft) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/trade-site/draft | Save the trade site draft (a complete SiteDesign; trade sections included). Validated strictly and sanitized.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_trade_site_enabled**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_trade_site_enabled) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/trade-site/enabled | Switch the trade site on (needs a publish first) or off.
*Crossly::StorefrontApi* | [**update_storefront_trade_site_gating**](docs/StorefrontApi.md#update_storefront_trade_site_gating) | **PUT** /v1/storefront/trade-site/gating | Who sees the trade site: public (everyone, with prices), prices_private (everyone, prices for approved buyers) or approved_only (approved buyers; others get the apply page).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront) | **POST** /v1/storefronts | Create a store: name (1-60) and optional slug (3-40 lowercase letters, numbers, hyphens; derived from the name when omitted). Your first store becomes the default.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_listing_move**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_listing_move) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/{id}/listings/{platformListingId}/move | Move a Crossly listing (by its platform_listing id) into this store.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_onboarding_skip**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_onboarding_skip) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/onboarding/skip | Skip (or dismiss) the setup wizard: ensures your default store exists, with a generated name you can rename any time. Idempotent.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_onboarding_suggest**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_onboarding_suggest) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/onboarding/suggest | Suggest another generated store name with a free matching slug.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_policy_from_term**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_policy_from_term) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/{id}/policies/from-terms | Create a policy preset from this store's current terms (body: { kind: 'shipping' | 'return' | 'payment' }) and assign it to the store. Returns { preset, policies }.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_policy_use_shared**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_policy_use_shared) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/{id}/policies/use-shared | Make a store use your shared policies: a non-default store drops its own return/shipping/payment policy copies and shows your default policies from then on. No change on the default store. Returns the store settings (policySource per kind, usesOwnPolicy).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_publish**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_publish) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/{id}/publish | Flip a store live. Fails with storefront_requirements_missing (and the missing list) when ship-from, shipping policy, return policy or payouts are not set.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**create_storefront_section_by_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#create_storefront_section_by_id) | **POST** /v1/storefronts/{id}/sections | Add a section to a store (kind hero | listings | collections | about | policies | social | announcement | faq | creations).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**delete_storefront**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#delete_storefront) | **DELETE** /v1/storefronts/{id} | Archive a store. If it was the default, your oldest remaining store becomes the default.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**delete_storefront_section_by_section_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#delete_storefront_section_by_section_id) | **DELETE** /v1/storefronts/{id}/sections/{sectionId} | Remove a section from a store. Listings on a removed shelf stay live.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**delete_storefront_team_access**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#delete_storefront_team_access) | **DELETE** /v1/storefronts/team-access | Revoke a team member's access to one store (or their all-stores grant).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_hub_setup**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_hub_setup) | **GET** /v1/hub/setup/{section} | A Hub section's setup checklist: each step, whether it is done and the page that completes it. Derived from what exists, never stored.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_by_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_by_id) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/{id} | One store: name, slug, status (draft|live|paused|archived), isDefault, nameAuto, onboardedAt, currency (null = never chosen).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_onboarding**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_onboarding) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/onboarding | Crossly Shop setup state: whether you have a store, the generated name/slug a skip would use, each optional step and whether it is done, the listing-gate requirements, payouts readiness and the public URL once live.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_policy**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_policy) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/{id}/policies | A store's policies: per kind (shipping, return, payment) the preset the store picked (presetId, null = your default) and what the store resolves to (resolvedPresetId, resolvedPresetName, level: store | seller_default | legacy | default, body).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_requirement**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_requirement) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/{id}/requirements | What this store still needs before a NEW Crossly listing can publish into it: ship_from, shipping_policy, return_policy, payouts — with where to fix each.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_setting**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_setting) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/{id}/settings | A store's settings: design (banner, logo, theme, SEO, social, published), ship-from, return address, policies (storePolicies: the store's preset picks + what each kind resolves to), custom-order flag, featured listings, bundle discount, custom domain. DEPRECATED aliases, resolved through the policy cascade: offersFreeShipping, freeShippingThresholdCents, offersLocalPickup, localCourierEnabled, acceptsLayaway, sellerProtections, requestArrivalCondition, arrivalConditionWindowHours — use /v1/storefronts/:id/policies and the preset fields.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**get_storefront_slug_available**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#get_storefront_slug_available) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/slug-available | Is this store slug free? Checks shape, reserved words, current slugs and retired ones.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**list_storefront_sections_by_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#list_storefront_sections_by_id) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/{id}/sections | A store's sections in display order.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**list_storefront_team_access**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#list_storefront_team_access) | **GET** /v1/storefronts/team-access | Every active per-store grant you have made to team members (storefrontId null = all stores).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**list_storefronts**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#list_storefronts) | **GET** /v1/storefronts | Your stores (default first). includeArchived=true adds archived ones.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_by_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_by_id) | **PATCH** /v1/storefronts/{id} | Rename a store. Never changes the slug or your @handle.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_currency**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_currency) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/currency | Set a store's currency (ISO 4217, e.g. GBP): what its inventory values, totals and analytics show in, and what a price sent without a currency is stored in.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_default**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_default) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/default | Make this store your default (where a Crossly publish with no storefrontId lands).
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_policy**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_policy) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/policies | Set a store's policies: shippingPresetId, returnPresetId, paymentPresetId (a policy preset of that kind you own, or null for your default). Omitted = unchanged. Listings in the store with no preset of their own follow these.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_section_by_section_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_section_by_section_id) | **PATCH** /v1/storefronts/{id}/sections/{sectionId} | Rename, show/hide, refill (listings shelves) or reconfigure one section of a store.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_section_reorder_by_id**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_section_reorder_by_id) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/sections/reorder | Set a store's section order in one atomic call: every section id, top to bottom.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_setting**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_setting) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/settings | Update a store's settings. Omitted fields are unchanged, null clears. Policies replace wholesale. shippingPresetId / returnPresetId / paymentPresetId pick the store's presets. The checkout-term fields (offersFreeShipping … arrivalConditionWindowHours) are DEPRECATED aliases: they are written into the store's preset.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_slug**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_slug) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/slug | Change a store's slug. The old slug keeps redirecting to the store.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_status**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_status) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/{id}/status | Set a store's status: draft | live | paused | archived. A live store is public once it has a live listing.
*Crossly::StorefrontsApi* | [**update_storefront_team_access**](docs/StorefrontsApi.md#update_storefront_team_access) | **PUT** /v1/storefronts/team-access | Grant (or replace) a team member's access to one store, or to every store (storefrontId null), with scopes.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_challenge**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_challenge) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/challenges | Open a 1v1 challenge with one of your decks; another player joins it.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_deck**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_deck) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/decks | Create (no id) or update a deck: its lead (Crew Boss, Mastermind or Keeper) and 40 cards you own, checked against the deck rules of its game.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_match_act**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_match_act) | **POST** /v1/tcg/matches/{id}/act | Make one move: { action } as listed in the match's legal moves. The engine checks it.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_match_join**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_match_join) | **POST** /v1/tcg/matches/{id}/join | Join an open 1v1 challenge with one of your decks.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_pack_open**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_pack_open) | **POST** /v1/tcg/packs/{id}/open | Open one of your earned packs: its eight cards join your collection.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_practice**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_practice) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/practice | Start a practice match against the house bot with one of your decks.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_queue**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_queue) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/queue | Join the ranked queue with an owned, legal deck (free). Returns the match at once when an opponent fits.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_starter**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_starter) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/starter | Claim the free starter deck for a game, choosing its faction (once per account; account-bound, untradeable).
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_table**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_table) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/table | Sit at a table with your free starter deck (claimed now if you never did: starter names it, else one is picked for you): joins the oldest open 1v1 challenge, or opens one; already waiting, your own seat back.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_tournament**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_tournament) | **POST** /v1/tcg/{game}/tournaments | Host a Swiss tournament: name, optional top cut (4 or 8), round length, player cap and start time.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_tournament_drop**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_tournament_drop) | **POST** /v1/tcg/tournaments/{id}/drop | Leave a tournament before it starts, or drop from it (your results stand).
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_tournament_register**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_tournament_register) | **POST** /v1/tcg/tournaments/{id}/register | Enter a tournament with an owned, legal deck before it starts.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**create_tcg_tournament_start**](docs/TCGApi.md#create_tcg_tournament_start) | **POST** /v1/tcg/tournaments/{id}/start | As the host, start the tournament: Swiss rounds are sized to the field and round 1 is dealt.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**delete_tcg_deck**](docs/TCGApi.md#delete_tcg_deck) | **DELETE** /v1/tcg/decks/{id} | Delete one of your decks.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**delete_tcg_queue**](docs/TCGApi.md#delete_tcg_queue) | **DELETE** /v1/tcg/{game}/queue | Leave the ranked queue.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game_print**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game_print) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/print | Print file links for your own creator card game (owner only): the set's print file, home sheets and a proof, for its newest version.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game_print_by_kind**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game_print_by_kind) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/print/{kind} | A PDF of your own creator card game (owner only): kind set | sheet | proof; ?version=, ?paper=letter|a4, ?card= for a proof. Binary (application/pdf).
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_ladder**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_ladder) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/ladder | The season's ranked leaderboard for a game: place, rating, tier and record (named only where the player chose to be).
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_ladder_me**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_ladder_me) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/ladder/me | Your ranked rating, tier, place and record this season, and whether you are queued.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_match**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_match) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/matches | Your recent matches, and open challenges you could join.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_match_by_id**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_match_by_id) | **GET** /v1/tcg/matches/{id} | Your view of a match (no hidden information) and, on your turn, every legal move.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_me**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_me) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/me | Your starter faction, collection (card id -> copies), decks and earned packs.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_print**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_print) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/print | Print files for one of Crossly's own card games: a card proof (template), 3 x 3 Letter/A4 home sheets with crop marks, and the set's print file (63 x 88 mm, 3 mm bleed).
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**get_tcg_tournament**](docs/TCGApi.md#get_tcg_tournament) | **GET** /v1/tcg/tournaments/{id} | A tournament's standings (points, opponents' and game win %), this round's pairings and your seat.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**list_tcg_cards**](docs/TCGApi.md#list_tcg_cards) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/cards | The game's card pool: every card with its rules text, stats, rarity and collector number.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**list_tcg_games**](docs/TCGApi.md#list_tcg_games) | **GET** /v1/tcg/games | Crossly's own card games: id, name, seats and card count.
*Crossly::TCGApi* | [**list_tcg_tournaments**](docs/TCGApi.md#list_tcg_tournaments) | **GET** /v1/tcg/{game}/tournaments | A game's recent and upcoming Swiss tournaments (free to enter; prizes are earned packs).
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**create_tcg_creator_game**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#create_tcg_creator_game) | **POST** /v1/tcg/creator/games | Submit a card-game package version for review: game.json, cards.json, rules/game.js, thumb.png, art/*.png as { path, base64 }. Checked, conformance-run, art quarantined, then reviewed by a person.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**create_tcg_creator_game_check**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#create_tcg_creator_game_check) | **POST** /v1/tcg/creator/games/check | Check a card-game package exactly as a submission is checked -- the static report and bot games played against your rules in the sandbox -- storing nothing. Files as { path, base64 }.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**create_tcg_creator_game_pack_open**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#create_tcg_creator_game_pack_open) | **POST** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/packs/{packId}/open | Open a booster you hold: the game's rules pick the pulls, the pack is used up, and one COSM copy per card joins your collection (logged with its seed).
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**create_tcg_creator_game_starter**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#create_tcg_creator_game_starter) | **POST** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/starter | Claim a creator game's free starter deck by its key (once per account; account-bound, never tradeable).
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**create_tcg_creator_game_version_withdraw**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#create_tcg_creator_game_version_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/versions/{v}/withdraw | Withdraw one of your versions that is still waiting for review.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id} | One live creator card game: its set, every card (rules text, rarity, art URL, game data) and its COSM prices.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game_collection**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game_collection) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/collection | Your playable copies of a creator game: card id -> copies (free starter plus every COSM copy you hold).
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game_popularity**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game_popularity) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/popularity | The set's progress toward going on chain: distinct collectors, packs opened and resales against the bar; when it passes, every holder may mint their cards as NFTs.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**get_tcg_creator_game_shelf**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#get_tcg_creator_game_shelf) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/shelf | The game's COSM shelf: the booster and every single, each with its COSM design id (buy via /api/v1/cosm/cosmetics/:id/buy), price, edition cap and copies issued.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**list_tcg_creator_game_packs**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#list_tcg_creator_game_packs) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games/{id}/packs | Your unopened boosters of a creator game.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**list_tcg_creator_games**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#list_tcg_creator_games) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/games | Live creator card games: name, set, description, typical match length, frame colours, thumbnail.
*Crossly::TCGCreatorsApi* | [**list_tcg_creator_mine**](docs/TCGCreatorsApi.md#list_tcg_creator_mine) | **GET** /v1/tcg/creator/mine | Your creator card games and every version with its review state and note.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**create_mileage**](docs/TaxApi.md#create_mileage) | **POST** /v1/mileage | Create a mileage entry.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**delete_mileage**](docs/TaxApi.md#delete_mileage) | **DELETE** /v1/mileage/{id} | Delete a mileage entry.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**get_mileage_summary**](docs/TaxApi.md#get_mileage_summary) | **GET** /v1/mileage/summary | Annual mileage totals + IRS deduction. Each trip is priced at the IRS rate on its own date (2026 has two rates, listed in `rates`); `ratePerMile` is the January rate.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**get_tax_sales_tax**](docs/TaxApi.md#get_tax_sales_tax) | **GET** /v1/tax/sales-tax | Crossly marketplace sales Crossly did not collect sales tax on, by jurisdiction.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**get_tax_schedule_c**](docs/TaxApi.md#get_tax_schedule_c) | **GET** /v1/tax/schedule-c | Schedule C JSON for a tax year (default the current year), from the same P&L as GET /v1/books: line 1 gross receipts before refunds, line 2 refunds, COGS in Part III, completed sales only. GET /v1/books has the full P&L by period.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**list_mileage**](docs/TaxApi.md#list_mileage) | **GET** /v1/mileage | List mileage entries, newest trip first, paged (limit up to 500). Narrow by year and/or an inclusive from/to date range.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**list_tax_crossly_collects**](docs/TaxApi.md#list_tax_crossly_collects) | **GET** /v1/tax/crossly-collects | Where Crossly collects sales tax today (its active Stripe Tax registrations).
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**list_tax_sales_tax_not_collected**](docs/TaxApi.md#list_tax_sales_tax_not_collected) | **GET** /v1/tax/sales-tax/not-collected | The individual Crossly sales Crossly did not collect sales tax on, newest first.
*Crossly::TaxApi* | [**update_mileage**](docs/TaxApi.md#update_mileage) | **PATCH** /v1/mileage/{id} | Update a mileage entry.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**get_category_by_id**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#get_category_by_id) | **GET** /v1/categories/{id} | One canonical category and the category it maps to on each marketplace.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**get_taxonomy_category**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#get_taxonomy_category) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/{platform}/categories | Categories for a platform. Default is top-level; pass `?parent=<categoryId>` to drill down one level (supported on cookie platforms whose recipe returns flat parent_id-linked rows).
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**get_taxonomy_category_aspect**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#get_taxonomy_category_aspect) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/{platform}/categories/{id}/aspects | Item-specific aspects (eBay) / properties (Etsy) / hard-coded enums (cookie platforms) for a category.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**get_taxonomy_category_children**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#get_taxonomy_category_children) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/{platform}/categories/{id}/children | Direct children of a category node.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**get_taxonomy_required_field**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#get_taxonomy_required_field) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/{platform}/required-fields | Normalized field schema the seller needs to fill before crossposting to this platform. Combines master fields (title/description/price/condition) with platform-specific overrides.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**list_category_aspects**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#list_category_aspects) | **GET** /v1/categories/{id}/aspects | Item specifics for a canonical category: required/optional, known values, per-marketplace names.
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**list_category_search**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#list_category_search) | **GET** /v1/categories/search | Search or browse Crossly's canonical categories (the ids listings take as categoryId).
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**list_taxonomy_ebay_charities**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#list_taxonomy_ebay_charities) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/ebay/charities | Search eBay for Charity organisations (the charityId an eBay listing donates to).
*Crossly::TaxonomyApi* | [**list_taxonomy_suggest**](docs/TaxonomyApi.md#list_taxonomy_suggest) | **GET** /v1/taxonomy/{platform}/suggest | Reverse lookup — suggest categories matching a search phrase: eBay live; Poshmark, Mercari, Depop, Grailed, Vinted, Whatnot, Vestiaire, OfferUp, Facebook and Curtsy over their harvested trees. Etsy answers an empty list (browse its tree instead).
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**create_team_accept**](docs/TeamApi.md#create_team_accept) | **POST** /v1/team/accept | Accept a team invitation by the raw token from its link.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**create_team_invite**](docs/TeamApi.md#create_team_invite) | **POST** /v1/team/invites | Invite someone to one or more stores, each with its permissions; returns the one-time accept URL.

*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**create_team_leave**](docs/TeamApi.md#create_team_leave) | **POST** /v1/team/leave | Leave every team this user is currently a member of.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**create_team_member_store**](docs/TeamApi.md#create_team_member_store) | **POST** /v1/team/members/{id}/stores | Add a team member to a store with its permissions.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**create_team_revoke**](docs/TeamApi.md#create_team_revoke) | **POST** /v1/team/revoke | Revoke a pending invite OR an active team member.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**delete_team_member_store**](docs/TeamApi.md#delete_team_member_store) | **DELETE** /v1/team/members/{id}/stores/{store} | Remove a team member from one store (their other stores stay).
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**get_assignee**](docs/TeamApi.md#get_assignee) | **GET** /v1/assignees | The teammates work can be assigned to (active seats; isMe marks yours).
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**get_team**](docs/TeamApi.md#get_team) | **GET** /v1/team | List pending team invitations + active members.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**get_team_member**](docs/TeamApi.md#get_team_member) | **GET** /v1/team/members/{id} | One team member: added, last active and the stores they staff.

*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**list_team_invites**](docs/TeamApi.md#list_team_invites) | **GET** /v1/team/invites | List team invitations with the stores each adds the person to.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**list_team_members**](docs/TeamApi.md#list_team_members) | **GET** /v1/team/members | List team members with the stores each staffs (search, store, preset, permission, status, last active).
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**list_team_stores**](docs/TeamApi.md#list_team_stores) | **GET** /v1/team/stores | The stores whose team you may see, invite to and manage.
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**update_team**](docs/TeamApi.md#update_team) | **PATCH** /v1/team/{memberId} | Update a team member's scopes (owner only).
*Crossly::TeamApi* | [**update_team_member_store**](docs/TeamApi.md#update_team_member_store) | **PUT** /v1/team/members/{id}/stores/{store} | Set a member's permissions and limits in one store.

*Crossly::TemplatesApi* | [**delete_me_template_share**](docs/TemplatesApi.md#delete_me_template_share) | **DELETE** /v1/me/templates/{id}/share | Revoke a template share link.
*Crossly::TemplatesApi* | [**get_me_template**](docs/TemplatesApi.md#get_me_template) | **GET** /v1/me/templates/{id} | Fetch one template in full.
*Crossly::TemplatesApi* | [**get_me_template_suggest**](docs/TemplatesApi.md#get_me_template_suggest) | **GET** /v1/me/templates/suggest | The seller's default template for a category.

*Crossly::TemplatesApi* | [**list_me_templates**](docs/TemplatesApi.md#list_me_templates) | **GET** /v1/me/templates | List the seller's templates.
*Crossly::TemplatesApi* | [**update_me_template**](docs/TemplatesApi.md#update_me_template) | **PATCH** /v1/me/templates/{id} | Patch a template.

*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**create_toolbox_asset_purchase**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#create_toolbox_asset_purchase) | **POST** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/purchase | Open a Crossly checkout for a paid asset. *Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**create_toolbox_asset_retire**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#create_toolbox_asset_retire) | **POST** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/retire | Take an asset off the store for good. Licences already sold keep working.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**create_toolbox_asset_use**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#create_toolbox_asset_use) | **POST** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/use | Get the asset's nodes stamped with its id for a place you can edit; insert:true also inserts them into the place's live session. *Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**create_toolbox_asset_version**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#create_toolbox_asset_version) | **POST** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/versions | A new version (reviewed); the live one keeps serving until it is approved.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**create_toolbox_purchase_refund**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#create_toolbox_purchase_refund) | **POST** /v1/toolbox/purchases/{purchaseId}/refund | Refund a licence within 14 days while no published version of yours uses the asset. *Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**delete_toolbox_asset_rating_mine**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#delete_toolbox_asset_rating_mine) | **DELETE** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/ratings/mine | Remove your rating of an asset.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**get_toolbox_asset**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#get_toolbox_asset) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId} | One asset: versions, credits (assets it includes), licence, contents, your purchase.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**get_toolbox_asset_rating**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#get_toolbox_asset_rating) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/ratings | Ratings of an asset: average stars, breakdown, recent reviews, yours, and whether you may rate it.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**get_toolbox_purchase**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#get_toolbox_purchase) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/purchases/{purchaseId} | A purchase: status, price, tax, refundable until.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**list_toolbox_assets**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#list_toolbox_assets) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/assets | Search the Toolbox: models, scripts, sounds, images, packages. Free and paid; `owned` says whether you may insert it now.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**list_toolbox_library**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#list_toolbox_library) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/library | Assets you may insert: licences you bought, your own, free ones you used.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**list_toolbox_mine**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#list_toolbox_mine) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/mine | Your published assets: review state, reviewer note, what each earned you.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**list_toolbox_sales**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#list_toolbox_sales) | **GET** /v1/toolbox/sales | Your Toolbox sales: gross, net, your 80% share after refunds. Never who bought.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**update_toolbox_asset**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#update_toolbox_asset) | **PATCH** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId} | Edit name, description, tags, thumbnail or price.
*Crossly::ToolboxApi* | [**update_toolbox_asset_rating_mine**](docs/ToolboxApi.md#update_toolbox_asset_rating_mine) | **PUT** /v1/toolbox/assets/{assetId}/ratings/mine | Rate an asset you bought or installed: 1–5 stars and an optional review (1000 characters). Not your own.

*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**create_trade_block_evolution_recipe**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#create_trade_block_evolution_recipe) | **POST** /v1/trade-block/evolution-recipes | Author a Trade Evolution recipe (a graded item or pair that, when swapped, unlocks a cosmetic you designed). *Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**create_trade_block_post_close**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#create_trade_block_post_close) | **POST** /v1/trade-block/posts/{id}/close | Close one of your posts so it stops taking offers.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**create_trade_block_post_offer**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#create_trade_block_post_offer) | **POST** /v1/trade-block/posts/{id}/offers | Respond to a post with a concrete swap: items on each side (0..N), optional cash.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**create_trade_block_set_recipe**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#create_trade_block_set_recipe) | **POST** /v1/trade-block/set-recipes | Author a set-completion recipe (a set of items that, once assembled through trading, unlocks a set-master cosmetic you designed). Born unreviewed until ops approves.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**get_trade_block_evolution**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#get_trade_block_evolution) | **GET** /v1/trade-block/evolutions | List your Trade Evolution unlocks — completed swaps that unlocked an evolved or set-master NFT you can mint by burning COSM.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**get_trade_block_post**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#get_trade_block_post) | **GET** /v1/trade-block/posts | Browse open Trade Block posts (wants + offers) on the Crossly Market.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**get_trade_block_post_by_id**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#get_trade_block_post_by_id) | **GET** /v1/trade-block/posts/{id} | Get one Trade Block post with its items and open-offer count.
*Crossly::TradeBlockApi* | [**get_trade_block_trade**](docs/TradeBlockApi.md#get_trade_block_trade) | **GET** /v1/trade-block/trades | List the trades you are a party to, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_lend**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_lend) | **POST** /v1/vault/{id}/lend | Turn lending on or off for a unit you own in the vault, optionally with your minimum borrow rate (APR in basis points). Returns `{ lendEnabled, minAprBps }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_account**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_account) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/account | Open your account at the custodian partner (once). Returns `{ status }`; the custodian verifies it before it is active.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_admin_mark**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_admin_mark) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/admin-marks | Upload your fund administrator's prices (`administrator`, `marks: [{ bookKey, day, cents }]`). Shown beside Crossly's marks and labelled as yours; they never change a Crossly figure.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_advance**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_advance) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/advances | Take an advance against a vault unit that is listed for sale: paid into your custodian account, netted from the sale. Free for 60 days, then the pool rate. Needs a verified ID.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_advance_relist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_advance_relist) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/advances/{id}/relist | Relist an advance's unit at a new price (never so low its sale could not repay the advance).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_agent_draft_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_agent_draft_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/agent/drafts/{id}/cancel | Throw a drafted order away (only while it is still a draft). Returns { ok, draft }.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_agent_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_agent_watch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/agent/watches | Watch a book key: tell me once when its best bid, lowest ask, last trade or market price crosses a price (cents). Leave out direction for \"crosses\" (whichever way it has to move). Never trades. At most 50 live, each for 90 days.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_alert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_alert) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/alerts | Alert me when a key's market price goes above or below a price (fires once).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_alert_pref_test**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_alert_pref_test) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/alert-prefs/test | Send yourself one test alert of a `kind` through your own switches. Returns `{ sent, kind, plan }`, where plan says per channel: send, off, quiet (held by quiet hours) or unavailable.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_auction_lot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_auction_lot) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots | Consign a stored, authenticated unit to the next weekly or premier event, with an optional opening price and reserve. The unit is held until the close. Returns `{ lotId, eventId, endsAt }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_auction_lot_bid**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_auction_lot_bid) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id}/bids | Leave or raise your maximum on a lot; the system bids for you up to it. A bid in the last 2 minutes extends the lot by 2 minutes. The winner's saved card is charged at the close through the order book. ID needed above $10,000.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_auction_lot_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_auction_lot_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your lot before any bid; the unit goes back on your shelf. Returns `{ ok }`.
