*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_auto_buy_pause**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_auto_buy_pause) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/auto-buy/pause | Pause Auto-buy with payouts: the budget and want list stay; no payouts are counted and nothing is bought.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_auto_buy_resume**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_auto_buy_resume) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/auto-buy/resume | Resume Auto-buy with payouts: payouts released from now on count.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_block**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_block) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/blocks | Place a block buy for an entity: one order booked to the first sub-account in `plan` (it pays from its custodian account), split across every sub-account in the plan at the average price, fees included, once it is done and the custodian confirmed each payment. Each share's cash moves between the entity's own custodian accounts; its units move only after that settles. Every sub-account must be one you may trade, and its share must fit its limits.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_block_allocate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_block_allocate) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId}/allocate | Allocate a done block now (the keeper does it too). Returns `{ outcome, block }`: allocated, waiting (a payment or transfer is not settled yet), nothing_filled or none.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_bullion_family_peg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_bullion_family_peg) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs | Set a family-wide spot-pegged quote: one \"any 1 oz gold coin\" quote resting on each product book of a bullion family at the chosen condition (DS, VNDS or Used). When any leg fills it comes off the others and goes back on only for what is left; two fills at once are settled one at a time against what is left. A bid pays from your custodian account. Replaces your live family quote on the same family, condition and side. ID required.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_bullion_peg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_bullion_peg) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs | Set a spot-pegged maker quote on a bullion book key: \"spot plus or minus N cents\" or \"plus or minus N basis points\", with an optional floor and cap. It rests as a pro limit order re-priced every spot tick and is pulled while spot is stale. Replaces your live quote on the same key and side. ID required.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_collection_worth_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_collection_worth_watch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/collection-worth/watch | Watch every collection item that sits on a vault book key (up to the watchlist cap). Returns `{ watched, skipped }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_creator_attribution**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_creator_attribution) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/creators/attribution | Use a creator code: the creator earns a capped share of the fee Crossly keeps on your Vault Markets trades for a limited window (paid by Crossly, never by you). Only for people new to Vault Markets, once, and never your own code.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_creator_cohost_invite_respond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_creator_cohost_invite_respond) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/creators/cohost-invites/{id}/respond | For creators: accept or decline an invite to co-host a Market Open launch show (`accept` true or false). Accepting needs your vault room verified and adds you to the show as a co-host; your room and the Commons then say you co-host it, with the creator-programme disclosure when it applies.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_credit_repay**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_credit_repay) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/credit/{id}/repay | Repay from your custodian account, interest first. Omit `amountCents` to repay everything; pass `requestId` to make a retry safe. Repaid in full, the items are free.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dcm_link**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dcm_link) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/link | Link a partner exchange account to your account (or to a firm sub-account you manage, `subaccountId`) so Crossly can show its positions: `accountRef` (the account id there) and `code` (the one-time link code it gave you). The partner must confirm the code; the code is never stored. Read-only: Crossly never trades on it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dcm_unlink**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dcm_unlink) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/unlink | Unlink the partner exchange account from your account (or the firm sub-account `subaccountId`): Crossly stops reading it and its hedges leave portfolio margin.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dealer_apply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dealer_apply) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/apply | Apply to be a dealer (ID check first). Ops review; approved dealers get raised limits and their bids show as dealer bids.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dealer_bid**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dealer_bid) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bids | Place a standing dealer bid: a resting GTC/GTD limit buy, flagged as a dealer bid. Approved dealers only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dealer_pool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dealer_pool) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools | Open a curve pool on a key (approved dealers, ID checked): linear (delta in cents) or exponential (delta in bps), spot, fee, levels per side, units it may sell, and a buy budget from your custodian account. Its ladder goes up at once.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dealer_pool_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dealer_pool_close) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id}/close | Close a curve pool: its legs come off the book; what filled stays filled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_dealer_pool_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_dealer_pool_preview) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/preview | The bids and asks a set of curve settings would show, and why each side stops where it does. Places nothing.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_deposit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_deposit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/deposits | Open a ship-in deposit for an ungraded catalogue item (`skuId`, `variantId`, `condition` DS / VNDS / Used). It is inbound until the vault receives and checks it, then stored in your name. Returns `{ vaultItem: { id, status } }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_donation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_donation) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/donations | Give a unit to Crossly Cares (once the charity can receive; otherwise its gate message). Returns `{ id, markCents }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities | Open an entity account: you become its admin and the custodian partner starts KYB (its own flow; Crossly keeps only its reference). Needs a verified ID and the custodian (503 names what is missing).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_ecp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_ecp) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/ecp | Admin: record the entity's attestation that it is an eligible contract participant. Raising its caps is still an ops decision.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_halt**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_halt) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/halts | Admin or risk: the firm kill switch. Halts the entity (no `subaccountId`) or one sub-account: open orders on it are cancelled and no new ones are placed until cleared.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_halt_clear**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_halt_clear) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/halts/{haltId}/clear | Admin or risk: lift a firm halt.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_invitation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_invitation) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/invitation | Accept or decline an invitation (`accept`). Trading, admin and risk roles need a verified ID to accept.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_kyb**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_kyb) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/kyb | Refresh the entity's KYB status from the custodian (and its onboarding link while KYB is open).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_member**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_member) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members | Admin: invite a Crossly user by email as admin, trader, risk, ops or viewer (a viewer can never place an order), with optional limits and sub-accounts.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_subaccount**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_subaccount) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts | Admin: open a sub-account once the custodian approved the entity; its limits are held to the entity's caps.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_entity_subaccount_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_entity_subaccount_close) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts/{subId}/close | Admin: close a sub-account that holds nothing and has no open orders.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_follow_copy_watch_alert_read**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_follow_copy_watch_alert_read) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/alerts/read | Mark every copy-watch alert read. Returns `{ marked }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_gift**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_gift) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/gifts | Give a unit in your vault to another Crossly user (by @username or email). It stays on its shelf; they have 7 days to accept. ID needed above $1,000 at the mark. Returns `{ id, expiresAt }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_gift_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_gift_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/accept | Accept a gift: the unit becomes yours in place. Returns `{ status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_gift_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_gift_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/cancel | Cancel a gift you sent before it is accepted. Returns `{ status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_gift_decline**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_gift_decline) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/gifts/{id}/decline | Decline a gift: the unit stays with the giver. Returns `{ status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault | Start Grade to Vault on one item (`source` inventory or vault_unit). With `runId` the grading slot is booked at once (free; your saved card is charged only if the run locks). Nothing is listed until you confirm a price after the grade.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_book) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/book | Book (or re-book) the grading slot of a flow still at \"picked\". Answers 503 naming GRADING_HUB_ADDRESS when runs are not open.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/cancel | Stop the flow before the card goes to the grader (still picked, or its slot only reserved: nothing was charged).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_keep**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_keep) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/keep | Keep the graded unit in your vault without listing it. The flow ends; list it any time from the vault.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_list**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_list) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/list | Confirm the price for the grade the unit came back with (`confirm: true`, `gradeKey` must match the unit). Pre-trade risk, the price band and the ID line apply, then one shelf ask is placed on the Crossly order book.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_shipped**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grade_to_vault_shipped) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id}/shipped | You mailed the card to the grading hub (an inventory card, once its run locked).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grading) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading | Send a raw vault unit on the grading hub's shelf to a Grading Run (`runId`). It is held until it comes back, graded, to the same vault item. The slot is free; your saved card is charged only if the run locks.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_grading_pull**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_grading_pull) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/grading/{submissionId}/pull | Pull a vault unit out of its run before the run locks (nothing was charged). It goes back on the shelf.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_hall_loan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_hall_loan) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans | Lend a unit in your Crossly vault to the Hall of Grails: { vaultItemId, credit: 'name' | 'anonymous' }. A standing loan of display rights only: the unit goes on a plinth, under a plate naming you (or \"a collector\"), in every weekly exhibition whose category it fits. It never moves and stays yours to list, sell or withdraw. Earns reputation per exhibition shown; no cash. Resumes a loan you opted out of while it still shows.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_hall_loan_credit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_hall_loan_credit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans/{id}/credit | Change how the plate on a lent unit's plinth names you, from now: { credit: 'name' } (your display name) or { credit: 'anonymous' } (\"a collector\").
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_hall_loan_opt_out**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_hall_loan_opt_out) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans/{id}/opt-out | Opt a Hall of Grails loan out, at any time. A unit on a plinth this week finishes this exhibition (status ending, showingUntil); otherwise the loan ends now (status ended).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_identity_start**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_identity_start) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/identity/start | Start (or resume) the one-time ID check the money features and larger orders need. Returns `{ url }` to open, or `{ alreadyVerified: true }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_index**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_index) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/indexes | Make an index of your own: 1 to 50 book keys, weighted by mark or equally. Up to 10 per account.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_index_buy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_index_buy) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/buy | Buy the basket: one limit buy per key in the index for 1-5 whole units each, at most `maxBpsOverMark` over each key's mark, IOC or GTC. Every unit is a real item that lands in your vault. Idempotent on `clientBasketId`; returns a result per key.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_index_complaint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_index_complaint) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/index-complaints | File a complaint about an index or the method (`slug` optional). Every complaint is answered. Returns `{ id, status, createdAt }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_index_consultation_comment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_index_consultation_comment) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations/{id}/comments | Comment on an open consultation (5 to 5,000 characters; up to 5 per person). Comments are published without your name. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_instant_exit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_instant_exit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit | Sell a unit now at the quoted price: a sell IOC at `priceCents` through the pro engine. Refused, with the new quote, if the best bid dropped below it. Crossly never buys or funds it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint | Open a joint vault (ID first): you hold full rights and accept the current joint terms. `invite` sends invitations (@username or email, each with view, trade or full rights). Returns `{ id, status, invited }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_close) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/close | Close the joint vault (full rights): open joint orders are cancelled and every unit goes back to its owner. Returns `{ status, unitsReturned }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_invite**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_invite) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/invite | Invite a Crossly user (full rights only). They have 14 days and show ID to join. Returns `{ userId, rights, expiresAt }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_member_remove**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_member_remove) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/members/{userId}/remove | Remove a member (full rights), or leave when userId is you. Their units go back to their own account and their joint orders are cancelled. Returns `{ userId, status, unitsReturned, jointStatus }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_member_right**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_member_right) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/members/{userId}/rights | Set a member rights to view, trade or full (full rights only; the vault always keeps a full member). Returns `{ userId, rights }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_respond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_respond) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/respond | Accept (ID first; accepts the current joint terms) or decline an invitation. Returns `{ status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_term**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_term) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/terms | Accept the current joint terms (needed to trade after the terms version changes). Returns `{ termsVersion }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_trade**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_trade) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades | Place a limit order for the joint vault (trade or full rights, ID shown). A buy (bookKey) is placed on you, paid from your own Vault Markets account, and what it buys is booked to the joint vault. A sell (vaultItemId of a unit in it) is placed on the unit owner of record, who gets the proceeds; pre-trade checks run against you too. Returns `{ tradeId, side, bookKey, ownerUserId, order }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_trade_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_trade_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades/{tradeId}/cancel | Cancel a working joint order (trade or full rights); what filled stays filled. Returns `{ tradeId, order }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_unit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_unit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/units | Put units you own into the joint vault (each stored and free). They keep you as owner of record and stay on the shelf. Returns `{ added, vaultItemIds }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_joint_unit_remove**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_joint_unit_remove) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/units/remove | Take units out of the joint vault, back to their owner own account (yours at any rights; anyone else with full rights). Returns `{ removed, vaultItemIds }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_large_trader_report_file**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_large_trader_report_file) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/large-trader-reports/{id}/file | File (or amend, or refile) a large-trader report: why you hold it (purpose), who you act for, other accounts you control, whether you plan to add, and a statement of 20+ characters. Returns the report.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_learn_quiz**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_learn_quiz) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/learn/{concept}/quiz | Answer a concept's quiz (`answers`: the chosen choice index per question). Graded on the server; 5 tries an hour.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_live_stream_featured_lot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_live_stream_featured_lot) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/live-streams/{id}/featured-lot | Feature an open lot on your live show (or `lotId: null` to take it off). Viewers hear LIVE_PIN_CHANGED. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_loan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_loan) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/loans | Borrow against stored vault units: they are pledged (no listing, lending or withdrawal) and the market pool lends `amountCents` into your custodian account. Needs a verified ID.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_loan_recall**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_loan_recall) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/recall | Recall a lent unit: the short has 3 days to return one. Returns `{ recallDueAt }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_loan_unit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_loan_unit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units | Pledge more stored units to a loan (answers a loan call, or makes room).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_loan_unit_release**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_loan_unit_release) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/release | Free one pledged unit, if what is owed still fits inside the other units' limit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_loan_unit_sell**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_loan_unit_sell) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/sell | Sell a pledged unit to repay its loan: it is listed at `priceCents` (at least the sale floor) and stays pledged until it sells; the proceeds repay the loan first and the rest is yours. Calling it again re-prices it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_locate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_locate) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/locates | Hold up to `qty` lendable units of `bookKey` for 15 minutes so a short can open against them (`termDays` 30/90 for a term loan). Needs lending (VAULT_LENDING_TERMS_VERSION) and the ID check. Returns the locate with how many units it holds.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_long**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_long) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/longs | Buy `units` units (default 1; at most 25/10/3/1 by liquidity tier A/B/C/D) into your vault with leverage (up to 2x) from the market staking pool, paying up to `maxCents` each. A partial fill opens with the units bought and the rest is cancelled. Returns `{ positionId, matched, units }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_midpoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_midpoint) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint | A hidden block order that trades only at the firm-book midpoint, with another midpoint order of at least your minimum size.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_midpoint_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_midpoint_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint/{id}/cancel | Cancel a midpoint order.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_mm_dmm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_mm_dmm) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/mm/dmm | Apply to be the designated market maker on a tier B or C key. Ops approves; one per key.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_mm_leave**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_mm_leave) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/mm/{id}/leave | Leave a market-maker programme.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_mm_lp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_mm_lp) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/mm/lp | Join a key as a Registered Liquidity Provider (ID needed). Meeting coverage earns the LP rates.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_mm_mass_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_mm_mass_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mass-cancel | Cancel every open order (one key, or only quotes) and every resting leg in one statement: nothing fills between the first cancel and the last.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_mm_mmp_reset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_mm_mmp_reset) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp/reset | End a market-maker protection freeze early.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_opening_waitlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_opening_waitlist) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug}/waitlist | Join a key's waitlist, optionally with a friend's invite code (`ref`). Inviting moves you up the queue; nobody is paid. Returns your place and your own code.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos | Place a strategy: 2 to 4 legs on one underlying with ratios, a quantity (units, three decimals) and ONE signed net limit per unit (+ the most you pay, - the least credit you take). Margined on the combined scenario loss of all its legs; matched against the combo book, then the legs' own books; every leg fills at once or none does. Crossly venue only (409 combos_self_venue_only on the partner venue).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working strategy.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq | Ask makers for a firm net price on a whole strategy (legs, quantity, an optional limit that is never shown). Crossly venue only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/{id}/accept | Take a live, firm answer: both strategies go through the normal path (margin, pre-trade risk), every leg at once.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/{id}/cancel | Cancel your open strategy quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_respond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_respond) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/{id}/respond | Answer a strategy quote request with a firm net price per unit in the canonical terms, good for 60 seconds; a new answer replaces your last.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_response_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_response_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/responses/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your live answer to a strategy quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_covered_call**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_covered_call) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/covered-calls | Write a covered call: lock a stored vault unit (`vaultItemId`) as collateral and sell up to one call on its own key (`contractId`, `quantity` <= 1) at a price or an implied vol. The unit cannot be sold, moved, lent or withdrawn until the call is bought back or expires; if assigned you pay cash, or the unit sells at the vault auction to cover.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_covered_call_release**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_covered_call_release) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/covered-calls/{id}/release | Free a covered call's vault unit once the call it covers is closed (or covered by other units).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_mm_heartbeat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_mm_heartbeat) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/mm/heartbeat | Keep your option quotes alive (cancel-on-disconnect pulls them after 30 s without a heartbeat).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_mm_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_mm_quote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/mm/quotes | Set (replace) a two-sided quote on an option contract: `bid` and / or `ask` each { priceCents, quantity in contracts }, posted as post-only GTC orders on the Crossly venue. Market-maker protection counts their fills; a quote inside 20% of its mid earns liquidity rewards (CBX).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_mm_quote_pull**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_mm_quote_pull) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/mm/quotes/pull | Pull your option quotes on one contract (`contractId`) or all of them.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/orders | Place an options order: `contractId`, `side`, `quantity` in contracts (three decimals), a `priceCents` or an `iv` (`quoteMode`), `tif` gtc / gtd (with `expiresAt`) / ioc, `postOnly`. Idempotent on `clientOrderId`. On the Crossly venue it matches price-time with self-trade prevention after a scenario-margin check; on the partner venue it routes to the partner (accept its `termsVersion`). `subaccountId` trades for a firm sub-account you may trade.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working options order (on the partner venue it is cancelled there first). A liquidation order cannot be cancelled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_practice_combo**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_practice_combo) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/combos | A practice strategy (play money): the same ticket as a real one, filled at the practice maker's quotes when the net is inside the limit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_practice_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_practice_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/orders | Place a practice options order (the same ticket as a real one; fills against the practice maker at the reference vol). Play money only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_practice_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_practice_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working practice options order.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_practice_reset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_practice_reset) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/reset | Start the options practice account again with $10,000: positions and working orders are cleared.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_quote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/quote | Price a ticket without placing it: give a price per contract or an implied vol (`quoteMode`), get the other back, the premium and fee, the Greeks for your size, the payoff at expiry and the margin it would need on your account (authoritative on the Crossly venue, an estimate when the partner clears). A bad entry is reported, not thrown.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_rfq) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq | Ask for a quote on an option contract: `side`, `quantity` in contracts, an optional `limitCents` (never shown). Makers quoting the series, the underlying's DMM and LPs and approved dealers are told anonymously; answers are firm for 60 s.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_rfq_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_rfq_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/{id}/accept | Take a live answer (`responseId`) to your option quote request: it trades through the options book at that price or better, with margin and self-trade prevention.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_rfq_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_rfq_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/{id}/cancel | Cancel your open option quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_rfq_respond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_rfq_respond) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/{id}/respond | Answer an option quote request with a firm price per contract for its full size (good for 60 s; a new answer replaces your last).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_rfq_response_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_rfq_response_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/responses/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your live answer to an option quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_roll**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_roll) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/roll | Roll a position to another contract as one strategy order at one net price (+ you pay, - you receive): the close and the open fill together or not at all. `practice` rolls a practice position.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_option_strategy_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_option_strategy_preview) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/options/strategies/preview | Preview a strategy before placing it: by template (underlying, kind, direction, strikes, expiries) or by legs. Returns each leg at its bid / ask, the net per unit (+ debit, - credit), the payoff at the first expiry, max profit and max loss (null = unbounded), breakevens, net Greeks, and the scenario margin of the whole strategy next to its legs margined one by one.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_call**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_call) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/call | Lender: call the loan (once it is a day old). A refinancing auction runs for about 30 hours, the rate a new lender gets climbing to the ceiling; repaid or refinanced, you are paid in full; neither by the end, the unit moves to your vault.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_cancel_call**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_cancel_call) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/cancel-call | Lender: take back your call before any other lender has taken the loan over. The refinancing auction stops and the loan carries on as before on its terms.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refi_auction**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refi_auction) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refi-auction | Borrower: look for a cheaper lender. A refinancing auction runs for `hours` (1-72, default 24); the rate a new lender gets climbs from `startAprBps` (default half the loan rate) to just under the loan rate now. The first lender to take it pays your lender off and the loan carries on at that lower rate. Nobody by the end: nothing changes.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refi_auction_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refi_auction_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refi-auction/cancel | Borrower: stop looking for a cheaper lender before anyone has taken the loan over. The loan carries on unchanged.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refinance**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_refinance) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/refinance | Take over a loan in a refinancing auction (a lender call or a borrower search for a cheaper lender): you pay the old lender everything owed now (refused above `maxPayoffCents`), which becomes your loan to the same borrower at the auction rate now. Needs a verified ID, an active account and the current lending terms. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_loan_repay**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_loan_repay) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id}/repay | Borrower: repay from your custodian account to the lender. Without `amountCents`, in full (principal plus interest to the second; the unit is free once the custodian has it). With `amountCents` below what is owed, a part payment: interest first, then principal, at least the interest owed now and at least $1; the loan stays open on the principal left. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_offer**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_offer) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers | Offer to lend: `principalCents` per loan at `aprBps` a year against an item (`bookKey`) or one unit (`vaultItemId`), for up to `maxLoans` loans, lasting `expiresInDays` (default 90). Nothing is held: the custodian pays from your own account when a borrower takes it. Needs a verified ID, an active account and the current lending terms.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_offer_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_offer_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/{id}/cancel | Withdraw one of your open offers. Loans already taken from it carry on.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_p2p_offer_take**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_p2p_offer_take) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/{id}/take | Borrow: take an offer against one of your stored vault units (`vaultItemId`). The unit is pledged (no listing, lending or withdrawal) and the principal is paid into your custodian account. Interest accrues by the second; repay any time. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_paper_mission_mark_check**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_paper_mission_mark_check) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/paper/missions/mark-check | Answer the \"Read the mark\" mission: the quality letter (A, B or C) you read on a market page, checked against the stored mark. One answer per market, 10 a day. Returns `{ bookKey, answer, correct, quality, mission, next }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_paper_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_paper_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders | Place a paper order (play money only). It takes visible real liquidity now at book prices with the taker fee simulated; a GTC limit rests the rest with a queue-position estimate, filled later as the real tape reaches it. Sells only paper units you hold (no paper shorts). Never touches the real book. Returns `{ order, fills }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_paper_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_paper_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working paper order: the play cash or paper units it held come back. Returns `{ order }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_paper_reset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_paper_reset) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/paper/reset | Reset your paper account to $10,000 of play money: working paper orders are cancelled, paper positions cleared, and your leaderboard week starts again. Once a day.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_partner_apply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_partner_apply) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/partners/apply | Apply as a Vault Drop-off Partner (a card shop that takes walk-ins for the vault). Needs the one-time ID check; ops approve.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_partner_batch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_partner_batch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/partners/batches | An approved partner sends walk-ins in under its code: each item (catalogue variant, condition, grade, photos) becomes an inbound vault unit; the box gets one prepaid label. The partner earns a capped share of Crossly's fee on each unit's first sale.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pool_stake**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pool_stake) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes | Stake CBX you hold at the custodian into the market pool (base units, as a string). Returns `{ stakeId, shares }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pool_stake_unstake**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pool_stake_unstake) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes/{id}/unstake | Unstake: paid out after the 7-day cooldown. Returns `{ cooldownUntil }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_enroll**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_enroll) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/enroll | Admin: put a firm sub-account under portfolio margin. Needs VAULT_LEVERAGE_VENUE, your ID, an ECP-attested entity that passed KYB, and the minimum equity. Its positions are then margined on the whole account, recalculated every 5 minutes; calls and auctions are unchanged. Audited.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_leave**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_leave) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/leave | Admin: take a firm sub-account off portfolio margin. Each position is then held to its own tier floors again and may be called at once. Audited.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_what_if**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_portfolio_margin_what_if) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/what-if | The portfolio margin requirement of a firm sub-account before and after a trade (`bookKey`, `side` buy or sell, `units`), at the Crossly mark with fees left out. Changes nothing.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_position_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_position_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/cancel | Cancel the working order: an unfilled opening order cancels the position; an unfilled cover or close leaves it open. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_position_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_position_close) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/close | Close a leveraged long: all its units, or `units` of them, each sold for at least `minCents`; each sale repays the pool first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_position_collateral**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_position_collateral) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/collateral | Answer a margin call: a short posts more collateral (USD, or CBX at half value); a leveraged long pays down its pool loan. Returns the position.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_position_cover**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_position_cover) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/cover | Cover a short: all its units, or `units` of them, each buying any unit of the key into the vault for up to `maxCents`; each goes back to its lender.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_account**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_account) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/account | Open your account at the live prediction venue (the partner DCM, or your own segregated account at the settlement bank). Returns `{ venue, status, opened }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_alert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_alert) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/alerts | Tell me when a market's probability crosses a level: `{ marketId, levelPercent, direction }`. Returns the alert.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_card) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/cards | Make an \"I called it\" share card for a position of yours that settled in your favour (`showAmounts` to include what you paid and won). Returns the card with its public URL.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs | Ask makers for a price on a combo: 2 to 6 legs (a market and a side each) on different underlyings, a size, and an optional limit makers never see. Pays $1 a contract if every leg comes true. `venue` paper or live.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs/{id}/accept | Take one live, firm answer to your combo request (`quoteId`): both stakes are locked and the combo opens. Returns the combo.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs/{id}/cancel | Withdraw your combo request; its answers are withdrawn too.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq_quote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs/{id}/quotes | Answer a combo request with a firm price a contract for the full size (good for 30 seconds; a new answer replaces yours). Real money needs an active prediction maker programme or approved dealer status.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_comment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_comment) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/comments | Comment on a market or a series, or reply to a comment (`parentId`). Moderated like every comment on Crossly.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_comment_report**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_comment_report) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/comments/{id}/report | Report a comment with a reason; enough reports hide it until ops look.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_conversion**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_conversion) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/conversions | Split ($1 a set buys one Yes in every bucket of an expiry), merge (one Yes in every bucket gives back $1 a set before settlement) or convert (No in one bucket becomes Yes in every other) on a full bucket set. `venue` paper or live. Returns the conversion and the event after it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_forecast**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_forecast) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecasts | Forecast a market: `probabilityPercent` 0 to 100. The one standing when trading closes is scored (Brier).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_follow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_follow) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecasters/{id}/follow | Follow a public forecaster: you are told when they open a position or forecast (copy-watch only; nothing is ever traded for you).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_maker**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_maker) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/makers | Apply to make markets in prediction ladders (one series, or all with `seriesId` null). Needs the ID check; ops approve. Returns the application.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_maker_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_maker_quote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/makers/quotes | Mass quote: replace your resting maker quotes on up to 50 markets with one Yes bid and one Yes ask each (post-only; an ask rests as a No bid at 100 minus it). Live quotes need an active maker programme. Returns what rests.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders | Place a prediction order: buy_yes, sell_yes, buy_no or sell_no; limit (priceCents 1-99 in the action's own terms) or market (IOC 10 cents through the best price); GTC, GTD (expiresAt) or IOC; post-only. Sells only what you hold. `venue` paper (practice) or live (503 until a venue is registered). Returns the order with its fills.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order_amend**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order_amend) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders/{id}/amend | Amend a resting order's price (in its own terms) and/or whole size. It keeps its place in the queue only when the size goes down at the same price; otherwise it is re-queued and may trade at once. Returns the order with `keptPriority`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order_batch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order_batch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders/batch | Place up to 20 orders in one call; each answers on its own (`results[]`), one refused never stops the rest.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order_batch_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order_batch_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders/batch-cancel | Cancel up to 20 orders in one call; each answers on its own.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel a working prediction order; the money (or practice money) it locked comes back. Returns the order.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_order_cancel_all**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_order_cancel_all) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders/cancel-all | Cancel every working prediction order you have, or only those on one market (`marketId`). Returns `{ cancelled }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_paper_reset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_paper_reset) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/paper/reset | Reset your practice prediction account to $1,000 of play money: working practice orders cancelled, practice positions cleared. Once a day.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_position_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_position_close) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/positions/close | Close a position: your resting sells on the market are cancelled and everything you hold is sold now (market, 10-cent band) or at `priceCents`. Returns the sell order.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_position_close_all**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_position_close_all) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/positions/close-all | Sell all: close every open position on a venue, or only one event's (`eventId`). Each answers on its own.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_proposal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_proposal) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/proposals | Propose a market: an index slug or a book key, a shape (close at or above / at or below, between, touch), its strike(s) and an expiry. Ops review it; once listed you earn the creator share of Crossly's take on its fees.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_proposal_check**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_proposal_check) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/proposals/check | Check a market proposal without sending it: whether the underlying may carry markets (a built-in index or a tier A key), and what is wrong with the strike or expiry.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_proposal_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_proposal_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/proposals/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw a proposal still waiting for review.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_settlement_dispute**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_settlement_dispute) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/settlements/{id}/dispute | Dispute a settlement inside its 24-hour review window (you must hold a position in the market). Payout waits for the venue's decision. Returns `{ settlementId, status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_prediction_term**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_prediction_term) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/terms | Accept the current prediction-market contract terms (PREDICTION_TERMS_VERSION), needed for every real-money order. Returns `{ termsVersion, venue }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_price_widget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_price_widget) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets | Make a price widget for one vault market (`kind` mark + `bookKey`) or one public index (`kind` index + `indexSlug`): range, layout (compact or chart), theme (auto, light, dark), optionally your own affiliate code for the link back and the sites allowed to show it (empty = any). Returns the widget with its snippets.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_price_widget_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_price_widget_preview) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/preview | What a widget would show before you save it: `view` (value, move, quality, n, as-of, link back) and `html`, the exact page the iframe serves. Nothing is stored.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_bracket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_bracket) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/brackets | A buy order with a take-profit and/or stop-loss attached; the exits arm as an OCO pair for what the entry fills.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_link_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_link_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/links/{id}/cancel | Cancel a linked order: its open child orders come off; a bracket's entry stays.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_oco**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_oco) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/oco | Two orders on one key, one cancels the other: when one fills or its stop triggers, the other is cancelled (a partial fill shrinks it).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders | Place an order on a vault book: limit, market (protected, IOC), stop or stop-limit; GTC, GTD, IOC or FOK; post-only; an iceberg `displayQuantity`; a TWAP or VWAP algo over a window. Idempotent on `clientOrderId`. `subaccountId` trades for a firm sub-account you may trade (your role, its KYB, its limits, no firm halt; buys pay from its custodian account and land in it).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel an order; what filled stays filled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_order_cancel_all**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_order_cancel_all) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/cancel-all | Cancel every open order, or every one on a book key. Returns `{ cancelled }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_order_replace**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_order_replace) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id}/replace | Amend an order's price, stop or quantity (cancel-replace: it re-queues at its new price).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_quote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Market makers: set a two-sided post-only quote on a book key (replaces the last). With cancel-on-disconnect (default) heartbeat every 30 s or it is pulled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_quote_heartbeat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_quote_heartbeat) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes/heartbeat | Keep your quotes alive (cancel-on-disconnect). Returns `{ quotes, at }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_pro_trailing_stop**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_pro_trailing_stop) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/pro/trailing-stops | A trailing stop: its stop follows the best price since it was placed, `trailBps` or `trailCents` behind (under the high for a sell, over the low for a buy).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_profile_follow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_profile_follow) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle}/follow | Follow someone with a public vault: their new vault buys appear in your feed. No copy trading.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_recurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_recurring) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/recurring | Start a recurring buy: one whole unit of a key every week, two weeks or month, at no more than `maxPriceCents` and/or the mark plus `capBpsOverMark`; card (with an optional backup) or custodian; optional auto-lend.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_rfq) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/rfq | Ask for a quote on a block: dealers, the key's DMM and LPs, and holders are told, anonymously. Answers are firm for 60 s. Your limit is never shown.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_rfq_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_rfq_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/accept | Take one answer: it fills as a block through the normal fill path (fees by role, self-trade prevention, halts, LULD).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_rfq_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_rfq_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/cancel | Cancel your open quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_rfq_respond**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_rfq_respond) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id}/respond | Answer a quote request for the full quantity with a firm price, good for 60 s. Selling needs the units free in your vault; buying pays from your Vault Markets custodian account.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_rfq_response_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_rfq_response_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/responses/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your live answer to a quote request.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_set_goal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_set_goal) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals | Start a goal on a catalogue set (category, brandSlug, modelSlug from the set search), optionally with a target: one condition, and any grade, ungraded, or one exact grade. One live goal per set.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_set_goal_basket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_set_goal_basket) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket | Place the \"complete my set\" basket bid: one GTC limit buy per missing piece, under a hard budget, paid by one of your saved cards or your custodian account. Every leg passes pre-trade risk and the ID line like any order. Idempotent on clientBasketId. One working basket per goal; its legs are cancelled once the set is complete.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_set_goal_basket_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_set_goal_basket_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket/cancel | Cancel the goal working basket bid: every working leg comes off the book; what already filled stays in your vault.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_auto_invest**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_auto_invest) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/auto-invest | Start auto-invest: `amountCents` ($5-$10,000) `every` week or month into `seriesIds` (equal weights) or `targets` (seriesId + weightBps summing to 10,000) or a `basketId` (with `rebalance` to keep your own shares on its weights). Whole shares only; the rest rolls over. Offerings are subscribed at their price; trading books are bought at the best ask up to `maxPremiumBps` over the indicative value. `practice` runs it on paper.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_auto_invest_run**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_auto_invest_run) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/auto-invest/{id}/run | Run a plan now (once a minute at most); the schedule does not move.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_auto_invest_update**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_auto_invest_update) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/auto-invest/{id}/update | Change a plan (amount, cadence, series or weights, cap, rebalance) or set its `status` active, paused or cancelled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_basket_buy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_basket_buy) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/baskets/{id}/buy | Buy a basket once: `amountCents` split by its live weights into whole shares of each series, in your own name (offerings subscribed, trading books at the best ask up to `maxPremiumBps`). `practice` buys on paper.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_buyout_fund**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_buyout_fund) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/buyouts/{id}/fund | Your whole-unit bid reached the reserve: fund the price through your investor account within 72 hours to buy the item from its holders (no vote).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_buyout_vote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_buyout_vote) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/buyouts/{id}/vote | Vote `yes` or `no` on a buyout with the shares you held when the vote opened. You can change your vote while it is open.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_buyout_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_buyout_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/buyouts/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your buyout bid before its vote opens; the escrow is refunded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_card) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards | Make a verified share card of a position, a vault unit, a public index or your portfolio (`kind`, `refId`). The % on it is the price move since your real fill; amounts stay hidden unless `showAmounts`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_gift_accept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_gift_accept) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/gifts/{id}/accept | Accept a share gift. Needs your own verified Vault Shares investor account; the venue then moves the shares to you on the register.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_gift_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_gift_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/gifts/{id}/cancel | Cancel a gift you offered, before it is accepted: the shares are free again.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_gift_decline**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_gift_decline) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/gifts/{id}/decline | Decline a share gift offered to you.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_investor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_investor) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/investor | Open or update your investor account at the venue: accept the current investor terms (`acceptTerms`), and state accredited status or your annual income and net worth for the Reg A+ limit. Needs a verified ID.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel one of your open share-book orders.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_paper_order_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_paper_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper/orders/{id}/cancel | Cancel an open practice order.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_paper_reset**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_paper_reset) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper/reset | Start the practice account over with fresh play money (once a day).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_paper_sery_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_paper_sery_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper/series/{id}/orders | Place a practice order (play money) with the same rules as the real share book.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_paper_sery_subscribe**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_paper_sery_subscribe) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper/series/{id}/subscribe | Subscribe with play money to a practice series’ offering.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_room_hide**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_room_hide) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/room/{id}/hide | Take down one of your own holder-room posts (`reason`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series | Propose a Vault Shares series for one of your units (`vaultItemId`), keeping `retainedShares` (0-50%), with an optional minimum raise, offering length (7-60 days) and reserve. Ops then qualify it, attach the offering circular and open the offering. With no venue it is a practice series. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_buyout**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_buyout) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/buyouts | Bid for the whole item (`offerCents`, at least the mark x 1.10). The bid is escrowed at the venue, then holders vote by shares: two-thirds yes accepts, a majority against rejects, 14 days with no majority against accepts. Accepted, the item is yours and the price is paid out pro rata after storage and insurance.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_gift**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_gift) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/gifts | Give whole `shares` of a series to someone (`to`: their @handle, email or user id), with an optional `message`. The shares are set aside until they accept (14 days). On acceptance the broker-dealer or transfer agent verifies them and moves the shares on the register: no money moves, and your cost basis and holding date carry over.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_order) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/orders | Place an order on a series’ share book: `side`, `type` limit or market (immediate-or-cancel inside a 10% band), `tif` gtc / gtd (`expiresAt`) / ioc, whole `shares` or a buy’s `amountCents` (turned into whole shares at the most it could pay), integer `limitCents` a share, `postOnly`. Fees: maker 0, taker 100 bps plus the broker-dealer pass-through. Self-trades are prevented. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_recombine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_recombine) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/recombine | You hold every share: take the item back whole. With `withdraw`, it ships home at once (any receipt token is burned as it leaves). Storage and insurance the series owed stay with the unit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_room**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_room) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/room | Post in the holder room (shareholders only), up to 1,000 characters.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_subscribe**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_subscribe) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/subscribe | Subscribe for whole `shares` in an open offering at its fixed price. The money goes into the venue’s escrow in your own name and is refunded if the offering misses its minimum. Reg A+ limits apply. A joint vault member with trade rights may pass `jointVaultId`. Pass `requestId` to make a retry safe.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_unwatch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_unwatch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/unwatch | Stop being told about a drop (`kind` notify) or leave its waitlist (`kind` waitlist).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_update**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_update) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/updates | Post an update on your own item’s series (you are its originator): `kind` story, provenance, condition, appraisal, exhibition or event, a `title`, a `body`, an optional https `mediaUrl`, `holdersOnly`. Holders are told.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_watch) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/watch | Ask to be told about a drop (`kind` notify: when your early access opens and when it opens to everyone) or join the waitlist of a sold-out first-come offering (`kind` waitlist, with the `shares` you want).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_sery_withdraw_proposal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_sery_withdraw_proposal) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/withdraw-proposal | Withdraw your own proposal before its offering opens.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_subscription_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_subscription_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/subscriptions/{id}/cancel | Cancel an escrowed subscription while its offering is still open: the escrow is refunded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_viewing_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_viewing_book) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/viewings/{id}/book | Book a place on a viewing day (shareholders only), with up to the day’s `guests`. Bookings close a day before; booking again changes your guests.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_share_viewing_booking_cancel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_share_viewing_booking_cancel) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shares/viewing-bookings/{id}/cancel | Cancel your viewing-day booking.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_short**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_short) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/shorts | Short a book key: borrow `units` lent units (default 1; at most 25/10/3/1 by liquidity tier A/B/C/D) and sell each at `limitCents` or better, posting collateral for all of them (50% of the mark each; CBX counts at half, up to 30%). All units are borrowed or none unless `allowPartial`. A partial sale opens with the units sold. Returns `{ positionId, matched, units }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_supply_batch_label**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_supply_batch_label) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/supply/batches/{id}/label | Buy a box's prepaid label again after it failed (for example once a ship-from address is saved). A box that has a label keeps it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_supply_invite**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_supply_invite) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/supply/invites/{id} | Accept or decline a Market Open invite (`accept`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_supply_send**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_supply_send) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/supply/send | Send inventory items matched to a catalogue variant to the vault as one box: each becomes an inbound vault unit, and the box gets one prepaid inbound label (a PDF to print). Optional: auto-list each unit in the vault book at a share of the mark once stored, and lend when idle. Items that cannot go say why. Crosslisted listings stay live.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_supply_send_in**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_supply_send_in) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/supply/send-in | Send inventory items to the vault: `method: label` (one box on a prepaid label) or `dropoff` at a partner (`partnerId`, from the finder; you get a counter code). Each item can carry its declared value; `insured` insures the box for it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_term**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_term) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/terms | Accept the current lending terms (needed to lend or short). Returns `{ termsVersion }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_claim_object**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_claim_object) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/claims/{id}/object | Stop a transfer-on-death claim made on your own account. Returns `{ id, status, estateUnlocked }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_my_claim**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_my_claim) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims | Start a claim as a named beneficiary (ID first), naming the account holder by @username or email. It stays a draft until you add documents and submit. Returns `{ id, status, existing }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_document**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_document) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/documents | Add one document to a draft claim (or one ops asked more of): kind, filename and the file as base64 (PDF, JPEG or PNG, up to 8 MB). Kept privately for ops review. Returns `{ id, kind, label, filename, bytes }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_submit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_submit) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/submit | Send a claim for ops review (it needs a death certificate). The account holder is told and can stop it. Returns `{ id, status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_withdraw**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_my_claim_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw a claim before it is released. Returns `{ id, status }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_tod_term**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_tod_term) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/tod/terms | Accept the current transfer-on-death terms. Returns `{ termsVersion }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_unit_evidence_scan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_unit_evidence_scan) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence/scans | Add a finished scan of your own (`captureId`, of the same catalogue item) to your vault unit. Shown as added by the owner, not as intake evidence.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_unit_sell_now**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_unit_sell_now) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/sell-now | Sell one unit of this item now into the best bid (Instant Exit: an immediate-or-cancel pro order), never below `minCents`. Returns `{ status: filled | missed, filledCents, quote, order }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_valuation_statement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_valuation_statement) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements | Issue an insurance or appraisal statement for a day: frozen with the sha256 of its record, sealed in the Crossly audit chain, with a public verify link. preparedFor and policyRef are printed on it and never shown on verify. Moves no money.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_watchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_watchlist) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Watch a book key. Returns `{ bookKey, watching }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_market_widget_token**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_market_widget_token) | **POST** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Mint a widget token for one phone (`deviceName`, `installId`); minting again with the same pair replaces that phone token. The token reads the widget summary and nothing else, and is returned once. At most five phones.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_receipt_token_confirm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_receipt_token_confirm) | **POST** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/confirm | After your wallet sent the export: read the chain and mark the token live once its one token exists. Pass the `signature` your wallet returned to record it (kept only if it landed).
