*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_receipt_token_export**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_receipt_token_export) | **POST** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/export | Send one unit (`vaultItemId`) warehouse receipt to one of your verified Solana wallets (`wallet`) as a token, accepting the current terms (`acceptTerms`). Returns a base64 transaction already signed by the new mint and the receipts authority: your wallet pays the rent and must sign and send it within about two minutes, then call confirm. Nothing is minted until it does. Needs a verified ID; the unit must be stored, unpledged, not lent and not for sale.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_receipt_token_redeem**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_receipt_token_redeem) | **POST** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/redeem | Redeem a receipt token held in one of your verified Solana wallets (`wallet`): returns an unsigned base64 burn your wallet signs and sends (it also closes the token account and returns its rent to you). Once it lands, call redeem/confirm and the unit is yours in the vault. Refused while the token is frozen.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_receipt_token_redeem_confirm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_receipt_token_redeem_confirm) | **POST** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id}/redeem/confirm | After your wallet sent the redeem: read the chain; once the token is burned the unit moves into your vault (if it was not yours already). Pass the `signature` to record it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**create_vault_receipt_token_wallet_allow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#create_vault_receipt_token_wallet_allow) | **POST** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/wallets/allow | Put one of your verified Solana wallets (`wallet`) on the receipt-token allow-list so it can hold and receive receipt tokens. Returns a base64 transaction already signed by the receipts authority: the wallet pays the small rent (about 0.001 SOL) and must sign and send it within about two minutes. Needs a verified ID and a wallet that passes the sanctions screen.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_agent_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_agent_watch) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/agent/watches/{id} | Cancel a live price watch. Returns the watch.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_alert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_alert) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/alerts/{id} | Delete a price alert. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_auto_buy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_auto_buy) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/auto-buy | Stop Auto-buy with payouts. The history stays.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_bullion_family_peg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_bullion_family_peg) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs/{id} | Stop a family-wide quote: every leg order comes off the book; what filled stays filled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_bullion_peg**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_bullion_peg) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs/{id} | Stop a spot-pegged quote: its resting order comes off the book; what filled stays filled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_dealer_band**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_dealer_band) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Stop a band on a key; its standing bid comes off.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_entity_member**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_entity_member) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members/{memberId} | Admin: remove a member (or leave, yourself). An entity always keeps one admin.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_follow_copy_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_follow_copy_watch) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/{handle} | Stop copy-watching a collector.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_index**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_index) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | Delete an index of your own. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_locate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_locate) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/locates/{id} | Release a live locate before it lapses.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_opening_waitlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_opening_waitlist) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug}/waitlist | Leave a key's waitlist.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_prediction_alert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_prediction_alert) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/alerts/{id} | Remove a probability alert. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_prediction_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_prediction_card) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/cards/{id} | Take an \"I called it\" card down; its link stops working. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_prediction_comment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_prediction_comment) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/comments/{id} | Delete your comment.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_follow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_follow) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecasters/{id}/follow | Stop following a forecaster.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_price_widget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_price_widget) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | Delete a widget. Pages that still paste it show that it is no longer available. Returns `{ id, archived }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_pro_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_pro_quote) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Pull your quote on a book key, or every quote. Returns `{ pulled }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_profile_follow**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_profile_follow) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle}/follow | Stop following someone.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_recurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_recurring) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id} | Stop a recurring buy for good; its history stays.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_set_goal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_set_goal) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | Remove a set goal. A working basket bid is cancelled first; what filled stays in your vault.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_share_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_share_card) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | Take a share card down: its link and image stop working. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_supply_round_up**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_supply_round_up) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Stop the round-up (its history stays; an armed buy is cancelled).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_unit_evidence**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_unit_evidence) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence/{evidenceId} | Take back a scan you added to your vault unit (intake evidence stays).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_watchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_watchlist) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Stop watching a book key (`?bookKey=`). Returns `{ bookKey, watching }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_widget_token**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_widget_token) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Turn the widget off on every phone: revokes all your widget tokens. Returns `{ revoked }`, how many.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**delete_vault_market_widget_token_by_id**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#delete_vault_market_widget_token_by_id) | **DELETE** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens/{id} | Turn the widget off on one phone: revokes that widget token at once. Only a widget token can be revoked here.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_attestation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_attestation) | **GET** /v1/vault/attestations | Vault attestations: independent physical counts, the insurance certificate (with a warning when insured value in the vault exceeds its aggregate or per-occurrence limit), SOC 1 Type II and SOC 2 reports. Each carries its signed document.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_account**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_account) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/account | Which Vault Markets registrations are live (and the env each waits on), your custodian account, limits and balances.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_adl**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_adl) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/adl | The insurance fund (booked and free to draw), the most one event may draw before auto-deleveraging (ADL) starts, the loss waterfall in order, and the latest ADL events (no user or position ids). Money in cents.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_adl_position**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_adl_position) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/adl/positions/{id} | One of your positions with its ADL lights (0 once it has ended).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_advance_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_advance_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/advances/quote | What an advance on a vault unit for sale could be: up to half of the lower of its mark and asking price, free for 60 days.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_agent_draft**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_agent_draft) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/agent/drafts/{id} | One drafted order: item, side, units, limit (cents), the book when drafted, status, and the orderBody that places it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_alert_pref**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_alert_pref) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/alert-prefs | Your vault alert settings: every kind (price, fill, margin_call, adl, p2p_loan, auction, market_open, receipt_token, set_goal, follow, account), every channel (push, telegram, terminal, email, webhook) with whether it is set up, the full on/off grid, the locked switches (a critical kind always pushes) and quiet hours.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_auction_lot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_auction_lot) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/lots/{id} | One lot: the price showing, the next minimum bid, whether a reserve exists and is met, the end (extended by late bids), the bid history and your standing.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_auction_mine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_auction_mine) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/auctions/mine | Lots you consigned (with your reserve) and lots you bid on (with your maximum and whether you lead).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_auto_buy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_auto_buy) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/auto-buy | Auto-buy with payouts: the share of each payout set aside as a vault buying budget, the budget, your want list with max prices, the purchase history and whether it is paused. `autoBuy` is null when it is not set up.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_block**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_block) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId} | One block order: its order, plan lines (planned and allocated units, average price, cash share, status).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_bullion_for**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_bullion_for) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/for | The bullion key families one book key belongs to, with spot for its metal as a labelled reference and its melt value at that spot. Empty families when the item is not keyed bullion.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_bullion_key**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_bullion_key) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/keys/{slug} | One bullion key family: its catalogue products, every live book key with best bid, best ask, vault units and its mark (from the book only), live pegged quote counts, and spot as a labelled reference.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_bullion_peg_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_bullion_peg_preview) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs/preview | The price a spot-pegged quote would rest at now (spot plus a premium in cents or basis points, clamped to floor and cap). No price when spot is stale or missing: such a quote waits off the book.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_call**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_call) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/calls/{bookKey} | Recent call-auction uncrosses on a book key: kind (open, reopen, periodic, daily, buy-in, sell-off), clearing price in cents and units.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_capacity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_capacity) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/capacity | Capacity from the book: `bookKey` (+ `units`) gives ADV, depth within ±5% of the mark, vault stock, lendable units and days to liquidate at 20% of ADV; `index` + `notional` (cents) gives how much of the basket the asks fill now and the days the rest needs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_channel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_channel) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/channels | Your Vault Markets channel switches: Telegram alerts and digest, the daily cap on vault nudges by push (default 5), whether a Telegram chat is linked, and how many nudges went out today.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_compare**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_compare) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/compare | Up to 5 book keys and/or index slugs (`items`, comma-separated) as daily series on the same days, each rebased to 100.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_confirmation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_confirmation) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations/{tradeId} | One trade confirmation.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_confirmation_pdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_confirmation_pdf) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations/{tradeId}/pdf | One trade confirmation as a PDF.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_correlation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_correlation) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/correlations | The correlation matrix of the sector indexes' weekly returns over `weeks` (default 52), pairwise-complete, with n per pair; a pair under 26 shared weeks has no number.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_creator_attribution**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_creator_attribution) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/creators/attribution | The creator who brought you to Vault Markets, when one did: when it started and ends, whether it still earns, and the disclosure. Your prices and fees are the same either way.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_creator_dashboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_creator_dashboard) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/creators/dashboard | For creators: whether you joined the programme, each of your codes with traders attributed and still earning (counts only), shares accrued, paid and voided (cents) and the latest shares.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_creator_programme**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_creator_programme) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/creators/programme | The Vault Markets creator programme: the share of the fee Crossly keeps that a creator earns, the per-fill and lifetime caps (cents), the attribution window in days, the 2% net floor it sits under, and the disclosure words.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_creator_room**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_creator_room) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/creators/room | For creators: your vault shelf room (applied, verified, rejected or paused) and whether your public vault profile is on, which the room needs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_credit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_credit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/credit/{id} | One loan or advance with its pledged units and its money log (draws, interest, repayments, sale proceeds).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dcm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dcm) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dcm | The partner exchange (DCM) that lists and clears futures and options on Crossly indexes (needs VAULT_INDEX_DCM + VAULT_INDEX_DCM_API_URL + VAULT_INDEX_DCM_API_KEY), and its contracts as last read: perps, futures and options with the index each settles on, multiplier, tick, expiry and strike. Read-only: Crossly is the licensor and data provider, never the venue.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dcm_contract**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dcm_contract) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/contracts/{symbol} | One partner contract: its spec, its funding prints (newest first; the partner computes and settles funding), annualised funding over the last 21 prints, and the Crossly index level it settles on.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dcm_contract_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dcm_contract_book) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/contracts/{symbol}/book | The partner exchange live order book for a contract (cached 5 seconds), with mark, index, last, open interest, 24 h volume, spread in bps and the basis of its mark over the Crossly index in bps. Prices are index points.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dcm_options_input**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dcm_options_input) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/options-inputs/{slug} | Options inputs for an index (indexes only, never a single item): Crossly realised volatility (raw and Geltner-desmoothed, from weekly returns), the forward (the partner future mark, else the Crossly close), Black-76 greeks on a strike x expiry grid and on every option the partner lists on the index. Model inputs, not quotes.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dcm_position**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dcm_position) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dcm/positions | The partner exchange account linked to your account (or to the firm sub-account `subaccountId`) and its positions, read-only (cached 15 seconds); index futures on category indexes count as portfolio-margin hedges for that account: contracts, side, entry and mark price, unrealised P&L and margin in cents, liquidation price and notional in cents. Not linked: `linked: false`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dealer**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dealer) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer | Your dealer status (applied, approved, revoked) and your Instant Exit bands.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_dealer_pool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_dealer_pool) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id} | One of your curve pools, with its ladder and live legs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_digest_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_digest_preview) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/digest/preview | This week's vault digest as it would go out: units at the display marks (unmarked units counted, not valued), the like-for-like change from a week ago, offers on your units, watched keys that got a first mark, and one Learn card.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_entity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_entity) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/entities | The entities (funds, LLCs, trusts, SPVs) you belong to, and invitations waiting for you.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_entity_by_id**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_entity_by_id) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id} | One entity: members and roles, sub-accounts with their limits, firm halts, caps.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_estimate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_estimate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/estimates/{bookKey} | The Crossly Estimate for a book key — a model, not a price. Method (own trades, index-adjusted last trade, grade ratio), cents with 68% and 95% bands, High/Medium/Low from the walk-forward backtest's FSD, and n. Hidden (`available: false`) under 50 backtested trades or when FSD > 0.30. Never used for margin, loans or settlement.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_execution_quality**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_execution_quality) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/execution-quality | The quarterly execution-quality report (Rule 605 style): fill rates, speed, effective vs quoted spread and shortfall by order type and size, and the busiest keys. `quarter` like 2026-Q4; the current quarter is provisional.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_fee_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_fee_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/fees/quote | Your fee for an order at a price: as maker (the order rests) and as taker (it fills at once), with the programme that sets your rate and any Crossly One trim. A buyer's fee is added to the price; a seller's comes out of the proceeds.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_fee_schedule**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_fee_schedule) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/fees/schedule | The fee schedule by role: maker and taker rates for retail, 30-day volume, Registered LPs and DMMs, the CBX discount, the referral share and the 2% net floor Crossly always keeps.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_follow_sharing**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_follow_sharing) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/sharing | Your sharing switches: the public vault profile, new buys, units sent in, sales, Hall loans, bids and asks for copy-watchers, and the leaderboards. All off until you turn them on.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_gem_rate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_gem_rate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/gem-rate/{bookKey} | The Crossly gem rate: of cards graded through Crossly, the share that came back at each grader's gem grade, for this item and its category, with a Wilson 95% band. 10 returns per grader or no number. Not a grader population report.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_grade_to_vault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_grade_to_vault) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/{id} | One Grade to Vault flow, brought up to date with its submission and unit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_grade_to_vault_candidate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_grade_to_vault_candidate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault/candidates | What you can start Grade to Vault on: raw inventory matched to a catalogue item, and raw vault units on the grading hub shelf; plus whether Grading Runs are open.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_grading_signal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_grading_signal) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grading-signal | For a raw book key: the marks of the same item's graded keys, the spread over raw, grading cost per grader, the margin tier grading lifts it to, your raw vault units it pays to grade, and where to act.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_hall_loan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_hall_loan) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/hall/loans | Your Hall of Grails desk: this week's exhibition, the rules (maxLive loans, shownPerHolder per exhibition, repPoints per exhibition shown), reputation (exhibitions finished), your loans (status active, ending or ended; onShowNow, showingUntil, exhibitions, the next exhibition it fits, the Lent to the Hall badge) and the vault units you could lend (canLend, or the blocker in words, and the next exhibition each fits). Display only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_health**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_health) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/health | Portfolio health of your vault holdings at Crossly marks: concentration by single item, category, grade and grader (shares, HHI on 0 to 10000, its band and effective number of holdings), value by liquidity tier, the stress grid (each category and everything down 10, 20 and 30 percent), value by mark quality, plain observations, and the held items a what-if can run on. Information only, not advice.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_health_what_if**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_health_what_if) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/health/what-if | What if one held item moved by moveBps (-10000 to 10000 basis points of its mark): the item, your vault total, the item and its category share of the vault, and the single-item HHI, before and after. A scenario on the mark, not a price you could sell at.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_holding_inventory_item**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_holding_inventory_item) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/holdings/{id}/inventory-item | The inventory item a vault unit of yours came from (null for a unit bought on the book).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_holding_withdraw_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_holding_withdraw_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/holdings/{id}/withdraw-quote | What shipping one unit you own home would charge now, for `standard` and `expedited`: shipping, the storage it owes and the early-withdrawal fee (within 90 days of arriving), and the total. Read-only; ship it home with POST /api/v1/buyer/vault/:id/withdraw.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_home_unit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_home_unit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/home/units | \"Show my vault\": the vault units you hold, the ones standing in your Crossly World home first. Each: vaultItemId, title, grade, imageUrl, bookKey (its market), inHome, visitors (visitors to your home see it). Also your home (slug, privacy, path), max units one home shows, shown, visitorsSee and a note on who sees what. Your home is made on first read. No prices.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | One index with its daily closes (oldest first) and its level now.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_calendar**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_calendar) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-calendar | The next four quarterly reviews: reference day (2nd Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec), announcement day, last close under the old membership and the effective day; June and December also review the Blue-chip 50.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_consultation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_consultation) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations/{id} | One consultation with its proposal, versions, dates, outcome and the public comments.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_feed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_feed) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug} | The licensed index feed for one built-in index (needs VAULT_INDEX_DCM + VAULT_INDEX_FEED_SECRET): the level now (intraday from A/B marks, or the last close below 50% constituent coverage), the last close, the frozen 7-day TWAP settlement value and every constituent with its mark source, quality and trade count. Signed: `x-crossly-signature: t=<ms>,v1=<hex HMAC-SHA256(secret, t + \".\" + body)>`. Every read is logged for the licence. Crossly is the licensor and data feed only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_feed_funding**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_feed_funding) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug}/funding | Reference funding for a perpetual-style future on the index: your premium `premiumBps` (TWAP of (impact mid - index)/index) through F = P + clamp(I - P, ±0.05%) per 8 h, |F| capped at 0.5%/day, with the index level it is relative to. The partner DCM computes and settles funding; this is the reference math.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_feed_settlement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_feed_settlement) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-feed/{slug}/settlement | The frozen 7-day TWAP settlement series for a licensed index (written once a day, never restated), with the closes behind each value. Signed like the feed.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_level**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_level) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/levels | An index's daily closes (up to 5 years), oldest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_licence_usage**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_licence_usage) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-licence/usage | Your licensed index feed reads over `days`, per index and endpoint (licence-usage report).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_index_methodology**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_index_methodology) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-methodology | The index method in machine-readable form: version (semver), inputs, weightings and caps, the family and its screens, the engines and their publication gate, revisions and the settlement series, the calendar, governance and limitations.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_instant_exit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_instant_exit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit | The Instant Exit quote for one vault unit you hold: the best resting bid on its book key (anyone's, dealers' included), its share of the mark, the bid depth, and whether it can be taken now.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_item_location**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_item_location) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/items/{inventoryItemId}/location | Where one inventory item's units are: in your own stock (warehouse) and in the vault per location, each vault unit's status (in transit, received, authenticated, graded, listed, shipped out), its Sell from first choice and whether it can be sent to the vault now (with the reason when not).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_joint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_joint) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/joint | Your joint vaults and the invitations waiting for you, with the gate (open, missing env), the joint terms in words and what each right allows. A JointVaultsHome object, not a collection.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_joint_by_id**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_joint_by_id) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id} | One joint vault (active members only): members and rights, the units in it at the mark with each owner of record, totals, recent joint trades, and the activity from the audit trail.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_key_stat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_key_stat) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/key-stats/{bookKey} | Everything derived about one book key in one read: the Crossly Estimate, the liquidity score, risk statistics and the gem rate. A part that cannot be read is `{ error }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_learn**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_learn) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/learn | The Learn hub: each concept with whether you passed its quiz. Passing earns cosmetic pins only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_learn_by_concept**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_learn_by_concept) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/learn/{concept} | One Learn concept: its sections and quiz questions (no answers).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_lending_election**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_lending_election) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/lending/election | Your lender election: `returnInKind` (shorts post 175% collateral, a 14-day buy-in, never cash-settled) and `allowTerm` (your units may go out on 30/90-day fixed-rate term loans, not recallable before term).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_lending_programme**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_lending_programme) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/lending/programme | Your lender programme report: lendable units, units out, utilisation, fee revenue (accrued / paid), term loans and return-in-kind loans in force, and each loan's rate against the key's rate today.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_lending_statement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_lending_statement) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/lending/statement | Your daily collateral statement as a lender (today live, or `day`): per open loan the mark and its quality, fees owed to you, the short's collateral share at the custodian and its coverage against 105% of mark + fees. A unit without a mark usable for risk shows no coverage number.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_liquidity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_liquidity) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/liquidity/{bookKey} | The liquidity score (0-100) of a book key and every component behind it: trades in 30/90 days, distinct counterparties, median days between trades, time-weighted quoted spread, two-sided share and depth within 5% (hourly book snapshots), Amihud and Roll. Each component is a percentile within the category. No score under the data threshold.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_loan_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_loan_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/loans/quote | How much you can borrow against vault units (`vaultItemIds`, comma-separated; none = every stored unit you hold): each unit's tier, mark and limit, the pool's rate and yours.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_loan_unit_sale_floor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_loan_unit_sale_floor) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/loans/{id}/units/{vaultItemId}/sale-floor | The lowest price a pledged unit can be sold at to repay its loan: after the sale (less the vault fee and storage) what is still owed must fit inside the other units' limit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_locate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_locate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/locates | A locate quote: how many units of `key` (a book key) you could borrow now (up to `qty`), the indicative rate (APR bps; `termDays` 30 or 90 for a fixed-rate term loan), and the collateral they need (150%, or 175% for units whose lenders elected return in kind only). Holds nothing; POST to hold.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_mark**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_mark) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/marks/{bookKey} | One book key mark with everything behind it: the price in cents, its quality (A trade-anchored, B firm two-sided, C indicative, none), its confidence in bps and the band in cents, when it was computed, whether risk may act on it (judged for margin), the related key it was implied from (impliedFrom: a grade, the model median of the other sizes, or an index; null when none), every stored input, and the learned relations it may be implied from.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_market**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_market) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/market | One book key (variantId:condition:gradeKey): its mark and history, lending depth and borrow rate, open interest and live auctions.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_market_model**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_market_model) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/market-model/{bookKey} | How a book key trades: its liquidity tier (A continuous, B call auction every 15 minutes, C daily call, D listings only), the call collecting now with its indicative price and volume and nominal close, the next call, the LULD band and any limit state, pause or halt.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_mm**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_mm) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/mm | Your market-maker programmes, the last 30 days of obligations, rebates earned (paid only from fees already taken), and the rules.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_mm_key**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_mm_key) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/mm/key | A key's designated market maker (by the name it chose) and how many LPs quote it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_mm_mmp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_mm_mmp) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp | Your market-maker protection: the fill trigger, the freeze, and whether you are frozen now.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_mode**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_mode) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/mode | Your Vault Markets mode: simple (buy, sell and what it is worth) or pro (the full terminal), whether you chose it or it was set for you (new people start in simple; people already trading start in pro), and when.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_nav**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_nav) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/nav | Mark NAV, bid NAV (the resting bids) and liquidation NAV (the bids at 20% of ADV for 10 days), holding by holding, each with its ASC 820 level, mark quality and source, and your administrator's price when uploaded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_nav_file**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_nav_file) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/nav/file/{format} | The NAV as a csv, pdf or json file.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_opening**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_opening) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/opening/{slug} | One opening key.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options | Fractional options: the venue mode (self = Crossly is the venue and clearing house, partner = routed to the index DCM, paper = practice only) with the env each mode still needs, the active series and the rule book in force.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_analytic**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_analytic) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/analytics/{underlying} | Options analytics on an underlying: the vol surface (expiry x moneyness) and one expiry's smile (`expiry`), IV rank and percentile against the series' own year, open interest and volume by strike and expiry, put/call ratios, the expected move to each expiry, and the historical vol of the underlying's own published series. Every implied vol is derived and display only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_auto_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_auto_close) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/auto-close | Your auto-close rule: close positions (or only in-the-money ones) a set time before expiry with a marketable limit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_chain**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_chain) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/chain/{underlying} | The option chain for an underlying (index slug or book key): its expiries, and for one expiry (`expiry`, default the nearest) each strike's call and put with best bid / ask, last, open interest, the theoretical price at the reference vol, the implied vol of the mid (display only, never a mark) and the Greeks. Prices are cents per contract.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_combo_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_combo_book) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/book | One strategy's combo book by its canonical key (`key`, from the preview or an order): bids and asks by net price and recent strategy trades.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_combo_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_combo_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/{id} | One strategy quote request and its answers (all of them to the requester, your own to a responder).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_contract**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_contract) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/contracts/{id} | One option contract: its terms, the self-venue book and tape, its published daily settlement prices and, once settled, its settlement (value, source, flagged, and the hash chained to the previous settlement).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_contract_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_contract_book) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/contracts/{id}/book | A contract's order book on the Crossly venue (`levels`, default 20): bids and asks by price with milli-contract sizes, and the last 50 prints.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_contract_settlement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_contract_settlement) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/contracts/{id}/settlement | A settled contract's settlement: the value (cents per unit of the underlying), its source (the index's frozen 7-day TWAP, the item's expiry-day A/B mark, or the last reliable value, flagged), and the hash chained to the previous settlement. Null until it settles.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_hedge**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_hedge) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/hedges | Your hedges: long puts protecting vault units you hold (protective puts) and the vault units locked as collateral for calls you wrote (covered calls).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_iv**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_iv) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/iv/{underlying} | The at-the-money implied volatility of the nearest expiry on an underlying, for display on its page: derived from the options book, quality C, never written as a mark.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_margin**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_margin) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/margin | Your options margin: the scenario requirement (worst loss over a +/-40% price x +/-50% vol grid, with offsets from vault units and positions you hold), initial margin, equity and state (ok / call / liquidate) per underlying. Authoritative on the Crossly venue; on the partner venue the partner's figure decides and this is an estimate.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_payoff**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_payoff) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/payoff | Your whole options position on one `underlying` at expiry: the P&L curve over 50%..150% of spot, breakevens, and the maximum gain and loss (null when unbounded).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_position**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_position) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/positions | Your options positions: signed milli-contracts, average open price, the price it is settled to (Crossly venue), the theoretical mark, unrealised and realised P&L in cents, the Greeks for your size, the protective-put flag and your auto-deleveraging queue lights. `status` open (default) / all, `subaccountId`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_practice**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_practice) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice | Your options practice account (play money only, $10,000 to start): cash, positions at the practice maker's price, equity and P&L.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/{id} | One option quote request: yours with every answer, or one you were sent with your own answer.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_option_risk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_option_risk) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/risk | Your options portfolio's Greeks (per underlying and total, with dollar delta and 1% gamma), a scenario's P&L (`priceShift`, `volShift`, `daysForward`), a price x vol P&L grid, and the beta-weighted delta against an index (`betaIndex`, default the deepest). `book` live (default) or practice.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_oracle**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_oracle) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/oracle | Crossly as an oracle publisher (needs CROSSLY_ORACLE_PUBLISHER_KEY + CROSSLY_ORACLE_NETWORK): the network (pyth or switchboard), the ed25519 key every value is signed with, each published index feed with its latest attempt, and 24 h counts of published, skipped and failed. Only built-in indexes priced from A/B marks are published; Crossly never reads its own feed back.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_oracle_by_slug**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_oracle_by_slug) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/oracle/{slug} | The latest published oracle value for an index: level, fixed-point price and confidence at the feed exponent, confidence in bps, staleness in seconds, coverage, the exact signed message and its ed25519 signature with the signer key, and whether it is fresh.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_order_handling**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_order_handling) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/order-handling | How orders are handled (the 606/607-style disclosure): one book, self-trade prevention, protected market orders, liquidations by auction, halts, dealers and fees.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_p2p_dashboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_p2p_dashboard) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/dashboard | Peer-to-peer vault loans at a glance: what you owe as a borrower and are owed as a lender (principal + interest to the second), daily interest, called loans, your open offers, the gates and the rules (auction hours, rate ceiling, size bounds, call grace).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_p2p_loan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_p2p_loan) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans/{id} | One P2P loan with its money log (fund, repay, refinance). A loan in a running refinancing auction can be read by any lender, without the log.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_paper**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_paper) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper | Your paper trading account (play money only, never real or convertible; opened at $10,000 the first time): play cash, buying power, paper positions at the real marks, P&L, working paper orders, recent paper fills and this week on the leaderboard.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_paper_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_paper_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper/quote | What a paper order would fill against the real book now, without placing it: units fillable inside the limit (a market order uses the 5% protection band around the mark), average price, simulated fee, and the queue ahead of any rest.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_partner_me**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_partner_me) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/partners/me | Your Vault Drop-off Partnership (null when you never applied): status, partner code, fee share and cap, finder profile, totals and batches; and whether your one-time ID check is done.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pool) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pool | The CBX market staking pool: size, value, what it lends, utilisation, rate, insurance fund.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pop_report**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pop_report) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pop-reports | Grading population report for one market (`bookKey`): focus (on a graded market: its grade, pop = copies graded at exactly that grade, higher = copies graded better by the same grader, total, asOf and source), graders (each grader ladder for the card, best grade first, from one report: the newest, the population feed over a hand entry on the same date), provider.connected, and status (ok, not_synced, no_report or no_source) with explain, which says in plain words what is missing. source is provider (the population feed) or ops (entered by Crossly from the grader published report). Counts only, never a price.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_portfolio**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_portfolio) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio | Your vault holdings at the market price, with cost and P&L, and units you have out on loan.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_portfolio_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_portfolio_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio/history | Your Vault Markets value by day at the marks (vault units, units on loan, positions) and today's return against yesterday's close.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_portfolio_margin**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_portfolio_margin) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin | Portfolio margin for a firm sub-account (`subaccountId`): eligibility with every reason (ECP, KYB, minimum equity $500k or $100k with evidenced intraday monitoring), whether it is enrolled, the latest requirement, equity, excess and state (healthy, call or buy_in), the breakdown by category and item, the partner index futures counted, and the policy. Before enrolling, a live preview.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_position**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_position) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id} | One position with its loans, auctions and custodian instruction log.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_position_exit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_position_exit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/exits | A position's take-profit / stop-loss, if set.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_position_risk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_position_risk) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/risk | One position's call and liquidation marks.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions | The prediction markets home: the busiest and soonest-expiring \"Will it hit?\" markets with their probability (each market's own price, display only), the underlyings with markets, which venue is live, and how many positions you hold.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_account**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_account) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/account | Your prediction-market account: the live venue, your account there and the terms you accepted, whether you may trade real money (region, age, self-exclusion) and why not, your self-set limits, and the practice account line.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_board**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_board) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/board | The prediction board the Trading Floor and the Market Open plank show: the busiest open markets and their odds. Anonymous: never who holds what.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_combo**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_combo) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/{id} | One of your combos.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs/{id} | One combo request: its legs and size, and the answers you can see (all of them if you asked; yours if you quoted).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_daily_price**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_daily_price) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/daily-prices | The published daily settlement price of every listed market for a day (default yesterday): the last hour's volume-weighted Yes price, else the book mid, else the prior day's, with volume and open interest.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_event**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_event) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/events/{id} | One expiry of a series (an event): its markets, what you hold in each, the real worst and best case of your holdings across them (what your limit counts), and, for a full bucket set, how many sets you could merge. `venue` paper or live.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_forecaster**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_forecaster) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecaster | Your forecaster profile: display name, whether it is public (opt-in) and since when, and your followers.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_by_id**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_forecaster_by_id) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecasters/{id} | A public forecaster: scores (all time and this month), open sides (never sizes), recent settled calls, followers. 404 when private.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_leaderboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/leaderboard | Opt-in forecaster leaderboards: `period` week, month or all; `metric` pnl, accuracy or brier; `venue` live or paper (practice has its own board).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_limit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_limit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/limits | Your self-set prediction limits: daily loss limit, self-exclusion, a raise waiting its 24 hours, and any hold the venue placed.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_market**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_market) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets/{id} | One prediction market: its terms, the Yes ladder of the live venue and of the practice book, recent trades, settlement history (outcome, value, hash, review), the series rules text and hash, and your positions in it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_market_forecast**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_market_forecast) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets/{id}/forecasts | How many people forecast a market and their median probability (no names).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_market_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_market_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets/{id}/history | A market's history: probability points (display only, never a mark), candles on the Yes price, volume and open interest over `range` 1d, 1w, 1m or all; `venue` live or paper.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_market_stream**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_market_stream) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets/{id}/stream | A server-sent event stream for one prediction market: `snapshot` first, then `pm_depth`, `pm_trades`, `pm_probability`, `pm_status` and `pm_settlement` as they change.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_market_trade**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_market_trade) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets/{id}/trades | The trade tape, newest first: Yes price, size, which side took, venue. Page with `before` (the last `seq`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_me_stream**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_me_stream) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/me/stream | A server-sent event stream of your own prediction events: `pm_order` (placed, filled, amended, cancelled, expired), `pm_fill` (your side, price and fee), `pm_position`, `pm_combo` and `pm_conversion`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_mission**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_mission) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/missions | The two \"Will it hit?\" practice missions (your first call; see one settle), whether the server saw each done, and the pin they lead to. Pins only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_paper**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_paper) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/paper | Your practice prediction account (play money only, $1,000 to start, never real or convertible): cash, money locked by working orders, positions and working orders.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/quote | What an order would do now without placing it: contracts fillable inside the limit (a market order uses the 10-cent band), average price, estimated fee (Kalshi's formula), the money it would lock, and the most it could pay out.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_rulebook**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_rulebook) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/rulebook | The prediction venue's rule book now and every version, each hashed and chained to the one before (`chainOk`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_stream**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_stream) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/stream | A server-sent event stream for up to 50 markets (`markets`=id,id): `ready`, then `pm_depth`, `pm_trades`, `pm_probability`, `pm_status` and `pm_settlement`, each carrying its marketId.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_venue**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_venue) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/venue | Which prediction venue is live: self (Crossly as the DCM, money at the segregated settlement bank), partner (a partner DCM) or paper (practice only), who runs the book, where money sits, and the env each real mode still needs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_prediction_will_it_hit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_prediction_will_it_hit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/will-it-hit | The \"Will it hit?\" panel for a key (`bookKey`) or an index (`index` slug): its open markets grouped by expiry, each with its probability. `eligible` is false when nothing is listed on it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_press**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_press) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/press | The press kit: each built-in index with level, quality, n, CSV link and a \"cite as\" line, plus the methodology links.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_price_widget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_price_widget) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | One of your price widgets, with its iframe and script-tag snippets and URLs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pro_depth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pro_depth) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/depth | The L2 depth ladder: price levels and units per side, best first; asks show how many units are authenticated vault stock.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pro_order**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pro_order) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders/{id} | One order with its full event log.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pro_snapshot**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pro_snapshot) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/snapshot | A book key's depth (20 levels a side), last 50 trades, 24 h stats, mark and any halt, in one call.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pro_stat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pro_stat) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/stats | 24 h open, high, low, last, volume and change on a book key.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_pro_stream**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_pro_stream) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/stream | A long-lived text/event-stream for a book key: `snapshot` first, then `depth`, `trades`, `mark` and `halt` events as they change.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_profile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_profile) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/profile | Your public vault profile switch: on or off, whether followers see new buys, your profile URL and follower count.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_profile_by_handle**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_profile_by_handle) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/profiles/{handle} | Someone's public vault: each market's weight (bps of the marked value), followers, whether you follow, their recent buys (no prices).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves | Proof of reserves: the latest daily Merkle root over every unit in custody (leaf count, stored / listed / grading / withdrawing, pledged, on loan, open loans, receipts, insured value), its Solana memo anchor when VAULT_POR_ANCHOR_KEY is set, the last `days` roots, and the exact hashing method.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_by_day**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_by_day) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/{day} | One day's proof-of-reserves snapshot (root, counts, anchor).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_me**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_me) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/me | Your inclusion proofs for a day (latest by default): each of your units' leaf fields, salt, owner commitment, leaf hash and sibling path. Verify with your OWN user id: commitment = SHA-256(JSON[\"crossly-por-owner-v1\", userId, salt]); then the leaf; then climb the path to the published root.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_rate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_rate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/rates | Daily borrow APR (lent units) and pool APR on a book key, with the pool as a whole, oldest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_report**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_report) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/report | The quarterly Crossly Vault Report (`quarter` like 2026-q3; default the latest): index levels, top movers, most-lent markets, grade premiums. Book data only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/{id} | One quote request: the requester sees every answer (anonymous); a responder sees the request and their own answer.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_risk_report**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_risk_report) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/risk-report | Historical VaR 95/99 1d/10d and Bangia LVaR (model figures: labelled, with n and a band, null below the data threshold), stress scenarios (category −30%, CBX −50%, books thin to 25%, index drawdowns), concentration and counterparty exposure.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_risk_stat**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_risk_stat) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/risk-stats/{bookKey} | Risk statistics for a book key (weekly returns from A-quality marks only) or an index slug (its closes): annualised volatility raw and Geltner-desmoothed (labelled), max drawdown and beta to the Composite. Needs 26 weekly returns, else no numbers.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_set_goal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_set_goal) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | One set goal: every piece in the set order, held (with the vault unit) or missing (with the cheapest marked book key that meets the target and its mark), the conditions and grades seen, and the working basket bid with its legs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_set_goal_basket_plan**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_set_goal_basket_plan) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/basket-plan | What a basket bid would place now, without placing it: one leg per missing piece (cheapest first, the mark plus maxBpsOverMark as the limit), the pieces skipped and why, and the most it can cost (limits plus the worst-case buyer fee), never more than budgetCents.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_setting**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_setting) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/settings | Your vault defaults: Sell from first, new-inventory suggestions, the insurance rule and the default grading service.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares | Vault Shares at a glance: the venue mode in force (self, partner or paper) and the env it still needs, the rules (about $5 a share, 100 to 100,000 shares, originator keeps 0-50%, 3% origination, maker 0 / taker 100 bps, 90-day lockup, buyouts at the mark x 1.10 with a two-thirds vote) and the disclosure.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_basket**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_basket) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/baskets/{id} | One theme basket (id or slug): its series, target and live weights, prices, and your own holdings against the weights.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_card) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | One share card as the public sees it, with its image and link paths.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_investor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_investor) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/investor | Your investor account at the venue in force: its status, the investor terms you accepted, and your Reg A+ limit for one offering (10% of the greater of income and net worth unless accredited).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_me**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_me) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/me | Your Vault Shares: shares and percent of each item, cost, implied value at the last price, your subscriptions and the payouts you were sent.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_paper**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_paper) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper | Your practice account for Vault Shares (play money only, never real, never convertible): cash, practice holdings, open practice orders, recent practice fills.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_paper_sery_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_paper_sery_book) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/paper/series/{id}/book | A series’ practice book: practice orders and the house quote 1% either side of the reference price.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_preview) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/preview | Fractionalize instead: whether one of your vault units can become a Vault Shares series now (stored, tier A or B, reliable mark of $1,000 or more, declared and insured, free) and the offering terms it would get.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_sery**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_sery) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id} | One series: the offering, the share book depth, the latest fills, holders as share percentages (nobody named), buyouts and your vote, your orders and subscriptions.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_sery_book**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_sery_book) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/book | A share book’s depth: whole shares per price level (integer cents a share), best first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_sery_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_sery_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/history | A series’ full price history: OHLC candles of every share-book fill (`interval` 1h, 1d or 1w), the offering price, the change since the offering, and the appraisal history beside it. Share prices are display only, never a mark.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_sery_perk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_sery_perk) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/perks | Holder perks on a series: whether you hold it, your holder badge, the links to visit the item in the world vault room and in AR, the viewing days with places left and your bookings.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_share_sery_room**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_share_sery_room) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/room | The holder room for a series (shareholders only): posts newest first under per-series aliases, never account names. `before` pages back.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_statement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_statement) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/statements | The last 24 months with whether each statement is issued (frozen, with its sha256).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_statement_by_month**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_statement_by_month) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/statements/{month} | A monthly statement (`month` like 2026-10): units held at month end, loans, pledges, positions, fills, fees, storage, and cash quoted from the custodian and labelled as its record. Issued (frozen) once the month ends.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_statement_file**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_statement_file) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/statements/{month}/file/{format} | A monthly statement as a csv, pdf or json file.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_supply**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_supply) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply | A book key's units in the vault and authentications passed, by day (oldest first, today live).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_supply_batch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_supply_batch) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/batches/{id} | A box sent to the vault: its prepaid label and tracking, or why it has no label.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_supply_round_up**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_supply_round_up) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Your seller round-up: the share of each payout that sets a vault buy's budget, the budget so far and what it is doing. `roundUp` is null when none is set.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_supply_shipment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_supply_shipment) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/shipments/{id} | One shipment into the vault, with each unit and whether it was received.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax | Your tax year on the vault markets: units bought and sold with cost, proceeds, gain and term; lending fees, staking interest (estimate), market-maker rewards; closed positions. Links to the CSV and PDF.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_csv**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_csv) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax.csv | The tax year as a CSV: one row per sale, purchase, gift, income line and closed position.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_loss_suggestion**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_loss_suggestion) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax/loss-suggestions | Tax-loss suggestions: the units you hold whose mark is below your cost, each with its lot (acquired when and how, cost in cents), short or long term if sold today and the day a short lot turns long, the estimated loss at the mark before selling fees, mark quality, what stops a sale, and a link to the ordinary sell ticket. With totals by term, the gains realised so far this year, and a not-tax-advice disclosure. Never sells.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_option**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_option) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax/options | Your fractional options tax lots for a calendar year (`year`, default this year): FIFO disposals (closed or settled) with proceeds, cost, gain in cents and holding term, each flagged as a possible Section 1256 contract, the totals with the 60/40 split, and the positions open at year end. Real fills only; practice never appears. Not tax advice.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_pdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_pdf) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax.pdf | The tax year as a PDF summary.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_prediction**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_prediction) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax/predictions | Your prediction-market results for a calendar year (`year`), as the venue reports them: each settled or voided real-money position with its venue, cost, fees, payout, refund, closing-trade gains and net in cents, and the totals. Practice never appears. Not tax advice.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tax_share**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tax_share) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tax/shares | Your Vault Shares tax lots for a calendar year (`year`): disposals (share-book sales, exit payouts) with holding periods, proceeds, cost and gain in cents, the open lots and the totals. The broker-dealer issues the 1099-B; not tax advice.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tca**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tca) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tca | Your pro orders in a window (`from`, `to`; default 90 days) with arrival mark and mid, average fill, implementation shortfall, slippage, fill rate and time to fill.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tca_by_order_id**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tca_by_order_id) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tca/{orderId} | One order's TCA, including slippage against the VWAP of eligible trades while it worked.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tod**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tod) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tod | Your transfer-on-death setup: the gate and terms, whether you accepted them, your beneficiaries and shares, units with their designations and who the plan gives them to today, and open claims on your account (which you can stop). A TodOverview object, not a collection.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_tod_my_claim**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_tod_my_claim) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/tod/my-claims | Claims you filed as a beneficiary: status in words, documents sent (names only), the waiting period, and units moved or held. A TodMyClaims object, not a collection.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_unit_ar**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_unit_ar) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/ar | A vault unit as an AR model, for \"See it in your room\" (the web's /ar/vault-<id> page hands it to Scene Viewer, WebXR or Quick Look): title, model (a path under /models, without .glb, at real size), scanUrl (the unit's own captured mesh, when it has one), imageUrl, art, tint, scale, bookKey, backPath and mine. Your own units always; somebody else's only while they show it to visitors in a home you may enter (else 404).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_unit_evidence**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_unit_evidence) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/evidence | Evidence on a vault unit:its 3D scans (model, texture, listing photos) and its grade report (grader, grade, cert, verified against the grader record, hub intake photos). No owner identity.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_unit_quote**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_unit_quote) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/quote | The market around a vault unit: its mark and source, the best bid that could take it, the Instant Exit price (the best bid) and a pack pull's own sell-back credit.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_unit_story**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_unit_story) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/units/{id}/story | One vault unit's story, oldest first: intake (with scan and grade evidence when it has some), grades, owners, sales at their prices, loans, pledges, Hall of Grails exhibitions, receipt-token exports and redemptions, and each warehouse-receipt hash-chain step (re-verified on read: chain.ok). People are named only while their public vault profile is on; others are 'A collector'.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_valuation_statement**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_valuation_statement) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id} | One statement you issued: every unit, the totals by mark quality, the methodology, its sha256, its audit-chain seal and the verify link.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_valuation_statement_pdf**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_valuation_statement_pdf) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id}/pdf | One statement you issued as a PDF.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_valuation_statement_preview**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_valuation_statement_preview) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/preview | An insurance or appraisal statement for a day (purpose, asOf YYYY-MM-DD, default today), built but not issued: every unit you held that day at its mark, with quality, last trade, grade and cert, and the total. No id, no hash.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_valuation_statement_verify**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_valuation_statement_verify) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements/{id}/verify | Check any issued statement by its id, as an insurer would: purpose, date, totals by mark quality and the sha256; intact (the stored record still hashes to it), seal.ok (its audit-chain entry still carries it) and, with ?hash=, whether yours matches. Never the holder or the units.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_value**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_value) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/value | A catalogue item's vault book keys (`?skuId=`, optional `variantId`): each key's display mark with quality and n, units in the vault, the Instant Exit price a holder would get now, and whether it can be shipped in (ungraded keys).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_vault_suggestion_item**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_vault_suggestion_item) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/vault-suggestions/items/{id} | One inventory item: its vault suggestion, or in plain words why there is none (`status`: suggested, not_found, no_stock, not_matched, in_vault or no_market).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_widget_index**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_widget_index) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/widgets/index/{slug} | What the <crossly-index> widget shows: level, closes, quality, n, as-of, or `insufficient`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_widget_mark**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_widget_mark) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/widgets/mark | What the <crossly-mark> widget shows for `bookKey`: mark, quality, trades over 30 days, as-of, sparkline, or `insufficient`.
