*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_widget_summary**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_widget_summary) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/widget/summary | The home-screen widget summary: portfolio value at the mark with its move since the previous marks (cents and bps) and the share resting on reliable A/B marks, the three biggest movers among what you hold (the market movers when you hold nothing; `movers.source` says which), and one watchlist key (`?key=` a book key on your watchlist; else your newest watch) with a 14-day sparkline. Every price is from the Crossly order book and carries its mark quality (A, B, C or none). Cached for five minutes.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_world_floor**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_world_floor) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/floor | The Trading Floor in Crossly World: trades (the newest trades on every vault book in the last day, at most 40, newest first: bookKey, name, priceCents, priceText, side -- buy when a buyer took an ask, sell when a seller hit a bid, cross for an auction -- and ageS), tickers (the big board: the most-traded markets, each with its last trade price, the change from the last trade before today in basis points, trades today and the display mark with its quality), bell (Market Open: ringing for ten minutes after a new key opens, with the key and its market link, and what opens next) and trades24h. Every price is a trade on the Crossly order book; it never shows who traded. Nothing can be traded in the room.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_world_floor_board**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_world_floor_board) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/floor/boards | The Trading Floor boards for the round-13 products: odds (the probability ladder: the busiest open \"Will it hit?\" markets with probabilityBps, the market’s own price as a probability, display only), heat (option-chain heat: per listed underlying, open interest and the last day’s volume in milli-contracts in strike buckets around the underlying, a 0..1 heat per bucket and the at-the-money implied vol, display only) and shares (Vault Shares tickers: last share price in cents, changeBps from the last fill before today, shares traded today and the implied item value, display only). quality is always C: none of these is ever a mark. It never shows who traded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_world_lounge**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_world_lounge) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/lounge | The Vault Holders Lounge: the rule to join (held value at reliable marks, or a unit count), whether you may join now and what you hold toward it. Anyone may look in; only holders are seen there. No perks, nothing to trade.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_world_place_vault_gate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_world_place_vault_gate) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/places/{place}/vault-gate | The vault-holder door on a Crossly World room or Space, by its presence slug (a room share slug, or space-<slug>): the rule (minValueCents held at reliable A/B marks and/or minUnits units, mode any or all, optionally one product, bullion family or category), the rule in words, and whether you may join now with what you hold toward it. gate is null when the place has no vault door. Anyone may look in; only holders join.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_worth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_worth) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/worth | What one book key (variantId:condition:gradeKey, `bookKey`) is worth: the mark in cents with its quality (A from trades, B from firm quotes, C indicative, none) and band, what Instant Exit would pay for one unit now from the best resting bid (yours left out), and your units: how many you hold, how many are free to sell, their value at the mark and what selling every free one now would fetch from today’s bids. Plain-words `headline` and `lines`. Read only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_market_worth_vault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_market_worth_vault) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/worth/vault | What your whole vault is worth now: value at the marks split into reliable and indicative marks, cost and P&L, today’s change, and an estimate of what the units free to sell would fetch from the bids resting on the book now. Read only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_receipt**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_receipt) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipts/{receiptNo} | One of your receipts with its full hash chain (issued, transferred, pledged, released, surrendered), re-verified on read: `chain.ok` is false if any event was altered.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_receipt_token**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_receipt_token) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/{id} | One receipt token (yours, or on a unit you own now) with its chain log: built, minted, frozen, thawed, new holder seen, redeem built, burned, with explorer links.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**get_vault_receipt_token_by_mint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#get_vault_receipt_token_by_mint) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/by-mint/{mint} | What a Solana mint is: the warehouse receipt number and item it stands for, and whether the token is live, frozen (unit pledged or lent) or burned, and whether the unit is still on a vault shelf. Names no person and no wallet.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_accounts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_accounts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/accounts | Every account you can act on: your own, and each sub-account your entity memberships reach, with your role.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_adl_events**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_adl_events) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/adl/events | The ADL events your positions were part of, newest first: role bankrupt (yours went under water) or counterparty (yours was first in line), the price both closed at and the transfer status.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_adl_positions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_adl_positions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/adl/positions | Your live positions, each with its place in its item ADL queue: lights 5 (first in line) to 1 (last), 0 when not in the queue (only profitable positions are), ranked by P&L% x effective leverage.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_agent_drafts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_agent_drafts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/agent/drafts | Limit orders the shopping agent drafted for you, newest first. A draft places nothing: send its orderBody to POST /api/v1/vault-markets/pro/orders to place it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_agent_watches**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_agent_watches) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/agent/watches | Your price watches on book keys (best bid, lowest ask, last trade or market price, in cents), live ones first. `?live=true` for live only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_alert_log**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_alert_log) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/alert-log | What the alerts hub did with your recent vault alerts (`?limit=` 1 to 200, default 50): kind, title, the channels it went to, what was held (channel:reason) and the email digest state.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_alerts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_alerts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/alerts | Your price alerts on book keys.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_auctions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_auctions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/auctions | Open vault auction events (weekly, ending Sunday; premier, units marked $10,000+) and their lots, soonest ending first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_blocks**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_blocks) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/blocks | An entity's block orders, newest first, each with its plan and how it was allocated.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_bullion_family_pegs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_bullion_family_pegs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/family-pegs | Your family-wide spot-pegged quotes (live, or all with status=all): one quote resting across a bullion family books at one condition, with units left, the peg, its status and every leg (the pegged quote on each book).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_bullion_keys**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_bullion_keys) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/keys | Bullion key families (metal, form, size, mint or any mint): members, vault units, best resting bid and ask across their books, and spot as a labelled reference with what one unit of metal is worth at it. Spot is never a Crossly price.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_bullion_pegs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_bullion_pegs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/bullion/pegs | Your spot-pegged quotes (live, or all with status=all): side, units, the peg, the order it rests as, the last price and spot it was priced from, and why it is pulled when it is.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_collection_worth**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_collection_worth) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/collection-worth | Your collection valued at the display marks: rows on a vault book key with a mark are summed; rows with no mark or no key are counted, never valued.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_confirmations**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_confirmations) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/confirmations | Trade confirmations for your fills in a window (default 31 days): price, sale fee, maker or taker, order, vault unit and warehouse receipt.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_creator_cohost_invites**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_creator_cohost_invites) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/creators/cohost-invites | For creators: your invites from Crossly to co-host a Market Open launch show (invited, accepted, declined or withdrawn), each with the key, its show and the guidance for co-hosts.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_credit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_credit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/credit | Your vault loans and advances (live by default): what is owed, interest accrued, loan-to-value, the liquidation line and the collateral value it is called at.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_dealer_bands**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_dealer_bands) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Your Instant Exit bands and whether each is being kept now.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_dealer_bids**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_dealer_bids) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bids | Your standing dealer bids: open (default) or all.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_dealer_pool_fills**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_dealer_pool_fills) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id}/fills | A curve pool's fills, newest first: side, price and spot after.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_dealer_pools**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_dealer_pools) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools | Your curve pools: open (default) or all, each with its curve, spot, units, buy budget, running totals, the ladder it shows and its live legs.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_digest_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_digest_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/digest/history | Past weekly digests: week, status (sent / skipped / failed), the channels used and a summary.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_entity_audit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_entity_audit) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/audit | The entity's audit trail, newest first: hash-chained rows with actor, account, API key, IP, session and source.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_entity_conversions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_entity_conversions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/conversions | The custodian's CBX-to-USD reward conversions for an entity that elected USD, newest first, with their status.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_estimates_accuracy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_estimates_accuracy) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/estimates-accuracy | The Crossly Estimate's track record: walk-forward backtest n, FSD and bias per method, category and liquidity bucket, and whether estimates in that cell are shown.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_feed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_feed) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/feed | New vault buys by the people you follow who share them, newest first (no prices). Page with `before` (the last item's `at`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_copy_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_copy_watch) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch | The collectors you copy-watch and whether each still shares bids and asks.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_copy_watch_alerts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_copy_watch_alerts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/alerts | Your copy-watch alerts, newest first: who, bid or ask, quantity, limit price, market, and whether it still rests. Page with `before`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_feed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_feed) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/feed | Public vault activity of the collectors you follow (buys, units sent in, sales, Hall loans), newest first, never a price. Filter with `kinds`; page with `before`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_following**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_following) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/following | The collectors you follow who have a public vault: what each shares and whether you copy-watch them.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_leaderboards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_leaderboards) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/leaderboards | An opt-in return leaderboard (`period` 30d, 90d or 365d; `category` all or a category slug), ranked by risk-adjusted return from the book A/B marks. Percent and risk only: no dollars, no volume.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_follow_profile_activity**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_follow_profile_activity) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/follows/profiles/{handle}/activity | One collector public vault activity, newest first, never a price. 404 when their vault is not public.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_gifts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_gifts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/gifts | Gifts you sent and received, newest first, with their status.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_grade_to_vault**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_grade_to_vault) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grade-to-vault | Your Grade to Vault flows, newest first, each brought up to date: its step, the grading submission, the unit, the grade, the market for it, and the ask once listed. `live=1` for those still going.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_grading**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_grading) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/grading | Your vault units sent to grading: where each card is in its run, and the grade once it is back.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_hall_lenders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_hall_lenders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/hall/lenders | The vault units lent to this week's Hall of Grails exhibition and on show now, in plinth order: the item (skuId, name, imageUrl, grade), the lender as the plate names them (a display name, or \"a collector\"), the plate lines and how many exhibitions the unit has stood in. meta.exhibition is the week (key, title, endsAt). Names no ids and no amounts.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_holdings**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_holdings) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/holdings | Everything you own in the Crossly vault, newest activity first: each unit with its status (inbound, stored, listed, withdraw_requested…) and situation (lent, pledged, in_grading…), what it is worth (`valueCents` with `valueSource` mark | last_sale | declared | none), declared and insured value, storage owed, its open ask, the deposit toggles when you sent it in, and what you may do with it now (`actions`). Units sold or shipped home in the last 90 days have `past: true`. `meta` carries the totals (units, value, declared, insured, storage owed), your recent boxes and their labels, the ship-home prices and the lending gate.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_committee_minutes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_committee_minutes) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-committee/minutes | Published Index Committee minutes, newest first: attendees (names and roles), quorum, agenda, minutes and decisions.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_complaints**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_complaints) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-complaints | Your complaints and their answers, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_constituents**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_constituents) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/constituents | The book keys in an index with their weight, move since the last close and contribution to the index's move (basis points).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_consultations**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_consultations) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-consultations | Consultations on index method changes (open for at least 30 days), newest first. Filter by `status`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_events**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_events) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-events | Every index event, newest first: add, delete, rebalance, method, correction, halt; each with when it was announced, when it takes effect, the detail and the reason. Filter by `kind`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_events_by_slug**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_events_by_slug) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/events | One index's events, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_judgments**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_judgments) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-judgments | The expert-judgment log: every Index Committee action (halt, resume, exclude a key, void a trade, restate, retire) with its written reason.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_level_series**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_level_series) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/level-series | An index's published levels. `engine=chain` (default): daily closes. `engine=tpd|repeat_sales`: weekly (else monthly) model estimates with a 95% band and n; labelled, never a price. `series=settlement` returns each value as first published (never restated). Below the data gate `data` is empty and `notice` explains.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_licence_mine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_licence_mine) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/index-licence/mine | Your index feed licences (the indexes each covers, or all), as ops recorded them. No licence: the feed answers 403 not_a_licensee.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_members**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_members) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug}/members | The members of an index on a day (`asOf`, default today) with their weights, shares, moves and contributions; for a past day, the inputs stored with that day's close and each member's effective dates.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_index_movers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_index_movers) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/movers | The built-in indexes that moved most since their last daily close.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_indexes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_indexes) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/indexes | Indexes over book keys: per category, per top brand, the Crossly 50 and your own. Each with its level now (base 1000 at inception) and change since the last daily close.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_instant_exit_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_instant_exit_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit/history | Your Instant Exits, newest first: quoted price, mark, and whether it filled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_instant_exit_holdings**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_instant_exit_holdings) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/instant-exit/holdings | Instant Exit quotes for everything you hold in the vault, one per book key, with units held and which are free to sell.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_joint_trades**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_joint_trades) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/joint/{id}/trades | The joint vault trades, newest first: side, book key, limit, who started it, whose account the order sits on, and the order standing on the book.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_large_trader_reports**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_large_trader_reports) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/large-trader-reports | Your large-trader reports (with any linked accounts, as one party): due first, then filed, then cleared in the last 90 days. Each has `action`: file | amend | refile | none.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_lending_statements**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_lending_statements) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/lending/statements | The days you have stored collateral statements for, newest first, with their summary.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_loans**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_loans) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/loans | Units you lent: rate, fees earned, recall due.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_locate_mine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_locate_mine) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/locates/mine | Your locates, newest first (held / used / released / expired).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_midpoint**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_midpoint) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/midpoint | Your midpoint block orders.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_movers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_movers) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/movers | Vault items with the biggest 24 h moves in their market price (from the order book), with units in the vault.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_nav_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_nav_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/nav/history | Daily NAV snapshots (mark, bid, liquidation, Level 2 / Level 3).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_offers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_offers) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/offers | The offers inbox: for each book key you hold, the best bid from someone else, units at it, the top bid levels, the mark, and your alert.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_opening**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_opening) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/opening | Keys opening soon on the vault book (and just opened): units on the shelf, dealers quoting, the waitlist, the mark when the book has one, and your place.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_combo_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_combo_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq | Your strategy quote requests, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_inbox**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_combo_rfq_inbox) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos/rfq/inbox | Open strategy quote requests you may answer (never your own; the requester stays anonymous).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_combos**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_combos) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/combos | Your strategies: `status` working (default), done or all, with their legs as you hold them, net per unit, filled size and status.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_contract_daily**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_contract_daily) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/contracts/{id}/daily | A contract's published daily settlement prices, newest first (`days`, default 30): the price, how it was set (volume-weighted fills, the mid, or Black-Scholes at the reference vol), its implied vol, the underlying, open interest and volume, and each row's chain hash.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_mm_quotes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_mm_quotes) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/mm/quotes | Your two-sided option quotes: the contract, the bid and ask orders behind them, cancel-on-disconnect and the last heartbeat.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_notices**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_notices) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/notices | Your options notices, newest first: expiry warnings, auto-closes, exercises (a long settled in the money), assignments (a short settled in the money), expiries worthless and rolls.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/orders | Your options orders, newest first: `status` working (default) / done / all, `limit`, `subaccountId`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_practice_combos**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_practice_combos) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/combos | Your practice strategies.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_practice_fills**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_practice_fills) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/fills | Your practice options fills, newest first (`limit`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_practice_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_practice_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/practice/orders | Your practice options orders: `status` working (default) / done / all, `limit`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq | Your option quote requests, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_rfq_inbox**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_rfq_inbox) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/rfq/inbox | Open option quote requests you can answer.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_series**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_series) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/series | Every options series: its underlying (a Crossly index, or a tier A item key with 90 days of reliable marks), status and the eligibility snapshot it last passed. `status`: active (default) or all.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_option_strategies**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_option_strategies) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/options/strategies | The strategies the picker builds (vertical, calendar, diagonal, straddle, strangle, butterfly, iron condor, collar): how many strikes and expiries each takes, whether it needs calls or puts, and what it does in plain words.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_oracle_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_oracle_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/oracle/{slug}/history | Recent publish attempts for an index over 30 days, newest first: published, skipped (with the reason: too few items priced, stale marks, confidence too wide...) or failed (with the error). Filter with `status`, cap with `limit` (default 100).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_p2p_auctions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_p2p_auctions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/auctions | Running refinancing auctions, soonest to end first: lenders calls (`auction`) and borrowers looking for a cheaper lender (`refiAuction`). The payoff now, the rate a new lender gets now, when it ends, and the item.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_p2p_loans**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_p2p_loans) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/loans | Your P2P loans as borrower and/or lender (`role`), live by default (`status`): what is owed now, daily interest, the refinancing auction when called, and the item mark for information only (it never triggers anything).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_p2p_offer_mine**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_p2p_offer_mine) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers/mine | Your P2P loan offers, newest first, with how many loans each has funded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_p2p_offers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_p2p_offers) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/offers | The open P2P loan offers book, lowest rate first: cents offered per loan, APR (bps), loans left, and the item (book key) or unit each lends against. `bookKey` narrows to one item. Lender identities are never shown.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_p2p_units**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_p2p_units) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/p2p/units | Your vault units for P2P borrowing: whether each can be pledged now (and why not), the best offers covering it, and its loan when it has one.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_paper_fills**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_paper_fills) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper/fills | Your paper fills since your last reset, newest first: price, simulated fee, taker or maker, and whether a visible real order (book) or a real trade (tape) filled it. Play money only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_paper_leaderboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_paper_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper/leaderboard | The weekly paper trading leaderboard (opt-in, display names only): best return on play equity this week (or a past `week`, its Monday as YYYY-MM-DD), with your own line. Nothing is won or paid.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_paper_missions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_paper_missions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper/missions | Your paper-trading missions (play money only): each mission with what to do, your count toward its target, whether it is done and when, plus a suggested market for the \"Read the mark\" mission. Progress is observed by the server from your paper orders and fills, price watches, set goals and graded mark checks. Missions earn world quest progress and cosmetic pins, never money.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_paper_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_paper_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/paper/orders | Your paper orders since your last reset, newest first: working, done (filled, cancelled, expired) or all. Play money only.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_partner_accruals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_partner_accruals) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/partners/accruals | Your partner fee shares, newest first: accrued on a unit's first sale, paid in Bucks once the sale is final, void if it was refunded.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_partner_finder**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_partner_finder) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/partners/finder | Vault drop-off partners near a `postalCode`, optionally offering a `service`: address, hours and services.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pool_stakes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pool_stakes) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pool/stakes | Your stakes in the market pool and what each redeems for now.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_portfolio_margin_history**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_portfolio_margin_history) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/portfolio-margin/history | The 5-minute portfolio margin recalculations for a firm sub-account over the last `hours` (default 24, at most 720), newest first: equity, requirement, excess, scenario and floor figures, gross and state.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_position_units**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_position_units) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/units | The vault units of one of your positions that you hold (a leveraged long's collateral), each with its story link.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_positions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_positions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions | Your positions, live (default) or closed.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_positions_risk**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_positions_risk) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/positions-risk | For each live position: its tier, health, margin state, the mark at which it is called and the mark at which it is liquidated, and the distance to it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_alerts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_alerts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/alerts | Your probability alerts: a market, a level (percent) and a direction; each fires once through the alerts hub (kind prediction_odds).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_combo_my_rfqs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_combo_my_rfqs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/my-rfqs | Your combo requests with every answer they got.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfqs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_combo_rfqs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos/rfqs | Open combo requests makers can answer: legs, size and the legs' own combined price; never who asked. Your own answers are shown on them.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_combos**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_combos) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/combos | Your combos, as buyer or maker: legs and their outcomes, stakes, the pot, any reprice after a voided leg, and how each settled.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_comments**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_comments) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/comments | A discussion thread on a market (`marketId`) or a series (`seriesId`): comments newest first with their replies. A commenter's side shows only when their forecaster profile is public.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_conversions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_conversions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/conversions | Your bucket-set conversions (split, merge, convert), newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_following**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_following) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/following | The forecasters you follow.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_forecasts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_forecasts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecasts | Your forecasts (a probability you attached to a market, money or not) and how the markets came out.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_makers**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_makers) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/makers | Your prediction maker programme applications and their status (applied, active, suspended, revoked) and rebate.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_markets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_markets) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/markets | Prediction markets, filtered by a book key, an index slug, an underlying kind (index | book_key | category_pair), a status (open | closed | settled | all), an expiry day or text. Each with its strike, expiry, best Yes bid and ask, last price, volume, open interest and probability.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/orders | Your prediction orders, newest first: working, done or all; practice, live or all.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_positions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_positions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/positions | Your prediction positions (practice, live or all; open, settled or all): side, contracts, cost, value at the last price, fees, realised P&L, payout or refund once settled, and the share card if you made one.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_proposal_listed**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_proposal_listed) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/proposals/listed | The markets you proposed that are listed: volume, status and what the creator share has earned you.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_prediction_proposals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_prediction_proposals) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/proposals | Your market proposals and where each stands (pending, listed, rejected, withdrawn).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_price_widgets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_price_widgets) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets | Your price widgets, newest first, each with its iframe and script-tag snippets. `meta.codes` lists your affiliate codes a widget can link through; `meta.max` is the cap on live widgets.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_candles**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_candles) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/candles | OHLCV candles (1m, 5m, 15m, 1h, 4h, 1d) from the trades on a book key.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_events**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_events) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/events | Your order event log after a sequence number (new, leg, fill, replaced, cancelled, expired, done): a replayable drop copy.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_links**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_links) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/links | Your linked orders (brackets, OCO pairs, trailing stops, position exits): live (default) or done.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/orders | Your orders: open (default) or done, optionally on one book key, or only those booked to one sub-account (`subaccountId`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_quotes**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_quotes) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/quotes | Your live quotes.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_rewards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_rewards) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/rewards | Your daily market-maker liquidity rewards (paid in CBX).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_pro_tape**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_pro_tape) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/pro/tape | The latest trades on a book key, newest first, with which side made and which took.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_leaves**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_proof_of_reserve_leaves) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/proof-of-reserves/{day}/leaves | One day's leaf hashes in tree order, a page at a time: enough to recount the units and rebuild the root yourself. Leaves are salted, so they name no owner.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_recurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_recurring) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/recurring | Your recurring buys: key, schedule, price cap, payment, auto-lend, next run.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_recurring_runs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_recurring_runs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id}/runs | One recurring buy's runs: filled, skipped (with the reason, e.g. the price was over the cap) or failed.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_recurring_runs2**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_recurring_runs2) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/recurring-runs | Every run of all your recurring buys, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_rfq**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_rfq) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/rfq | Your quote requests, newest first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_rfq_inbox**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_rfq_inbox) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/inbox | Open quote requests you were sent (as a dealer, DMM, LP or holder), with your live answer.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_rfq_open**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_rfq_open) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/rfq/open | Open quote requests on one key (all-to-all: anyone with ID may answer). Anonymous.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_set_goal_baskets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_set_goal_baskets) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id}/baskets | A set goal basket bids, newest first, each with its budget, what it committed and spent, and every leg (piece, book key, limit, pro order id, status).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_set_goal_sets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_set_goal_sets) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/sets | Sets to start a goal on. With q (2+ characters): a word-start search of every catalogue set. Without: the sets you hold pieces of in the vault, then the collections you are building. Each with your live goal on it, if any.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_set_goals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_set_goals) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals | Your set goals: the set, the target a piece must meet, pieces held of the total (from your vault), and the working basket bid.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_auto_invest**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_auto_invest) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/auto-invest | Your recurring auto-invest plans: amount, weekly or monthly, the series and weights or the basket, what rolled over (budget too small for a whole share; your money stays at the venue), the next run.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_auto_invest_runs**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_auto_invest_runs) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/auto-invest/{id}/runs | A plan’s runs, newest first: the budget, what each series bought, what was spent and what rolled over; rebalances too.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_baskets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_baskets) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/baskets | Theme baskets: curated model portfolios of Vault Shares series with target weights. You buy and own every share directly in your own name; a basket is never a fund.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_cards**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_cards) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards | Your share cards (not taken down), each as the public sees it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_drops**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_drops) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/drops | The Vault Shares drops calendar: announced and open offerings, soonest first, each with its phase (announced, early_access, requests, open), the countdown, the allocation (fcfs, pro_rata or lottery with a per-person cap), your Crossly One early-access time (it moves when you may ask, never the price) and whether you asked to be told.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_gifts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_gifts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/gifts | Your share gifts, sent and received, newest first, with where each stands (offered, instructed, settled, declined, cancelled, expired, rejected).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_orders**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_orders) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/orders | Your share-book orders, newest first; `seriesId` and `open` narrow it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_series**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_series) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series | Vault Shares series: open offerings first, then trading, then the rest. `status` and `bookKey` narrow it. Each with its terms, progress, last share price and implied item value (display only, never a mark), and your holding.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_sery_appraisals**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_sery_appraisals) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/appraisals | The item’s appraisal history: the offering valuation (sealed valuation statement), independent and insurance appraisals, and any buyout or exit price.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_sery_tape**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_sery_tape) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/tape | The latest fills on a share book, newest first, with which side made the market.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_sery_updates**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_sery_updates) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/series/{id}/updates | The item’s story, provenance, condition reports, appraisals and exhibitions, newest first. Holders also see holders-only updates.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_subscriptions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_subscriptions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/subscriptions | Your offering subscriptions, newest first, with where each stands (pending, escrowed, allocated, refunded).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_share_world**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_share_world) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/shares/world | The world vault home’s fractional tiles: each item you own a share of, with \"you own 3.2%\".
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_supply_declared_values**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_supply_declared_values) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/declared-values | What each item's declared value starts at, by your insurance rule (market, cost or price). `inventoryItemIds` comma-separated.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_supply_deposits**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_supply_deposits) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/deposits | Your units sent to the vault, newest first, each with its state and its key's Instant Exit line (\"Instant Exit now: $X (N% of mark)\" or \"No market yet\"). Filter with `inventoryItemIds` (comma-separated).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_supply_eligibility**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_supply_eligibility) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/eligibility | Can these inventory items (`inventoryItemIds`, comma-separated, or every item linked to `listingId`) be sent to the vault now? Each says why not.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_supply_invites**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_supply_invites) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/invites | Market Open invites to you as a dealer: keys Crossly is opening and asks you to quote.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_supply_shipments**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_supply_shipments) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/supply/shipments | Your shipments into the vault, newest first: method, label or drop-off code, tracking and how many units were received.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_valuation_statements**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_valuation_statements) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/valuation-statements | The insurance and appraisal statements you issued, newest first: purpose, date, units, total at the marks, hash and verify link.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_vault_suggestions**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_vault_suggestions) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/vault-suggestions | What to send to the vault: your inventory items whose book key has a liquid vault market (tier A, B or C), best first. Each has a score (0 to 100) and its reasons, the market (tier, mark and its quality, best bid, best vault ask, spread), Instant Exit now, your list price, days listed without selling, and the vault fee. `meta.summary` counts what was left out and why. Inform only; send with POST /api/v1/vault-markets/supply/send.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_watchlist**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_watchlist) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/watchlist | Your watched book keys with mark, 24 h and 7 d change, and the last 30 daily marks (sparkline).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_widget_tokens**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_widget_tokens) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/widget/tokens | Your live home-screen widget tokens, one per phone: id, name (the phone), public prefix, created and last used. The secret is never returned again.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_world_cohosts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_world_cohosts) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/cohosts | Market Open launch shows a verified creator co-hosts that are on now or opening soon: the key, its phase, the show, the creator (named only while their vault profile is public), their room link and, when the creator is in the creator programme, the disclosure that they may earn a share of the fee Crossly keeps. Never a prompt to trade.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_world_creator_rooms**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_world_creator_rooms) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/creator-rooms | Verified creators whose public vault shelf is a world room: title, the creator, the room link and the disclosure. Rooms show each market share of the shelf, never amounts.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_market_world_market_open**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_market_world_market_open) | **GET** /v1/vault-markets/world/market-open | Market Open events in the Crossly World Commons: keys opening soon and those that opened in the last few hours, live first then soonest, each with its board line, units on the shelf, dealers quoting and the launch show when there is one. Never a prompt to trade.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_receipt_token_wallets**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_receipt_token_wallets) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens/wallets | Your verified Solana wallets and whether each is on the receipt-token allow-list (receipt tokens move only between allow-listed wallets). meta.state is the gate.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_receipt_tokens**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_receipt_tokens) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipt-tokens | Your vault units with their warehouse receipt and receipt-token state (not exported, waiting for a signature, in a wallet, frozen, redeemed), whether each can be exported now and why not. meta.state is the gate (open, missing envs, program id, authority, terms version, cluster); meta.history is your last 50 exports.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_receipts**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_receipts) | **GET** /v1/vault/receipts | Your warehouse receipts (documents of title for your vault units), live first. meta.receipts says whether receipts are registered (VAULT_WAREHOUSE_ISSUER) and their terms version.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**list_vault_unit_list**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#list_vault_unit_list) | **GET** /v1/vault/unit-list | The published unit list (bar-list equivalent): every unit on a vault shelf with its warehouse receipt number (when receipts are registered), book key, shelf location code, insured value, status, pledged / on-loan flags and a per-receipt hashed holder (never a name or id). Filter with `bookKey`; pages with `page` / `limit`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_alert_pref**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_alert_pref) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/alert-prefs | Change vault alert settings: `matrix` ({ kind: { channel: true|false } }, merged onto what is set), `quietHours` ({ start: \"HH:MM\", end: \"HH:MM\", tz } or null to clear) and `telegram` (the Telegram master switch). Locked switches are ignored. Returns the new settings.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_auto_buy**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_auto_buy) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/auto-buy | Set up or change Auto-buy with payouts: `shareBps` (1–50%) and the whole want list in order, each with an optional `maxPriceCents` (fee included).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_block_allocation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_block_allocation) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/blocks/{orderId}/allocation | Post-trade allocation: set or replace the plan for a buy you placed for one of the entity's sub-accounts (that sub-account leads). Refused once any share has moved.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_channel**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_channel) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/channels | Change the switches: `telegramAlerts`, `telegramDigest`, `pushCapPerDay` (0 to 20). Margin calls and recalls are never capped. Returns the new view.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_dealer_band**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_dealer_band) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/bands | Commit to an Instant Exit band on a key: a standing bid for `units` kept at `bandBps` of the mark, never above `maxPriceCents`, re-quoted as the mark moves.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_dealer_pool**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_dealer_pool) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/dealer/pools/{id} | Change a curve pool: spot, step, fee, levels, units, buy budget, floor, ceiling, or pause / resume. Re-quoted at once.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_entity_member**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_entity_member) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/members/{memberId} | Admin: change a member's role, limits or sub-accounts. The last admin cannot be demoted.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_entity_subaccount_limit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_entity_subaccount_limit) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/subaccounts/{subId}/limits | Admin: set a sub-account's limits (per-order notional, units per key, gross, net, leverage), held to the entity's caps. `limits: null` clears them.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_entity_usd_election**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_entity_usd_election) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/entities/{id}/usd-election | Admin: elect USD for the entity (`usdOnly`). Its sub-accounts then post collateral in USD only and pay every fee in USD, and any CBX reward paid to one of them is converted to USD by the custodian in that account. Audited; it can be turned off again.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_follow_copy_watch**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_follow_copy_watch) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/follows/copy-watch/{handle} | Copy-watch a collector who shares bids and asks: you get an alert when they rest a new one. It never places an order for you.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_follow_sharing**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_follow_sharing) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/follows/sharing | Turn sharing switches on or off (`isPublic`, `showBuys`, `deposits`, `sales`, `hallLoans`, `orders`, `leaderboard`). A switch shows only what happens after it goes on; all but the profile itself need the profile public.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_home_unit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_home_unit) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/home/units | Show exactly these vault units in your Crossly World home: { units: [{ vaultItemId, visitors }] }. Units left out come out of their cases; units already standing stay where they are; new ones go into a free display-case or shelf slot, else a glass case of their own along a wall. visitors: true lets people who may enter your home see that unit (default false: only you). Returns placed, unplaced (no free case: the home is full), refused (with why) and the desk. Display only: nothing moves.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_index**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_index) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/indexes/{slug} | Rename, re-weight, share or change the keys of an index of your own; its level carries on.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_item_sell_from**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_item_sell_from) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/items/{inventoryItemId}/sell-from | Set which unit a sale of this item ships first: `vault_first`, `warehouse_first` or `cheapest` (to ship); null = your default. Quantities stay the same on every channel: only who ships changes.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_lending_election**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_lending_election) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/lending/election | Change your lender election (applies to loans opened after the change). Needs lending.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_mm_mmp**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_mm_mmp) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/mm/mmp | Set market-maker protection: N units or $X filled on your quotes within T seconds pulls every quote and freezes quoting for a set time.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_mode**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_mode) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/mode | Choose your Vault Markets mode: `mode` simple or pro. Remembered for you on the web and in the app. A display preference only: it places nothing and moves no money.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_offer_alert**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_offer_alert) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/offers/alert | Set (or clear with null) the bid price that should always notify you on a key you hold, and mute or unmute offer notices on it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_option_auto_close**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_option_auto_close) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/options/auto-close | Set your auto-close rule: `enabled`, `minutesBefore` (30 to 10080), `onlyItm`, `maxSlippageBps` (0 to 2000).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_paper_leaderboard**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_paper_leaderboard) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/paper/leaderboard | Join or leave the weekly paper leaderboard. Joining needs a display name (2 to 24 letters, numbers, spaces, dots, dashes or underscores), never your real name. Returns `{ optIn, displayName }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_partner_me**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_partner_me) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/partners/me | Your drop-off partnership's finder profile: postal code, opening hours, services and whether you are listed.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_position_exit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_position_exit) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/positions/{id}/exits | Set a position's take-profit / stop-loss: a long is closed, a short covered, when the last trade reaches one. Both null clears them.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_prediction_comment**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_prediction_comment) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/comments/{id} | Edit your comment.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_prediction_forecaster**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_prediction_forecaster) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/forecaster | Go public under a display name (`public: true`), rename, or go private (`public: false`: nothing is shown again).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_prediction_limit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_prediction_limit) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/predictions/limits | Set your daily loss limit (`dailyLossLimitCents`, null = none) or exclude yourself (`excludeDays`, `excludedUntil` or \"forever\"). Tighter takes effect at once; looser waits 24 hours.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_price_widget**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_price_widget) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/price-widgets/{id} | Change a widget: name, range, layout, theme, affiliate code (null for none) or allowed sites. The market or index it shows never changes: make a new widget for another.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_profile**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_profile) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/profile | Turn your public vault profile on or off (`isPublic`) and whether followers see your new buys (`showBuys`). It shows weights by market, never dollars.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_recurring**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_recurring) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/recurring/{id} | Change a recurring buy's schedule, caps, cards or auto-lend, or pause / resume it.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_set_goal**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_set_goal) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/set-goals/{id} | Change what a piece must be to count (condition, grade mode, grade). Refused while a basket bid works: cancel it first.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_setting**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_setting) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/settings | Change your vault defaults; send only the fields you change.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_share_card**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_share_card) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/share-cards/{id} | Show or hide the dollar amounts on one of your share cards (`showAmounts`).
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_supply_deposit**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_supply_deposit) | **PATCH** /v1/vault-markets/supply/deposits/{id} | Change a deposit's toggles: `autoListBps` (list at that share of the mark once stored; null = do not) and `lendWhenIdle`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_supply_round_up**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_supply_round_up) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/supply/round-up | Set the round-up: `shareBps` (1%-50%) of each payout released to you adds to a budget; when it covers the cheapest vault unit on `bookKey`, one unit is bought with your saved card, never above the budget.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_tod_beneficiary**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_tod_beneficiary) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/tod/beneficiaries | Replace your beneficiary list (keep an id to edit one): full name, contact (email or @username), relationship, and share in basis points; shares add up to 10000. Returns `{ beneficiaries, removed }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_tod_designation**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_tod_designation) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/tod/designations | Leave one of your units to one beneficiary (beneficiaryId null clears it; the unit then follows the shares). Returns `{ vaultItemId, beneficiaryId }`.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_world_room_vault_gate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_world_room_vault_gate) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/world/rooms/{id}/vault-gate | Set the vault-holder door on your own room (its collection rooms follow): visitors must hold minValueCents (cents, at reliable A/B marks) and/or minUnits units in the Crossly vault, mode any (either) or all (both), optionally only one product (scope kind product, ref the catalogue product id), bullion family (bullion, the family slug) or category (category, the slug). Send null to open it again. Returns `{ gate }`. Moves no money.
*Crossly::VaultMarketsApi* | [**update_vault_market_world_space_vault_gate**](docs/VaultMarketsApi.md#update_vault_market_world_space_vault_gate) | **PUT** /v1/vault-markets/world/spaces/{slug}/vault-gate | Set the vault-holder door on a Space you host: the same rule as a room (minValueCents at reliable marks and/or minUnits, mode, optional scope). Send null to open it again. Returns `{ gate }`. Moves no money.
*Crossly::WalletApi* | [**delete_wallet_default**](docs/WalletApi.md#delete_wallet_default) | **DELETE** /v1/wallet/defaults | Clear a default (the purpose falls back to your only wallet, or none) or one override (`refKind` + `refKey`).
*Crossly::WalletApi* | [**list_wallet_defaults**](docs/WalletApi.md#list_wallet_defaults) | **GET** /v1/wallet/defaults | Your default wallet per purpose (royalties, deploys, drops, game rewards, staking, CBX withdrawals), with per-collection and per-game overrides and what each resolves to today.
*Crossly::WalletApi* | [**update_wallet_default**](docs/WalletApi.md#update_wallet_default) | **PUT** /v1/wallet/defaults | Set the default wallet for a purpose, or an override for one collection (`ref: { kind: \"collection\", chain, contract }`) or game (`ref: { kind: \"game\", experienceId }`). The wallet must be yours, verified, and on a network the purpose accepts.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_alert**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_alert) | **POST** /v1/web3/alerts | Set a price alert on a token, or on a whole collection without tokenId: you are notified when one is listed at or below `price` (the chain's native coin). Setting it again moves the price.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_auction**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_auction) | **POST** /v1/web3/auctions | Start an English auction on a token in your linked wallet: `reserve` is the opening bid in the chain's native coin, `hours` its length (1–720). Bids must rise 5%; a late bid extends the end by 10 minutes.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_auction_cancel**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_auction_cancel) | **POST** /v1/web3/auctions/{id}/cancel | Call off your auction while it has no bids.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_creator_claim**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_creator_claim) | **POST** /v1/web3/creator/claims | Claim an EVM collection as its creator: its owner() must be one of your verified wallets. Returns `{ chain, contract }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_crosslist**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_crosslist) | **POST** /v1/web3/crosslist | Submit signed Seaport listings for several marketplaces at once (crossly, opensea); results per marketplace.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_drop_remind**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_drop_remind) | **POST** /v1/web3/drops/{id}/remind | Turn the 15-minute reminder for a drop on, or off if it already is. Returns `{ remindMe }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_favorite**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_favorite) | **POST** /v1/web3/favorites | Favourite an NFT, or unfavourite it if it already is. Returns `{ favorited }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches | Draft a launchpad collection: name, symbol, supply, mint price (wei of the chain’s native currency), schedule, optional holder phase and royalty. Crossly takes 5% of each mint inside the contract.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch_airdrop**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch_airdrop) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/airdrop | Plan an airdrop (one free NFT each, up to 500) to `addresses` and/or the holders of `holdersOf`. Returns the transactions for the owner wallet; no payment moves.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch_art**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch_art) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/art | Append per-token art (image URL, name, traits), up to the supply, before the reveal. Returns `{ count }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch_confirm_deploy**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch_confirm_deploy) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/confirm-deploy | Confirm a sent deploy transaction: the contract must be the Crossly launch contract with this draft’s settings; it then goes live.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch_follow_creator**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch_follow_creator) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/follow-creator | Follow the creator of a launch: their launches are announced to you, and again when each opens. Returns `{ following }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_launch_reveal**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_launch_reveal) | **POST** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/reveal | Reveal the art: one random offset maps every token to its asset for good. Returns `{ revealedAt }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_order**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_order) | **POST** /v1/web3/orders | Place a signed Seaport 1.6 order (listing, offer or collection offer) in the Crossly book.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_order_cancel**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_order_cancel) | **POST** /v1/web3/orders/{chain}/{hash}/cancel | Take one of your orders out of the Crossly book.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_order_fulfillment_batch**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_order_fulfillment_batch) | **POST** /v1/web3/orders/fulfillment-batch | Fulfilments for sweeping several listings in one Seaport transaction; unfillable ones are skipped with a reason.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_phygital**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_phygital) | **POST** /v1/web3/phygitals | Make an NFT you hold redeemable for one of your physical inventory items.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_phygital_redeem**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_phygital_redeem) | **POST** /v1/web3/phygitals/{id}/redeem | Redeem a phygital you hold (checked on-chain) for its physical item, with a shipping address. Once per phygital.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_phygital_redemption_ship**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_phygital_redemption_ship) | **POST** /v1/web3/phygitals/redemptions/{id}/ship | Mark a redemption shipped with its carrier and tracking number; the redeemer is notified.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_rarible_draft**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_rarible_draft) | **POST** /v1/web3/rarible/draft | Draft a Rarible V2 listing for an NFT in your linked wallet: the EIP-712 order to sign and whether Rarible's transfer proxy is approved.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_rarible_listing**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_rarible_listing) | **POST** /v1/web3/rarible/listings | Submit a signed Rarible V2 listing: the signature is verified, then the order is posted to Rarible and recorded.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_list_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_list_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/{marketplace}/list-tx | Build an unsigned listing transaction on the Crossly book, Tensor or Magic Eden for a Solana NFT in your linked wallet.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_listing**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_listing) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/{marketplace}/listings | Record a Crossly-book, Tensor or Magic Eden listing your wallet sent (read back from the chain first).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_offer**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_offer) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/offers | Record a sent bid; it is recorded only once its trade state exists on chain.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_offer_accept_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_offer_accept_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/accept-tx | Build the sale into a bid from your linked wallet that holds the NFT (sell + execute_sale, one transaction).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_offer_cancel_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_offer_cancel_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/cancel-tx | Build the cancel of your bid: the SOL your other bids do not need comes back.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_offer_confirm**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_offer_confirm) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/offers/{hash}/confirm | Apply a sent accept or cancel once the chain shows the bid closed.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_offer_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_offer_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/offers/tx | Build a bid on a Solana NFT on Crossly's book for your linked wallet to sign; the SOL goes into your Auction House escrow.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_order_buy_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_order_buy_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/buy-tx | Build the unsigned transaction that buys a Solana listing (price capped at the listed price).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_order_confirm**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_order_confirm) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/confirm | Apply a sent Solana buy or delist to the book (the transaction is read back from the chain).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_order_delist_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_order_delist_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/orders/{hash}/delist-tx | Build the unsigned transaction that takes your Solana listing down.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_accept_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_accept_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/accept-tx | Build the transaction that sells your NFT into a Tensor bid, never below `minLamports`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_cancel_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_cancel_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/cancel-tx | Build the transaction that cancels your Tensor bid.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_confirm**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_confirm) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/confirm | After your wallet’s Tensor transaction confirms: `action` place, cancel or accept, with its `signature` and the `wallet` that signed. Checked on chain, then recorded in your Activity. Accept needs listings:write; place and cancel need buyer:offers:write.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_place_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_solana_tensor_bid_place_tx) | **POST** /v1/web3/solana/tensor-bids/place-tx | Build a Tensor collection bid (or trait bid with `trait`) for your Solana wallet to sign. Price in lamports.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_staking_stake**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_staking_stake) | **POST** /v1/web3/staking/stake | Lock earned CBX (`baseUnits`, a string) into the best tier it reaches. 409 while a stake or unstake is in progress.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_staking_unstake**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_staking_unstake) | **POST** /v1/web3/staking/unstake | Start unstaking: the earn boost stops now, the tokens release when the tier’s cooldown ends. Returns `{ unlocksAt, cooldownDays }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_store_wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_store_wallet) | **POST** /v1/web3/store-wallets | Link one of your verified wallets to the store so it sells NFTs from it and is paid into it.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_store_wallet_challenge**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_store_wallet_challenge) | **POST** /v1/web3/store-wallets/challenge | Start adding a selling wallet to the store: the message the wallet signs.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_store_wallet_import**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_store_wallet_import) | **POST** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId}/import | Add picked NFTs (`tokens: [{ chain, contract, tokenId }]`) from a store wallet to inventory.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_store_wallet_sync**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_store_wallet_sync) | **POST** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId}/sync | Sync a store wallet: refresh its NFTs in inventory; new ones are added when the store's link auto-imports.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_store_wallet_verify**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_store_wallet_verify) | **POST** /v1/web3/store-wallets/verify | Finish adding a selling wallet: verify its signature and link it to the store.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_token_refresh**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_token_refresh) | **POST** /v1/web3/tokens/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId}/refresh | Re-read a token's metadata from the chain's provider (name, traits, image — a new image is copied to Crossly's CDN). Once per token per 10 minutes.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_transaction**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_transaction) | **POST** /v1/web3/transactions | Apply a Seaport transaction's fills and cancels to the Crossly book (read from the receipt).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_wallet_challenge**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_wallet_challenge) | **POST** /v1/web3/wallets/challenge | Start linking a wallet: returns the message the wallet must sign.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_wallet_import**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_wallet_import) | **POST** /v1/web3/wallets/{id}/import | Add picked NFTs (`tokens`: chain, contract, tokenId; up to 200) from a linked wallet to inventory. Tokens the wallet does not hold are counted in `notInWallet`, never added.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_wallet_sync**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_wallet_sync) | **POST** /v1/web3/wallets/{id}/sync | Import a linked wallet's NFTs into inventory; archives ones that have left the wallet.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**create_web3_wallet_verify**](docs/Web3Api.md#create_web3_wallet_verify) | **POST** /v1/web3/wallets/verify | Finish linking a wallet with its signature over the challenge message.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_alert**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_alert) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/alerts/{id} | Delete a price alert.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_holder_perk**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_holder_perk) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/holder-perks/{id} | DEPRECATED: use /api/v1/discounts/holders/:id. Remove a Collection holders deal. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_launch**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_launch) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/launches/{id} | Delete a launch draft that never went live. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_launch_follow_creator**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_launch_follow_creator) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/follow-creator | Stop following the creator of a launch. Returns `{ following }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_launch_perk**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_launch_perk) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/perks | Remove every collection perk of a launch. Returns `{ ok }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_marketplace_key**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_marketplace_key) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys/{marketplace} | Remove your own key for a marketplace; Crossly's key is used again.
