*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_store_wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_store_wallet) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId} | Stop the store selling from a wallet (the wallet and its other stores are unaffected).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**delete_web3_wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#delete_web3_wallet) | **DELETE** /v1/web3/wallets/{id} | Unlink a wallet. Its NFTs stay in inventory but stop syncing.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_auction**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_auction) | **GET** /v1/web3/auctions/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | A token's live (or latest) auction: reserve, end, top bid, bid count and the minimum next bid.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_balance**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_balance) | **GET** /v1/web3/balances | Every CBX you have, in one read, with the three balances kept apart.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_browse**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_browse) | **GET** /v1/web3/browse | The Web3 directory's categories and chains, with how many collections each holds.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract} | A collection: metadata and Crossly stats (floor, listed, volume, best collection offer).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection_depth**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection_depth) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/depth | A collection's Crossly order book by price level — asks cheapest first, bids dearest first, with running totals — and the floor of every trait value.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection_insight**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection_insight) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/insights | On-chain insights for a collection: holders, trait counts across the whole collection, recent transfers and a 30-day activity series.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection_item**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection_item) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/items | A collection's NFTs: filter by traits (repeat trait=Type:Value; OR within a type, AND across), search by name or token id, sort by id, rarity or Crossly price. Whole-collection once indexed.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection_trait**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection_trait) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/traits | Every trait type and value in a collection, with how many tokens have each.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collection_trait_token**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collection_trait_token) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/trait-tokens | Token ids in a collection with one trait — what a trait offer commits to.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_collector**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_collector) | **GET** /v1/web3/collector | Your collector perks: how many different NFTs you bought through Crossly, your Collector pin tier, relist rewards paid, and each NFT you still hold with the Bought on Crossly mark.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_creator**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_creator) | **GET** /v1/web3/creator/{chain}/{contract} | A collection’s creator dashboard: sales by marketplace and day, estimated royalties, holders, and for a launch the mint revenue and what is waiting to be withdrawn (wei of the chain’s native currency).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_crosslist_quote**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_crosslist_quote) | **GET** /v1/web3/crosslist/quote | Each marketplace's terms for listing a token: Crossly fee, OpenSea required fees and zone, Tensor availability.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_featured**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_featured) | **GET** /v1/web3/featured | The NFT home's featured collections: chosen by Crossly, else the hottest.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_inventory**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_inventory) | **GET** /v1/web3/inventory/{itemId} | The NFT behind an inventory item: its token, wallet, and open listings on every marketplace (null if it is not an NFT).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_launch_allowlist_root_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_launch_allowlist_root_tx) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/allowlist/root-tx | The setAllowlistRoot transaction for the allowlist Crossly holds (EVM, live): `{ chain, from, to, data }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_launch_deploy_tx**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_launch_deploy_tx) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/deploy-tx | The contract-creation transaction for the draft: send it from the owner wallet, then confirm it. Returns `{ chain, from, data }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_launch_stat**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_launch_stat) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/stats | A launch’s phase timeline, mint progress, proceeds (gross, Crossly’s cut, yours; airdrops excluded), allowlist and art counts, and recent mints.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_launch_tool**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_launch_tool) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/tools | A launch’s creator tools: allowlist size and root (and whether the contract has it yet), uploaded art and reveal, supply minted.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_order_fulfillment**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_order_fulfillment) | **GET** /v1/web3/orders/{chain}/{hash}/fulfillment | The signed order a taker passes to Seaport, after re-checking it can still fill.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_phygital**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_phygital) | **GET** /v1/web3/phygitals/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | A token's phygital: the physical item it can be redeemed for, and whether it has been.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_phygital2**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_phygital2) | **GET** /v1/web3/phygitals | Phygitals you linked (with each redemption and its shipping address) and ones you redeemed.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_portfolio**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_portfolio) | **GET** /v1/web3/portfolio | Your NFTs with value (Crossly floor, else recent sales) and cost (what a linked wallet paid), realised sales, and USD totals at each day’s coin price.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_portfolio_tax**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_portfolio_tax) | **GET** /v1/web3/portfolio/tax | One calendar year of NFT disposals from your linked wallets as CSV text: acquired, sold, proceeds, cost basis, gain (coin and USD).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_profile**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_profile) | **GET** /v1/web3/profiles/{address} | A wallet's public profile: NFTs held, open Crossly listings, and activity.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_resolve**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_resolve) | **GET** /v1/web3/resolve/{chain}/{slug} | A collection's chain and contract from its readable URL slug (e.g. eth / boredapeyachtclub).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_solana_collection_tensor_bid**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_solana_collection_tensor_bid) | **GET** /v1/web3/solana/collections/{collection}/tensor-bids | A Solana collection’s Tensor bids (collection-wide and by trait), best first; price in lamports. Needs your Tensor key.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_staking**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_staking) | **GET** /v1/web3/staking | Your CBX stake: what is locked, its tier, the tier ladder and your past positions.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_store_wallet_holding**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_store_wallet_holding) | **GET** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId}/holdings | What a store wallet holds (chain + contract + token id), each marked in-inventory or not.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_store_wallet_nft**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_store_wallet_nft) | **GET** /v1/web3/store-wallets/nfts | The store's Crossly NFTs: plots (deeds and leases), streets its owner founded and vault receipt tokens in its wallets, with the wallets a plot purchase and a receipt export use.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_token**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_token) | **GET** /v1/web3/tokens/{chain}/{contract}/{tokenId} | One NFT: metadata, traits, owner, open listings and offers, activity.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**get_web3_wallet_holding**](docs/Web3Api.md#get_web3_wallet_holding) | **GET** /v1/web3/wallets/{id}/holdings | What a linked wallet holds right now, each NFT marked `inInventory` or not. Reads the chain; writes nothing.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_activity**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_activity) | **GET** /v1/web3/activity | Listings, offers, sales and cancels in the Crossly book, newest first.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_alerts**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_alerts) | **GET** /v1/web3/alerts | Your NFT price alerts, with the cheapest open Crossly listing each one covers now.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_approvals**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_approvals) | **GET** /v1/web3/approvals | Your collections the OpenSea conduit is approved to move, per linked wallet.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_auctions**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_auctions) | **GET** /v1/web3/auctions | Live English auctions on the Crossly book, ending soonest first, each with its top bid and the least the next bid can be.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_browse_collections**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_browse_collections) | **GET** /v1/web3/browse/collections | Browse NFT collections by chain, category and name; sort hot (Crossly sales, listings, favourites), new, volume, floor or name.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_browse_tokens**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_browse_tokens) | **GET** /v1/web3/browse/tokens | Hot NFTs: what is for sale on Crossly first, then tokens from the hottest collections.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_bundles**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_bundles) | **GET** /v1/web3/bundles | Open bundle listings (2–30 NFTs for one price), newest first, by chain or by a contract any item is from.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_collection_history**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_collection_history) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections/{chain}/{contract}/history | Daily floor, sales, volume, holders and listed count for a collection, oldest first (up to 365 days).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_collections**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_collections) | **GET** /v1/web3/collections | Collections ranked by 30-day Crossly volume, with floor and listed count.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_creator_collections**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_creator_collections) | **GET** /v1/web3/creator/collections | Collections you are the creator of: your launches, and contracts you claimed by owning them.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_drops**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_drops) | **GET** /v1/web3/drops | Upcoming and live NFT drops (curated mints on the creator's own page), soonest first, with whether you asked to be reminded.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_holder_perks**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_holder_perks) | **GET** /v1/web3/holder-perks | DEPRECATED: use /api/v1/discounts/holders. Your Collection holders deals in the old holder-perk shape, best first.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_launch_perks**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_launch_perks) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/perks | The perks holders of this launch’s collection get (percent off your shop, early drop access, a World item, a loyalty tier, live early entry, an event ticket).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_launches**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_launches) | **GET** /v1/web3/launches | Your launchpad collections (drafts and live), newest first, with minted count and phase.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_listings**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_listings) | **GET** /v1/web3/listings | Open NFT listings in the Crossly book — the cheapest per token.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_marketplace_keys**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_marketplace_keys) | **GET** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys | Your own marketplace API keys (last four characters only) and whether Crossly's key covers each marketplace.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_nfts**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_nfts) | **GET** /v1/web3/nfts | List the NFTs in your inventory, imported from your linked wallets.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_orders**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_orders) | **GET** /v1/web3/orders | Your orders in the Crossly book (listings and offers), any status. With X-Storefront-Id: that store's listings (offers always).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_purchases**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_purchases) | **GET** /v1/web3/purchases | NFTs your linked wallets bought — on Crossly or any marketplace the chain feed saw — newest first, with price and whether you still hold each.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_rankings**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_rankings) | **GET** /v1/web3/rankings | Collections by sales volume over 24h, 7d or 30d, in one coin: the ETH chains together, or Polygon / Solana on their own.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_reserved**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_reserved) | **GET** /v1/web3/reserved | Private listings reserved for one of your linked wallets — only you can see or buy them.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_sales**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_sales) | **GET** /v1/web3/sales | The latest NFT sales across the directory, from the chain (Seaport and Blur) and the Crossly book, newest first; page with `before`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_search**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_search) | **GET** /v1/web3/search | Find collections by contract address (every chain) or by name.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_search_tokens**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_search_tokens) | **GET** /v1/web3/search/tokens | Find tokens by collection name and number: 'pudgy 42', 'Bored Ape #1234'.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_store_wallet_balances**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_store_wallet_balances) | **GET** /v1/web3/store-wallets/balances | The native-coin balance (SOL, ETH, POL, …) of each of the store's selling wallets, read on chain.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_store_wallet_marketplaces**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_store_wallet_marketplaces) | **GET** /v1/web3/store-wallets/marketplaces | Each NFT marketplace's status for this store: Live only with a usable API key AND a verified store wallet of its network switched on for it. `keyMode` is `crossly` (Crossly's key, nothing to set up) or `own_key` (connect your own key).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_store_wallets**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_store_wallets) | **GET** /v1/web3/store-wallets | List the store's selling wallets (X-Storefront-Id; default store when omitted).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_wallets**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_wallets) | **GET** /v1/web3/wallets | List your verified, linked wallets (EVM and Solana).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**list_web3_watchlist**](docs/Web3Api.md#list_web3_watchlist) | **GET** /v1/web3/watchlist | Your favourited NFTs, with the cheapest open Crossly listing and the last sale of each.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_holder_perk**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_holder_perk) | **PUT** /v1/web3/holder-perks | DEPRECATED: use /api/v1/discounts/holders. Set the percent off (100–5000 bps) holders of a collection get in your store (the key’s store, else the default); one per collection.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_launch**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_launch) | **PATCH** /v1/web3/launches/{id} | Edit a launch draft (or a failed deploy) before it deploys: any creation field except chain and owner. The schedule is re-checked; 409 once live.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_launch_allowlist**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_launch_allowlist) | **PUT** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/allowlist | Replace the early-phase allowlist (up to 10,000 wallets). EVM live launches get back `rootTx`, the setAllowlistRoot transaction for the owner wallet.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_launch_perk**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_launch_perk) | **PUT** /v1/web3/launches/{id}/perks | Replace a launch’s collection perks: `{ perks: [{ config: { type, ... }, active }] }`, one per type; a type left out is removed.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_marketplace_key**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_marketplace_key) | **PUT** /v1/web3/marketplace-keys/{marketplace} | Save your own API key for a marketplace (checked against the marketplace first where it can be); stored encrypted.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_store_wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_store_wallet) | **PATCH** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId} | Turn on or off whether syncing this store wallet adds new NFTs to the store’s inventory (X-Storefront-Id).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_store_wallet_default**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_store_wallet_default) | **PUT** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId}/default | Make a linked wallet the store's default selling wallet on its network (EVM or Solana).
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_store_wallet_marketplace**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_store_wallet_marketplace) | **PUT** /v1/web3/store-wallets/{walletId}/marketplaces/{marketplace} | Switch one NFT marketplace (opensea, rarible on EVM; magic_eden, tensor on Solana) on or off for a store wallet: `{ enabled }`.
*Crossly::Web3Api* | [**update_web3_wallet**](docs/Web3Api.md#update_web3_wallet) | **PATCH** /v1/web3/wallets/{id} | Change a linked wallet: `autoImport` (a sync adds new NFTs to inventory; off, it only refreshes the ones there) and `label`.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**create_webhook**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#create_webhook) | **POST** /v1/webhooks | Register a webhook of any type (kind: json|slack|discord|teams|telegram|google_chat|mattermost|zapier; default json). eventDelivery false = automation only (notify.* steps). Returns the signing secret once (json).
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**create_webhook_delivery_redeliver**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#create_webhook_delivery_redeliver) | **POST** /v1/webhooks/{id}/deliveries/{deliveryId}/redeliver | Redeliver one past delivery: a new delivery (new Crossly-Delivery-Id) of the same event id and payload.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**create_webhook_rotate_secret**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#create_webhook_rotate_secret) | **POST** /v1/webhooks/{id}/rotate-secret | Rotate the signing secret of a webhook, keeping its id, events and delivery history.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**create_webhook_test**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#create_webhook_test) | **POST** /v1/webhooks/{id}/test | Fire a test to one webhook: test.ping (json/zapier) or a test message (chat types), or a sample of any event (body {\"event\":\"order.created\"}, payload marked test: true).
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**delete_webhook**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#delete_webhook) | **DELETE** /v1/webhooks/{id} | Delete a webhook endpoint.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**get_webhook_stream**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#get_webhook_stream) | **GET** /v1/webhooks/stream | Stream this account's webhook events as they happen (SSE).
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**list_webhook_deliveries**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#list_webhook_deliveries) | **GET** /v1/webhooks/{id}/deliveries | List deliveries to one webhook (30 days, newest first): event, status, attempts, last HTTP status and error, payload.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**list_webhook_events**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#list_webhook_events) | **GET** /v1/webhooks/events | List every webhook event you can subscribe to: id, group (family), label, description, and whether a workflow can start on it.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**list_webhook_types**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#list_webhook_types) | **GET** /v1/webhooks/types | List webhook types (json, slack, discord, teams, telegram, google_chat, mattermost, zapier) with their fields and hosts.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**list_webhooks**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#list_webhooks) | **GET** /v1/webhooks | List your webhooks of every type. Optional: q (url, name, label or app), status (healthy|failing|disabled), event, kind, failing=true, page, pageSize.
*Crossly::WebhooksApi* | [**update_webhook**](docs/WebhooksApi.md#update_webhook) | **PATCH** /v1/webhooks/{id} | Edit a webhook: name, description, events, enabled, eventDelivery, and its destination fields (url, botToken, chatId, channel, headers). The type never changes.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_buyer_invite**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_buyer_invite) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/buyers/invite | Invite a buyer by email to one or more of your catalogs. Binds to their account now if they have one, or when they sign up.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_buyer_remove**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_buyer_remove) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/buyers/remove | Revoke every catalog grant a buyer (or invited email) has with you. Their terms and invoices are kept.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_catalog**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_catalog) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/catalogs | Create a catalog. visibility public | public_prices_private (prices for approved buyers) | private_discoverable (listed, opened on approval) | link (anyone with the URL) | invite (named buyers only; \"private\" is accepted as invite); autoApprove never | verified | all; the cascade terms (minOrderQuantity, casePack, leadTimeDays, verifiedOnly, priceVisibility, maxPerOrder, backorder, restockEta, preset ids) default every item; listed advertises a public catalog in the wholesale directory (never its prices).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_catalog_access_resend**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_catalog_access_resend) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/access/{grantId}/resend | Email a pending catalog invite again (an invited address with no Crossly account yet). One send per invite every 15 minutes: 429 with Retry-After inside that window. grantId is the catalogs[].grantId of GET /wholesale/buyers. Uses the X-Storefront-Id store.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_catalog_item**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_catalog_item) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items | Add your Crossly listings to a catalog (up to 500), each with an optional wholesalePriceCents, minOrderQuantity and casePack. Items already in the catalog have their terms updated.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_drop**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_drop) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/drops | Create a draft trade drop: name, dropAt (ISO; when it opens), audience trade|catalog (+ audienceCatalogId), kind release|prebook. A pre-book needs depositBps (100-9900) and shipBy (YYYY-MM-DD), and takes balanceNetDays (0-90) for the balance invoice. listingIds are your listing ids, each on Crossly. A pre-book makes its listings pre-orders, so buyers pay the deposit at checkout. Schedule it with PATCH status \"scheduled\".
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_drop_release**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_drop_release) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/drops/{id}/release | Release a live pre-book: every reserved balance becomes an invoice on account to its buyer (due in the drop balanceNetDays), emailed, and the drop ends. Safe to retry: only balances not yet invoiced are invoiced. Returns { invoiced, balanceCents, invoiceIds }.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_freight_request_answer**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_freight_request_answer) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/freight-requests/{id}/answer | Answer an open freight request with a figure in CENTS (and an optional note).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_group**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_group) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/groups | Create a customer group with a discount in basis points (up to 9000). A buyer in several groups gets the best rate, never a stacked one.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_group_member**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_group_member) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/groups/{id}/members | Add a buyer to a group by account id or by the email of an existing Crossly account.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_credit_note**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_credit_note) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/credit-notes | Issue a credit note on an on-account invoice (short shipment, damaged, pricing error, returned, goodwill, other): at most the balance due. Numbered <invoice>-CN<n>; settling the rest closes the invoice paid. Not a refund.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_late_fee**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_late_fee) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/late-fee | Add your late fee (invoice settings lateFeeBps) to an invoice by hand, once. The hourly sweep adds it automatically when an invoice goes overdue.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_mark_paid**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_mark_paid) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/mark-paid | Record a payment the buyer made to you directly: method ach | check | wire | card | other, a reference, and amountCents (omit for the whole balance). When the invoice is settled, Crossly's fee and the tax it remits are charged to your billing card.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_send**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_send) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/send | Email the invoice to the buyer again (an overdue one goes as an overdue notice), with the link to pay it.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_void**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_void) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/void | Void an invoice nobody has paid anything on (e.g. the order was cancelled). It stops counting against the buyer's credit limit.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_invoice_write_off**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_invoice_write_off) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id}/write-off | Write off what is left on an invoice (bad debt): it closes as written_off and stops counting against the buyer's credit.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_profile_ships_from_validate**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_profile_ships_from_validate) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/profile/ships-from/validate | Check a ships-from origin ({ postalCode, country, city?, region? }) with the shipping validator without saving it. 400 invalid_ships_from with the issues; 422 for an embargoed origin.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_quote_answer**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_quote_answer) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/quotes/{id}/answer | Answer (or revise) a quote: a unit price in CENTS per item you quote, an expiresAt (ISO, at most 90 days out) and an optional note. Items left out are not quoted. The buyer is notified and can accept until expiry.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_quote_decline**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_quote_decline) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/quotes/{id}/decline | Decline a quote request, with an optional reason the buyer sees.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_request_decide**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_request_decide) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/requests/{id}/decide | Approve (grants access to that catalog; optional groupId adds the buyer to a customer group) or decline (optional reason) an access request.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**create_wholesale_tier_bulk**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#create_wholesale_tier_bulk) | **POST** /v1/wholesale/tiers/bulk | One ladder for many listings: breaks as [{ minQuantity, discountBps }] off each listing's own price. Returns how many were updated and which were skipped and why.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_buyer_term**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_buyer_term) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/buyers/{buyerUserId}/terms | Switch a buyer's account terms off. Invoices already issued are unaffected.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_catalog**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_catalog) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id} | Delete a catalog with its items, grants and access requests. Orders and invoices are untouched.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_catalog_access**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_catalog_access) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/access/{grantId} | Revoke one buyer's (or invited address's) access to this ONE catalog; their other catalogs, terms, groups and invoices are kept. /wholesale/buyers/remove revokes every catalog. Uses the X-Storefront-Id store.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_catalog_item**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_catalog_item) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items/{platformListingId} | Take an item out of a catalog. The listing itself is untouched.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_group**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_group) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/groups/{id} | Delete a customer group. Its members keep any other group or account rate.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**delete_wholesale_group_member**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#delete_wholesale_group_member) | **DELETE** /v1/wholesale/groups/{id}/members/{buyerUserId} | Take a buyer out of a group.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_catalog_line_sheet**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_catalog_line_sheet) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/line-sheet | The catalog's line sheet: your company details and terms, then every item with retail and trade price, minimum, case pack and quantity breaks.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_catalog_share**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_catalog_share) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/share | The catalog's buyer link (null until you have a shop username), to share or print as a QR code.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_category**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_category) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/categories | The wholesale category taxonomy (id, label, group). A catalog or trade profile takes up to 12 ids, or \"other:<words>\".
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_drop**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_drop) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/drops/{id} | One trade drop with its listings and, for a pre-book, every reservation: buyer, item, quantity, deposit, balance, status and the balance invoice once released.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_invoice**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_invoice) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/invoices/{id} | One invoice with its lines, PO reference, payments and whether Crossly dues on it are collected.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_overview**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_overview) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/overview | Your wholesale store at a glance: the setup checklist, 30-day wholesale revenue, open orders, outstanding invoices, active buyers and pending access requests.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_profile**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_profile) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/profile | A store's trade profile (X-Storefront-Id; default store when omitted): company, tagline, logo and banner, contact, ships-from origin, lead time, order minimums (opening, first order, reorder), the default MOQ / case pack / max per order / backorder / price visibility, return, payment and shipping preset ids, invoicing and its settings, buyer requirements and the directory defaults.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_quote**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_quote) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/quotes/{id} | One quote request.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_reserve**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_reserve) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/reserve/{inventoryItemId} | How many units of an inventory item are held back for wholesale, and what retail channels advertise.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**get_wholesale_term_preview**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#get_wholesale_term_preview) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/terms/preview | The trade terms that apply to a catalog item (?catalogId&platformListingId), a catalog (?catalogId) or the trade profile alone, each with its source (item, catalog, profile, default), plus the return, payment and shipping policies they resolve to.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_buyers**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_buyers) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/buyers | Your trade buyers: catalogs they can open, customer groups, account terms, outstanding balance, wholesale spend, business verification and resale-certificate status (read-only — Crossly reviews those).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_catalog_items**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_catalog_items) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items | A catalog's items in merchandising order, each with its retail price, trade price, minimum order quantity, case pack and quantity breaks.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_catalogs**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_catalogs) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/catalogs | Your wholesale catalogs with item, buyer-grant and pending-request counts.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_drops**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_drops) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/drops | Your trade drops and pre-books: audience (trade = your approved trade buyers, catalog = whoever can open audienceCatalogId), kind (release | prebook), deposit, ship date, status, and for a pre-book its reservation totals (buyers, units, deposits taken, balances still to invoice).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_freight_requests**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_freight_requests) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/freight-requests | Freight (LTL) quote requests from buyers whose order is over your pallet threshold.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_groups**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_groups) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/groups | Your customer groups with their discount and members.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_invoices**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_invoices) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/invoices | On-account invoices, newest first, with balance due. ?status=unpaid for open, partial and overdue together.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_orders**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_orders) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/orders | Wholesale orders: Crossly orders priced through your trade terms (catalog price, quantity break or account rate) or placed on account. Fulfil them like any order.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_quotes**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_quotes) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/quotes | Quote requests (RFQ) from trade buyers: items with quantity and target price, your quoted prices and expiry. ?status=open lists the ones waiting on you or the buyer.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_requests**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_requests) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/requests | Requests from buyers to open your private or trade-only catalogs, with each buyer's business-verification and tax-exempt status. ?status=all includes decided ones.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**list_wholesale_setup_steps**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#list_wholesale_setup_steps) | **GET** /v1/wholesale/setup-steps | The wholesale setup checklist for a store: profile, ships-from, payment, returns, a catalog with items, visibility chosen, live. Each step is done because the thing exists.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_buyer_term**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_buyer_term) | **PUT** /v1/wholesale/buyers/{buyerUserId}/terms | Set a buyer's account terms: netDays (0-120) for paying on account, creditLimitCents (the most they may owe you at once; null = no limit) and discountPercentBps (their negotiated rate).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_catalog**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_catalog) | **PATCH** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id} | Edit a catalog: name, slug, description, cover image, visibility, opening-order minimum, trade gate, directory listing, active.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_catalog_item**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_catalog_item) | **PATCH** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items | Change many catalog items at once: items[] sets each row (trade price, MOQ, case pack, lead time, max per order, backorder, restock date, verified-only, price visibility, preset ids); or platformListingIds + set applies the same values to each, set.pctOffRetailBps pricing each from its retail price.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_catalog_item_by_platform_listing_id**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_catalog_item_by_platform_listing_id) | **PATCH** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items/{platformListingId} | Change one item's trade price and its own trade terms in this catalog (MOQ, case pack, lead time, max per order, backorder, restock date, verified-only, price visibility, preset ids). null clears = inherit from the catalog.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_catalog_item_order**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_catalog_item_order) | **PUT** /v1/wholesale/catalogs/{id}/items/order | Set the merchandising order: platform listing ids, first to last. Items left out keep their relative order after these.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_drop**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_drop) | **PATCH** /v1/wholesale/drops/{id} | Edit a trade drop (any create field but kind), schedule it (status \"scheduled\", with dropAt in the future) or cancel it. A live drop can only be cancelled or have endsAt / perCustomerLimit changed.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_group**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_group) | **PATCH** /v1/wholesale/groups/{id} | Rename a group, change its discount, or pause it (active=false).
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_listing_tier**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_listing_tier) | **PUT** /v1/wholesale/listings/{platformListingId}/tiers | Replace one listing's quantity breaks: [{ minQuantity ≥ 2, unitPriceCents }], each cheaper than the last and than the list price. An empty list removes them. Applies to every buyer at checkout.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_profile**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_profile) | **PUT** /v1/wholesale/profile | Save a store's trade profile. Omitted fields are unchanged; null clears. shipsFrom is checked by the shipping validator (400 invalid_ships_from). The profile is the bottom of the trade-terms cascade: catalogs and items that leave a term empty inherit it — saving never rewrites catalogs. Preset ids must be your own presets of that kind.
*Crossly::WholesaleApi* | [**update_wholesale_reserve**](docs/WholesaleApi.md#update_wholesale_reserve) | **PUT** /v1/wholesale/reserve/{inventoryItemId} | Reserve N units of an inventory item for wholesale: retail channels advertise the rest and come down when only the reserve is left. 0 clears it.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow) | **POST** /v1/workflows | Create a workflow: from blank ({ name, graph? }), from a template ({ from: \"template\", templateId, setup?, activate? }) or from an export document ({ from: \"import\", document }). New workflows start paused; activate one with POST /v1/workflows/{id}/activate. Returns the workflow and its validation issues.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_activate**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_activate) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/activate | Activate a workflow: its triggers start firing. A graph with blocking issues is refused (422, details.issues).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_approval_decide**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_approval_decide) | **POST** /v1/workflow-approvals/{id}/decide | Approve or reject a pending flow.approval request: the waiting run resumes down the approved or rejected port. 409 when it was already decided (another device, Telegram, or the timeout).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_chain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_chain) | **POST** /v1/workflow-chains | Create a multi-step workflow chain.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_chain_run_now**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_chain_run_now) | **POST** /v1/workflow-chains/{id}/run-now | Enqueue an ad-hoc run of a workflow chain.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_chain_toggle**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_chain_toggle) | **POST** /v1/workflow-chains/{id}/toggle | Flip a workflow chain between active and inactive.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_deactivate**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_deactivate) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/deactivate | Pause a workflow: its triggers stop firing. Runs already started finish.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_duplicate**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_duplicate) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/duplicate | Copy a workflow (graph, description, tags) into a new paused workflow.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_expression_preview**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_expression_preview) | **POST** /v1/workflows/expressions/preview | Resolve one param value (\"{{ $json.priceCents * 0.9 }}\") with the engine's evaluator against an item, the trigger item, earlier nodes' items or a stored run. A bad expression is an answer (ok=false with the message), not an HTTP error. Changes nothing.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_import**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_import) | **POST** /v1/workflows/import | Import a crossly.workflow export document (or { document, name }) as a new, paused workflow. Integration and workflow references are cleared (they belong to the exporting account) and listed in stripped.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_run**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_run) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/run | Run a workflow (active or paused) live now, on items you pass or on selected listings / orders / inventory items (one kind per call, up to 1,000). The graph must validate (422 with issues otherwise).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_run_cancel**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_run_cancel) | **POST** /v1/workflow-runs/{runId}/cancel | Cancel a queued, running or waiting run. 409 when it already finished.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_run_on**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_run_on) | **POST** /v1/workflows/run-on | \"Run workflow on…\": the same as POST /v1/workflows/{id}/run with the workflow named in the body.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_run_retry**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_run_retry) | **POST** /v1/workflow-runs/{runId}/retry | Retry a failed run from the node that failed: a new run (retryOfRunId) seeded from the failed run's checkpoint, so nodes that succeeded are not repeated. 409 unless the run failed.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_sale_end**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_sale_end) | **POST** /v1/workflows/sales/{saleEventId}/end | End a sale now. A sale that has not started is cancelled; a live one closes and every discounted price is put back now (reverted = how many listings were restored). 404 for a sale that is not yours.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_template_use**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_template_use) | **POST** /v1/workflows/templates/{id}/use | Create a workflow from a template, filling its setup fields; activate=true activates it when it validates.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_test**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_test) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/test | Test a workflow (the saved graph, or a draft graph you pass). mode=test runs it for real, using pinned data where a node has it; mode=dry is a safe test: write, destructive and money nodes report what they would do and change nothing. Trigger items: the ones you pass, else pinned, else the last real ones, else the trigger sample.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_validate**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_validate) | **POST** /v1/workflows/validate | Validate a graph without saving it: unknown node types, missing required params, bad ports, cycles, expression errors. blocking=true means activation would be refused.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_version_restore**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_version_restore) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/versions/{v}/restore | Restore an earlier version's graph as a new version. Pass version (the current one you read) to refuse the restore when someone saved meanwhile (409). Body optional: { version?: integer }.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**create_workflow_webhook_rotate**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#create_workflow_webhook_rotate) | **POST** /v1/workflows/{id}/webhooks/{nodeId}/rotate | Issue a new inbound URL for an on.webhook trigger node (the old one stops working). The URL, and the signing secret when withSecret=true, are returned ONCE: store them now.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**delete_workflow**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#delete_workflow) | **DELETE** /v1/workflows/{id} | Delete (archive) a workflow: its triggers stop, open runs are cancelled, run history stays readable.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**delete_workflow_chain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#delete_workflow_chain) | **DELETE** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Delete a workflow chain (cascades steps + runs).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow) | **GET** /v1/workflows/{id} | Get one workflow with its full graph and current version.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow_catalog**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow_catalog) | **GET** /v1/workflows/catalog | Everything needed to build a workflow: every node type (params, ports, output fields, risk, platforms capability), which platforms each capability covers and which of those you have connected, the expression helpers and bindings ({{ $json.listingId }}…) and the condition operators. Read this before composing a graph rather than guessing node types or param keys.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow_chain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow_chain) | **GET** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Get one workflow chain with its steps.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow_export**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow_export) | **GET** /v1/workflows/{id}/export | Export a workflow as a portable document (format crossly.workflow) that POST /v1/workflows/import accepts. Credentials and account handles are stripped; pinned test data only with pinnedData=true.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow_run**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow_run) | **GET** /v1/workflow-runs/{runId} | One run: its summary, the graph version it ran, and every node execution (status, item counts per port, timing, errors). Item data per node: GET …/nodes/{nodeId}.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**get_workflow_run_node**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#get_workflow_run_node) | **GET** /v1/workflow-runs/{runId}/nodes/{nodeId} | Every execution of one node in a run with its input and output items (each port keeps the first 50 items, 16 KB per item; truncated=true when cut). Data older than the retention window, or not kept by settings.saveSuccessData=false, is pruned: that execution has input/output null and pruned=true, and when every execution is pruned the response is { data: null, meta: { pruned: true } } (never an empty list).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_approvals**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_approvals) | **GET** /v1/workflow-approvals | Approval requests from flow.approval nodes, newest first (pending only unless status says otherwise). Decide one with POST /v1/workflow-approvals/{id}/decide.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_chains**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_chains) | **GET** /v1/workflow-chains | List workflow chains with their step graph.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_runs**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_runs) | **GET** /v1/workflows/{id}/runs | Run history of one workflow, newest first, with per-node status and item counts.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_runs2**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_runs2) | **GET** /v1/workflow-runs | Runs of every workflow, newest first. Filter by workflow, status, mode and time.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_sale_active**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_sale_active) | **GET** /v1/workflows/sales/active | Every live or upcoming sale (a sales event started by a sale.start node) with the workflow that runs it: percent off, window, and how many listings it applies to.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_templates**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_templates) | **GET** /v1/workflows/templates | Ready-made workflows (weekend sale, stale-listing markdowns, offer responder, ship-by alerts…), each a complete graph plus the setup fields its \"use\" call asks for.
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflow_versions**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflow_versions) | **GET** /v1/workflows/{id}/versions | The saved versions of a workflow, newest first (restore one with POST …/versions/{v}/restore).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**list_workflows**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#list_workflows) | **GET** /v1/workflows | List workflows, newest change first. A workflow is a graph of nodes: one or more triggers, then actions, lookups and flow nodes (if, switch, wait, approval…). Each row carries its trigger and node types and a 7-day run sparkline, not the graph (GET /v1/workflows/{id} has it).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**update_workflow**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#update_workflow) | **PUT** /v1/workflows/{id} | Save a workflow. Pass the version you edited: a save over a newer version is 409 version_conflict (re-read and merge). Saving an active workflow validates it like activation does (422 with issues).
*Crossly::WorkflowsApi* | [**update_workflow_chain**](docs/WorkflowsApi.md#update_workflow_chain) | **PUT** /v1/workflow-chains/{id} | Replace a workflow chain wholesale.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_game_pack_break**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_game_pack_break) | **POST** /v1/games/pack-break | Pack Break: your open pack of five real market pieces, or a fresh one. No prices until you pick.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_game_pack_break_pick**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_game_pack_break_pick) | **POST** /v1/games/pack-break/{id}/pick | Pick the pull you think is worth the most: every lowest open ask is revealed, and a win earns a fee-back coupon.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_showroom) | **POST** /v1/showrooms | Add a showroom. The first is included; an extra one has a monthly fee and is added in the dashboard (402 world_payment_failed here).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_showroom_placement**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_showroom_placement) | **POST** /v1/showrooms/{id}/placement | Stand a showroom on a plot your store owns or rents (403 when it does not; 409 when another showroom stands there).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_invite_accept**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_invite_accept) | **POST** /v1/spatial/invites/accept | Redeem an invite code (dashes optional); returns the room it opens.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_property_lease_withdraw**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_property_lease_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/spatial/property/leases/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw a lease offer nobody has accepted yet.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes | Start a 3D room: a collection (your items on shelves) or a warehouse (racks you arrange). Private until you make it public.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_grant**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_grant) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants | Let a specific user into one of your rooms (visitor or staff), no code needed.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_invite) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/invites | Mint an invite code to one of your rooms (visitor or staff, optional expiry in hours). The code is in this response only: just its hash is stored.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_node**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_node) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes | Add a unit (rack, shelf, bin) to one of your warehouse rooms.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_place**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_place) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/place | Pin an inventory item to a slot in one of your rooms: by warehouse nodeId, or by a collection container key and index.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_rename**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_rename) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/rename | Rename one of your rooms.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_shop_place**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_shop_place) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/shop/place | Place an inventory item in a section and display of your shop room.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_unpin**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_unpin) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/unpin | Clear every pin in one of your rooms, so it arranges itself again.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_visibility**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_visibility) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/visibility | Make one of your rooms private, unlisted (anyone with the link) or public (in the lobby and searchable).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_scene_warehouse_stock**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_scene_warehouse_stock) | **POST** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/stock | Put an inventory item (or unit) on a rack in one of your warehouse rooms.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_street_admission_accept**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_street_admission_accept) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/admissions/{id}/accept | Accept a street admission: an application to a street your store owns, or an invitation to your store shop. Seats the shop.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_street_admission_decline**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_street_admission_decline) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/admissions/{id}/decline | Decline a street admission: an application to a street your store owns, or an invitation to your store shop.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_street_admission_withdraw**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_street_admission_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/admissions/{id}/withdraw | Take back a street admission your store sent: its shop application, or an invitation from its street.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_street_apply**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_street_apply) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/apply | Ask to open your shop on a street whose owner reviews applications.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_spatial_street_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_spatial_street_invite) | **POST** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/invite | Invite a shop (by its slug) to open on a street you own.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_attendant_test_chat**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_attendant_test_chat) | **POST** /v1/world/attendant/test-chat | One test answer from your AI attendant, on your own AI key (409 ai_key_required without one).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_avatar_equip**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_avatar_equip) | **POST** /v1/world/avatar/equip | Wear something you already own -- exactly ONE of { cosmeticId } (a cosmetic you hold), { receiptId } (a Crossly purchase from your closet) or { inventoryItemId } (your own stock). The slot comes from the thing itself; slot is only a fallback for an item with no catalog shape. Replaces whatever was in that slot. Moves no money. Answers the slot and your loadout after the change.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_avatar_unequip**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_avatar_unequip) | **POST** /v1/world/avatar/unequip | Take off whatever is in one slot. Idempotent: an empty slot answers removed:false. Answers your loadout after the change.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_avatar_wearable**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_avatar_wearable) | **POST** /v1/world/avatar/wearables | Author a wearable for a body slot (a mesh profile and params, an asking price in COSM base units as a string). A submission: inactive until an operator approves it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_contest**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_contest) | **POST** /v1/world/contests | Set up a skill contest on a game placed in your place: title, game (its instance id), entry { mode, feeCents? }, prizes by place, funder, window, attempts. Paid entry needs the operator to allow it where it runs.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_contest_entry_settle_payment**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_contest_entry_settle_payment) | **POST** /v1/world/contests/entries/{id}/settle-payment | Read the fee of a paid entry back from Stripe and settle it (safe to repeat).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_contest_join**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_contest_join) | **POST** /v1/world/contests/{id}/join | Enter a contest. Free and sponsored contests take you in at once; a paid one answers a client secret to pay the entry fee.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions | Host a convention in one of your rooms: title, dates, tables and how sellers get one (invite, free, a fee in cents, or a revenue share in basis points).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_accept**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_accept) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/accept | Accept: your own invitation (no body), or, as host, an application (username).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_apply**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_apply) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/apply | Ask for a table at a convention.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_booth_apply**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_booth_apply) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/booths/apply | Apply for a booth tier (or book it at once in open mode): { tierId, note?, categories? }.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_booth_fee**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_booth_fee) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/booth-fee | Pay your booth fee to the organiser on a Worlds rail: { rail: \"cash\" | \"cbx\" }. Once per booth. Full-access token and Idempotency-Key required.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_check_in**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_check_in) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/check-in | Check in: the organiser names { exhibitorId }; an exhibitor checks themselves in with {}.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_full**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_full) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/full | Create a convention in full: venue + days (rented and paid now on the rail), hours, theme, booth tiers and prices, application or open booking, seller limits, promotion. Full-access token and Idempotency-Key required.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_invite) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/invite | Invite a seller (by shop handle) to a convention you host.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_message**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_message) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/messages | Send a convention message: { body, toUserId? }. The organiser writes to one seller or every seller; an exhibitor writes to the organiser.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_paid**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_paid) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/paid | As host, record that a seller (username) paid their table fee off-platform. Moves no money.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_seller_assign**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_seller_assign) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/sellers/assign | Move an accepted seller to another booth of their tier (swaps with whoever is there): { exhibitorId, boothKey }.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_seller_decide**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_seller_decide) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/sellers/decide | Approve (terms frozen, a booth assigned) or decline an application: { exhibitorId, decision }.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_convention_withdraw**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_convention_withdraw) | **POST** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/withdraw | Withdraw your table or decline your invitation; as host, decline someone (username).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_crew**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_crew) | **POST** /v1/world/crews | Start a crew you own: { name (2-32 chars), tag (2-5 letters or digits, shown on members' nameplates) }. Answers its id and join code. 409 when you are already in a crew.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_crew_chat**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_crew_chat) | **POST** /v1/world/crew/chat | Say a line to your crew (body: text, up to 400 characters). Returns data.messages, the crew chat ring (the last 50 lines, kept in memory only), and data.said when your line went out; a blocked or too-quick line is dropped silently. 404 when you are in no crew.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_crew_join**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_crew_join) | **POST** /v1/world/crews/join | Join a crew by the code a member gave you. 404 for an unknown code, 409 when you are already in a crew or it is full (30).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_crew_leave**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_crew_leave) | **POST** /v1/world/crews/leave | Leave your crew. If you own it, the crew is disbanded. Idempotent: leaving when in none succeeds.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_decor_design**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_decor_design) | **POST** /v1/world/decor/designs | Author a decor design (what it unlocks, a mesh profile and params, an asking price in COSM base units as a string). Inactive until an operator approves it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_decor_design_submit**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_decor_design_submit) | **POST** /v1/world/decor/designs/{id}/submit | Send one of your decor designs to the review queue.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_decor_retract**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_decor_retract) | **POST** /v1/world/decor/retract | Take a placed decor piece back into your inventory; answers the inventory after.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_dm**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_dm) | **POST** /v1/world/dms/{userId} | Send a direct message to a friend (a mutual follow; never across a block), up to 400 characters, through the world chat guard. 403 when they are not a friend, 429 when sent too quickly.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_follow**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_follow) | **POST** /v1/world/follows/{userId} | Follow somebody. When they follow you back you are friends (friends-here, invites, messages). Idempotent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_friend_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_friend_invite) | **POST** /v1/world/friends/{userId}/invite | \"Join me\": send one friend (a mutual follow) a notification with a link to the PUBLIC room you name by slug. 404 when they are not a friend or the room is not public; 429 when you invited them in the last minute.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages | Submit a game package version for review: files as { path, base64 } (game.json, thumb.png, logic/, scene/, audio/, images/). Checked like the CLI, assets quarantined, the entry document dry-run, then reviewed by a person.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package_check**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package_check) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages/check | Validate a game package exactly as a submission is checked (the shared intake report, your version still free) without storing, uploading or queuing anything: world-kit validate for an agent. Same files body as submit.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package_publish**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package_publish) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/publish | Make an approved version the one every placed copy runs (rollback or roll-forward; running copies restart on it) and list the game again if it was unpublished. Body { version }. Starter seller plan or higher.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package_restore**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package_restore) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/restore | Restore a deleted Classic game within 30 days (it comes back unpublished).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package_unpublish**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package_unpublish) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/unpublish | Take a Classic game off the Game Directory and the Carnival Plaza launcher, its store off sale. Copies people already placed keep running. Needs no plan: a lapsed creator can always take a game down.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_package_version_submit**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_package_version_submit) | **POST** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/versions/{semver}/submit | Send a draft version (uploaded with submit: false) to review: the same static check, sandbox dry run and human review as an upload. Starter seller plan or higher.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_rating**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_rating) | **POST** /v1/world/games/{id}/rating | Rate a game you played, 1 to 5 stars (rating again replaces it). Only players the runner recorded in that game can rate it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_report**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_report) | **POST** /v1/world/games/{id}/report | Report a game: reason broken | unfair | offensive | scam | other, optional detail. Staff review it; the creator is told the reason, never who.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_game_store_checkout**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_game_store_checkout) | **POST** /v1/world/games/{id}/store/{item}/checkout | Buy an item or pass from a game store: answers a client secret for the payment; the item is yours once it succeeds.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_grail_match**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_grail_match) | **POST** /v1/world/grails/match | Grail glints: send the ids on show somewhere (listingIds -- listings.id, inventoryIds, skuIds -- catalogue market SKUs; up to 400 each) and get back data.listingIds / data.inventoryIds / data.skuIds: the ones that are things you already want -- an open bid, a watched or wishlisted listing (or one selling the same SKU), a market watch, or a card your deck is missing. Read-only.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_hall_loan**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_hall_loan) | **POST** /v1/world/hall/loans | Lend a piece from your vault to this week's Hall of Grails exhibition: { vaultItemId, credit: 'name' | 'anonymous' }. It shows under your plaque until the exhibition ends; it stays in your vault (still listable and withdrawable), and stops showing if it leaves.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_home_ask**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_home_ask) | **POST** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/ask | Ask a home owner whether they would sell a piece you saw there (an item or a decor piece).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_home_guestbook**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_home_guestbook) | **POST** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook | Sign a home guestbook (one short line).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_home_guestbook_report**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_home_guestbook_report) | **POST** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook/{id}/report | Report a guestbook entry to moderation.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_hunt_start**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_hunt_start) | **POST** /v1/world/hunt/start | Start the Great Hunt this season by reading its first riddle (the Copper key's). Idempotent: starting again answers your progress unchanged. Answers the same shape as GET /v1/world/hunt. 409 when no season is running.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_asset**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_asset) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/assets | Upload one asset: kind glb (4 MB), wav (4 MB) or png (1 MB), the file as base64; purpose product (your designs, the default) or game (Carnival Studio files such as badge icons). Answers its sha256 and the path to reference from MML.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_design**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_design) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/designs | Create an interactive design: name, footprint (s, m, l or f), optional tint and description, an asking price in COSM base units (a string, six decimals) and optional max supply. Inactive until ops approve a submitted version.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_design_check**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_design_check) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/check | Check the draft: static rules, that every referenced upload exists, and a sandboxed dry run. Budgeted per person.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_design_preview**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_design_preview) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/preview | A private preview token for the draft (author only) and the socket path that runs it. Budgeted per person.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_design_submit**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_design_submit) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/submit | Freeze the draft as a new version and send it to ops review. Up to 10 a day.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_interactive_reset**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_interactive_reset) | **POST** /v1/world/interactive/reset | Wipe the stored state of an interactive piece standing in a place you own (a home or your boutique).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_job_appraisal_answer**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_job_appraisal_answer) | **POST** /v1/world/jobs/appraisals/{id}/answers | Answer a price check: your estimate of the item value in CENTS and an optional note. Moves no money; the asker may accept it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_job_shift_claim**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_job_shift_claim) | **POST** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id}/claim | Claim an open shift. You work it by being in the place while it runs.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_job_shift_release**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_job_shift_release) | **POST** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id}/release | Give a shift you claimed back to the board.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_market_buy**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_market_buy) | **POST** /v1/world/market/{kind}/{id}/buy | Buy a plot or a street.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_market_listing**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_market_listing) | **POST** /v1/world/market/listings | List a plot for sale or rent, or a street for sale.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_market_plot_rent**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_market_plot_rent) | **POST** /v1/world/market/plot/{id}/rent | Rent a plot offered for lease.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_outfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_outfit) | **POST** /v1/world/outfits | Save what you are wearing now as a named look: { name (1-32 chars), appearance? (skin, hair, hairColour, eyes, build -- unknown keys fall back to the default) }. Each worn thing is recorded as the door it goes back on by; one that no longer traces to something you own is listed in skipped and not saved. 409 when you already keep 6.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_outfit_apply**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_outfit_apply) | **POST** /v1/world/outfits/{id}/apply | Put a saved look on. Every item goes through the same equip as POST /v1/world/avatar/equip, so ownership still holds: something you no longer own is listed in skipped with the reason and not worn. Slots the look left empty are emptied (cleared). Answers the appearance of the look (for your character -- appearance lives on your device), applied, skipped and cleared. Moves no money.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party) | **POST** /v1/world/parties | Start a party with you as leader (up to 8 members).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_chat**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_chat) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/chat | Say something in your party chat (up to 2000 characters).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_invite) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/invite | Invite a friend (userId) to your party.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_invite_accept**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_invite_accept) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/invites/{inviteId}/accept | Accept a party invite.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_invite_decline**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_invite_decline) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/invites/{inviteId}/decline | Decline a party invite.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_kick**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_kick) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/kick | As leader, remove a member (userId).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_leader**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_leader) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/leader | As leader, pass the lead to a member (userId).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_leave**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_leave) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/leave | Leave your party (the lead passes on if you led it).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_wishlist**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_wishlist) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist | Add a listing (listingId, or its shop slug) to the party wishlist; answers the wishlist.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_party_wishlist_vote**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_party_wishlist_vote) | **POST** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist/{listingId}/vote | Thumb a wishlist listing up or down (null clears your vote); answers the wishlist.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_place_item_want**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_place_item_want) | **POST** /v1/world/places/{slug}/items/{itemId}/want | Tell a room owner you would buy one of their items (once per person per item; they see the count). Moves no money.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_property_plot_photo_upload_url**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_property_plot_photo_upload_url) | **POST** /v1/world/property/plots/{plotId}/photo/upload-url | A presigned upload for a plot's photo (WebP/PNG/JPEG, up to 4 MB): PUT the image to uploadUrl with the same Content-Type and exact Content-Length, then store publicUrl with PUT .../photo.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_property_plot_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_property_plot_showroom) | **POST** /v1/world/property/plots/{plotId}/showroom | Stand one of your showrooms on a plot you own or rent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_property_trespassed**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_property_trespassed) | **POST** /v1/world/property/trespassed | Ban someone owner-wide or from one showroom.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces | Open a Space (sellers only, at most two): slug, name, venue, optional membership and about.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_event**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_event) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events | As host or co-host, put an event on: title, kind, optional about, ISO start and end with offset.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_event_rsvp**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_event_rsvp) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events/{id}/rsvp | Say whether you are going to an event.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_invite**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_invite) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/invite | Invite somebody (userId) to a Space.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_join**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_join) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/join | Join a Space, or ask to when it is request-only.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_leave**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_leave) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/leave | Leave a Space.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_request_by_user_id**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_request_by_user_id) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/requests/{userId} | As host or co-host, accept or turn down a request to join.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_space_role**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_space_role) | **POST** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/role | As host, make a member a co-host or back to a member.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_staff_application_decide**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_staff_application_decide) | **POST** /v1/world/staff/applications/{id}/decide | Accept or decline an application.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_staff_assignment_rate_worker**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_staff_assignment_rate_worker) | **POST** /v1/world/staff/assignments/{id}/rate-worker | Rate the worker of one of your seats.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_staff_posting**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_staff_posting) | **POST** /v1/world/staff/postings | Post a job: a showroom, stall or convention, pay, recurrence and rules.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_staff_posting_status**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_staff_posting_status) | **POST** /v1/world/staff/postings/{id}/status | Open, pause or close a posting.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_street_owned_upkeep_pay**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_street_owned_upkeep_pay) | **POST** /v1/world/streets/owned/{id}/upkeep/pay | Pay your store's street upkeep forward.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_venue_booking_cancel**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_venue_booking_cancel) | **POST** /v1/world/venue-bookings/{id}/cancel | Cancel a hall rental: the whole rent back until 7 days before the first day, nothing after.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_work_assignment_rate_employer**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_work_assignment_rate_employer) | **POST** /v1/world/work/assignments/{id}/rate-employer | Rate the employer of a public-job shift you worked: paid on time, job as described, conduct.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**create_world_work_posting_apply**](docs/WorldApi.md#create_world_work_posting_apply) | **POST** /v1/world/work/postings/{id}/apply | Apply for a job.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_showroom) | **DELETE** /v1/showrooms/{id} | Archive a showroom: private, off its plots, billing stopped. Never deletes; the primary cannot be archived (409 showroom_primary).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_showroom_placement**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_showroom_placement) | **DELETE** /v1/showrooms/{id}/placement/{plotId} | Take a showroom off a plot (the plot stays yours).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_spatial_scene_grant**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_spatial_scene_grant) | **DELETE** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants/{grantId} | Take somebody's access to your room away, or kill an invite nobody has used yet.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_spatial_scene_node**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_spatial_scene_node) | **DELETE** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes | Remove units (by id, up to 500) from one of your warehouse rooms.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_spatial_scene_warehouse_stock**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_spatial_scene_warehouse_stock) | **DELETE** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/stock | Take an inventory item (or unit) off its rack.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_spatial_street_request**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_spatial_street_request) | **DELETE** /v1/spatial/streets/requests/{id} | Withdraw a street request you made that has not been founded yet.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_block**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_block) | **DELETE** /v1/world/blocks/{id} | Unblock somebody (by the block id from GET /v1/world/blocks).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_collection_capability**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_collection_capability) | **DELETE** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | Withdraw your declaration for a collection.
