*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_follow**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_follow) | **DELETE** /v1/world/follows/{userId} | Unfollow somebody. Idempotent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_game_package**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_game_package) | **DELETE** /v1/world/games/packages/{id} | Delete a Classic game: unpublished, then a tombstone restorable for 30 days.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_hall_loan**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_hall_loan) | **DELETE** /v1/world/hall/loans/{id} | Take one of your loans off show in the Hall of Grails.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_home_guestbook**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_home_guestbook) | **DELETE** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/guestbook/{id} | As the home owner, hide a guestbook entry.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_interactive_design**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_interactive_design) | **DELETE** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id} | Delete a draft design, or archive a submitted one (off sale; owned copies keep working).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_job_shift**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_job_shift) | **DELETE** /v1/world/jobs/shifts/{id} | Cancel a shift you posted that nobody has worked yet.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_market_listing**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_market_listing) | **DELETE** /v1/world/market/listings/{kind}/{id}/{mode} | Withdraw a plot or street from the Market.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_outfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_outfit) | **DELETE** /v1/world/outfits/{id} | Delete a saved look. 404 when it is not yours.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_party_wishlist**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_party_wishlist) | **DELETE** /v1/world/parties/{id}/wishlist/{listingId} | Take a listing you added off the party wishlist; answers the wishlist.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_place_ban**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_place_ban) | **DELETE** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bans/{userId} | Lift a ban from a place you own; they can walk in again right away.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_property_plot_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_property_plot_showroom) | **DELETE** /v1/world/property/plots/{plotId}/showroom | Take the showroom off a plot.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_property_trespassed**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_property_trespassed) | **DELETE** /v1/world/property/trespassed/{userId} | Lift a ban.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_space**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_space) | **DELETE** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | As host, close a Space.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_space_event**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_space_event) | **DELETE** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/events/{id} | As host or co-host, cancel an event.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**delete_world_staff_assignment**](docs/WorldApi.md#delete_world_staff_assignment) | **DELETE** /v1/world/staff/assignments/{id} | Take a worker off a seat that has not started.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_showroom) | **GET** /v1/showroom | Your store's showroom in Crossly World: the room and its look, fixtures with their slot grids, where every item stands (pinned by a person or placed automatically), sharing settings and the stock that can stand there. The ETag is the version.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_showroom_billing**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_showroom_billing) | **GET** /v1/showrooms/{id}/billing | A showroom's billing: included, active (paid through), past due or cancelled, and the rails a payment can use.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_showroom_by_id**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_showroom_by_id) | **GET** /v1/showrooms/{id} | One showroom: room and look, fixtures (with holders), placements, settings (hours, auto-include) and stock. The ETag is the version.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_showroom_catalog**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_showroom_catalog) | **GET** /v1/showroom/catalog | Everything a showroom can be made of: fixture displays (kind, slots per unit), room sizes, themes, surfaces, shopfronts, facades, sign styles and fonts, window moods, and `holders`: every fixture holder with `owned` (free, or a premium one you bought in the in-world cosmetics market; an unowned premium holder is refused with holder_not_owned).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_showroom_placement**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_showroom_placement) | **GET** /v1/showrooms/{id}/placement | The plots a showroom stands on, and the plots your store owns or rents that it could stand on.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_scene_attention**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_scene_attention) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{sceneId}/attention | What visitors looked at in one of your rooms: viewers, looks, inspects and dwell per item over the last days (1-35, default 30).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_scene_window**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_scene_window) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/window | The listings you chose for the street window of your shop, in slot order (left, middle, right; empty = newest first), and `choices`: your listings that can go in it, newest first.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_shop_neighbourhood**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_shop_neighbourhood) | **GET** /v1/spatial/shop/neighbourhood | Where your shop stands, and the district theme your stock fits.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_space**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_space) | **GET** /v1/spatial/spaces | The room catalogue: every space profile (what it holds, how it is sorted, its containers) and the warehouse units.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_street_admission**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_street_admission) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/admissions | Street applications and invitations: yours, and those waiting on streets you own.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_spatial_street_decor**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_spatial_street_decor) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/decor | The decor on a street you may dress, and what you can place.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_arcade**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_arcade) | **GET** /v1/world/arcade | The Arcade in Crossly World: data.cabinets (slot, where it stands, host -- piece, price_guess or empty --, game, title, blurb, the piece it hosts and where, record, packageId and thumbnail for a creator game from the Game Directory, players) and data.scores (the High Score wall: per game a title, kind -- leaderboard, record or top -- and up to five ASCII lines; a name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name, everyone else is \"A player\"), plus creatorGames (creator games with a cabinet now). Games are played in the world.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_attendant**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_attendant) | **GET** /v1/world/attendant | Your AI attendant and whether your AI key is connected.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_aurora**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_aurora) | **GET** /v1/world/aurora | The Sales Aurora over the Commons in Crossly World: every real sale on Crossly in the last data.windowDays (30) days. data.total (sales in the window), data.families (key, label, colour, count per colour family: cards, coins, jewellery, electronics, media, fashion, toys, other) and data.sales (newest first, at most 48: family, ageHours, fresh -- sold in the last ten minutes, which shimmers). Aggregate only: no item, price, seller or platform. data.updatedAt is when it was read.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_basic**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_basic) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/basics | The free basic wardrobe every account owns: 25 garments (tees, hoodie, overshirt, polo, jacket, vest, jeans, shorts, skirt, sneakers, boots, loafers, caps...) by slot, each with its colourways. Wear one by saving appearance.outfit = { top, outer, bottom, shoes, hat } as \"garment:colour\" (outer and hat may be \"none\") with PUT /v1/world/avatar/look.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_identity**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_identity) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/identity | Whether others see your name in the world (named), or not (anonymous); null if you have never chosen.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_loadout**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_loadout) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/loadout | What you are wearing: the rig, its slots (avatar_head, avatar_body, avatar_outer -- a jacket over the top, avatar_legs, avatar_feet, avatar_back) and each worn entry -- a cosmetic or a real item -- with its verified-purchase mark. Carries no price, receipt id, seller or order.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_loadout_by_user_id**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_loadout_by_user_id) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/loadout/{userId} | What somebody else is wearing, with the verified-purchase mark on each real item -- public on purpose, since a check only its owner can see is not a check. No price, receipt id, seller or order.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_look**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_look) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/look | Your world look on the account: the handle you chose (null if anonymous) and your appearance; null if you never made one.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_avatar_nft_badge**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_avatar_nft_badge) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/nft-badge | The NFT you wear on your nameplate in the world, or null.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_billing_rail**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_billing_rail) | **GET** /v1/world/billing/rails | Which rails (card, CBX) can pay in the world right now.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_capability**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_capability) | **GET** /v1/world/capabilities | What your held NFTs unlock in Crossly World: each token with the capabilities its collection declared (wear, display, emote, gate, ...). Holding is your verified wallets (their synced tokens); the capabilities of a transferred token go within five minutes.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_capability_catalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_capability_catalogue) | **GET** /v1/world/capabilities/catalogue | The capability kinds of spec crossly.nft-capabilities/1: what each does, whether only the creator of the collection may declare it, whether it needs review, and which clients honour it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_capability_wear**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_capability_wear) | **GET** /v1/world/capabilities/wear | What you wear from your NFTs and COSM copies: your pick per avatar slot (head, body, legs, feet, back) and what the room sees.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_chest**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_chest) | **GET** /v1/world/chests | The Commons chest: data.window (this 20-minute window, UTC), spot ([x, z] on the plaza), endsAt (when the next chest comes) and mine (openedThis, and opened -- how many windows you have opened one in; the Chest opener pin comes at 10). A chest is opened by walking up to it in Crossly World, never over the API.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_collection_capability**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_collection_capability) | **GET** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | The live capability declaration of a collection (404 when it has none).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_contest_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_contest_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/contests/mine | Your contest entries (best score, attempts used, payment state) and prizes (granted, or owed until their gate opens).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_control**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_control) | **GET** /v1/world/controls | Your world controls settings (look sensitivity, invert look, stick size, left-handed, camera distance, reduced camera motion, tap-to-walk, haptics) and whether you have seen the controls tutorial.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_convention**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_convention) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id} | One convention. 404 for an unknown or malformed id alike.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_convention_exhibit**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_convention_exhibit) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/exhibit | A convention from the exhibitor side: tiers with price, size, capacity and booths left, the rails that pay now, and your own booth (status, fee, paid, checked in, listings).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_convention_hall**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_convention_hall) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/hall | The hall design (booths, stage, signs, entrance, kiosk), its version and any problems.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_convention_manage**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_convention_manage) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/manage | A convention you organise: its settings, booth tiers, every seller (status, booth, fee, paid, checked in) and the venue.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_convention_status_page**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_convention_status_page) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/status-page | The status page: sellers, booths filled per tier, walk-ins, sales of brought listings, booth fees paid and owed.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_cosm_balance**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_cosm_balance) | **GET** /v1/world/cosm/balance | Your COSM balance: baseUnits (a STRING -- COSM has 6 decimals and overflows a JSON number), decimals, available (false = COSM is not provisioned on this deployment, not a zero balance).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_crew**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_crew) | **GET** /v1/world/crew | Your crew: data.crew is null when you are in none, else its name, tag, join code and whether you own it; data.members lists up to 30 members, each with the public room they are in right now when they chose to be seen by name (else null).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_crew_chat**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_crew_chat) | **GET** /v1/world/crew/chat | Your crew chat: data.messages is the last 50 lines (from, name, text, at), kept in memory only, so it starts empty after a restart. 404 when you are in no crew.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_crew_leaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_crew_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/world/crew/leaderboards | The game leaderboards (Price Guess, hunts, speed trades; this week and all time) ranked among your crew only, each member by the name the crew sees. 404 when you are in no crew.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_dm**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_dm) | **GET** /v1/world/dms/{userId} | Your direct-message thread with one friend, oldest first (the last 100 lines: id, from, text, at, mine). Reading it marks their lines read.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_emote_showable**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_emote_showable) | **GET** /v1/world/emotes/showable | The things you own that a show-off emote can hold up.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_firefly**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_firefly) | **GET** /v1/world/fireflies | Fireflies on the Commons lawn: data.day (today, UTC), today (fireflies everyone has caught today) and mine (today -- your catches today, up to perDay -- total, all your catches; the Firefly Catcher pin comes at 30 -- and perDay). Fireflies are caught by walking up to a lit one in Crossly World after dark, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_fit_stat**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_fit_stat) | **GET** /v1/world/fits/stats | Your walking storefront, last 7 days: distinct people who tapped \"Shop this\" or \"Offer\" on what you wore, and how many of each. Counts only; nobody is identified.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_fit_value**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_fit_value) | **GET** /v1/world/fit-value | What your outfit is worth on the market: cents (sum of each catalogued piece's order-book price -- last sale, else lowest ask), pieces worn, how many were priced, and share (whether you show it on your card).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game) | **GET** /v1/world/games/{id} | One live game: its manifest, its store items and which you own.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game_leaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/world/games/{id}/leaderboard | A live game leaderboard, this week and all time, by its manifest scoring (wins, best points, or best time). Only people who chose to be named show by name. scoring is null for a game that keeps no board.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game_package**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game_package) | **GET** /v1/world/games/packages/{id} | One of your game packages with every version, its review state and decision note.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game_package_stat**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game_package_stat) | **GET** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/stats | Play stats for one of your Classic games: 30-day totals, the daily series the launcher ranks on (uniques, sessions, playtime, first-play bounces, D1/D7/D28), its ranking score, test impressions and players right now. ?days=1-90.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game_package_version**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game_package_version) | **GET** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/versions | Every version of one of your Classic games: draft, pending, approved, rejected, withdrawn or cancelled, which one is live, its documents and report.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_game_template**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_game_template) | **GET** /v1/world/games/templates/{kind} | A fresh game package from a template (table, arena, object; collection is an NFT capability declaration), as { path, base64 } files: the world-kit init, for an agent with no local kit. ?id=my-game&name=My Game.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_hall**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_hall) | **GET** /v1/world/hall | The Hall of Grails in Crossly World: data.exhibition (key, title, blurb, endsAt -- a new one every Monday UTC -- and next, the coming one's title) and data.exhibits, up to twelve real catalogue items in slot order (skuId, name, brand, imageUrl, thumbUrl, retailCents, lowestAskCents, lastSaleCents: the live order book, null when the market has none). The first stands on the centre plinth. Buy, bid or watch an exhibit through the market endpoints with its skuId.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_hall_loan**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_hall_loan) | **GET** /v1/world/hall/loans | Lend to the Hall: this week's exhibition (key, title, categorySlug, endsAt), your loans on show (id, vaultItemId, skuId, name, credit, since) and the vault pieces you can lend (eligible: vaultItemId, skuId, name, imageUrl, grade) -- held in your Crossly vault, not pledged, in the exhibition's category. perMember is how many you may lend each week.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_home_placeable**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_home_placeable) | **GET** /v1/world/home/placeable | What you can place in your home: your items and your furniture.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_hunt**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_hunt) | **GET** /v1/world/hunt | Your Great Hunt: data.season (id, name, startsAt, endsAt, the title and how many earn it, live), started (you read the first clue), held (keys held, 0-3, always in the order copper, jade, crystal), keys (each with its name, colour, its riddle once it is yours to read, and when you picked it up), and cleared (your rank through the Gate this season, when, and whether you earned the title) or null. Keys are picked up by walking to them in Crossly World; nothing here takes one.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_hunt_scoreboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_hunt_scoreboard) | **GET** /v1/world/hunt/scoreboard | The Great Hunt Scoreboard, as the monolith in the Commons shows it: data.season (the running season, or the last one between seasons), finishers (the first ten through the Gate in order: rank, name, short, named, at, titled -- a name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in the world, everyone else is \"A player\") and total (everybody through the Gate this season).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_interactive_design**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_interactive_design) | **GET** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id} | One of your interactive designs: its draft source, the last static check, and every submitted version with its review state.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_job**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_job) | **GET** /v1/world/jobs | The jobs board: open staff shifts you could claim, your claimed shifts and open price checks (appraisals), as the world shows them.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_kite**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_kite) | **GET** /v1/world/kites | Kites flown on the Commons lawn: data.day (today, UTC), today (kites everyone has flown today) and mine (today -- yours today, up to perDay -- perDay, and days -- the days you have flown one on; the Kite flyer pin comes at 5). Kites are flown by standing out on the lawn in Crossly World by day, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_lantern**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_lantern) | **GET** /v1/world/lanterns | Sky lanterns in the Commons: data.day (today, UTC), today (lanterns everyone has released today) and mine (today -- yours today, up to perDay -- perDay, and days -- the days you have released one on; the Lantern lighter pin comes at 5). Lanterns are released at the lantern stand in Crossly World, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_launcher**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_launcher) | **GET** /v1/worlds/launcher | The Carnival Plaza launcher: portals for Classic games, Crossly's own card games and the Commons card tables, each with players right now, your friends inside, its ranking score (published weights, retention-weighted, first-play bounce negative) and where Play goes. Twelve ring slots: friends' pick, new & rising (guaranteed test impressions), then the top, first-party capped at 25%.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_manage_ledger**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_manage_ledger) | **GET** /v1/world/manage/ledger | World money over 7, 30 or 90 days: plot purchases, maintenance, rent, deed sales, staff wages, shifts and stalls, in and out.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_manage_overview**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_manage_overview) | **GET** /v1/world/manage/overview | Your Crossly World at a glance: shop and addresses, property, staff and shifts, conventions, stalls, 30-day visitors and money.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_manage_visitor**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_manage_visitor) | **GET** /v1/world/manage/visitors | Who came to your world shop over 7, 30 or 90 days: the funnel, attention by day, most-looked and most-wanted items.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_market**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_market) | **GET** /v1/world/market/{kind}/{id} | One plot or street in the Market: price, renders, neighbours, foot traffic, history.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_neighbourhood_claim**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_neighbourhood_claim) | **GET** /v1/world/neighbourhoods/{slug}/claim | Whether you may claim a plot on a neighbourhood street (its top sellers by recent sales), and why not.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_party_chat**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_party_chat) | **GET** /v1/world/parties/{id}/chat | Your party chat: the recent lines. 404 when this is not your party.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_board**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_board) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/boards | The Trade Floor's wall of wants, its authentication desk and its Vault Markets ticker (vaultMovers: bookKey, name, label, units, markCents, change24hBps; only the trade-floor place has them).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_collection**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_collection) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/collections/{category}/{brand}/{model} | One collection room: every piece of the set, owned or missing. 404 for an unknown room or set.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_drop**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_drop) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/drop | The owner's next drop (name, cover, time, live) and how many people are waiting in the room.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_item_interest**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_item_interest) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/items/{itemId}/interest | How many people looked at one item in this room today.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_live**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_live) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/live | The owner's live sale: their stream and the lot on the block, or null.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_seller**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_seller) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/seller | The shop whose public room this is (username and name), or null.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_place_shop_pet**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_place_shop_pet) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/shop-pet | The shop's pet kind, or null.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_property_plot**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_property_plot) | **GET** /v1/world/property/plots/{plotId} | One plot of yours: showroom, rental, value, upkeep.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_property_rental**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_property_rental) | **GET** /v1/world/property/rentals/{leaseId} | One plot you rent out, with its payments.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_property_renting**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_property_renting) | **GET** /v1/world/property/renting/{leaseId} | One plot you rent, with its payments.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_rail**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_rail) | **GET** /v1/world/rails | Which Worlds payment rails are on now: cash (card), CBX, or both.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_space**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_space) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | One Space: its layout, host, events and your place in it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_space_can_host**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_space_can_host) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces/can-host | Whether you may open a Space: sellers only, at most two active.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_staff_posting**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_staff_posting) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/postings/{id} | One of your postings, with its shifts.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_staff_reputation**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_staff_reputation) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/reputation | Your employer reputation, rated by the people who worked your public jobs.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_stand**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_stand) | **GET** /v1/world/stands/{kind}/{id} | The stands of one of your places: kind plot (the showroom standing on it; none = no stands) or showroom. Same fields as /api/v1/world/stands/shop.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_stand_shop**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_stand_shop) | **GET** /v1/world/stands/shop | The stands of your shop (its primary showroom's visible fixtures, in order): index (what a drop's world.boothSpotIndex stores), name, display, units and capacity, whether another of your drops already stands there (takenBy) and priceCents (null: your own place costs nothing extra).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_street_fund**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_street_fund) | **GET** /v1/world/streets/{slug}/fund | A street's fund: the CBX property payments on its plots over the last four weeks, and the street events it unlocked.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_street_owned**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_street_owned) | **GET** /v1/world/streets/owned/{id} | A street your store owns: tenants, applications, upkeep, look, fund, and the owning `store`.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_street_trespass_barrier**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_street_trespass_barrier) | **GET** /v1/world/streets/{slug}/trespass-barriers | Plot numbers on the street you are trespassed from.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_trial_leaderboard**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_trial_leaderboard) | **GET** /v1/world/trials/{course}/leaderboard | A Time Trials course's boards, as the board beside it in the Commons shows them: data.course, week, weekly (this week's ten fastest: rank, name, short, named, ms, at) and allTime (the ten fastest ever, each person once). A name shows only for somebody who chose to be seen by name in the world; everyone else is \"A player\". 404 for an unknown course; course 1 is commons-loop.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_trial_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_trial_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/trials/mine | Your Hoverboard Time Trials bests: data.week (its Monday, YYYY-MM-DD, UTC) and courses, each with course (id, name, parMs, rings, lengthM), week (your best lap this week in ms, your place on the weekly board, laps finished, and the split at each ring) or null, and allTime (your best ever in ms, your place on the all-time board, when) or null. Laps are timed by Crossly from where it saw you ride in the world; nothing here writes one.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_venue_quote**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_venue_quote) | **GET** /v1/world/venues/{id}/quote | What a hall costs for those days (startsOn, endsOn), the full-refund date, and whether it is free.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_wallet**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_wallet) | **GET** /v1/world/wallet | Every balance you hold in Crossly World, read-only: bucksCents (Crossly Bucks, cents); cbx (available, decimals, and the three CBX balances kept apart as base-unit STRINGS -- granted (spend here only), earned (withdrawable once matured), connected (a delegation ceiling on your own wallet, not cash on hand) -- plus pendingCents of cashback not yet matured); cosm (available, decimals, baseUnits string). available:false means that currency is not provisioned on this deployment, not that you hold none.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_wish**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_wish) | **GET** /v1/world/wishes | The Wishing Fountain in the Commons: data.day (today, UTC), today (wishes everyone has made today) and mine (madeToday, days -- how many days you have wished on, the Wishmaker pin comes at 7 -- and nextAt, when tomorrow's wish opens). One wish a day; a wish is made by tossing a coin at the fountain in Crossly World, never over the API, and nothing is spent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_work_earning**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_work_earning) | **GET** /v1/world/work/earnings | What your shifts paid, by rail.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**get_world_work_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#get_world_work_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/work/mine | Where you work: your seats, applications and your teams’ open jobs.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_game_coupons**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_game_coupons) | **GET** /v1/games/coupons | Your fee-back coupons from house-game wins: Bucks paid out of the fee on your next market purchase.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_showroom_item_search**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_showroom_item_search) | **GET** /v1/showrooms/{id}/item-search | Search this store's in-stock items (title, brand, SKU) to put on a fixture or in a window slot; each with listed / passport / multi_unit badges.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_showrooms**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_showrooms) | **GET** /v1/showrooms | Your store's showrooms, primary first: name, visibility, auto-include, pinned items, fixtures, billing and the plots each stands on.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_spatial_leases**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_spatial_leases) | **GET** /v1/spatial/leases | Plots offered for lease on every street, cheapest first: address, weekly rent and maintenance in CENTS.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_spatial_scene_grants**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_spatial_scene_grants) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/grants | Everyone who can currently walk into one of your rooms, and unused invites. Never returns codes.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_spatial_scene_warehouse_unplaced**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_spatial_scene_warehouse_unplaced) | **GET** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/warehouse/unplaced | Stock not yet on a shelf in a warehouse room you can arrange (q filters by title).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_spatial_shared_with_me**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_spatial_shared_with_me) | **GET** /v1/spatial/shared-with-me | Rooms other people let you into.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_spatial_street_leases**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_spatial_street_leases) | **GET** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/leases | Plots on a street offered for lease: weekly rent in CENTS and terms.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_attendant_usage**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_attendant_usage) | **GET** /v1/world/attendant/usage | Your AI attendant’s usage log.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_avatar_closet**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_avatar_closet) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/closet | Everything you can verifiably wear (your Crossly purchase receipts), newest first: receiptId, item title, brand, size, image, catalog sku/variant, quantity, purchasedAt. Pass a receiptId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip to put one on. limit 1-200, offset for paging.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_avatar_stock**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_avatar_stock) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/stock | Items of your OWN inventory you could wear into a room as a walking storefront: inventoryItemId, title, image and the slot it would go on. Pass an inventoryItemId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_avatar_wearables**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_avatar_wearables) | **GET** /v1/world/avatar/wearables | Wearables you have authored as a designer: id, sku, name, slot, priceBaseUnits (COSM base units, a string), maxSupply, mintedCount, active (false until reviewed). meta.available is false when COSM is not provisioned here.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_best_dressed**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_best_dressed) | **GET** /v1/world/best-dressed | This week's best-dressed board (Monday-based), top ten: name, profile username and loves. Only people still choosing to be seen by name.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_capability_declarations**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_capability_declarations) | **GET** /v1/world/capabilities/declarations | Your capability declarations, in every review state (pending, live, rejected with the note from the reviewer).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_contest_standings**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_contest_standings) | **GET** /v1/world/contests/{id}/standings | The standings of a contest now: best score first (or lowest, for a time game), ties to whoever got there first.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_contests**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_contests) | **GET** /v1/world/contests | Open skill contests on Crossly World games, soonest ending first: entry mode (free, sponsored, paid) and prizes.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_convention_exhibitors**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_convention_exhibitors) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/exhibitors | The host roster: who applied, who was invited, who is coming (by shop handle). Host only; 404 otherwise.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_convention_messages**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_convention_messages) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/messages | Convention messages you may read: the organiser all of them (?with=<userId> for one seller), an exhibitor theirs and the broadcasts.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_convention_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_convention_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions/mine | Conventions you host and conventions you are exhibiting at.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_conventions**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_conventions) | **GET** /v1/world/conventions | Conventions open to sellers now or soon: title, room, dates, and how to get a table (invite, free, fee or revenue share).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_cosm_cosmetics**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_cosm_cosmetics) | **GET** /v1/world/cosm/cosmetics | The active cosmetics catalogue, optionally by tier (premium | earned | stake_exclusive) or slot: sku, name, slot, tier, priceCents (premium only), priceBaseUnits (earned only, COSM base units string), supply, fusable, preview and what it unlocks. Read-only: cosmetics are bought on crossly.net. meta.available / meta.decimals.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_cosm_designs**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_cosm_designs) | **GET** /v1/world/cosm/designs | Your COSM designs in the world: popularity toward the free chain unlock a popular limited design earns, whether it is on chain, and your opt-out. Declare what a design does with chain cosm and the design id as the contract.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_cosm_inventory**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_cosm_inventory) | **GET** /v1/world/cosm/inventory | Cosmetics you hold, newest first: ownershipId, cosmeticId, sku, name, slot, tier, how it was acquired, bound, edition number, acquiredAt and what it unlocks. Pass a cosmeticId to POST /v1/world/avatar/equip to wear one. meta.available.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_catalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_catalogue) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/catalogue | The Decor Shop: every active cosmetic grouped by surface (avatar | home | shop | stall | street) and slot -- name, tier, priceCents (premium: the dollar price, paid in CBX at spot) or priceBaseUnits (earned: COSM, base-unit string), edition supply and remaining, tint swatch, preview, what it unlocks, owned + your edition number, and buyWith. Read-only: buying and minting happen on crossly.net/shop/decor. meta.available, meta.mint {open, reason}, meta.cbx {open, reason} (CBX opens at mint), meta.balances (your COSM and CBX, base-unit strings), meta.surfaces. Never sold by card.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_designs**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_designs) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/designs | Your decor designs and their review state. available is false until COSM is provisioned.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_inventory**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_inventory) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/inventory | Your decor as an INVENTORY. Decor is a quantity: every copy is one unit -- a placed prop (home_prop, prop) uses one per placement, a finish (wallpaper, floor, theme, shopfront, facade, sign style, street lamp/banner/paving/arch, booth canopy/pattern/lights/counter) one per space it dresses; free values are unlimited. Per item: key, value, pair (key:value), name, sku, surface, placeable, tint, owned, placed, available, placements [{ space { kind: home | shop | stall | street, id, name }, count }], copies [{ ownershipId, editionNumber }]. meta.available, meta.spaces (every space you dress). Read-only: placing and retracting happen in the app or on crossly.net.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_market**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_market) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/market | Decor copies other people are reselling (asks in COSM); optional pair filter. Read only: listing and buying stay on crossly.net.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/mine | Decor you hold AND decor you have minted to your wallet, newest first: ownershipId, cosmeticId, sku, name, surface, slot, tier, acquiredVia, bound, editionNumber of maxSupply, mintState (held | minting | minted), mintAddress, mintable, and what it unlocks. meta.available, meta.mint.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_stalls**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_stalls) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/stalls | Your stall bookings, to dress their booths.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_decor_streets**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_decor_streets) | **GET** /v1/world/decor/streets | Streets you may dress.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_dms**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_dms) | **GET** /v1/world/dms | Your friends (mutual follows) for direct messages: each with userId, name, the last line between you (text, at, mine) or null, and how many of theirs you have not read; most recent first.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_following_rooms**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_following_rooms) | **GET** /v1/world/following/rooms | The public rooms of people you follow, to walk into.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_follows**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_follows) | **GET** /v1/world/follows | Everybody you follow in Crossly World, newest first, each with mutual: true when they follow you back (a friend).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_friend_here**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_friend_here) | **GET** /v1/world/friends/here | Your friends in Crossly World right now, and where (when they share it).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_friends**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_friends) | **GET** /v1/world/friends | Your friends (mutual follows), each with the PUBLIC room they are in right now when they chose to be seen by name there (else null), and their home address slug when they let friends visit. Invisible and anonymous friends are never located.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_game_package_receipts**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_game_package_receipts) | **GET** /v1/world/games/packages/{id}/receipts | Store sales of one of your game packages, newest first: item, price paid, edition, when. Never who bought. Cursor-paged: pass pagination.nextCursor back as ?cursor=.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_game_packages**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_game_packages) | **GET** /v1/world/games/packages | Your game packages, each with its latest version and review state.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_game_rewards**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_game_rewards) | **GET** /v1/world/games/rewards | Rewards you won in games: granted, or recorded as unfunded for COSM and CBX (a game prints no value).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_games**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_games) | **GET** /v1/world/games | The Game Directory: every live, reviewed game package with its players, places, rewards, contests and the piece people place.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_home_building**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_home_building) | **GET** /v1/world/home/building | Homes whose owner made you a co-builder.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_interactive_catalogue**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_interactive_catalogue) | **GET** /v1/world/interactive/catalogue | Interactive objects: every piece that runs a shared script in a room (first-party and approved creator designs). Per item: model (the piece key a home layout or boutique prop stores), decorValue (the home_prop value its SKU claims), source (first_party | creator), name, blurb, size [x, z] metres, fallbackMesh, cosmeticId (the SKU on the Decor Shop, null when not on sale) and version (a creator design: the approved version every copy runs). Read-only: buying is on crossly.net/shop/decor.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_interactive_designs**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_interactive_designs) | **GET** /v1/world/interactive/designs | Your interactive-object designs (MML documents you wrote), newest first: id, handle, name, footprint (s | m | l | f), tint, priceBaseUnits (COSM, base-unit string), maxSupply, model, decorValue, cosmeticId, liveVersion (the approved version every copy runs; null = none), withdrawnReason, archivedAt (you archived it: off sale, owned copies keep running; null = not archived), latestVersion { version, state: pending | approved | rejected | withdrawn | cancelled, decisionNote }, inReview. `archived`: exclude (default), include or only archived designs. Read-only: writing, previewing, submitting and archiving happen in the Creator Studio on crossly.net (/studio/creator/creations/interactive/new/edit).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_job_board**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_job_board) | **GET** /v1/world/jobs/board | The public Job Board, filterable.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_job_posted**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_job_posted) | **GET** /v1/world/jobs/posted | Shifts you posted for your rooms, with who claimed each and how it went.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_market**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_market) | **GET** /v1/world/market | Plots and streets for sale or rent in the Worlds Market.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_market_street_signs**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_market_street_signs) | **GET** /v1/world/market/streets/{slug}/signs | The street's for-sale and for-rent boards.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_outfits**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_outfits) | **GET** /v1/world/outfits | Your saved outfits (up to 6, oldest first): id, name, appearance (skin, hair, hairColour, eyes, build) and items -- each worn thing as the door it goes back on by (slot, door: cosmetic | receipt | stock, id, label). Put one on with POST /v1/world/outfits/:id/apply.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_bans**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_bans) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bans | Who is banned from a place you own (your boutique or home).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_bundles**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_bundles) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/bundles | The owner's bundle deals mapped onto this room's items (itemIds null = any N from the shop). The discount is decided at checkout.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_collections**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_collections) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/collections | The owner's collection rooms: one per category, brand and model they collect.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_offers**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_offers) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/offers | What is for sale in a public room: price, condition and stock, with market references. 404 when no such room is shared.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_radar**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_radar) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/radar | What you have that someone here wants, and what is here that you want: matched on catalog ids.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_trade_tables**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_trade_tables) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/trade-tables | The open trade tables in a place: who is sitting and what is laid on the mat.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_place_trades**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_place_trades) | **GET** /v1/world/places/{slug}/trades | Items in this room open to trades, each with its trade post path.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_promoted**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_promoted) | **GET** /v1/world/promoted | Promoted places in Crossly World: boosted showrooms and conventions (Sponsored, labelled \"Promoted\"), with the token to send when one is opened.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_property_plots**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_property_plots) | **GET** /v1/world/property/plots | Plots your store owns or rents.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_property_rentals**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_property_rentals) | **GET** /v1/world/property/rentals | Plots you rent out.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_property_renting**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_property_renting) | **GET** /v1/world/property/renting | Plots you rent.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_property_trespassed**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_property_trespassed) | **GET** /v1/world/property/trespassed | People your store has banned, owner-wide or from one showroom.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_provenance**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_provenance) | **GET** /v1/world/provenance | Provenance trails: where in Crossly World your purchases were found, newest first (optionally ?listingIds=a,b -- platform listing ids). data: [{ platformListingId, slug (the listing public slug), kind (room | window | stall | space | drop | auction | worn | agent), place (its name, or null when gone), at, line -- e.g. \"Found at The Sneaker Vault, Oct 2026\" }]. Yours only.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_quests**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_quests) | **GET** /v1/world/quests | Every world quest with your progress: key, the pin it earns, goal, target, count, done, colour. meta.wearing is the pin on your nameplate (or null); meta.streak your current and best daily visit streak. Progress only comes from what the server saw -- there is no write of it.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_space_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_space_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces/mine | Spaces you belong to.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_space_requests**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_space_requests) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/requests | As host or co-host, who asked to join.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_space_whats_on**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_space_whats_on) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces/whats-on | Live and upcoming events across Spaces (streets=1 and stage=1 add street and Main Stage events).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_spaces**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_spaces) | **GET** /v1/world/spaces | The Spaces directory: seller-hosted clubs with a venue, members and events (optional venue filter).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_staff_applicants**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_staff_applicants) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/applicants | Who applied to your postings, with their worker reputation.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_staff_people**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_staff_people) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/people | Everyone working for you: your Settings team and public workers, with their seats.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_staff_places**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_staff_places) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/places | The showrooms, stalls and conventions a job can be posted at, and the pay rails that are live.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_staff_postings**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_staff_postings) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/postings | Your job postings, with their upcoming shifts.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_staff_shifts**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_staff_shifts) | **GET** /v1/world/staff/shifts | Your scheduled seats and who is on them.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_streaks**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_streaks) | **GET** /v1/world/streaks | The longest current daily visit streaks (2+ days), top ten: name and days. Only people still choosing to be seen by name.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_trade_mine**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_trade_mine) | **GET** /v1/world/trades/mine | Your trades and each item progress through the authentication gate (verified or failed).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_trade_table_binder**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_trade_table_binder) | **GET** /v1/world/trade-tables/binder | Your binder: unsold collection rows that are catalog items and may be laid on a trade mat -- key, variantId, condition, grade, portfolioId, name, image and valueCents (market value, cents).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_trade_table_listings**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_trade_table_listings) | **GET** /v1/world/trade-tables/listings | Your open Trade Block trade listings a trade table can be opened on in the world, each with its items' names.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**list_world_venues**](docs/WorldApi.md#list_world_venues) | **GET** /v1/world/venues | The Convention Center halls for rent: size, floor, booths, price per day, and the days already taken (from, to).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_showroom**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_showroom) | **PUT** /v1/showroom | Replace the whole showroom layout (room, fixtures, placements, settings). Validated as a whole: every item must be this store's, every pin must fit its fixture, no two pins share a slot. Send If-Match (or expectedVersion) to refuse with 409 version_conflict if it changed meanwhile.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_showroom2**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_showroom2) | **PATCH** /v1/showroom | Apply showroom ops in order, all or nothing: place, move, remove, fill (from a category / tag / brand / collection / search, in price, age, value or title order), sort, clear, add_fixture, update_fixture, remove_fixture, set_room, set_settings. Idempotent; refusals name the op (details.opIndex) with codes slot_occupied, item_not_in_store, fixture_capacity, unknown_fixture, unknown_display, version_conflict.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_showroom3**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_showroom3) | **PATCH** /v1/showrooms/{id} | Apply ops to one showroom in order, all or nothing (the same ops as PATCH /api/v1/showroom).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_showroom_by_id**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_showroom_by_id) | **PUT** /v1/showrooms/{id} | Replace one showroom's whole layout. If-Match (or expectedVersion) refuses with 409 version_conflict on a concurrent edit.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_scene_cosmetic**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_scene_cosmetic) | **PATCH** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/cosmetics | Redecorate one of your rooms (wall, floor, lighting, trim...). Unknown keys are dropped; the response is what survived. `clear` lists keys to reset.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_scene_node**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_scene_node) | **PATCH** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/nodes/{nodeId} | Move a rack in one of your warehouse rooms (position in metres).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_scene_setting**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_scene_setting) | **PATCH** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/settings | How one of your rooms is laid out and lit: grouping and sort keys, theme, overlay and the arrival camera. Cosmetics and the shop layout have their own writers.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_scene_shop_layout**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_scene_shop_layout) | **PUT** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/shop/layout | Save your shop room's sections and displays (validated server-side; the response is the stored layout).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_scene_window**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_scene_window) | **PUT** /v1/spatial/scenes/{id}/window | Choose up to three of your live Crossly listings for the street window of your shop, in slot order. Picks that sell drop out and the newest fill in; an empty list goes back to newest first.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_street_decor**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_street_decor) | **PUT** /v1/spatial/streets/{slug}/decor | Dress a street you own with decor you hold (validated against your inventory).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_spatial_street_owned**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_spatial_street_owned) | **PATCH** /v1/spatial/streets/owned/{id} | Who may open a shop on a street you own (open, apply or invite).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_attendant**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_attendant) | **PUT** /v1/world/attendant | Create or replace your AI attendant (BYOK: switching it on needs your AI key).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_avatar_identity**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_avatar_identity) | **PUT** /v1/world/avatar/identity | Choose named or anonymous. Rooms you are already in keep the old answer until you rejoin.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_avatar_look**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_avatar_look) | **PUT** /v1/world/avatar/look | Save your world look to the account, so every device starts from it. look: null stays anonymous. appearance.outfit = { top, outer, bottom, shoes, hat } picks the basic outfit (GET /v1/world/avatar/basics).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_avatar_nft_badge**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_avatar_nft_badge) | **PUT** /v1/world/avatar/nft-badge | Wear an NFT you hold on your nameplate (null to take it off). Checked again each time you join a room.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_capability_wear**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_capability_wear) | **PUT** /v1/world/capabilities/wear | Wear a held token on an avatar slot (refId from GET /v1/world/capabilities), or pass refId null to take it off. Its collection must declare a wear capability for that slot.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_collection_capability**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_collection_capability) | **PUT** /v1/world/collections/{chain}/{contract}/capabilities | Declare what the tokens of a collection can do (the whole declaration, spec crossly.nft-capabilities/1). As its creator (launched on Crossly or claimed) any kind; as a holder, cosmetic kinds only. Replaces your previous declaration; pending if it names an uploaded asset or an always-reviewed kind.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_control**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_control) | **PUT** /v1/world/controls | Save your world controls settings to the account (any subset of prefs; out-of-range values take the default) and/or mark the controls tutorial seen.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_convention_hall**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_convention_hall) | **PUT** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/hall | Save the hall design: { doc, expectedVersion }. Refused on overlaps, narrow aisles, too many booths or a booth with a seller removed.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_convention_my_booth**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_convention_my_booth) | **PUT** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/my-booth | Pick what your booth shows: { listingIds } — any of your live listings, up to the tier’s capacity.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_convention_status**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_convention_status) | **PUT** /v1/world/conventions/{id}/status | Open, start, end or cancel a convention you host.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_cosm_design_chain_auto_unlock**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_cosm_design_chain_auto_unlock) | **PUT** /v1/world/cosm/designs/{id}/chain-auto-unlock | Opt one of your COSM designs in or out of the free chain unlock it earns when popular (on by default). Answers its popularity.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_emote_wheel**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_emote_wheel) | **PUT** /v1/world/emotes/wheel | Arrange your emote wheel: up to 8 slots, each an emote id, a moment slot or null. Returns the saved wheel (locked or unknown ids are dropped).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_fit_value_share**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_fit_value_share) | **PUT** /v1/world/fit-value/share | Whether people in a room can see what your outfit is worth.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_home**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_home) | **PATCH** /v1/world/home | Your home: privacy (open, friends, private), theme, and wall, floor and mood decor you hold.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_home_builder**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_home_builder) | **PUT** /v1/world/home/builders | Set the co-builders of your home: { userIds } -- friends (mutual follows) only, at most 4, replacing the list ([] clears it). They may rearrange your home in the world with your things and add free furniture, never your bought pieces. New co-builders get a world notice.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_home_layout**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_home_layout) | **PUT** /v1/world/home/layout | Arrange your home: the full layout (up to 200 pieces you own), validated server-side.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_home_layout_by_slug**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_home_layout_by_slug) | **PUT** /v1/world/homes/{slug}/layout | Rearrange a home you co-build (never adding the owner bought pieces).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_interactive_design_draft**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_interactive_design_draft) | **PUT** /v1/world/interactive/designs/{id}/draft | Save the draft MML source (up to 64 KB); the response carries its static check.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_listing_holders_only**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_listing_holders_only) | **PUT** /v1/world/listings/{id}/holders-only | Reserve one of your listings for holders of a collection you declared a holders_only perk for (or release it). Checkout refuses anybody else.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_outfit**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_outfit) | **PATCH** /v1/world/outfits/{id} | Rename a saved look: { name (1-32 chars) }. 404 when it is not yours.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_pet_active**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_pet_active) | **PUT** /v1/world/pets/active | Walk one of your pets with you in the world, or none (kind: null).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_property_plot_photo**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_property_plot_photo) | **PUT** /v1/world/property/plots/{plotId}/photo | Store a plot's photo: an image of its frontage you uploaded through POST .../photo/upload-url, a render (buildKey must be the plot's current one) or an in-world capture.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_property_renting**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_property_renting) | **PATCH** /v1/world/property/renting/{leaseId} | Turn weekly autopay on or off (card leases).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_quest_pin**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_quest_pin) | **PUT** /v1/world/quests/pin | Wear a pin you EARNED from a quest on your nameplate, or { pin: null } to wear none. 403 for a pin you have not earned. Shows from the next room you walk into.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_room_holder_gate**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_room_holder_gate) | **PUT** /v1/world/rooms/{id}/holder-gate | Make your room holders-only (its collection rooms follow). Send null to open it again. Returns `{ gate }`.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_space**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_space) | **PATCH** /v1/world/spaces/{slug} | As host, change a Space name, about or membership.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_space_holder_gate**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_space_holder_gate) | **PUT** /v1/world/spaces/{slug}/holder-gate | Make your Space holders-only: only holders of an NFT collection join it (others can look in). Send null to open it again. Returns `{ gate }`.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_staff_posting**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_staff_posting) | **PUT** /v1/world/staff/postings/{id} | Replace a posting; its future unstaffed shifts are re-made.
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_stall_booking_booth**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_stall_booking_booth) | **PUT** /v1/world/stalls/bookings/{id}/booth | Dress the booth of a stall you booked (decor you hold).
*Crossly::WorldApi* | [**update_world_street_owned_theme**](docs/WorldApi.md#update_world_street_owned_theme) | **PUT** /v1/world/streets/owned/{id}/theme | Set your store's street theme.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences | Create an experience with a start place (no version yet). Counts against the plan's experience limit.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_access**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_access) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/access | Who can play: private, friends, unlisted or public (public/unlisted need a reviewed publish).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_alert_ack**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_alert_ack) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/alerts/{alertId}/ack | Acknowledge an alert.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_analytic_query**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_analytic_query) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/analytics/query | Query one custom event over ≤ 31 days: count, sum, avg or players, by day or hour, optionally grouped by a field. Groups under 50 players are suppressed.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_archive**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_archive) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/archive | Archive: closes it, drains its servers, frees its plan slot; data is kept.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_ban**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_ban) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/bans | Ban a player from your experience: { sub, duration (seconds, null = permanent), reason, displayReason?, applyToAlts? }. Enforced at join and on every running server.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_close**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_close) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/close | Make the experience private again (owner-only joins).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_config_rollback**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_config_rollback) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/configs/rollback | Instant rollback: an earlier version becomes the newest one.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_event**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_event) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/events | Schedule a live event: start and end, config values while live, an optional Messaging topic the platform publishes to at start and end.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_event_cancel**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_event_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/events/{eventId}/cancel | Cancel a scheduled event, or end a live one now.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_experiment**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_experiment) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments | Create a draft experiment: 2–5 variants of config values with traffic weights (+ holdout = 100), targeting, a primary metric and guardrails, minimum 7 days.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_experiment_ship**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_experiment_ship) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments/{experimentId}/ship | Ship a variant: its values become the base config for everyone and the experiment ends.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_experiment_start**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_experiment_start) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments/{experimentId}/start | Start: running servers begin assigning players by their per-experience id.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_experiment_stop**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_experiment_stop) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments/{experimentId}/stop | Stop: every player sees the base config again.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_join**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_join) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/join | Join as the token's owner: a server and a 60-second single-use ticket for wss …/v1/realtime/worlds/{serverId}. 202 while a new server boots.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_localization_import**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_localization_import) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/localization/import | Import a sheet: { csv } with a \"key\" column then one column per language code; rows @name and @description carry the game listing. { dryRun: true } previews (issues with line and column) without saving; an error refuses the whole import.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_moderation_appeal**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_moderation_appeal) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/moderation/appeals | Appeal a moderation decision on your game within 6 months (183 days): { kind: action | game | version | asset, targetId (from GET …/moderation/appeals targets), reason (20-4000 characters), evidence?: up to 5 https links }. The older shape { actionId, statement } still appeals an action. One open appeal per decision; a different reviewer answers and you are notified.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_open**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_open) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/open | Open the experience to players (needs a published start place).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places | Add a place (at most 10 per experience in P1).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_check**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_check) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/checks | Run the publish checks (script lint, budgets, shop-object listings) on a project. Writes nothing.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_publish**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_publish) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/publishes | Step 1: run checks and freeze a version (the saved draft, or an uploaded versionId). Blocking problems answer 400 with the list.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_start**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_start) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/start | Make this the start place (where a plain join lands).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_test**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_test) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/test | Cloud playtest: a private test version from the saved draft, joined on a fleet server (owner only, never live). 202 while it boots.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_version**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_version) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/versions | Upload a version bundle (crossly-worlds-project/1). Not live until published.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_version_publish**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_version_publish) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/versions/{versionId}/publish | Make a version current (publish or roll back). New joins land on it; older servers drain as they empty.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_version_rollback**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_place_version_rollback) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/versions/{versionId}/rollback | Make an earlier reviewed version current again. Unreviewed versions go through …/publishes.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_playtester**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_playtester) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/playtesters | Add a playtester by Crossly handle (max 200 per game).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_publish_cancel**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_publish_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId}/cancel | Give up a publish that is not live.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_publish_halt**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_publish_halt) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId}/halt | Stop a rollout: new joins go back to the current version.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_publish_rollout**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_publish_rollout) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId}/rollout | Step 7: serve the approved version to percent (1–100) of new joins; 100 makes it current. Crash-heavy rollouts halt automatically.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_publish_submit**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_publish_submit) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId}/submit | Step 6: moderation. First publishes and script changes go to a person (24 h SLA); layout-only and private publishes are approved automatically.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_reserved_server**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_reserved_server) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/reserved-servers | An access code for a reserved server of a place (teleports, matches, tournaments). The code is shown once.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_restore**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_restore) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/restore | Restore an archived game, or a deleted one within 30 days; it comes back private.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_server_shutdown**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_server_shutdown) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/servers/shutdown | Shut down servers: mode soft (default) teleports players to fresh servers of the current version first; hard stops now. scope all | outdated (only servers not on a served version).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_experience_transfer_request**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_experience_transfer_request) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/transfer-requests | Ask another Crossly account (handle or email) to take over a 3D game you own. One waiting request per game; it expires after 14 days.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_mature_verify**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_mature_verify) | **POST** /v1/worlds/mature/verify | Start the ID check Mature needs (Stripe Identity: photo ID + selfie; the date of birth is read off the ID). Returns a hosted URL and client secret, or alreadyVerified.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_parental_accept**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_parental_accept) | **POST** /v1/worlds/parental/accept | Accept a child's invite as the parent: { code, pin }. The parent must be an ID-verified adult (18+ by the date of birth on the ID). The PIN protects every later change.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_parental_children_pin**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_parental_children_pin) | **POST** /v1/worlds/parental/children/{childId}/pin | Change the parent PIN: { pin, newPin } (4-8 digits). Five wrong PINs lock changes for 15 minutes.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_parental_children_unlink**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_parental_children_unlink) | **POST** /v1/worlds/parental/children/{childId}/unlink | Remove the parent link (and its limits) with your PIN: { pin }.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_parental_invite**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_parental_invite) | **POST** /v1/worlds/parental/invite | An under-18 account asks for a parent: a one-time code (72 hours) the parent enters while signed in to their own account. Replaces any pending code.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_rating_compute**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_rating_compute) | **POST** /v1/worlds/rating/compute | Rate a set of questionnaire answers exactly as publishing does: { answers } → rating (null when refused), reasons, content descriptors, interactive notices and refusals.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_report**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_report) | **POST** /v1/worlds/reports | Report a player (targetSub), the experience, an object (targetInstanceId) or a chat line (chatLineId). Crossly attaches the server, place version and its own chat log; you send ids only.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_transfer_request_accept**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_transfer_request_accept) | **POST** /v1/worlds/transfer-requests/{requestId}/accept | Accept a transfer asked of you: the game becomes yours now. Earnings already credited stay with the previous owner; new sales pay you.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_transfer_request_cancel**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_transfer_request_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/transfer-requests/{requestId}/cancel | Cancel a transfer request you sent while it waits.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**create_world_transfer_request_decline**](docs/WorldsApi.md#create_world_transfer_request_decline) | **POST** /v1/worlds/transfer-requests/{requestId}/decline | Decline a transfer asked of you; nothing changes.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**delete_world_experience**](docs/WorldsApi.md#delete_world_experience) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId} | Delete a game (a tombstone, restorable for 30 days). Body { confirmName } = the game's name.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**delete_world_experience_ban**](docs/WorldsApi.md#delete_world_experience_ban) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/bans/{sub} | Lift your experience's ban on a player. Bans placed by Crossly safety are lifted only by appeal.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**delete_world_experience_localization**](docs/WorldsApi.md#delete_world_experience_localization) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/localization/{locale} | Remove one language (its name, description and strings).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**delete_world_experience_playtester**](docs/WorldsApi.md#delete_world_experience_playtester) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/playtesters/{userId} | Remove a playtester.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**delete_world_parental_invite**](docs/WorldsApi.md#delete_world_parental_invite) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/parental/invite | Withdraw a pending parent invite.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_chart**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_chart) | **GET** /v1/worlds/charts/{chart} | A chart: top-playing (players now), trending (this week vs last, retention-weighted), top-rated (Wilson lower bound of thumbs up, 10+ votes), new-rising, friends, most-favorited; ?genre= cuts it to one genre. Organic only: nothing paid.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_discover_experience**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_discover_experience) | **GET** /v1/worlds/discover/experiences/{key} | An experience's thumbs up/down, favourites, your vote and favourite, and whether you have played enough to vote (10 minutes). key: worlds:<id>, classic:<id>, tcg:<game>, cards:<game>.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_discover_row**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_discover_row) | **GET** /v1/worlds/discover/rows | The Play hub's rows for you: Continue playing, Friends are playing, Recommended for you, Featured (editorial, first-party capped), Top Playing, Trending, New & Rising, Top Rated, Made in Carnival Studio, Shop the world, card games, Classic games and genre rows. At most one tile is paid, always labelled \"Sponsored\", never in the first two rows or a row's first two places.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId} | One of your experiences with its places, live CCU and server count.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_access_limit**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_access_limit) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/access-limits | The game's crew-only and country limits, and the owner's crew.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_alert_setting**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_alert_setting) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/alerts/settings | Alert rules (threshold, on/off, inbox, webhook) and quiet hours.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_analytic**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_analytic) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/analytics/{report} | An analytics report: overview, engagement, retention, monetization, funnels, economy, performance, custom or costs (fleet CPU-minutes, egress, estimated cost). ?from=&to= (YYYY-MM-DD), retention ?segment=, funnels ?funnel=, economy ?currency=.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_ban**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_ban) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/bans/{sub} | The live ban on one player, or 404.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_chat_setting**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_chat_setting) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/chat-settings | The game's text chat setting and filter level.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_config**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_config) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/configs | The remote config document ({ values, rules }) and its version.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_experiment**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_experiment) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments/{experimentId} | Results per variant: n, mean, lift, fixed and always-valid 95% intervals, the verdict and a sample-size estimate.
