*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_localization**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_localization) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/localization | A game's translations: per language its name, description and in-game strings (key -> text), the English source (the game's Details and string table) and what each language still misses.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_localization_export**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_localization_export) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/localization/export | The game's translations as one CSV sheet (key, en, then each language; ?locales=es,fr picks the columns).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_maturity**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_maturity) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/maturity | The content maturity questionnaire: questions, answers, the published rating and any rating waiting for the next publish.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_moderation**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_moderation) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/moderation | Moderation status of your experience: holds (unlisted / taken down), the latest review, the automated scan, strikes, trusted-lane status, open reports and live bans.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_moderation_appeal**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_moderation_appeal) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/moderation/appeals | What you can appeal on your game and every appeal of it: data.targets (Crossly's actions, a moderation hold, rejected versions, blocked assets; each with appealableUntil and its latest appeal) and data.appeals (newest first: kind, status open | upheld | overturned, reason, evidence, the reviewer's note).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_place_draft**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_place_draft) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/draft | The Studio draft of a place ({ world, place }, revision). Falls back to the current version, or an empty place.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_publish**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_publish) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId} | One publish with its review state and rollout.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_shop**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_shop) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/shop | An open experience's way back to commerce: its creator's shop and how many live listings it has.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_experience_showcase**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_experience_showcase) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/showcase | The listings the game's page showcases, and the owner's live Crossly listings that can be picked.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_friend_playing**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_friend_playing) | **GET** /v1/worlds/friends/playing | Your friends (mutual follows) and where each is playing right now, with the link that joins them.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_game_by_game**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_game_by_game) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/{game} | A game's page, as the /games launcher shows it: the tile (players now, thumbs-up %, friends inside), description, tags, creator (Crossly for its own), rating with its content descriptors, visits, created and updated, release notes, gallery, what it sells (bought through Crossly checkout) and the Crossly World app link. :game is a Worlds experience id or any experience key.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_game_mine**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_game_mine) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/mine/{gameId} | A game's summary: status, timeline, the sub-pages you may open, health and next steps.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_game_mine_assets_used**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_game_mine_assets_used) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/mine/{gameId}/assets-used | Everything a game's published build ships (Assets used): models, sounds, images, scripts, Toolbox assets and other files, each with the places that ship it. A 3D world reads each place's current version; a Classic game its latest reviewed package version.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_game_server**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_game_server) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/{game}/servers | A Crossly Carnival game's public servers that are up: players, max, whether a stranger still fits, your friends on each (friends' servers first, then the fullest with room); and the private servers you pay for. Join one with POST /v1/worlds/experiences/:id/join { serverId }.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_link_card_game**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_link_card_game) | **GET** /v1/worlds/links/card-game/{game} | Where to play a card game: Crossly's own game, its Commons tables, and creator worlds that tag it.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_link_listing**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_link_listing) | **GET** /v1/worlds/links/listing/{slug} | \"Try it in Crossly Carnival\": open games whose live version shows this Crossly listing (its shop slug), then other games by the same seller. Links only: a sale is attributed to a game only through in-world touches its server reports.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_link_seller**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_link_seller) | **GET** /v1/worlds/links/seller/{username} | \"Games by this seller\": the seller's open games, best first.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_mature**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_mature) | **GET** /v1/worlds/mature | Mature (17+) for this account: eligible or not, the steps still to take in order (birth month, ID check, opt-in; region, parental controls and safety holds are final), and the ID age check status.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_me_pref**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_me_pref) | **GET** /v1/worlds/me/prefs | Your Crossly Carnival choices (whether games may see your Crossly wallet address).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_parental_children**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_parental_children) | **GET** /v1/worlds/parental/children/{childId} | One linked account with ?days= (1-90) of playtime per experience.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_parental_me**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_parental_me) | **GET** /v1/worlds/parental/me | This account's parent link: none, pending (an invite is out) or active (the parent's name and the limits they set).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_plaza**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_plaza) | **GET** /v1/worlds/plaza | The Carnival Plaza district: the twelve-portal ring (a sponsored portal can only take the last slot, labelled), the new & rising row, the featured stage's editorial pick and four chart boards, with players now and friends inside.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_policy_me**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_policy_me) | **GET** /v1/worlds/policy/me | What this account may do in experiences, by age and region: chat level, purchases, paid random items, trading, links, ads, contests, rating ceiling — with the reason for each \"no\".
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_rating_questionnaire**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_rating_questionnaire) | **GET** /v1/worlds/rating/questionnaire | The content-rating questionnaire (IARC-aligned): every question and choice, the three ratings (Everyone, Teen 13+, Mature 17+), what is never allowed, and the regions where Mature is restricted.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_search**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_search) | **GET** /v1/worlds/search | Search experiences by title, creator, tags and genre (typo-tolerant, synonyms such as obby / obstacle). Filters: genre, rating (everyone keeps to Everyone), device, players (one server holds at least N), minThumbs (percent), live, friends. No results suggests a chart.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_transfer_request**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_transfer_request) | **GET** /v1/worlds/transfer-requests/{requestId} | One ownership transfer request (only its two people can read it), with the actions you may take.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**get_world_transparency**](docs/WorldsApi.md#get_world_transparency) | **GET** /v1/worlds/transparency/{month} | One month's transparency report (YYYY-MM): reports by reason and source, decisions and median time to decide, actions by kind and issuer, appeals and overturn rate, publish reviews, second reviews of creator bans.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_appeals**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_appeals) | **GET** /v1/worlds/appeals | Your appeals and their decisions.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_alerts**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_alerts) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/alerts | Alerts that fired, newest first.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_audit**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_audit) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/audit | The live-ops audit log: every config, experiment, event and alert change, who made it, when.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_bans**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_bans) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/bans | Players banned from your experience (by pairwise sub), oldest first (cursor).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_config_versions**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_config_versions) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/configs/versions | Config history, newest first: who changed which keys, when.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_events**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_events) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/events | Scheduled and live events (?includeEnded=true for history).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_experiments**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_experiments) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments | A/B experiments, newest first, with their live verdict.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_moderation_reports**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_moderation_reports) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/moderation/reports | Reports about your experience, newest first (cursor). Reporter identity, account ids and other players' words are removed.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_place_publishes**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_place_publishes) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/publishes | Publishes of a place, newest first: state, checks, rating, access, review, rollout.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_place_versions**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_place_versions) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/versions | Version history of a place, newest first; `current` marks the live one.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_places**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_places) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places | The places of an experience, start place first.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_playtesters**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_playtesters) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/playtesters | People who may join while the game is private.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experience_servers**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experience_servers) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/servers | Live servers: place, version, region, players, health.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_experiences**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_experiences) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences | Your experiences, newest first; cursor-paged (?cursor=&limit=).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_favorites**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_favorites) | **GET** /v1/worlds/favorites | Your favourite experiences that are still open, newest first.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_game_mine**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_game_mine) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/mine | Every game you own or collaborate on, 3D worlds and Classic, filtered (?q&status&engine&genre&template&owner&flag&sort; sort=revenue = highest earning first). Each row carries revenueCents: your creator-ledger earnings from it in USD cents (null for Classic).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_parental_children**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_parental_children) | **GET** /v1/worlds/parental/children | Accounts you are the linked parent of: limits (max rating, chat, monthly spend), spend this month, and the last 7 days of playtime per experience.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_recent**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_recent) | **GET** /v1/worlds/recent | Continue playing: what you played in the last 90 days that is still open, newest first (from what the servers recorded).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_regions**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_regions) | **GET** /v1/worlds/regions | Live server regions with a probe URL each: GET it a few times, send the round trips as `pings` with the join to land in the nearest region.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_shop_listings**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_shop_listings) | **GET** /v1/worlds/shop-listings | Your live Crossly listings a game can show and sell (shop objects bind to their id).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_transfer_requests**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_transfer_requests) | **GET** /v1/worlds/transfer-requests | Ownership transfer requests you were asked (incoming) or sent (outgoing), newest first (?direction&status&experienceId).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**list_world_transparency**](docs/WorldsApi.md#list_world_transparency) | **GET** /v1/worlds/transparency | Published monthly transparency reports, newest first: reports received and actions taken, by type (counts only).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId} | Edit name, description, genre, rating or icon.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_access_limit**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_access_limit) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/access-limits | Limit who can play: { crewOnly } (only members of the owner's crew), { countryMode: all|allow|block, countries: ['US', ...] }. The owner and team are never limited.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_alert_setting**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_alert_setting) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/alerts/settings | Change alert rules and quiet hours.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_chat_setting**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_chat_setting) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/chat-settings | Turn text chat off or use the strict filter for everyone (never looser than the platform).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_config**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_config) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/configs | Save the whole config document. Running servers get it within seconds (ConfigService.Changed). baseVersion guards against overwriting a newer save (409).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_config_value**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_config_value) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/configs/values | Set and/or remove individual config keys.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_event**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_event) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/events/{eventId} | Reschedule or change a scheduled event; a live event may only move its end.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_experiment**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_experiment) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experiments/{experimentId} | Edit a draft (a running experiment may only rename or reword).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_localization**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_localization) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/localization | Save languages: { locales: { es: { name, description, strings: { \"shop.title\": \"Tienda\" } } } }. Each language sent replaces that language; the rest stay. English holds the source string table (its name and description are the Details).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_maturity**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_maturity) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/maturity | Answer the questionnaire: { answers }. A stricter rating applies now; a looser or first Mature rating waits for the next reviewed publish (takesEffect).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_place**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_place) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId} | Place settings: name, max players (1-50), fill target, social slots, empty shutdown.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_place_draft**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_place_draft) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/draft | Save the draft. baseRevision is the revision you loaded (0 for none); a newer save elsewhere answers 409 draft_conflict.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_place_version_note**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_place_version_note) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/versions/{versionId}/notes | Edit a release version's changelog (the game page's What's new): { notes } up to 2000 characters.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_publish**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_publish) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/publishes/{requestId} | Steps 2–5: details, rating questionnaire answers, access (private | friends | unlisted | public), money review acknowledgement.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_experience_showcase**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_experience_showcase) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/showcase | Set the showcased listings: { listingIds } (Crossly shop slugs of the owner's live listings, max 12, in order).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_favorite**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_favorite) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/favorites | Favourite or un-favourite an experience (idempotent).
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_mature**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_mature) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/mature | Switch \"Show Mature experiences\" on or off: { showMature }. On is refused while a final step blocks (too young, region, parental controls, safety hold); it never skips the ID check.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_me_pref**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_me_pref) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/me/prefs | Set your Crossly Carnival choices.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_parental_children**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_parental_children) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/parental/children/{childId} | Change a linked account's limits with your PIN: { pin, settings: { maxRating: everyone|teen, chat: on|friends|off, monthlySpendLimitCents (0 = no purchases, null = no limit) } }. Enforced server-side at join, chat, the purchase prompt and checkout.
*Crossly::WorldsApi* | [**update_world_vote**](docs/WorldsApi.md#update_world_vote) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/votes | Thumbs up, down, or null to withdraw: one vote per player per experience, counted only after 10 minutes of play (403 vote_needs_playtime before). A light ranking signal.
*Crossly::WorldsAdsApi* | [**get_world_experience_ad**](docs/WorldsAdsApi.md#get_world_experience_ad) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ads | The ads for a game's in-world ad surfaces (parts tagged CrosslyAdSurface), for the token's owner: Sponsored Listings labelled \"Ad\", served only to signed-in adults where the game's creator turned ads on, never to its team. A click is POST /api/public/shop/ads/click { token }.
*Crossly::WorldsAdsApi* | [**get_world_experience_ad_report**](docs/WorldsAdsApi.md#get_world_experience_ad_report) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ads/report | In-game ads over the last N days (default 30, max 90): impressions, clicks, charged clicks, advertiser spend and your earned share, per day.
*Crossly::WorldsAdsApi* | [**get_world_experience_ad_setting**](docs/WorldsAdsApi.md#get_world_experience_ad_setting) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ad-settings | Whether this game shows in-game ads (opt-in, default off) and the creator's share of Crossly's ad revenue in force.
*Crossly::WorldsAdsApi* | [**update_world_experience_ad_setting**](docs/WorldsAdsApi.md#update_world_experience_ad_setting) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ad-settings | Turn in-game ads on or off for this game. Ads fill the game's ad surfaces (parts tagged CrosslyAdSurface); nothing shows until it has one.
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**create_world_experience_badge**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#create_world_experience_badge) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/badges | Add a badge (at most 100 per game). name 1-50 characters, description up to 1000, iconSha256 = the sha256 a png upload answered (POST /v1/world/interactive/assets, kind png); a new icon is shown once a person approved it.
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**get_world_badge_mine**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#get_world_badge_mine) | **GET** /v1/worlds/badges/mine | Every game badge you hold, newest first, and whether your person page shows them.
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**get_world_experience_badge**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#get_world_experience_badge) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/badges | A game's badges, oldest first: name, description, icon and its review state, on/off, how many players hold each; plus the player count (the rarity denominator) and the limits. Scripts award them with BadgeService.AwardBadge(player, badgeId).
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**get_world_game_badge**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#get_world_game_badge) | **GET** /v1/worlds/games/{game}/badges | A Crossly Carnival game's badges as players see them: name, description, approved icon, how many hold it, rarity (% of the game's players) and, for you, when you earned it. :game is a Worlds experience id or key.
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**get_world_player_badge**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#get_world_player_badge) | **GET** /v1/worlds/players/{username}/badges | The game badges a person shows on their person page (only when they switched it on; Mature and non-public games never appear).
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**update_world_badge_profile**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#update_world_badge_profile) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/badges/profile | Show (or hide) the game badges you hold on your person page. Off by default.
*Crossly::WorldsBadgesApi* | [**update_world_experience_badge**](docs/WorldsBadgesApi.md#update_world_experience_badge) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/badges/{badgeId} | Edit a badge: name, description, icon (null removes it), enabled. Badges are never deleted (players keep what they earned); turn one off to hide it and stop AwardBadge.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**create_world_experience_datastore_entry_increment**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#create_world_experience_datastore_entry_increment) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key}/increment | Atomically add `by` to a numeric key (absent counts as 0).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**create_world_experience_memory_queue_item**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#create_world_experience_memory_queue_item) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/queues/{queue}/items | Add an item to a MemoryStore queue (ttlSeconds ≤ 45 days, priority 0-1000).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**create_world_experience_memory_queue_read**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#create_world_experience_memory_queue_read) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/queues/{queue}/read | Read and hide up to `count` queue items for invisibilitySeconds; remove them with the readId.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**create_world_experience_messaging**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#create_world_experience_messaging) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/messaging/{topic} | Publish a message (≤ 1 KB) to every live server of the experience subscribed to the topic.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**create_world_experience_ordered_entry_increment**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#create_world_experience_ordered_entry_increment) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ordered/{store}/entries/{key}/increment | Atomically add `by` to an OrderedStore entry.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**delete_world_experience_datastore_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#delete_world_experience_datastore_entry) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key} | Remove a key (a tombstone version; history stays readable for 30 days). Honours If-Match.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**delete_world_experience_memory_map**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#delete_world_experience_memory_map) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/maps/{map}/{key} | Remove a hash map item.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**delete_world_experience_memory_queue_read**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#delete_world_experience_memory_queue_read) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/queues/{queue}/reads/{readId} | Remove the items of one read (409 if the read lapsed).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**delete_world_experience_memory_sorted**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#delete_world_experience_memory_sorted) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/sorted/{map}/{key} | Remove a sorted map item.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**delete_world_experience_ordered_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#delete_world_experience_ordered_entry) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ordered/{store}/entries/{key} | Remove an OrderedStore entry.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_data_usage**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_data_usage) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/data/usage | Live quotas and usage: CCU, request budgets per minute, DataStore storage, MemoryStore memory.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_datastore_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_datastore_entry) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key} | The current value of a key: version (ETag \"v<n>\"), userIds and attributes.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_datastore_entry_version**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_datastore_entry_version) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key}/versions/{version} | One version of a key, including a removed one.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_memory_map**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_memory_map) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/maps/{map} | Items of a MemoryStore hash map (cursor-paged, unordered).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_memory_map_by_key**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_memory_map_by_key) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/maps/{map}/{key} | One MemoryStore hash map item with its version and expiry.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_memory_queue**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_memory_queue) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/queues/{queue} | Queue length (visible, in flight) and the head, without hiding anything.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_memory_sorted**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_memory_sorted) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/sorted/{map} | A MemoryStore sorted map in order (sortKey, then key); ?order=&cursor=&limit=.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_memory_sorted_by_key**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_memory_sorted_by_key) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/sorted/{map}/{key} | One sorted map item.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_ordered**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_ordered) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ordered/{store} | An OrderedStore page ranked by value; ?order=desc|asc&min=&max=&cursor=&limit=.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**get_world_experience_ordered_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#get_world_experience_ordered_entry) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ordered/{store}/entries/{key} | One OrderedStore entry.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**list_world_experience_datastore_entries**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#list_world_experience_datastore_entries) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries | Keys of a DataStore in key order; ?prefix=&scope=&cursor=&limit=.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**list_world_experience_datastore_entry_versions**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#list_world_experience_datastore_entry_versions) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key}/versions | Versions of a key, newest first (30-day history; the current version is kept).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**list_world_experience_datastores**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#list_world_experience_datastores) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores | The DataStores of an experience, with key counts and bytes.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**update_world_experience_datastore_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#update_world_experience_datastore_entry) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/datastores/{store}/entries/{key} | Write a new version. If-Match \"v<n>\" (or ifVersion) writes only at that version; If-None-Match: * only if absent. 412 on a mismatch.
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**update_world_experience_memory_map**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#update_world_experience_memory_map) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/maps/{map}/{key} | Set a hash map item (ttlSeconds required; ifVersion for compare-and-set).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**update_world_experience_memory_sorted**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#update_world_experience_memory_sorted) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/memory/sorted/{map}/{key} | Set a sorted map item (ttlSeconds, optional sortKey and ifVersion).
*Crossly::WorldsDataApi* | [**update_world_experience_ordered_entry**](docs/WorldsDataApi.md#update_world_experience_ordered_entry) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/ordered/{store}/entries/{key} | Set an OrderedStore entry to a whole number.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**create_world_experience_experience_event**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#create_world_experience_experience_event) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events | Schedule an Experience Event shown on the game's card: title, description, start/end (5 min–14 days, ≤ 180 days ahead), thumbnail, place. With liveEventId it follows that live-ops event's schedule.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**create_world_experience_experience_event_cancel**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#create_world_experience_experience_event_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events/{eventId}/cancel | Cancel an event (it disappears from the game's card; no reminder goes out).
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**create_world_experience_experience_event_rsvp**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#create_world_experience_experience_event_rsvp) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events/{eventId}/rsvp | RSVP (going: true) or take it back. remind: true also turns the experience's notifications on, so the 15-minute reminder can reach you.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**create_world_experience_notification**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#create_world_experience_notification) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notifications | Send an approved template to one player (sub) or up to 100 (subs). Only players who turned notifications on, 13+, not banned, at most 2 a day per player and outside their quiet hours receive it; every other send is logged as suppressed with its reason.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**create_world_experience_notification_template_retire**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#create_world_experience_notification_template_retire) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notification-templates/{key}/retire | Retire a template: sends by its key stop.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**get_world_experience_notification_opt_in**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#get_world_experience_notification_opt_in) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notifications/opt-in | Whether you turned this experience's notifications on.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**get_world_experience_notification_report**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#get_world_experience_notification_report) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notifications/report | Sends over the last N days (default 7, max 90): delivered, suppressed by reason, per template, and how many players turned notifications on.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_experience_event_upcoming**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_experience_event_upcoming) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experience-events/upcoming | Events across public experiences in the next 7 days (and live now), soonest first — the Carnival Plaza's \"Coming up\" board.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_experience_experience_event_upcoming**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_experience_experience_event_upcoming) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events/upcoming | An experience's upcoming and live events as players see them, with whether you are going.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_experience_experience_events**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_experience_experience_events) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events | Your scheduled Experience Events (upcoming, live and the last 30 days) with RSVP counts.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_experience_notification_templates**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_experience_notification_templates) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notification-templates | Notification templates: key, title and body with {placeholders}, review status (approved ones can be sent).
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_me_experience_events**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_me_experience_events) | **GET** /v1/worlds/me/experience-events | Events you are going to that have not ended.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**list_world_me_notification_opt_ins**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#list_world_me_notification_opt_ins) | **GET** /v1/worlds/me/notification-opt-ins | Every experience whose notifications you turned on.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**update_world_experience_experience_event**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#update_world_experience_experience_event) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/experience-events/{eventId} | Change an event. A started event keeps its start; a new start time earns RSVPs a new reminder.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**update_world_experience_notification_opt_in**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#update_world_experience_notification_opt_in) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notifications/opt-in | Turn this experience's notifications on or off for you. timeZone (IANA) keeps them out of your night.
*Crossly::WorldsEngagementApi* | [**update_world_experience_notification_template**](docs/WorldsEngagementApi.md#update_world_experience_notification_template) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/notification-templates/{key} | Create or reword a template. Clean words with no link are approved at once; anything the text filter flags, or any link, waits for Crossly review (24 h). Rewording re-reviews.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**create_world_experience_gallery**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#create_world_experience_gallery) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery | Add an uploaded screenshot or video (your own upload's publicUrl; a video may carry a poster image). Images are scanned at once; every item waits for a person and shows on the game's page once approved.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**create_world_experience_gallery_upload_url**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#create_world_experience_gallery_upload_url) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/upload-url | A presigned upload for a gallery screenshot (JPEG/PNG/WebP/GIF, ≤ 10 MB) or video (MP4/QuickTime/WebM): PUT the file to uploadUrl with the same Content-Type and exact Content-Length, then add publicUrl to the gallery.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**delete_world_experience_gallery**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#delete_world_experience_gallery) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/{mediaId} | Take an item off the game's page now.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**get_world_experience_gallery**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#get_world_experience_gallery) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery | The game's gallery: every screenshot and video with its review state (pending / approved / rejected with the reviewer's note), position and caption, plus the limits.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**update_world_experience_gallery**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#update_world_experience_gallery) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/{mediaId} | Change an item's caption (filtered; an approved item with a new caption goes back to review).
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**update_world_experience_gallery_order**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#update_world_experience_gallery_order) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/order | Set the order on the game's page: the ids you list first, the rest after in their current order.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**update_world_experience_gallery_role**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#update_world_experience_gallery_role) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/{mediaId}/role | Make an item the game's icon or thumbnail (an image), its trailer (a video), or put it back in the gallery. The previous holder of that role goes back to the gallery.
*Crossly::WorldsGalleryApi* | [**update_world_experience_gallery_trailer_url**](docs/WorldsGalleryApi.md#update_world_experience_gallery_trailer_url) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/gallery/trailer-url | Set the game's trailer to a YouTube or Vimeo video link (https; youtube.com, youtu.be, vimeo.com, player.vimeo.com only; any other host is 400 trailer_host_not_allowed). It waits for a person like any gallery item; the current trailer goes back to the gallery (an uploaded clip) or off the page (a link).
*Crossly::WorldsHttpApi* | [**create_world_experience_http_domain**](docs/WorldsHttpApi.md#create_world_experience_http_domain) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/http/domains | Declare a domain server scripts may call (example.com or *.example.com). It is reviewed by a person before it works.
*Crossly::WorldsHttpApi* | [**delete_world_experience_http_domain**](docs/WorldsHttpApi.md#delete_world_experience_http_domain) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/http/domains/{domainId} | Remove a domain: scripts lose access at once.
*Crossly::WorldsHttpApi* | [**delete_world_experience_http_secret**](docs/WorldsHttpApi.md#delete_world_experience_http_secret) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/http/secrets/{name} | Delete a secret.
*Crossly::WorldsHttpApi* | [**get_world_experience_http**](docs/WorldsHttpApi.md#get_world_experience_http) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/http | Http settings: declared domains with their review state, secret names (never values), limits and rules.
*Crossly::WorldsHttpApi* | [**update_world_experience_http_secret**](docs/WorldsHttpApi.md#update_world_experience_http_secret) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/http/secrets/{name} | Set a secret (UPPER_SNAKE name). Scripts reference it as Http.secret(\"NAME\"); the value is never returned.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_earning_payout_account_onboard**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_earning_payout_account_onboard) | **POST** /v1/worlds/earnings/payout-account/onboard | Start or resume Stripe onboarding for your creator payout account. Returns a one-time Stripe URL. Body: { country? } (fixed once the account exists).
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_experience_drop_table**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_experience_drop_table) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/drop-tables | Declare a drop table for a paid random item: cosmetic or in-experience outcomes only. Crossly pre-simulates and hashes it.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_experience_product**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_experience_product) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/products | Create a product: price 50-100,000 cents (a private server may be free), optional per-country prices. Paid random items need an approved drop table and are never tradeable.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_experience_product_archive**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_experience_product_archive) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/products/{productId}/archive | Take a product off sale for good. Owned passes and running subscriptions keep working.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_experience_purchase**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_experience_purchase) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/purchases | Open a purchase sheet for a product (paid access, a pass…). Returns checkoutPath: the player pays only in the Crossly checkout.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_experience_receipt_ack**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_experience_receipt_ack) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/receipts/{orderId}/ack | Acknowledge a grant your back-end made off-server. Idempotent: a second ack reports duplicate.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_me_receipt_refund**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_me_receipt_refund) | **POST** /v1/worlds/me/receipts/{receiptId}/refund | Refund a developer product bought by mistake: within the self-refund window and before the experience granted it.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_me_subscription_cancel**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_me_subscription_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/me/subscriptions/{id}/cancel | Cancel: no more charges; the benefit lasts to the end of the paid month.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_me_subscription_private_server_code**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_me_subscription_private_server_code) | **POST** /v1/worlds/me/subscriptions/{id}/private-server-code | A fresh access code for your private server (the previous one stops working). Join with reservedCode.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**create_world_purchase_cancel**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#create_world_purchase_cancel) | **POST** /v1/worlds/purchases/{intentId}/cancel | Cancel an unpaid purchase sheet.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_earning**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_earning) | **GET** /v1/worlds/earnings | Your Crossly Carnival earnings: pending, available, held, paid; next payout; what cashing out still needs.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_earning_payout_account**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_earning_payout_account) | **GET** /v1/worlds/earnings/payout-account | Your creator payout account (your own, separate from any store payout account): not_started, pending, restricted, active or payouts_unavailable.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_economy**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_economy) | **GET** /v1/worlds/economy | The published payout formula: splits, thresholds, scheduled changes (90-day notice) and guarantees.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_experience_revenue**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_experience_revenue) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/revenue | Gross, tax, refunds, chargebacks, net and your share per product, plus pending / available / paid.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_experience_store**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_experience_store) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/store | An experience's store as you see it: your regional price, odds for paid random items, what you own.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**get_world_purchase**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#get_world_purchase) | **GET** /v1/worlds/purchases/{intentId} | A purchase sheet: item, price, tax, total, odds, status.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_earning_affiliate**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_earning_affiliate) | **GET** /v1/worlds/earnings/affiliate | Real-goods orders attributed to your games: the fee, your 20% share, and whether it is posted.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_earning_ledger**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_earning_ledger) | **GET** /v1/worlds/earnings/ledger | Your creator ledger, newest first: every earning and reversal. Cursor-paged.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_earning_payouts**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_earning_payouts) | **GET** /v1/worlds/earnings/payouts | Your monthly Crossly Carnival payouts.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_experience_drop_tables**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_experience_drop_tables) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/drop-tables | Declared drop tables with their odds, simulation (10^6 draws) and hash.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_experience_products**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_experience_products) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/products | What an experience sells: passes, developer products, subscriptions, private servers, paid access (?includeArchived=true).
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_experience_receipts**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_experience_receipts) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/receipts | Every purchase: orderId, sub, product, price, net, state (paid/delivered/granted/refunded/revoked). Cursor-paged; ?since=.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**list_world_me_subscriptions**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#list_world_me_subscriptions) | **GET** /v1/worlds/me/subscriptions | Your experience subscriptions and private servers.
*Crossly::WorldsMoneyApi* | [**update_world_experience_product**](docs/WorldsMoneyApi.md#update_world_experience_product) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/products/{productId} | Edit a product: name, description, icon, price, regional prices, hourly limit, on/off sale. Kind and place never change.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#create_world_experience_place_package) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/packages | Insert a copy of a package (default: the newest version, auto-update on).
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**create_world_experience_place_package_update**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#create_world_experience_place_package_update) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/packages/{nodeId}/update | Update a copy to a version (default newest). A copy edited in the place needs force: true (its edits are replaced).
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**create_world_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#create_world_package) | **POST** /v1/worlds/packages | Create a package (v1) from one node (place JSON) or from a node in a place — which then becomes copy v1, auto-updating.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**create_world_package_toolbox**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#create_world_package_toolbox) | **POST** /v1/worlds/packages/{packageId}/toolbox | List the latest version in the Toolbox as an asset of kind package (Toolbox review and pricing rules apply).
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**create_world_package_version**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#create_world_package_version) | **POST** /v1/worlds/packages/{packageId}/versions | Publish a new version; every auto-updating, unmodified copy in your places is updated through its co-editing session. Returns what happened per place.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**delete_world_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#delete_world_package) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/packages/{packageId} | Archive a package. Copies in places keep working; they just stop receiving updates.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**get_world_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#get_world_package) | **GET** /v1/worlds/packages/{packageId} | A package with its versions.

*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**list_world_packages**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#list_world_packages) | **GET** /v1/worlds/packages | Your package library: reusable models and modules, newest first, with how many places hold copies.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**update_world_experience_place_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#update_world_experience_place_package) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/packages/{nodeId} | Turn auto-update on or off for one copy.
*Crossly::WorldsPackagesApi* | [**update_world_package**](docs/WorldsPackagesApi.md#update_world_package) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/packages/{packageId} | Rename a package or change its description.

*Crossly::WorldsRewardsApi* | [**create_world_experience_reward_definition**](docs/WorldsRewardsApi.md#create_world_experience_reward_definition) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/rewards/definitions | Add a reward scripts can grant: pin, emote, decor, item (your own COSM design), cosm or cbx (with an amount).
*Crossly::WorldsRewardsApi* | [**create_world_experience_reward_deposit**](docs/WorldsRewardsApi.md#create_world_experience_reward_deposit) | **POST** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/rewards/deposits | Move available Crossly Carnival earnings into the pool ($1-$10,000). *Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**create_world_collaboration_accept**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#create_world_collaboration_accept) | **POST** /v1/worlds/collaborations/{experienceId}/accept | Accept an invitation.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**create_world_collaboration_decline**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#create_world_collaboration_decline) | **POST** /v1/worlds/collaborations/{experienceId}/decline | Decline an invitation.

Their revenue share returns to the owner from next month.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**delete_world_experience_place_collab_script**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#delete_world_experience_place_collab_script) | **DELETE** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/collab/scripts/{nodeId} | Discard your draft of a script.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**get_world_experience_place_collab**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#get_world_experience_place_collab) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/collab | The live co-editing document of a place (node ids every editor shares), its op sequence and your role.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**get_world_experience_place_collab_op**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#get_world_experience_place_collab_op) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/collab/ops | Ops after a sequence number, grouped by batch. 409 resync when they are no longer kept: reload the session.

*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**get_world_experience_team**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#get_world_experience_team) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/team | The team: owner, collaborators with roles (owner, admin, editor, scripter, builder, tester, analyst), your role and permissions, the plan cap.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**list_world_collaborations**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#list_world_collaborations) | **GET** /v1/worlds/collaborations | Experiences you are invited to or work on, with your role.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**list_world_experience_activity**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#list_world_experience_activity) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/activity | Activity history, newest first: who changed what, when, from where (studio, api, mcp, cli); instance changes are revertible.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**list_world_experience_place_collab_scripts**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#list_world_experience_place_collab_scripts) | **GET** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/collab/scripts | Your script drafts in this place, with whether someone committed since you started.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**update_world_experience_collaborator**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#update_world_experience_collaborator) | **PATCH** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/collaborators/{userId} | Change a collaborator's role.
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**update_world_experience_place_collab_script**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#update_world_experience_place_collab_script) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/places/{placeId}/collab/scripts/{nodeId} | Save your own draft of a script (needs edit scripts).
*Crossly::WorldsTeamApi* | [**update_world_experience_split**](docs/WorldsTeamApi.md#update_world_experience_split) | **PUT** /v1/worlds/experiences/{experienceId}/splits | Owner only: split the creator share (70% of net) in basis points summing to 10000. Takes effect the 1st of next month; every party is notified. The 70% total never changes.


## Where things are

| what | where |
|---|---|
| one class per API group (`OrdersApi`, `ListingsApi`, …) | `lib/crossly/api/` |
| one model per response/request type, grouped by product area | `lib/crossly/models/<area>/` |
| a reference page per API group, every method with an example | `docs/` |

The area is the word after the verb in the model name: `GetVaultLendResponse`
is under `vault`, `ListOrdersItem` under `order`. Areas with only a handful of
models, and small shared shapes, are under `common`.

Models are named after where they appear: `GetOrderResponse` is what `getOrder`
returns, `GetOrderResponseDataLines` the type of its `data.lines`, `ListOrdersItem`
one row of `listOrders`. A type several endpoints share appears ONCE, under the
first name it was given — or, for small shared shapes like `{ ok }`, a name from
its fields (`OkResponse`). There is no per-model doc page: the model source file
is the reference, and your editor will jump to it.

## Documentation for Authorization


### PersonalAccessToken

- **Type**: Bearer authentication
